# Decomposition Map: PostgreSQL

## Introduction

PostgreSQL adalah **object-relational database management system** yang perlu dipahami bukan hanya sebagai “SQL database”, tetapi sebagai kombinasi dari **query engine, storage engine, transaction engine, security boundary, operational system, replication system, dan extension platform**. Untuk onboarding senior engineer, fokus utamanya bukan menghafal command, tetapi memahami batas tanggung jawab PostgreSQL dalam sistem: data model, consistency, locking, performance, migration, observability, backup, recovery, dan integrasi aplikasi Java.

Rujukan ini memakai dokumentasi PostgreSQL terbaru yang tersedia saat ini: **PostgreSQL 18.4 sebagai current documentation**, sementara **PostgreSQL 19 masih beta per 4 Juni 2026**. Jadi untuk sistem produksi, baseline aman tetap mengacu ke versi stabil/current kecuali perusahaan memang sedang mengevaluasi beta. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/))

## Tabular Decomposition

| Domain / Skill | Fokus Utama | Kenapa Penting untuk Senior Engineer |
|---|---|---|
| PostgreSQL Versioning & Compatibility | Memahami versi stabil, versi supported, versi beta, release notes, upgrade path. | Salah memilih fitur versi bisa memicu incompatibility di production. Saat ini dokumentasi current mengarah ke PostgreSQL 18, sementara 19 masih beta. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/)) |
| SQL Language | SQL syntax, DDL, DML, query, data type, function/operator, type conversion. | Ini fondasi semua akses data. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL memisahkan SQL language ke syntax, data definition, manipulation, queries, data types, functions, indexes, concurrency, dan performance. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/index.html)) |
| Data Modelling | Table, schema, primary key, foreign key, constraint, unique, check, enum/domain, normalization/denormalization. | Model data yang buruk akan bocor ke service layer, reporting, migration, dan integritas bisnis. |
| PostgreSQL Data Types | Numeric, text, boolean, temporal, JSON/JSONB, array, UUID, range, network, geometric, custom types. | Banyak bug aplikasi muncul dari mismatch antara tipe DB dan tipe Java/API. |
| Constraints & Integrity | NOT NULL, UNIQUE, FK, CHECK, exclusion constraint, deferrable constraint. | Constraint adalah guardrail terakhir saat aplikasi, job async, atau migration gagal menjaga invariant. |
| Querying | SELECT, JOIN, subquery, CTE, window function, aggregate, grouping, set operation. | Dibutuhkan untuk debugging, reporting, data repair, audit trail, dan validasi workflow. |
| Indexing | B-tree, Hash, GIN, GiST, SP-GiST, BRIN, partial index, expression index, covering index. | Index menentukan latency, write overhead, lock behavior saat migration, dan biaya storage. PostgreSQL secara eksplisit menyediakan bab khusus untuk indexes dan built-in index access methods. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/index.html)) |
| Query Planner & Statistics | EXPLAIN, EXPLAIN ANALYZE, planner statistics, cardinality estimate, join strategy. | Query lambat sering bukan karena SQL “salah”, tetapi karena planner salah estimasi atau statistik tidak sehat. |
| Performance Tuning | work_mem, shared_buffers, effective_cache_size, autovacuum, checkpoint, WAL, query rewrite. | Tuning harus berbasis workload, bukan copy-paste parameter. PostgreSQL docs juga memisahkan performance tips, parallel query, dan JIT sebagai area khusus. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/index.html)) |
| MVCC | Snapshot isolation, row versioning, visibility, transaction snapshot. | Core mental model PostgreSQL. Dokumentasi concurrency menegaskan bahwa developer aplikasi perlu memahami perilaku akses data ketika beberapa session berjalan bersamaan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/mvcc.html)) |
| Transaction Isolation | Read Committed, Repeatable Read, Serializable, serialization failure. | Penting untuk mencegah lost update, phantom read, race condition, dan bug approval/enforcement lifecycle. |
| Locking | Table lock, row lock, page lock, advisory lock, deadlock. | Locking memengaruhi API latency, migration safety, background job, scheduler, dan batch processing. |
| Deadlock & Contention Handling | Lock ordering, retry policy, timeout, small transaction, SKIP LOCKED. | Sistem workflow dan case management sering punya high-contention rows seperti case status, task queue, assignment, SLA counter. |
| Storage Engine Basics | Heap table, page, tuple, TOAST, tablespace, physical layout. | Tidak perlu menjadi PostgreSQL internals engineer, tetapi perlu cukup paham untuk membaca gejala bloat, vacuum, dan I/O pressure. |
| WAL & Durability | Write-ahead log, checkpoint, crash recovery, archive WAL. | WAL adalah basis durability, replication, backup, PITR, dan recovery. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menaruh WAL sebagai bagian reliability tersendiri. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/index.html)) |
| Vacuum & Bloat | autovacuum, VACUUM, ANALYZE, dead tuple, freezing, bloat. | PostgreSQL tidak “membersihkan” update/delete seperti naive storage; MVCC membuat vacuum menjadi operasi penting. |
| Server Configuration | postgresql.conf, ALTER SYSTEM, memory, connection, WAL, logging, replication parameters. | Banyak incident berasal dari konfigurasi default yang tidak sesuai workload. |
| Client Authentication | pg_hba.conf, password auth, SCRAM, certificate, host-based rules. | Ini boundary keamanan utama untuk siapa boleh connect dari mana dan dengan metode apa. Dokumentasi admin PostgreSQL mencakup client authentication dan role management sebagai area terpisah. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/index.html)) |
| Roles & Privileges | Role, user, group role, GRANT, REVOKE, default privileges, ownership. | Penting untuk least privilege, auditability, separation of duty, dan environment hardening. |
| Schema Governance | Schema ownership, search_path, naming convention, migration ownership. | search_path dan ownership sering menjadi sumber bug security atau deployment drift. |
| Backup & Restore | pg_dump, pg_restore, pg_basebackup, physical backup, restore rehearsal. | Backup bukan valid sampai restore pernah dites. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyediakan area khusus untuk backup dan restore. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/index.html)) |
| Point-in-Time Recovery | WAL archiving, recovery target time, restore command. | Wajib untuk incident data corruption, bad migration, dan accidental delete/update. |
| High Availability | Primary/standby, failover, hot standby, read replica, sync vs async replication. | Dokumentasi HA menekankan bahwa read/write database sulit digabungkan karena perubahan harus disinkronkan; tidak ada satu solusi yang cocok untuk semua workload. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/high-availability.html)) |
| Streaming Replication | WAL sender/receiver, replication slot, standby config, lag. | Penting untuk read scaling, HA, DR, dan zero/low downtime maintenance. PostgreSQL membedakan primary sebagai sender dan standby sebagai receiver pada streaming replication. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-replication.html)) |
| Logical Replication | Publication, subscription, table-level replication, migration, CDC. | Berguna untuk data migration, integration, event-driven architecture, dan selective replication. |
| Monitoring & Observability | pg_stat_activity, pg_stat_statements, logs, slow query, locks, replication lag. | Tanpa observability, tuning hanya tebakan. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menempatkan monitoring database activity sebagai bab administrasi tersendiri. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/index.html)) |
| Maintenance Operations | REINDEX, VACUUM, ANALYZE, CLUSTER, partition maintenance, statistics refresh. | Menjaga performa jangka panjang, terutama pada workload update-heavy dan partitioned table. |
| Partitioning | Range/list/hash partition, pruning, local index, retention strategy. | Penting untuk data besar, audit log, event log, historical case data, dan lifecycle retention. |
| PL/pgSQL | Function, procedure, control flow, exception, trigger function. | PL/pgSQL adalah layer procedural di server. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menempatkannya sebagai bagian server programming. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/index.html)) |
| Triggers & Event Triggers | BEFORE/AFTER trigger, row/statement trigger, audit trigger, DDL trigger. | Berguna untuk audit, derived data, enforcement invariant, tetapi bisa menjadi hidden side-effect jika tidak dikelola. |
| Extensions | CREATE EXTENSION, pg_stat_statements, uuid-ossp/pgcrypto, PostGIS, FDW. | PostgreSQL kuat karena extensibility, tetapi extension harus dikontrol dari sisi compatibility, permission, dan deployment. |
| Full Text Search | tsvector, tsquery, dictionary, GIN index. | Berguna untuk search internal tanpa langsung membawa Elasticsearch/OpenSearch. |
| JSON/JSONB | Semi-structured data, GIN indexing, hybrid relational-document model. | Berguna untuk metadata fleksibel, tetapi berbahaya jika dipakai menggantikan model relasional yang seharusnya eksplisit. |
| Migration & Schema Change | Transactional migration, backward-compatible change, lock-aware DDL, rollback strategy. | Critical untuk aplikasi Java microservice yang butuh deployment aman tanpa downtime. |
| Java / pgJDBC Integration | JDBC URL, driver, prepared statement, transaction boundary, batching, timeout, SSL. | pgJDBC adalah driver resmi untuk Java; driver ini memungkinkan program Java connect ke PostgreSQL memakai standard Java database-independent code dan berkomunikasi via native PostgreSQL protocol. ([pgJDBC](https://jdbc.postgresql.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Connection Pooling | HikariCP/PgBouncer, max_connections, pool sizing, transaction pooling caveat. | Pool salah sizing bisa membuat DB collapse walaupun aplikasi terlihat “scalable”. |
| Application Transaction Design | Unit of work, idempotency, retry serialization failure, optimistic/pessimistic locking. | Sangat relevan untuk workflow/case management: status transition harus atomic dan defensible. |
| Operational Deployment | Linux service, Docker image, Kubernetes StatefulSet/operator, managed cloud DB. | Perlu memahami mana yang tanggung jawab PostgreSQL, mana yang tanggung jawab platform. |
| Cloud / On-Prem Differences | RDS/Aurora, Azure Database for PostgreSQL, self-managed VM/bare metal, backup control, parameter group. | Fitur sama belum tentu operational control-nya sama. Cloud managed mengurangi beban ops, tapi membatasi akses internals tertentu. |

## Roadmap Alur Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Fokus | Target Pemahaman |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | SQL & Data Modelling | Bisa membaca schema, constraint, query bisnis, dan relasi antar-entity. |

| 4 | Admin Basics | Bisa memahami config, roles, auth, logging, monitoring, dan maintenance. |
| 5 | Backup, Restore, WAL | Bisa membedakan dump, physical backup, WAL archive, dan PITR. |

| 7 | Java Integration | Bisa mendesain boundary JDBC/pool/transaction/timeouts secara aman. |
| 8 | PL/pgSQL & Triggers | Bisa membaca server-side logic tanpa membuat hidden coupling. |
| 9 | Production Patterns | Migration strategy, partitioning, observability, incident debugging, capacity planning. |

Lanjut paling tepat: **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL MVCC, Transaction Isolation, Locking, Deadlock, dan Retry Pattern untuk aplikasi Java**. Ini paling berpengaruh untuk sistem regulatory/case management karena langsung terkait correctness, escalation workflow, SLA processing, dan auditability.

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL SQL Language

## 1. Scope

**PostgreSQL SQL Language** adalah lapisan bahasa untuk mendefinisikan data, memanipulasi data, membaca data, memakai tipe data, memakai fungsi/operator, mengatur transaksi, dan mengekspresikan query performan. Ini berbeda dari **PL/pgSQL**, yang merupakan procedural language untuk function, procedure, dan trigger. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL current membagi SQL Language ke area: SQL syntax, data definition, data manipulation, queries, data types, functions/operators, type conversion, indexes, full text search, concurrency control, performance tips, dan parallel query. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL SQL Language))
    SQL Syntax
      Tokens
      Identifiers
      Literals
      Operators
      Expressions
      Function Calls
    DDL
      CREATE
      ALTER
      DROP
      Schema
      Table
      View
      Constraint
      Index
    DML
      INSERT
      UPDATE
      DELETE
      MERGE
      RETURNING
      UPSERT
    Query
      SELECT
      FROM
      JOIN
      WHERE
      GROUP BY
      HAVING
      WINDOW
      ORDER BY
      LIMIT
    Data Model
      Types
      Constraints
      Defaults
      Generated Columns
      Sequences
      Identity
    Advanced Querying
      CTE
      Recursive Query
      Subquery
      LATERAL
      Set Operations
      Window Functions
    Transaction Semantics
      BEGIN
      COMMIT
      ROLLBACK
      SAVEPOINT
      Isolation
      Row Locks
    PostgreSQL Extensions
      RETURNING
      ON CONFLICT
      DISTINCT ON
      JSON/JSONB
      Arrays
      Range Types
      :: Cast
```

---

# 3. SQL Syntax Essentials

PostgreSQL SQL input terdiri dari command yang dibentuk oleh token dan biasanya diakhiri dengan semicolon `;`. Token dapat berupa keyword, identifier, quoted identifier, literal, operator, atau special character. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-syntax-lexical.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Basic command shape

```sql
SELECT now();

CREATE TABLE app_user (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    username text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

## Identifier rules

| Bentuk | Contoh | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Unquoted identifier | `app_user`, `created_at` | Praktik terbaik: lowercase + snake_case. |
| Quoted identifier | `"User"`, `"Created At"` | Case-sensitive, sering menyulitkan query dan migration. |
| Schema-qualified name | `public.app_user` | Aman untuk menghindari ambiguity `search_path`. |

**Praktik senior:** hindari quoted identifier untuk object aplikasi kecuali benar-benar perlu.

```sql
-- Recommended
SELECT created_at FROM app_user;

-- Avoid unless intentionally created this way
SELECT "Created At" FROM "User";
```

## Literals & casting

PostgreSQL mendukung cast SQL-standard `CAST(x AS type)` dan juga syntax historis PostgreSQL `x::type`. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyebut `::` sebagai historical PostgreSQL usage. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-syntax-lexical.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
SELECT CAST('2026-07-12' AS date);

SELECT '2026-07-12'::date;

SELECT 42::bigint;
```

---

# 4. DDL: Data Definition Language

DDL dipakai untuk membuat dan mengubah struktur database: table, schema, constraint, view, function, trigger, partition, dan object lain. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menjelaskan Data Definition sebagai area untuk membuat struktur yang menyimpan data, terutama table, constraint, schema, privilege, inheritance, partitioning, view, function, dan trigger. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## CREATE TABLE

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_file (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    status text NOT NULL,
    priority integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

## ALTER TABLE

`ALTER TABLE` mengubah definisi table. Perlu hati-hati karena beberapa subcommand mengambil lock yang kuat; dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyebut lock level bisa berbeda per subform dan `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` digunakan kecuali dinyatakan lain. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_file
ADD COLUMN assigned_user_id bigint;

ALTER TABLE case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT case_file_status_check
CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT', 'OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'CLOSED'));
```

## DROP

```sql
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS temp_import_case;

DROP VIEW IF EXISTS active_case_view;
```

## Schema

```sql
CREATE SCHEMA enforcement;

CREATE TABLE enforcement.case_file (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE
);
```

**Praktik senior:** gunakan schema untuk boundary domain, bukan hanya `public`.

---

# 5. Constraints: Guardrail Invariant

Constraint adalah cara database menjaga invariant ketika aplikasi, batch job, migration, atau service lain gagal menjaga validitas data.

| Constraint | Contoh | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|---|
| `PRIMARY KEY` | `id bigint PRIMARY KEY` | Identitas row. |
| `UNIQUE` | `case_number text UNIQUE` | Business key harus unik. |
| `NOT NULL` | `status text NOT NULL` | Field wajib. |
| `CHECK` | `priority >= 0` | Validasi domain sederhana. |
| `FOREIGN KEY` | `user_id REFERENCES app_user(id)` | Referential integrity. |
| `EXCLUDE` | no-overlap range | Constraint lanjutan, misalnya booking/time range. |

```sql
CREATE TABLE task_assignment (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL REFERENCES case_file(id),
    assignee_id bigint NOT NULL,
    valid_from timestamptz NOT NULL,
    valid_to timestamptz,
    CHECK (valid_to IS NULL OR valid_to > valid_from)
);
```

---

# 6. DML: Data Manipulation Language

DML dipakai untuk mengisi dan mengubah data: `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, `MERGE`, dan sering dikombinasikan dengan `RETURNING`. PostgreSQL mendukung `RETURNING` pada `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, dan `MERGE`, sehingga aplikasi bisa mengambil data hasil mutasi tanpa query tambahan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/dml-returning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## INSERT

```sql
INSERT INTO case_file (case_number, status, priority)
VALUES ('CASE-2026-001', 'OPEN', 10)
RETURNING id, created_at;
```

## Multi-row INSERT

```sql
INSERT INTO case_file (case_number, status, priority)
VALUES
    ('CASE-2026-002', 'OPEN', 5),
    ('CASE-2026-003', 'DRAFT', 1)
RETURNING id, case_number;
```

## UPDATE

`UPDATE` mengubah column pada row yang memenuhi kondisi; column yang tidak disebut di `SET` tetap menyimpan nilai lama. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-update.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
UPDATE case_file
SET status = 'IN_REVIEW',
    updated_at = now()
WHERE id = 1001
RETURNING id, status, updated_at;
```

## DELETE

`DELETE` menghapus row yang memenuhi `WHERE`; jika `WHERE` tidak ada, seluruh row table akan dihapus. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-delete.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
DELETE FROM case_file
WHERE status = 'DRAFT'
  AND created_at < now() - interval '90 days'
RETURNING id, case_number;
```

## UPSERT: INSERT ... ON CONFLICT

`ON CONFLICT` dipakai untuk menangani unique/exclusion conflict dengan `DO NOTHING` atau `DO UPDATE`. PostgreSQL mendokumentasikan `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` sebagai atomic `INSERT` atau `UPDATE` outcome, sering disebut UPSERT. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
INSERT INTO case_file (case_number, status, priority)
VALUES ('CASE-2026-001', 'OPEN', 10)
ON CONFLICT (case_number)
DO UPDATE SET
    status = EXCLUDED.status,
    priority = EXCLUDED.priority,
    updated_at = now()
RETURNING id, case_number, status;
```

## MERGE

```sql
MERGE INTO case_file AS target
USING staging_case_file AS source
ON target.case_number = source.case_number
WHEN MATCHED THEN
    UPDATE SET status = source.status, updated_at = now()
WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
    INSERT (case_number, status, priority)
    VALUES (source.case_number, source.status, source.priority)
RETURNING target.id, target.case_number;
```

---

# 7. SELECT Query Processing

`SELECT` mengambil row dari nol atau lebih table. Secara konseptual, PostgreSQL memproses `WITH`, `FROM`, `WHERE`, `GROUP BY`, `HAVING`, select list, `DISTINCT`, set operations, `ORDER BY`, `LIMIT/OFFSET`, lalu row locking seperti `FOR UPDATE`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Canonical SELECT shape

```sql
WITH recent_cases AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM case_file
    WHERE created_at >= now() - interval '30 days'
)
SELECT
    status,
    count(*) AS total_cases
FROM recent_cases
WHERE priority >= 5
GROUP BY status
HAVING count(*) > 0
ORDER BY total_cases DESC
LIMIT 10;
```

## Logical query pipeline

| Stage | Clause | Fungsi |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `WITH` | Definisikan CTE sementara. |
| 2 | `FROM` | Tentukan sumber data. |
| 3 | `JOIN` / `LATERAL` | Gabungkan sumber data. |
| 4 | `WHERE` | Filter row sebelum grouping. |
| 5 | `GROUP BY` | Bentuk group. |
| 6 | Aggregate | Hitung agregasi. |
| 7 | `HAVING` | Filter hasil group. |
| 8 | `SELECT` | Hitung output expression. |
| 9 | `DISTINCT` | Hilangkan duplicate. |
| 10 | `ORDER BY` | Urutkan hasil. |
| 11 | `LIMIT/OFFSET` | Potong hasil. |
| 12 | `FOR UPDATE` | Lock row terpilih. |

---

# 8. Table Expressions & JOIN

Table expression menghasilkan virtual table dari `FROM`, lalu dapat diproses oleh `WHERE`, `GROUP BY`, dan `HAVING`. PostgreSQL mendokumentasikan `FROM` sebagai sumber table reference, subquery, join construct, atau kombinasi kompleks. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-table-expressions.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## INNER JOIN

```sql
SELECT c.id, c.case_number, a.assignee_id
FROM case_file c
JOIN task_assignment a ON a.case_id = c.id
WHERE c.status = 'OPEN';
```

## LEFT JOIN

```sql
SELECT c.id, c.case_number, a.assignee_id
FROM case_file c
LEFT JOIN task_assignment a ON a.case_id = c.id;
```

## Anti-join pattern

```sql
SELECT c.*
FROM case_file c
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM task_assignment a
    WHERE a.case_id = c.id
);
```

## LATERAL

`LATERAL` berguna ketika subquery di `FROM` perlu mereferensikan row dari table sebelumnya.

```sql
SELECT c.id, c.case_number, latest_event.event_type, latest_event.created_at
FROM case_file c
LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT e.event_type, e.created_at
    FROM case_event e
    WHERE e.case_id = c.id
    ORDER BY e.created_at DESC
    LIMIT 1
) latest_event ON true;
```

---

# 9. WHERE, NULL, Boolean Logic

SQL memakai three-valued logic: `TRUE`, `FALSE`, dan `UNKNOWN`. Kesalahan umum: membandingkan `NULL` dengan `=`.

```sql
-- Salah
SELECT * FROM case_file WHERE closed_at = NULL;

-- Benar
SELECT * FROM case_file WHERE closed_at IS NULL;

SELECT * FROM case_file WHERE closed_at IS NOT NULL;
```

## Common predicates

```sql
WHERE status = 'OPEN'

WHERE priority BETWEEN 5 AND 10

WHERE status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW')

WHERE case_number LIKE 'CASE-2026-%'

WHERE created_at >= now() - interval '7 days'

WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM case_event e
    WHERE e.case_id = case_file.id
)
```

---

# 10. Aggregation

```sql
SELECT
    status,
    count(*) AS total,
    min(created_at) AS oldest_case,
    max(created_at) AS newest_case
FROM case_file
GROUP BY status
ORDER BY total DESC;
```

## Conditional aggregate

```sql
SELECT
    count(*) AS total_cases,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'OPEN') AS open_cases,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'CLOSED') AS closed_cases
FROM case_file;
```

## GROUPING SETS / ROLLUP / CUBE

```sql
SELECT
    status,
    priority,
    count(*) AS total
FROM case_file
GROUP BY ROLLUP (status, priority);
```

---

# 11. Window Functions

Window function menghitung nilai lintas row tanpa mereduksi jumlah row seperti `GROUP BY`.

```sql
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    created_at,
    row_number() OVER (
        PARTITION BY status
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
    ) AS rank_in_status
FROM case_file;
```

## Common window functions

| Function | Kegunaan |
|---|---|
| `row_number()` | Nomor urut unik dalam window. |
| `rank()` | Ranking dengan gap. |
| `dense_rank()` | Ranking tanpa gap. |
| `lag()` | Ambil nilai row sebelumnya. |
| `lead()` | Ambil nilai row sesudahnya. |
| `first_value()` | Nilai pertama dalam frame. |
| `last_value()` | Nilai terakhir dalam frame, hati-hati frame default. |

```sql
SELECT
    case_id,
    status,
    changed_at,
    lag(status) OVER (
        PARTITION BY case_id
        ORDER BY changed_at
    ) AS previous_status
FROM case_status_history;
```

---

# 12. CTE: WITH Queries

`WITH` menyediakan auxiliary statement untuk query yang lebih besar. PostgreSQL mendokumentasikan CTE sebagai temporary-table-like expression untuk satu query; auxiliary statement bisa berupa `SELECT`, `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, atau `MERGE`, dan primary statement juga bisa memakai jenis command tersebut. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-with.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Read CTE

```sql
WITH open_cases AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM case_file
    WHERE status = 'OPEN'
)
SELECT count(*)
FROM open_cases;
```

## Data-modifying CTE

```sql
WITH updated AS (
    UPDATE case_file
    SET status = 'IN_REVIEW',
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = 1001
    RETURNING id, status
)
INSERT INTO case_event (case_id, event_type, payload, created_at)
SELECT id, 'STATUS_CHANGED', jsonb_build_object('status', status), now()
FROM updated;
```

## Recursive CTE

```sql
WITH RECURSIVE org_tree AS (
    SELECT id, parent_id, name, 1 AS depth
    FROM organization_unit
    WHERE parent_id IS NULL

    UNION ALL

    SELECT child.id, child.parent_id, child.name, parent.depth + 1
    FROM organization_unit child
    JOIN org_tree parent ON child.parent_id = parent.id
)
SELECT *
FROM org_tree
ORDER BY depth, name;
```

---

# 13. Set Operations

```sql
-- Gabungkan dan hilangkan duplicate
SELECT user_id FROM case_reviewer
UNION
SELECT user_id FROM case_approver;

-- Gabungkan tanpa deduplication
SELECT user_id FROM case_reviewer
UNION ALL
SELECT user_id FROM case_approver;

-- Irisan
SELECT user_id FROM case_reviewer
INTERSECT
SELECT user_id FROM case_approver;

-- Ada di kiri, tidak ada di kanan
SELECT user_id FROM case_reviewer
EXCEPT
SELECT user_id FROM suspended_user;
```

**Praktik senior:** default ke `UNION ALL` jika memang tidak butuh deduplication, karena `UNION` perlu kerja tambahan untuk menghilangkan duplicate.

---

# 14. Data Types yang Paling Sering Dipakai

PostgreSQL punya banyak native data type, termasuk numeric, boolean, character/text, temporal, UUID, JSON, array, range, network, geometric, dan user-defined type. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL juga menyebut user dapat menambahkan type baru dengan `CREATE TYPE`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/datatype.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Kategori | Tipe | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Integer | `smallint`, `integer`, `bigint` | Gunakan `bigint` untuk ID jangka panjang. |
| Decimal | `numeric(p,s)` | Uang/perhitungan presisi; hindari floating untuk nilai finansial. |
| Floating | `real`, `double precision` | Untuk approximate scientific value. |
| Text | `text`, `varchar(n)` | `text` umum dipakai di PostgreSQL. |
| Boolean | `boolean` | `true`, `false`, `NULL`. |
| Time | `date`, `time`, `timestamp`, `timestamptz`, `interval` | Untuk aplikasi lintas timezone, prefer `timestamptz`. |
| UUID | `uuid` | Cocok untuk distributed ID atau public identifier. |
| JSON | `json`, `jsonb` | Prefer `jsonb` untuk indexing/query. |
| Array | `text[]`, `bigint[]` | Berguna, tapi jangan mengganti relasi jika butuh referential integrity. |
| Range | `tsrange`, `tstzrange`, `int4range` | Cocok untuk validity period, schedule, interval. |
| Network | `inet`, `cidr`, `macaddr` | Berguna untuk audit/security/networking. |

---

# 15. JSON / JSONB Querying

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_payload (
    case_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL
);

SELECT payload -> 'subject'
FROM case_payload;

SELECT payload ->> 'status'
FROM case_payload;

SELECT *
FROM case_payload
WHERE payload @> '{"priority": "high"}'::jsonb;
```

PostgreSQL current juga mendukung SQL/JSON capability seperti `JSON_TABLE`, yang dapat digunakan di `FROM` clause untuk `SELECT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, dan sebagai data source dalam `MERGE`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-json.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
SELECT *
FROM JSON_TABLE(
    '[{"id":1,"status":"OPEN"},{"id":2,"status":"CLOSED"}]'::jsonb,
    '$[*]'
    COLUMNS (
        id integer PATH '$.id',
        status text PATH '$.status'
    )
) AS jt;
```

---

# 16. Text Search

```sql
SELECT *
FROM case_file
WHERE to_tsvector('english', case_number || ' ' || status)
      @@ plainto_tsquery('english', 'open case');
```

Untuk production search sederhana, PostgreSQL full-text search bisa cukup. Untuk ranking, multilingual search, typo tolerance, dan search analytics kompleks, baru pertimbangkan OpenSearch/Elasticsearch.

---

# 17. Index SQL

Index adalah bagian SQL Language di PostgreSQL, bukan sekadar admin concern. Dokumentasi SQL Language memasukkan Indexes sebagai chapter tersendiri dalam Part II. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/index.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## B-tree index

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_status
ON case_file (status);
```

## Composite index

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_status_created_at
ON case_file (status, created_at DESC);
```

## Partial index

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_open_only
ON case_file (created_at DESC)
WHERE status = 'OPEN';
```

## Expression index

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_lower_case_number
ON case_file (lower(case_number));
```

## GIN index for JSONB

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_payload_payload_gin
ON case_payload
USING gin (payload);
```

**Rule of thumb:** index harus mengikuti query pattern, bukan sekadar column yang “sering dipakai”.

---

# 18. Transaction SQL

```sql
BEGIN;

UPDATE case_file
SET status = 'IN_REVIEW'
WHERE id = 1001
  AND status = 'OPEN';

INSERT INTO case_event (case_id, event_type, created_at)
VALUES (1001, 'STATUS_CHANGED', now());

COMMIT;
```

## Rollback

```sql
BEGIN;

UPDATE case_file
SET status = 'CLOSED'
WHERE id = 1001;

ROLLBACK;
```

## Savepoint

```sql
BEGIN;

SAVEPOINT before_optional_step;

UPDATE case_file
SET priority = priority + 1
WHERE id = 1001;

ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT before_optional_step;

COMMIT;
```

## Row locking

```sql
BEGIN;

SELECT *
FROM case_file
WHERE id = 1001
FOR UPDATE;

UPDATE case_file
SET status = 'IN_REVIEW'
WHERE id = 1001;

COMMIT;
```

`SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`, `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, `FOR SHARE`, dan `FOR KEY SHARE` adalah bagian akhir dari processing `SELECT` yang mengunci row terhadap concurrent update. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 19. Common PostgreSQL-Specific Patterns

## Idempotent insert

```sql
INSERT INTO processed_message (message_id, processed_at)
VALUES ('msg-001', now())
ON CONFLICT (message_id) DO NOTHING;
```

## Atomic state transition

```sql
UPDATE case_file
SET status = 'IN_REVIEW',
    updated_at = now()
WHERE id = 1001
  AND status = 'OPEN'
RETURNING id, status;
```

Jika `RETURNING` kosong, berarti transition tidak terjadi. Ini cocok untuk workflow/case lifecycle karena bisa menghindari blind overwrite.

## Claim next work item

```sql
WITH next_task AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM case_task
    WHERE status = 'READY'
    ORDER BY priority DESC, created_at ASC
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    LIMIT 1
)
UPDATE case_task t
SET status = 'CLAIMED',
    claimed_at = now()
FROM next_task
WHERE t.id = next_task.id
RETURNING t.*;
```

## Audit event after mutation

```sql
WITH changed AS (
    UPDATE case_file
    SET status = 'CLOSED',
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = 1001
      AND status = 'IN_REVIEW'
    RETURNING id, status
)
INSERT INTO case_event (case_id, event_type, payload, created_at)
SELECT
    id,
    'CASE_CLOSED',
    jsonb_build_object('new_status', status),
    now()
FROM changed;
```

---

# 20. Pitfalls yang Sering Terjadi

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| `UPDATE` / `DELETE` tanpa `WHERE` | Data massal berubah/hilang. | Biasakan transaction + `RETURNING` / preview `SELECT`. |
| `NULL = NULL` | Query tidak match. | Gunakan `IS NULL` atau `IS NOT DISTINCT FROM`. |
| `ORDER BY` tidak ada tapi berharap urutan stabil | Hasil bisa berubah. | Selalu tulis `ORDER BY` jika urutan penting. |
| `LIMIT/OFFSET` untuk pagination besar | Makin lambat saat offset besar. | Gunakan keyset pagination. |
| Overusing JSONB | Invariant tidak eksplisit. | Pakai column relational untuk field penting. |
| Index asal tambah | Write lambat, storage naik. | Tambah index berdasarkan query + `EXPLAIN`. |
| Quoted identifier | Query/migration ribet. | Gunakan lowercase snake_case. |
| CTE dianggap selalu optimal | Bisa jadi materialization/optimization issue tergantung query. | Cek `EXPLAIN ANALYZE`; gunakan `MATERIALIZED` / `NOT MATERIALIZED` jika perlu. |
| Blind upsert | Bisa overwrite data valid. | Tambahkan `WHERE` di `DO UPDATE` jika ada rule bisnis. |
| Tidak memakai `RETURNING` | Aplikasi perlu query tambahan dan rawan race. | Gunakan `RETURNING` untuk ID/default/state terbaru. |

---

# 21. Mini Reference

## SELECT

```sql
SELECT column_expression
FROM table_expression
WHERE condition
GROUP BY group_expression
HAVING group_condition
ORDER BY sort_expression
LIMIT n OFFSET m;
```

## INSERT

```sql
INSERT INTO table_name (col1, col2)
VALUES (value1, value2)
RETURNING *;
```

## UPDATE

```sql
UPDATE table_name
SET col1 = value1
WHERE condition
RETURNING *;
```

## DELETE

```sql
DELETE FROM table_name
WHERE condition
RETURNING *;
```

## UPSERT

```sql
INSERT INTO table_name (business_key, value)
VALUES ('key-1', 'value-1')
ON CONFLICT (business_key)
DO UPDATE SET value = EXCLUDED.value
RETURNING *;
```

## CTE

```sql
WITH x AS (
    SELECT ...
)
SELECT ...
FROM x;
```

## Transaction

```sql
BEGIN;
-- SQL statements
COMMIT;
```

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | SQL syntax, identifier, literal, cast | Bisa membaca SQL PostgreSQL tanpa bingung syntax. |
| 2 | DDL: table, schema, constraint | Bisa memahami model data dan invariant. |
| 3 | DML: insert/update/delete/merge/returning | Bisa membuat mutation aman dan observable. |

| 8 | Transaction SQL + row locking | Bisa membuat state transition aman untuk concurrency. |

Lanjut paling tepat: **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL DDL, Schema Design, Constraints, Identity, Sequence, View, Materialized View, dan Index dasar**. Ini akan memperkuat kemampuan membaca struktur database production sebelum masuk ke query tuning dan transaction concurrency.

## Response:
7/12/2026, 11:31:53 PM

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL DDL, Schema Design, Constraints, Identity, Sequence, View, Materialized View, dan Index Dasar

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus ke **SQL DDL layer**: bagaimana struktur database PostgreSQL dibuat, diubah, diberi constraint, dipisahkan ke schema, diberi identity/sequence, diekspos lewat view/materialized view, dan dipercepat dengan index dasar. Ini bukan pembahasan query SELECT lanjutan, MVCC, PL/pgSQL, atau tuning mendalam.

Secara resmi, dokumentasi PostgreSQL menempatkan **Data Definition** sebagai area untuk membuat struktur penyimpanan data, terutama table, column, schema, privilege, constraint, default value, generated column, partitioning, view, dan objek terkait. PostgreSQL current documentation saat ini berada pada PostgreSQL 18. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="d7l5zu"
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL DDL))
    Namespace
      Database
      Schema
      Search Path
      Ownership
    Table Design
      Columns
      Data Types
      Defaults
      Generated Columns
      Identity Columns
    Integrity
      Primary Key
      Foreign Key
      Unique
      Not Null
      Check
      Exclusion
      Deferrable
    Object Lifecycle
      CREATE
      ALTER
      DROP
      RENAME
      COMMENT
    Derived Structures
      View
      Materialized View
      Index
    Physical/Operational
      Lock Impact
      Large Table Migration
      Concurrent Index
      Partitioning
```

---

## 3. DDL Object Map

| Object | Fungsi | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|---|
| `SCHEMA` | Namespace untuk table, view, sequence, function, type, index, dan object lain. | Pisahkan domain/module, contoh `case_mgmt`, `audit`, `integration`. |
| `TABLE` | Struktur utama penyimpanan row. | Entity utama, transaction table, audit table, outbox table. |
| `COLUMN` | Field dalam table. | Harus punya tipe, nullability, default, dan semantic jelas. |
| `CONSTRAINT` | Guardrail integritas data. | Menjaga invariant walaupun aplikasi gagal. |
| `IDENTITY` | Auto-generated key berbasis implicit sequence. | Primary key numeric modern. |
| `SEQUENCE` | Generator angka independen. | Nomor dokumen, counter, legacy ID, atau kebutuhan khusus. |
| `VIEW` | Query tersimpan, tidak menyimpan hasil fisik. | Abstraction/read model ringan. |
| `MATERIALIZED VIEW` | Query tersimpan yang hasilnya dipersist seperti table. | Read model/report berat yang boleh stale. |
| `INDEX` | Struktur akses untuk mempercepat lookup/sort/join/filter. | Query performance, uniqueness enforcement, materialized view refresh. |
| `PARTITIONED TABLE` | Table dibagi ke partition berdasarkan key. | Data besar, retention, time-series, audit/event log. |

PostgreSQL schema adalah namespace; satu database dapat berisi banyak schema, dan schema dapat berisi table, type, function, operator, view, materialized view, sequence, index, dan object lain. Dalam satu schema, beberapa object berbagi namespace, sehingga nama table, view, materialized view, sequence, index, dan foreign table tidak boleh saling bentrok. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-schemas.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Schema Design

### Basic schema

```sql id="9du4gj"
CREATE SCHEMA case_mgmt;
CREATE SCHEMA audit;
CREATE SCHEMA integration;
```

`CREATE SCHEMA` membuat schema baru di database saat ini, dan nama schema harus berbeda dari schema yang sudah ada. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createschema.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Table dengan schema-qualified name

```sql id="412x4k"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_file (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### Rule praktis schema

| Rule | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Jangan taruh semua di `public` | Gunakan schema per bounded context/domain. |
| Selalu tahu `search_path` | Query production dan migration sebaiknya eksplisit: `case_mgmt.case_file`. |
| Pisahkan audit/event | Contoh: `audit.case_event`, `audit.case_status_history`. |
| Pisahkan integration/staging | Contoh: `integration.inbound_message`, `integration.import_batch`. |
| Jangan jadikan schema sebagai tenant kalau belum perlu | Multi-tenant per schema punya overhead migration dan permission. |

---

## 5. Naming Convention

| Object | Pattern | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Schema | `snake_case` domain | `case_mgmt`, `audit_log` |
| Table | singular atau plural konsisten | `case_file`, `case_task` |
| PK constraint | `<table>_pkey` default PostgreSQL cukup | `case_file_pkey` |
| FK constraint | `<table>_<column>_fkey` | `case_task_case_id_fkey` |
| Unique constraint | `uq_<table>_<columns>` | `uq_case_file_case_number` |
| Check constraint | `ck_<table>_<rule>` | `ck_case_file_status` |
| Index | `idx_<table>_<columns>` | `idx_case_file_status_created_at` |
| Partial index | `idx_<table>_<purpose>` | `idx_case_task_ready_queue` |
| Materialized view | `mv_<purpose>` | `mv_case_daily_status` |
| View | `v_<purpose>` atau semantic name | `v_open_case` |

**Prinsip:** nama object harus membantu debugging migration, error constraint, deadlock report, dan query plan.

---

## 6. Production-Grade `CREATE TABLE` Template

```sql id="hhi9gz"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_file (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    case_number text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    priority integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    assigned_user_id bigint,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT case_file_pkey
        PRIMARY KEY (id),

    CONSTRAINT uq_case_file_case_number
        UNIQUE (case_number),

    CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_status
        CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT', 'OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'CLOSED')),

    CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_priority_non_negative
        CHECK (priority >= 0)
);
```

### Template decision

| Area | Keputusan |
|---|---|
| `bigint` ID | Aman untuk volume besar. |
| `GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY` | Prefer modern PostgreSQL dibanding legacy `serial`. |
| `timestamptz` | Simpan timestamp dengan timezone awareness. |
| Explicit constraint name | Memudahkan migration, error tracing, dan rollback. |
| Business key unique | `case_number` dijaga database, bukan hanya aplikasi. |
| Status check | Cocok untuk finite state sederhana; untuk state besar bisa pakai lookup table. |

Identity column adalah column yang nilainya dibuat otomatis dari implicit sequence dan dibuat memakai `GENERATED ... AS IDENTITY`. PostgreSQL mendukung `GENERATED ALWAYS` dan `GENERATED BY DEFAULT`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-identity-columns.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Defaults vs Generated Columns vs Identity vs Sequence

| Mechanism | Fungsi | Contoh | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DEFAULT` | Isi nilai saat insert jika value tidak diberikan. | `created_at DEFAULT now()` | Dievaluasi saat row pertama kali dibuat. |
| Generated column | Column dihitung dari column lain. | `full_name GENERATED ALWAYS AS (...)` | Bisa stored atau virtual di PostgreSQL current. |
| Identity column | Auto-generated number untuk column. | `id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY` | Dibuat dari implicit sequence. |
| Sequence | Generator angka eksplisit. | `CREATE SEQUENCE case_no_seq` | Bisa dipakai manual oleh banyak object. |

PostgreSQL membedakan default dan generated column: default dievaluasi sekali saat row diinsert jika value tidak disediakan, sedangkan generated column diperbarui ketika row berubah dan tidak bisa dioverride seperti column biasa. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-generated-columns.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Default value

```sql id="m0vwx3"
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### Generated column

```sql id="o4tdyi"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.person (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    first_name text NOT NULL,
    last_name text NOT NULL,
    full_name text GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        first_name || ' ' || last_name
    ) STORED
);
```

PostgreSQL current mendokumentasikan generated column sebagai column yang selalu dihitung dari column lain, dengan dua jenis: `stored` dan `virtual`. Stored generated column dihitung saat write dan memakai storage, sedangkan virtual generated column dihitung saat read. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-generated-columns.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Sequence eksplisit

```sql id="0n9xvc"
CREATE SEQUENCE case_mgmt.case_number_seq
    AS bigint
    START WITH 1
    INCREMENT BY 1
    NO CYCLE;
```

```sql id="ag9bkv"
INSERT INTO case_mgmt.case_file (case_number, status)
VALUES (
    'CASE-' || to_char(now(), 'YYYY') || '-' ||
    lpad(nextval('case_mgmt.case_number_seq')::text, 6, '0'),
    'OPEN'
);
```

`CREATE SEQUENCE` membuat sequence number generator sebagai special single-row table yang diinisialisasi dengan nama tertentu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createsequence.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Constraint Cheatsheet

Constraint adalah enforcement layer di database. SQL constraint memberi kontrol atas data di table; jika value melanggar constraint, PostgreSQL akan menolak perubahan data. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-constraints.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Constraint | Tujuan | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| `PRIMARY KEY` | Identitas row unik dan non-null. | `PRIMARY KEY (id)` |
| `UNIQUE` | Business key unik. | `UNIQUE (case_number)` |
| `NOT NULL` | Field wajib. | `status text NOT NULL` |
| `CHECK` | Validasi rule per row. | `CHECK (priority >= 0)` |
| `FOREIGN KEY` | Referential integrity antar table. | `REFERENCES app_user(id)` |
| `EXCLUDE` | Cegah overlap / konflik kompleks. | Time range overlap prevention. |

### Foreign key

```sql id="hmjoo1"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_task (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    task_type text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT case_task_case_id_fkey
        FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
        REFERENCES case_mgmt.case_file (id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT
);
```

### Check constraint

```sql id="yoj7w7"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_task
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_task_status
CHECK (status IN ('READY', 'CLAIMED', 'DONE', 'CANCELLED'));
```

### Unique composite constraint

```sql id="34v2sj"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_task
ADD CONSTRAINT uq_case_task_case_task_type
UNIQUE (case_id, task_type);
```

### Deferrable constraint

```sql id="89dd1v"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_task
ADD CONSTRAINT case_task_case_id_fkey
FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
REFERENCES case_mgmt.case_file (id)
DEFERRABLE INITIALLY IMMEDIATE;
```

**Kapan pakai `DEFERRABLE`:** ketika satu transaction perlu melakukan beberapa perubahan yang sementara tampak melanggar FK/unique, tetapi valid di akhir transaction. Jangan jadikan default karena menambah kompleksitas reasoning.

---

## 9. Safe DDL untuk Table Besar

DDL bukan cuma syntax; DDL juga memengaruhi lock, rewrite, dan availability.

### Tambah column nullable

```sql id="0pfweu"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN external_reference text;
```

### Tambah column dengan constant default

```sql id="5b4d2o"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN source_system text DEFAULT 'INTERNAL';
```

PostgreSQL mendokumentasikan bahwa menambahkan column dengan constant/non-volatile default tidak harus langsung memperbarui semua row lama; default disimpan di metadata dan diterapkan saat row diakses atau saat table rewrite, sehingga operasi ini cepat bahkan pada table besar. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-alter.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Tambah constraint dengan validasi tertunda

```sql id="fs1v05"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_priority_limit
CHECK (priority BETWEEN 0 AND 100)
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_priority_limit;
```

`NOT VALID` berguna untuk mengurangi dampak penambahan constraint pada table besar karena PostgreSQL tidak perlu langsung scan seluruh table saat constraint ditambahkan; validasi bisa dilakukan belakangan. Pada PostgreSQL current, dokumentasi menyebut ini relevan untuk foreign-key, check, dan not-null constraints. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. ALTER TABLE Patterns

| Kebutuhan | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Tambah column baru | `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN ...` |
| Rename column | `ALTER TABLE ... RENAME COLUMN old TO new` |
| Ubah default | `ALTER TABLE ... ALTER COLUMN ... SET DEFAULT ...` |
| Hapus default | `ALTER TABLE ... ALTER COLUMN ... DROP DEFAULT` |
| Tambah constraint | `ALTER TABLE ... ADD CONSTRAINT ...` |
| Validasi constraint | `ALTER TABLE ... VALIDATE CONSTRAINT ...` |
| Rename constraint | `ALTER TABLE ... RENAME CONSTRAINT ... TO ...` |
| Drop constraint | `ALTER TABLE ... DROP CONSTRAINT ...` |

```sql id="8nqw14"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
RENAME COLUMN external_reference TO external_case_reference;
```

```sql id="ghbspm"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ALTER COLUMN priority SET DEFAULT 1;
```

```sql id="cw0g9u"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
DROP CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_priority_non_negative;
```

`ALTER TABLE` dapat mengubah nama table, index, sequence, view, materialized view, foreign table, column, dan constraint; ketika constraint yang punya underlying index diganti namanya, index terkait ikut berganti nama. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. View

View adalah query yang disimpan sebagai object database. View **tidak menyimpan hasil fisik**; query view dijalankan setiap kali view direferensikan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="ojj1eu"
CREATE VIEW case_mgmt.v_open_case AS
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    assigned_user_id,
    created_at
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE status = 'OPEN';
```

### View use cases

| Use Case | Cocok? | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Abstraction untuk read model sederhana | Ya | Hindari duplikasi query di banyak service/report. |
| Security projection | Ya | Expose column tertentu saja. |
| Complex reporting berat | Kadang | Bisa lambat karena tidak materialized. |
| API contract internal | Hati-hati | Perubahan view bisa memengaruhi consumer diam-diam. |

### Replace view

```sql id="jgv5x1"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_mgmt.v_open_case AS
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    assigned_user_id,
    created_at,
    updated_at
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE status = 'OPEN';
```

**Praktik senior:** jangan anggap view sebagai boundary versioning yang aman jika banyak consumer. Tetap perlakukan seperti contract.

---

## 12. Materialized View

Materialized view menyimpan hasil query dalam bentuk mirip table, berbeda dari view biasa yang query-nya dieksekusi saat direferensikan. PostgreSQL materialized view memakai rule system seperti view, tetapi hasilnya dipersist. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/rules-materializedviews.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="8ncd2z"
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW case_mgmt.mv_case_daily_status AS
SELECT
    date_trunc('day', created_at)::date AS case_date,
    status,
    count(*) AS total_cases
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
GROUP BY 1, 2
WITH DATA;
```

### Refresh materialized view

```sql id="49owko"
REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW case_mgmt.mv_case_daily_status;
```

`REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW` mengganti isi materialized view dengan hasil query terbaru; jika memakai `WITH NO DATA`, materialized view menjadi tidak bisa discan sampai dipopulasi ulang. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-refreshmaterializedview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Concurrent refresh

```sql id="zfrqaa"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_mv_case_daily_status
ON case_mgmt.mv_case_daily_status (case_date, status);

REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY case_mgmt.mv_case_daily_status;
```

`REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY` hanya boleh digunakan jika materialized view sudah populated dan memiliki setidaknya satu unique index yang memakai nama column saja, mencakup semua row, bukan expression index, dan bukan partial index. PostgreSQL juga hanya mengizinkan satu refresh berjalan pada satu materialized view pada satu waktu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-refreshmaterializedview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### View vs Materialized View

| Aspek | View | Materialized View |
|---|---|---|
| Menyimpan data fisik | Tidak | Ya |
| Data selalu fresh | Ya, karena query dieksekusi saat read | Tidak, tergantung refresh |
| Bisa di-index langsung | Tidak seperti table biasa | Ya, materialized view bisa diberi index |
| Cocok untuk | Abstraction, security projection | Reporting/read model berat |
| Risiko | Query berat tersembunyi | Data stale, refresh cost |

---

## 13. Index Dasar

`CREATE INDEX` membuat index pada satu atau lebih column atau expression dari table atau materialized view. Index terutama dipakai untuk meningkatkan performa query, tetapi index yang tidak tepat bisa memperlambat write dan menambah storage. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### B-tree index

```sql id="2s2ur5"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_status
ON case_mgmt.case_file (status);
```

Cocok untuk equality, range, sort, join key umum.

### Composite index

```sql id="2j5ws2"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_status_created_at
ON case_mgmt.case_file (status, created_at DESC);
```

Cocok jika query sering filter `status` lalu sort/filter `created_at`.

### Unique index

```sql id="a19ca4"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_case_file_case_number_idx
ON case_mgmt.case_file (case_number);
```

Biasanya lebih baik pakai `UNIQUE CONSTRAINT` untuk invariant bisnis, karena semantic-nya lebih jelas. Unique index tetap berguna untuk kasus teknis tertentu.

### Partial index

```sql id="f3bh5a"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_task_ready_queue
ON case_mgmt.case_task (priority DESC, created_at ASC)
WHERE status = 'READY';
```

Cocok untuk queue/work item yang hanya sering query subset tertentu.

### Expression index

```sql id="0z25d4"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_lower_case_number
ON case_mgmt.case_file (lower(case_number));
```

Cocok jika query memakai expression yang sama:

```sql id="bguu9u"
SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE lower(case_number) = lower('CASE-2026-0001');
```

### JSONB GIN index

```sql id="c0xd9k"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_payload_gin
ON audit.case_event
USING gin (payload);
```

Cocok untuk query containment JSONB, misalnya:

```sql id="wur0ox"
SELECT *
FROM audit.case_event
WHERE payload @> '{"source":"SYSTEM"}'::jsonb;
```

---

## 14. Constraint vs Index

| Kebutuhan | Pakai |
|---|---|
| Menjamin business key unik | `UNIQUE CONSTRAINT` |
| Mempercepat query | `CREATE INDEX` |
| Mempercepat subset query | `PARTIAL INDEX` |
| Mempercepat expression lookup | `EXPRESSION INDEX` |
| Mendukung FK reference target non-PK | `UNIQUE CONSTRAINT` atau suitable unique index |
| Mendukung materialized view concurrent refresh | Unique index pada materialized view |

**Rule:** constraint menyatakan **kebenaran data**; index menyatakan **cara akses data**. Kadang constraint membuat index di balik layar, tetapi niat desainnya tetap berbeda.

---

## 15. Schema Design Patterns untuk Case Management / Regulatory System

### Core entity

```sql id="b7gz4f"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_file (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    severity text NOT NULL,
    opened_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    closed_at timestamptz,

    CONSTRAINT uq_case_file_case_number UNIQUE (case_number),
    CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_status
        CHECK (status IN ('OPEN', 'INVESTIGATING', 'ESCALATED', 'CLOSED')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_closed_at
        CHECK (
            (status <> 'CLOSED' AND closed_at IS NULL)
            OR
            (status = 'CLOSED' AND closed_at IS NOT NULL)
        )
);
```

### Status history

```sql id="0w0j6w"
CREATE TABLE audit.case_status_history (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL REFERENCES case_mgmt.case_file(id),
    old_status text,
    new_status text NOT NULL,
    changed_by text NOT NULL,
    changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT ck_case_status_changed
        CHECK (old_status IS DISTINCT FROM new_status)
);
```

### Outbox table

```sql id="tbp8zd"
CREATE TABLE integration.outbox_message (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    aggregate_type text NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id bigint NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at timestamptz,

    CONSTRAINT ck_outbox_status
        CHECK (status IN ('PENDING', 'PUBLISHED', 'FAILED'))
);

CREATE INDEX idx_outbox_pending
ON integration.outbox_message (created_at ASC)
WHERE status = 'PENDING';
```

---

## 16. DDL Review Checklist

Sebelum merge migration DDL, cek ini:

| Checklist | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Naming | Apakah nama table/column/constraint/index jelas? |
| Schema | Apakah object berada di schema yang tepat? |
| Nullability | Apakah `NULL` memang valid secara domain? |
| Default | Apakah default aman untuk data lama dan data baru? |
| Constraint | Invariant penting sudah dijaga DB? |
| FK behavior | `ON DELETE RESTRICT`, `CASCADE`, atau `SET NULL` sudah tepat? |
| Index | Index mengikuti query pattern nyata? |
| Write overhead | Apakah index tambahan memperlambat insert/update? |
| Large table | Apakah DDL akan scan/rewrite/lock table besar? |
| Rollback | Apakah migration bisa di-rollback atau forward-fixed? |
| Consumer | Apakah view/materialized view dipakai service/report lain? |
| Ownership | Apakah owner dan privilege sesuai? |

---

## 17. Common Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Semua object di `public` | Boundary domain kabur, privilege sulit. | Buat schema per domain. |
| Tidak memberi nama constraint | Error production sulit dipahami. | Beri nama constraint eksplisit. |
| Pakai `serial` untuk desain baru | Legacy style, kurang eksplisit dibanding identity. | Prefer `GENERATED ... AS IDENTITY`. |
| FK selalu `ON DELETE CASCADE` | Data penting bisa hilang berantai. | Default ke `RESTRICT`, pakai cascade hanya jika lifecycle benar-benar parent-owned. |
| JSONB untuk field penting | Constraint dan FK hilang. | Jadikan column normal untuk field penting. |
| View terlalu kompleks | Query berat tersembunyi. | Materialized view atau query eksplisit dengan observability. |
| Materialized view dianggap fresh | Data stale. | Definisikan refresh policy dan freshness SLA. |
| Index semua column | Write lambat, storage naik. | Index berdasarkan query, bukan intuisi. |
| DDL besar tanpa lock analysis | Potensi outage. | Gunakan safe migration pattern dan validasi bertahap. |
| Rename/drop object langsung | Consumer rusak. | Gunakan expand-contract migration. |

---

## 18. Mini Pattern: Expand-Contract Migration

Cocok untuk aplikasi Java/JAX-RS yang deploy rolling di Docker/Kubernetes.

### Phase 1 - Expand

```sql id="0hxvvd"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN external_case_reference text;
```

Aplikasi versi baru mulai menulis column baru, tetapi masih membaca column lama.

### Phase 2 - Backfill

```sql id="an2cva"
UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
SET external_case_reference = case_number
WHERE external_case_reference IS NULL;
```

Untuk table besar, lakukan batch, bukan satu update besar.

### Phase 3 - Validate

```sql id="u74aoe"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_external_ref_present
CHECK (external_case_reference IS NOT NULL)
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_external_ref_present;
```

### Phase 4 - Contract

Setelah semua aplikasi tidak memakai field lama:

```sql id="93xlgq"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
DROP COLUMN old_external_reference;
```

---

## 19. Roadmap Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Schema & naming | Bisa membaca struktur database production dengan cepat. |
| 2 | Table, column, type, default | Bisa mendesain entity table yang stabil. |
| 3 | Constraint | Bisa menaruh invariant bisnis di database. |
| 4 | Identity & sequence | Bisa memilih generator ID/nomor yang tepat. |
| 5 | ALTER TABLE safe pattern | Bisa membuat migration minim downtime. |
| 6 | View | Bisa membuat abstraction read model ringan. |
| 7 | Materialized view | Bisa membuat read model/report ter-cache. |
| 8 | Index dasar | Bisa menambah index yang relevan tanpa membebani write path. |
| 9 | Expand-contract migration | Bisa mendukung rolling deployment aplikasi. |
