- [Documentation: 18: Chapter 25. Backup and Restore](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/backup.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:55:14 AM

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Replication & High Availability

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only**: physical replication, streaming replication, WAL shipping, replication slot, hot standby, synchronous/asynchronous replication, failover, timeline, `pg_rewind`, replication lag, dan monitoring. Tidak membahas Kubernetes operator, cloud-managed PostgreSQL, PgBouncer, Patroni, repmgr, atau tooling eksternal secara detail.

PostgreSQL HA umumnya berbasis **WAL replication**: primary menghasilkan WAL, standby menerima dan me-replay WAL. PostgreSQL menyediakan log-shipping standby, streaming replication, replication slots, cascading replication, synchronous replication, failover, dan hot standby sebagai bagian dari high availability/load balancing chapter. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/high-availability.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Replication & HA))
    WAL Based Replication
      Primary
      Standby
      WAL Sender
      WAL Receiver
      Replay
    Streaming Replication
      Async
      Sync
      Cascading
      Physical Slot
    Standby Modes
      Warm Standby
      Hot Standby
      Read Only Query
      Recovery Conflict
    Durability
      WAL
      Flush
      Replay
      Synchronous Commit
    Failover
      Promotion
      Timeline
      Split Brain
      Fencing
      pg_rewind
    Monitoring
      pg_stat_replication
      pg_replication_slots
      pg_stat_wal_receiver
      Replication Lag
      WAL Retention
```

**Core idea:** PostgreSQL replication bukan “copy SQL statement”, tetapi umumnya **copy WAL**. Standby menerima perubahan fisik dari primary dan me-replay perubahan tersebut agar cluster standby mengikuti primary.

---

## 3. Replication Strategy Matrix

| Strategy | Level | Data Scope | Read di Standby | Kelebihan | Batasan |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| File-based WAL shipping | Physical | Seluruh cluster | Bisa jika hot standby | Simpel, cocok DR/PITR | Lebih lag dibanding streaming karena menunggu WAL segment/archive. |
| Streaming replication | Physical | Seluruh cluster | Ya, dengan hot standby | Lebih near-real-time | Tetap primary-standby, bukan multi-primary. |
| Synchronous streaming replication | Physical | Seluruh cluster | Ya | Bisa mengurangi/meniadakan data loss saat failover | Latency commit naik; availability tergantung standby sync. |
| Cascading replication | Physical | Seluruh cluster | Ya | Mengurangi beban primary | Topologi lebih kompleks. |
| Logical replication | Logical | Object/table tertentu | Subscriber bisa read/write tergantung desain | Selective replication, migration, integration | Bukan HA fisik penuh secara default; DDL tidak otomatis semua ikut. |

PostgreSQL membedakan physical replication yang memakai exact block/WAL-level replication dan logical replication yang mereplikasi perubahan data object berdasarkan replication identity, biasanya primary key. Logical replication memberi kontrol lebih granular atas object dan security, tetapi berbeda dari physical HA standby. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Primary, Standby, WAL Sender, WAL Receiver

Dalam streaming replication, server berperan sebagai **primary** atau **standby**. Primary mengirim data, standby menerima data. Dalam cascading replication, standby juga bisa menjadi sender untuk standby lain. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-replication.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
Primary
  ├─ WAL generation
  ├─ WAL sender process
  ↓
Standby
  ├─ WAL receiver process
  ├─ WAL replay
  └─ Optional read-only query via hot_standby
```

| Komponen | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| Primary | Server read-write utama. |
| Standby | Server recovery/read-only yang mengikuti WAL primary. |
| WAL sender | Process di primary yang mengirim WAL ke standby. |
| WAL receiver | Process di standby yang menerima WAL dari primary. |
| Startup/replay process | Process di standby yang me-replay WAL. |
| Replication connection | Koneksi khusus dari standby ke primary. |

---

## 5. Streaming Replication

Streaming replication memungkinkan standby lebih up-to-date daripada file-based log shipping karena standby connect ke primary dan menerima WAL record saat dihasilkan, tanpa menunggu WAL file penuh. Streaming replication bersifat asynchronous secara default; biasanya ada delay kecil antara commit di primary dan perubahan terlihat di standby, tergantung kemampuan standby mengikuti load. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/warm-standby.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
Transaction commit on primary
   ↓
WAL generated
   ↓
WAL streamed to standby
   ↓
WAL written/flushed/replayed on standby
   ↓
Data visible on standby
```

### Minimal primary-side settings

```conf
wal_level = replica
max_wal_senders = 10
max_replication_slots = 10
hot_standby = on
```

`wal_level = replica` menulis cukup informasi WAL untuk WAL archiving dan replication, termasuk menjalankan read-only query di standby. Level `logical` diperlukan jika ingin logical decoding/logical replication. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-wal.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Standby-side setting inti

```conf
primary_conninfo = 'host=primary-db port=5432 user=replicator application_name=standby_1'
primary_slot_name = 'standby_1'
hot_standby = on
```

Standby menjadi streaming replication standby ketika `primary_conninfo` menunjuk ke primary. Primary juga perlu `listen_addresses` dan `pg_hba.conf` yang mengizinkan standby connect ke replication pseudo-database. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/warm-standby.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. Creating Standby dengan `pg_basebackup`

Physical standby biasanya dibuat dari base backup primary.

```bash
pg_basebackup \
  -h primary-db \
  -D /var/lib/postgresql/data \
  -U replicator \
  -Fp \
  -Xs \
  -P \
  -R
```

| Option | Arti |
|---|---|
| `-D` | Destination data directory. |
| `-Fp` | Plain format. |
| `-Xs` | Stream WAL selama backup. |
| `-P` | Progress. |
| `-R` | Tulis konfigurasi recovery/standby agar backup langsung bisa mengikuti primary. |

`pg_basebackup` dapat mengambil base backup dari primary maupun standby. Jika mengambil backup dari standby, standby harus bisa menerima replication connection, misalnya dengan `max_wal_senders`, `hot_standby`, dan `pg_hba.conf` yang sesuai. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/app-pgbasebackup.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Warm Standby vs Hot Standby

| Mode | Standby Bisa Query? | Fungsi Utama |
|---|---:|---|
| Warm standby | Tidak / tidak untuk read workload aktif | Recovery target / failover standby. |
| Hot standby | Ya, read-only query | Read scaling terbatas, reporting, DR standby. |

PostgreSQL hot standby memungkinkan standby menjalankan query read-only saat tetap dalam recovery. Namun query standby dapat mengalami conflict dengan WAL replay, misalnya saat replay perlu menghapus row version yang masih dibutuhkan query standby. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/high-availability.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Cek apakah server sedang recovery

```sql
SELECT pg_is_in_recovery();
```

| Result | Makna |
|---|---|
| `false` | Server primary/read-write. |
| `true` | Server standby/recovery. |

---

## 8. Asynchronous vs Synchronous Replication

### Asynchronous replication

```text
Primary COMMIT acknowledged
   ↓
WAL may still be in transit/replay on standby
```

Asynchronous streaming replication adalah default. Ada kemungkinan data loss saat primary gagal setelah commit di primary tetapi sebelum WAL diterima/ditulis/replay di standby. PostgreSQL documentation menyebut streaming replication async memiliki delay kecil, biasanya lebih kecil daripada file-based log shipping jika standby cukup kuat mengikuti load. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/warm-standby.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Synchronous replication

```text
Primary COMMIT waits
   ↓
Configured synchronous standby confirms WAL state
   ↓
COMMIT acknowledged
```

Synchronous replication berarti transaksi yang memodifikasi data tidak dianggap committed sampai server-server yang disyaratkan juga mengonfirmasi commit sesuai konfigurasi. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menjelaskan bahwa synchronous solution dapat menjamin failover tanpa data loss untuk transaksi yang sudah committed, tetapi ada trade-off latency/availability. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/high-availability.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Config example

```conf
synchronous_standby_names = 'FIRST 1 (standby_1, standby_2)'
synchronous_commit = on
```

| Setting | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `synchronous_standby_names` | Standby mana yang dianggap synchronous candidate. |
| `synchronous_commit` | Seberapa kuat commit menunggu WAL local/remote. |
| `application_name` di `primary_conninfo` | Nama standby yang dicocokkan dengan sync standby config. |

---

## 9. Commit Durability Levels

`synchronous_commit` mengontrol kapan commit dianggap selesai dari perspektif client. Dalam desain HA, ini menentukan trade-off latency vs data safety.

| Value | Intuisi | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| `off` | Tidak menunggu WAL flush lokal penuh | Commit bisa hilang jika crash segera. |
| `local` | Tunggu WAL flush lokal primary | Standby belum tentu menerima. |
| `remote_write` | Tunggu standby menulis WAL ke OS | Belum tentu flushed/replayed penuh. |
| `on` | Tunggu sesuai sync replication default | Lebih aman, latency lebih tinggi. |
| `remote_apply` | Tunggu standby apply/replay | Read-after-write di standby lebih kuat, latency paling tinggi. |

Gunakan `remote_apply` hanya untuk kebutuhan khusus, misalnya caller harus yakin perubahan sudah visible di synchronous standby sebelum commit dianggap sukses.

---

## 10. Replication Slots

Replication slot membuat primary mempertahankan WAL yang dibutuhkan consumer tertentu agar tidak dihapus terlalu cepat. Slot bisa physical atau logical. Dalam streaming replication fisik, physical replication slot membantu standby yang disconnect sementara agar masih bisa mengejar WAL yang tertinggal. PostgreSQL high availability docs memiliki bagian khusus replication slots dalam log-shipping standby server setup. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/high-availability.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Create physical slot

```sql
SELECT pg_create_physical_replication_slot('standby_1');
```

### Cek slot

```sql
SELECT
    slot_name,
    slot_type,
    active,
    restart_lsn,
    wal_status,
    safe_wal_size
FROM pg_replication_slots;
```

### Drop slot

```sql
SELECT pg_drop_replication_slot('standby_1');
```

| Benefit | Risk |
|---|---|
| Standby tidak kehilangan WAL saat tertinggal sementara. | Slot orphan/inactive bisa membuat WAL menumpuk sampai disk penuh. |
| Lebih reliable daripada hanya mengandalkan WAL retention default. | Perlu monitoring serius. |

**Rule:** setiap physical replication slot harus punya owner/topologi yang jelas. Slot standby yang sudah tidak dipakai harus di-drop.

---

## 11. WAL Retention dan Slot Risk

```text
Standby disconnects
   ↓
Physical slot remains active/inactive but retained
   ↓
Primary keeps WAL from restart_lsn
   ↓
WAL grows
   ↓
Disk full risk
```

### Monitoring retained WAL risk

```sql
SELECT
    slot_name,
    slot_type,
    active,
    restart_lsn,
    pg_size_pretty(
        pg_wal_lsn_diff(pg_current_wal_lsn(), restart_lsn)
    ) AS retained_wal
FROM pg_replication_slots
WHERE restart_lsn IS NOT NULL;
```

| Signal | Arti |
|---|---|
| `active = false` lama | Standby/consumer tidak connect. |
| `retained_wal` terus naik | WAL tertahan karena slot. |
| `wal_status` tidak normal | Slot mulai bermasalah terhadap WAL availability. |
| `safe_wal_size` kecil/null | Risiko kehilangan WAL atau pressure tergantung config. |

---

## 12. Cascading Replication

Cascading replication berarti standby dapat mengirim WAL ke standby lain. Ini mengurangi fan-out connection dari primary dan bisa berguna untuk topologi regional/DR.

```text
Primary
  ↓
Standby A
  ↓
Standby B
```

Dalam PostgreSQL, ketika cascading replication digunakan, standby bisa menjadi sender dan receiver. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-replication.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Mengurangi beban primary. | Lag bisa bertingkat. |
| Cocok topologi geografis. | Failover/reparenting lebih kompleks. |
| Standby downstream tidak langsung bergantung primary. | Monitoring harus melihat seluruh chain. |

---

## 13. Hot Standby Read Scaling: Batasan Penting

Standby hot standby bisa melayani read-only query, tetapi tidak boleh dianggap sama dengan read replica yang selalu konsisten real-time.

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Replication lag | Data standby bisa tertinggal dari primary. |
| Recovery conflict | Query panjang di standby bisa dibatalkan agar WAL replay bisa lanjut. |
| Stale read | Read setelah write ke primary belum tentu terlihat di standby. |
| Query load mengganggu replay | Query berat di standby bisa memperbesar lag. |
| Different execution profile | Cache/statistics/load standby bisa berbeda dari primary. |

**Rule:** standby cocok untuk report/read-heavy yang toleran terhadap lag, bukan untuk transaksi yang butuh read-your-write guarantee kecuali memakai desain khusus seperti synchronous commit `remote_apply`.

---

## 14. Failover

Failover terjadi ketika primary gagal dan standby dipromosikan menjadi primary baru. PostgreSQL menyatakan jika primary gagal, standby harus mulai prosedur failover; jika standby gagal, failover tidak perlu dilakukan, dan standby bisa dibuat ulang jika tidak dapat direstart. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/warm-standby-failover.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
Primary fails
   ↓
Choose best standby
   ↓
Stop accepting writes to old primary / fence it
   ↓
Promote standby
   ↓
Redirect clients
   ↓
Rebuild or rewind old primary as standby
```

### Promote standby

```sql
SELECT pg_promote();
```

atau:

```bash
pg_ctl promote -D /var/lib/postgresql/data
```

| Step | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Detect failure | Pastikan primary benar-benar tidak sehat. |
| Fence old primary | Mencegah split brain. |
| Select candidate standby | Pilih standby dengan WAL paling maju dan sehat. |
| Promote | Standby keluar dari recovery dan menjadi read-write. |
| Reconfigure clients | Arahkan write ke primary baru. |
| Rebuild old primary | Jadikan standby baru, jangan langsung dipakai write. |

---

## 15. Split Brain

**Split brain** adalah kondisi dua node sama-sama menerima write sebagai primary. Ini salah satu risiko terbesar HA.

```text
Network partition
   ↓
Old primary masih hidup tapi tidak terlihat
   ↓
Standby dipromosikan
   ↓
Dua primary menerima write
   ↓
Data diverge
```

### Prevention checklist

| Guardrail | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| Fencing / STONITH | Pastikan old primary tidak bisa menulis. |
| Single writer endpoint | Aplikasi hanya menulis ke endpoint primary terkontrol. |
| Controlled promotion | Jangan promote manual tanpa prosedur. |
| Timeline awareness | Deteksi divergence setelah failover. |
| Rewind/rebuild old primary | Jangan rejoin old primary begitu saja. |

PostgreSQL documentation index/high availability material juga menekankan konsep STONITH/fencing dalam HA context. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/bookindex.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 16. Timeline dan Divergence

Setiap kali standby dipromosikan, PostgreSQL membuat **timeline** baru. Timeline membantu membedakan sejarah WAL setelah failover.

```text
Timeline 1:
  Primary old writes WAL
      ↓
  standby follows

Failover:
  standby promoted

Timeline 2:
  New primary writes new WAL history
```

Jika old primary kembali hidup setelah failover, ia mungkin berada di timeline lama dan datanya diverge dari primary baru. Ia tidak boleh langsung dimasukkan kembali sebagai standby tanpa sinkronisasi.

---

## 17. `pg_rewind`

`pg_rewind` digunakan untuk menyinkronkan kembali cluster PostgreSQL yang timeline-nya sudah diverge, misalnya old primary setelah failover agar bisa mengikuti primary baru sebagai standby. Tool ini menghindari full base backup ulang jika kondisi terpenuhi. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/app-pgrewind.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```bash
pg_rewind \
  --target-pgdata=/var/lib/postgresql/data_old_primary \
  --source-server="host=new-primary port=5432 user=rewind_user dbname=postgres"
```

| Syarat Praktis | Catatan |
|---|---|
| Target dan source berasal dari cluster history yang sama | Bukan untuk cluster unrelated. |
| WAL yang dibutuhkan masih tersedia | Jika hilang, harus base backup ulang. |
| Data directory target tidak sedang running | Jangan rewind server aktif. |
| Setelah rewind, config standby harus benar | `primary_conninfo`, slot, recovery signal. |

**Rule:** setelah failover, old primary harus dianggap unsafe sampai di-rewind atau dibangun ulang.

---

## 18. Replication Lag

Replication lag bisa dilihat dari beberapa dimensi:

| Lag Type | Makna |
|---|---|
| Send lag | WAL belum terkirim dari primary. |
| Write lag | WAL sudah diterima tapi belum ditulis di standby. |
| Flush lag | WAL belum fsync/flushed di standby. |
| Replay lag | WAL belum diaplikasikan ke data files standby. |
| Time lag | Selisih waktu replay terakhir dengan waktu saat ini. |
| Byte lag | Selisih LSN primary dengan LSN standby. |

### Primary-side monitoring

`pg_stat_replication` menampilkan satu row per WAL sender process dan statistics replication ke standby yang tersambung. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/monitoring-stats.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
SELECT
    application_name,
    client_addr,
    state,
    sync_state,
    sent_lsn,
    write_lsn,
    flush_lsn,
    replay_lsn,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_wal_lsn_diff(pg_current_wal_lsn(), replay_lsn)) AS replay_byte_lag,
    write_lag,
    flush_lag,
    replay_lag
FROM pg_stat_replication
ORDER BY application_name;
```

### Standby-side monitoring

```sql
SELECT
    pg_is_in_recovery() AS is_standby,
    pg_last_wal_receive_lsn() AS receive_lsn,
    pg_last_wal_replay_lsn() AS replay_lsn,
    now() - pg_last_xact_replay_timestamp() AS time_since_last_replay;
```

| Signal | Interpretasi |
|---|---|
| `replay_byte_lag` naik | Standby tertinggal dalam apply WAL. |
| `write_lag` tinggi | Network/standby write path bottleneck. |
| `flush_lag` tinggi | Disk/fsync standby bottleneck. |
| `replay_lag` tinggi | Standby replay/query conflict/load bottleneck. |
| `time_since_last_replay` tinggi | Tidak selalu masalah jika primary idle; interpretasi perlu workload context. |

---

## 19. Monitoring Replication Slots

```sql
SELECT
    slot_name,
    slot_type,
    database,
    active,
    restart_lsn,
    confirmed_flush_lsn,
    wal_status,
    safe_wal_size
FROM pg_replication_slots
ORDER BY slot_name;
```

| Column | Makna |
|---|---|
| `slot_type` | `physical` atau `logical`. |
| `active` | Consumer sedang connect atau tidak. |
| `restart_lsn` | WAL tertua yang masih perlu disimpan untuk slot. |
| `confirmed_flush_lsn` | Biasanya relevan untuk logical slot. |
| `wal_status` | Status availability WAL untuk slot. |
| `safe_wal_size` | Estimasi ruang aman sebelum slot berisiko kehilangan WAL, jika applicable. |

---

## 20. Monitoring WAL Receiver di Standby

```sql
SELECT
    status,
    receive_start_lsn,
    written_lsn,
    flushed_lsn,
    received_tli,
    last_msg_send_time,
    last_msg_receipt_time,
    latest_end_lsn,
    latest_end_time,
    conninfo
FROM pg_stat_wal_receiver;
```

| Signal | Arti |
|---|---|
| No row | Standby tidak sedang menerima WAL streaming. |
| `status = streaming` | WAL receiver aktif. |
| `last_msg_receipt_time` tua | Koneksi replication idle/putus atau primary idle; cek context. |
| `latest_end_lsn` tidak bergerak | Tidak ada WAL baru atau receiver stuck. |

---

## 21. Read Routing Decision

| Query Type | Primary | Standby |
|---|---:|---:|
| Write transaction | Ya | Tidak |
| Read-after-write strict | Ya | Hanya jika sync/remote_apply atau lag-aware logic |
| Report toleran lag | Bisa | Ya |
| Long analytical query | Hindari jika mengganggu OLTP | Bisa, tapi monitor replay lag/conflict |
| Admin health check | Ya | Ya |
| Migration DDL | Primary | Tidak langsung ke standby |

**Rule:** jangan route read secara buta ke standby jika business flow butuh data terbaru setelah write.

---

## 22. Recovery Conflict di Hot Standby

Query read-only di standby bisa konflik dengan WAL replay. Contoh: primary melakukan vacuum/drop/cleanup yang harus direplay standby, tetapi standby sedang menjalankan query yang masih membutuhkan versi row lama. PostgreSQL hot standby docs memiliki bagian khusus handling query conflicts. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/high-availability.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Setting | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `max_standby_streaming_delay` | Berapa lama standby menunda replay WAL streaming sebelum membatalkan query konflik. |
| `max_standby_archive_delay` | Sama, tetapi untuk WAL dari archive. |
| `hot_standby_feedback` | Standby memberi tahu primary agar primary tidak vacuum row yang masih dibutuhkan standby. |

| Trade-off | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Naikkan delay | Query standby lebih jarang dibatalkan, tapi lag bisa naik. |
| Aktifkan `hot_standby_feedback` | Mengurangi conflict, tapi bisa membuat bloat di primary karena vacuum tertahan. |
| Batasi query panjang di standby | Replay lebih lancar, tapi report berat perlu strategi lain. |

---

## 23. Synchronous Replication Decision Matrix

| Kebutuhan | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Tidak boleh kehilangan committed transaction saat failover | Synchronous replication. |
| Latency commit sangat sensitif | Async atau sync hanya untuk transaksi tertentu. |
| Standby antar-region dengan latency tinggi | Hati-hati sync; latency commit ikut naik. |
| Butuh read-your-write dari standby | Pertimbangkan `remote_apply`, tapi mahal. |
| Banyak transaksi high-throughput | Async + WAL archive + strong RPO mungkin lebih realistis. |
| Regulatory/audit transaction sangat penting | Sync untuk subset workload bisa dipertimbangkan. |

**Prinsip:** synchronous replication meningkatkan data safety, tetapi bisa menurunkan availability: jika synchronous standby tidak tersedia, commit bisa tertahan tergantung konfigurasi.

---

## 24. HA Failure Scenarios

| Scenario | Gejala | Tindakan |
|---|---|---|
| Standby lagging | `replay_lag` / byte lag naik | Cek network, disk, query conflict, standby CPU/I/O. |
| Standby disconnected | `pg_stat_replication` row hilang | Cek network, auth, primary_conninfo, WAL availability. |
| Replication slot inactive | WAL retained naik | Reconnect standby atau drop slot jika obsolete. |
| Primary down | Write gagal | Failover procedure: fence, promote, redirect clients. |
| Old primary returns | Timeline diverged | `pg_rewind` atau rebuild as standby. |
| WAL archive broken | PITR/standby fallback risk | Perbaiki archive; jangan klaim backup sehat. |
| Sync standby unavailable | Commit latency/hang | Cek sync config; decide degrade/failover. |
| Read standby stale | Business inconsistency | Route critical reads ke primary atau lag-aware gating. |

---

## 25. Operational Runbook: Add New Physical Standby

### A. Primary preparation

```conf
wal_level = replica
max_wal_senders = 10
max_replication_slots = 10
```

```sql
CREATE ROLE replicator WITH REPLICATION LOGIN PASSWORD '...';

SELECT pg_create_physical_replication_slot('standby_1');
```

`pg_hba.conf` example:

```conf
host replication replicator 10.0.0.20/32 scram-sha-256
```

Reload:

```sql
SELECT pg_reload_conf();
```

### B. Take base backup

```bash
pg_basebackup \
  -h primary-db \
  -D /var/lib/postgresql/data \
  -U replicator \
  -Fp \
  -Xs \
  -P \
  -R \
  -S standby_1
```

### C. Start standby

```bash
pg_ctl start -D /var/lib/postgresql/data
```

### D. Verify from primary

```sql
SELECT
    application_name,
    state,
    sync_state,
    sent_lsn,
    replay_lsn
FROM pg_stat_replication;
```

### E. Verify from standby

```sql
SELECT pg_is_in_recovery();

SELECT
    pg_last_wal_receive_lsn(),
    pg_last_wal_replay_lsn();
```

---

## 26. Operational Runbook: Manual Failover

### A. Confirm primary failure

```text
- Primary tidak bisa menerima connection
- Storage/node/network status jelas
- Tidak ada kemungkinan primary lama tetap menerima write
```

### B. Fence old primary

```text
- Stop service jika masih reachable
- Disable write endpoint
- Remove from load balancer
- Block storage/network if needed
```

### C. Promote standby

```sql
SELECT pg_promote();
```

### D. Verify new primary

```sql
SELECT pg_is_in_recovery(); -- harus false

CREATE TABLE failover_smoke_test (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    checked_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### E. Redirect clients

```text
- Update primary endpoint
- Validate write path
- Validate read path
- Monitor errors and replication topology
```

### F. Rejoin old primary

```text
- Jangan langsung start sebagai primary
- Gunakan pg_rewind jika memungkinkan
- Jika tidak, rebuild via pg_basebackup
```

---

## 27. PostgreSQL HA Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Menganggap replication = backup | Standby ikut menerima bad delete/update. | Tetap punya backup + PITR. |
| Promote standby tanpa fencing | Split brain. | Fence old primary sebelum promote. |
| Tidak monitor replication slot | WAL memenuhi disk. | Alert retained WAL dan inactive slot. |
| Route semua read ke standby | Stale read / inconsistent UX. | Lag-aware routing. |
| Query report panjang di standby tanpa kontrol | Replay lag naik atau query dibatalkan. | Batasi query, tune standby delay, gunakan reporting replica khusus. |
| Synchronous replication antar-region tanpa evaluasi latency | Commit lambat/hang. | Uji latency dan pilih sync scope. |
| Old primary rejoin langsung setelah failover | Data divergence. | `pg_rewind` atau rebuild. |
| Tidak latihan failover | Runbook gagal saat incident. | Failover drill berkala. |
| Tidak tahu RPO/RTO | Desain HA tidak bisa dievaluasi. | Tetapkan target eksplisit. |
| Slot untuk standby obsolete tidak dihapus | WAL retention tak terbatas. | Lifecycle management slot. |

---

## 28. Replication vs Backup vs HA

| Concern | Replication | Backup/PITR | HA Orchestration |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hardware/node failure | Membantu | Membantu restore | Membantu failover cepat |
| Accidental delete/update | Tidak cukup, karena ikut tereplikasi | Ya, PITR/restore | Tidak |
| Read scaling | Ya, hot standby | Tidak | Bisa bantu routing |
| Low RTO | Ya | Tergantung restore speed | Ya |
| Low RPO | Sync replication / frequent WAL archive | WAL archive | Tergantung design |
| Split brain prevention | Tidak otomatis penuh | Tidak | Perlu fencing/orchestration |
| Restore object tertentu | Tidak | Logical/side restore | Tidak |

**Kesimpulan:** replication meningkatkan availability, tetapi bukan pengganti backup. HA tanpa backup tetap rentan terhadap human error, corruption, bad migration, dan application bug.

---

## 29. Health Check Query Pack

### Primary role

```sql
SELECT
    pg_is_in_recovery() AS is_standby,
    pg_current_wal_lsn() AS current_wal_lsn;
```

### Standby role

```sql
SELECT
    pg_is_in_recovery() AS is_standby,
    pg_last_wal_receive_lsn() AS receive_lsn,
    pg_last_wal_replay_lsn() AS replay_lsn,
    now() - pg_last_xact_replay_timestamp() AS replay_time_lag;
```

### Replication status on primary

```sql
SELECT
    application_name,
    client_addr,
    state,
    sync_state,
    sent_lsn,
    write_lsn,
    flush_lsn,
    replay_lsn,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_wal_lsn_diff(pg_current_wal_lsn(), replay_lsn)) AS byte_lag
FROM pg_stat_replication;
```

### Slot retention

```sql
SELECT
    slot_name,
    slot_type,
    active,
    pg_size_pretty(
        pg_wal_lsn_diff(pg_current_wal_lsn(), restart_lsn)
    ) AS retained_wal
FROM pg_replication_slots
WHERE restart_lsn IS NOT NULL;
```

### WAL receiver

```sql
SELECT
    status,
    receive_start_lsn,
    written_lsn,
    flushed_lsn,
    latest_end_lsn,
    latest_end_time
FROM pg_stat_wal_receiver;
```

---

## 30. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | WAL replication model | Paham bahwa physical replication mengikuti WAL. |
| 2 | Streaming replication setup | Bisa memahami primary/standby, WAL sender/receiver. |
| 3 | Hot standby | Bisa memakai standby untuk read-only workload dengan sadar lag. |
| 4 | Replication slots | Bisa mencegah WAL hilang tanpa membuat disk penuh. |
| 5 | Async vs sync replication | Bisa memilih trade-off data loss vs latency. |
| 6 | Monitoring lag | Bisa membaca `pg_stat_replication`, LSN, dan slot retention. |
| 7 | Failover | Bisa memahami promotion, fencing, dan client redirect. |
| 8 | Timeline dan `pg_rewind` | Bisa menangani old primary setelah failover. |
| 9 | HA drill | Bisa menguji RPO/RTO secara realistis. |

Ini masih PostgreSQL-only dan melengkapi physical replication dengan replikasi granular untuk integrasi, migrasi, reporting, dan event-driven data movement.
