## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:57:42 AM

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Logical Replication, Publication, Subscription, Replication Identity, Initial Sync, Conflict, dan CDC Pattern

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk **logical replication**: publication, subscription, replication identity, initial table synchronization, row filtering, column list, conflict, DDL limitation, sequence limitation, logical replication slot, monitoring, dan pola CDC/internal integration.

Logical replication berbeda dari physical replication. Physical replication mereplikasi perubahan level block/WAL secara byte-by-byte untuk seluruh cluster; logical replication mereplikasi perubahan **data object** berdasarkan **replication identity**, biasanya primary key. PostgreSQL mendukung physical dan logical replication secara bersamaan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="8h9nrn"
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Logical Replication))
    Publisher
      Database
      Publication
      Table Set
      Row Filter
      Column List
      WAL Decode
    Subscriber
      Subscription
      Apply Worker
      Table Sync Worker
      Replication Slot
      Initial Copy
    Replication Identity
      Primary Key
      Unique Index
      Full Row
      Nothing
    Data Flow
      INSERT
      UPDATE
      DELETE
      TRUNCATE
      Transaction Order
    Limitations
      DDL Not Replicated
      Sequence Not Replicated
      Large Objects Not Replicated
      Tables Only
      Conflict Handling
    Operations
      Monitor Lag
      Refresh Publication
      Disable Enable
      Resync Table
      Drop Subscription
```

**Core idea:** logical replication adalah **publish-subscribe data movement**. Publisher mendefinisikan data apa yang diterbitkan; subscriber menarik perubahan dari publication tersebut dan menerapkannya sebagai DML lokal.

---

## 3. Logical vs Physical Replication

| Aspek | Physical Replication | Logical Replication |
|---|---|---|
| Unit replikasi | Seluruh cluster / database files / WAL fisik | Table/data object tertentu |
| Granularity | Kasar | Halus |
| Cross-version | Terbatas | Lebih fleksibel untuk major version tertentu |
| Cross-platform | Terbatas | Bisa antar-platform |
| DDL | Mengikuti physical state | **Tidak direplikasi otomatis** |
| Sequence state | Mengikuti physical state | **Sequence tidak direplikasi sebagai sequence** |
| Read/write subscriber | Standby biasanya read-only | Subscriber adalah database normal; bisa ditulis, tapi rawan conflict |
| Use case utama | HA/DR/read replica | Migrasi, selective replication, consolidation, CDC, reporting |
| Failover readiness | Lebih cocok | Perlu desain tambahan |

PostgreSQL mendeskripsikan logical replication sebagai model publish-subscribe. Subscriber dapat subscribe ke satu atau lebih publication, menarik data dari publisher, lalu bahkan dapat re-publish untuk konfigurasi cascading atau topologi lebih kompleks. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication.html))

---

## 4. Use Case yang Cocok

| Use Case | Cocok? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Replicate subset table ke reporting DB | Ya | Cocok karena granular. |
| Migrasi major version PostgreSQL | Ya | Salah satu use case resmi logical replication. |
| Migrasi antar-platform | Ya | Misalnya OS/platform berbeda. |
| Consolidate beberapa database ke satu analytics DB | Ya | Perlu conflict/key design. |
| CDC internal untuk downstream processor | Ya | Perlu slot/lag monitoring. |
| HA primary-standby penuh | Tidak ideal | Physical replication lebih cocok. |
| Replicate schema changes otomatis | Tidak | DDL tidak direplikasi. |
| Multi-primary write aktif | Sangat hati-hati | PostgreSQL built-in logical replication bukan magic conflict-free multi-master. |

Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyebut use case logical replication meliputi mengirim incremental changes dari satu database atau subset database, men-trigger perubahan individual di subscriber, consolidation beberapa database untuk analitik, replication antar-major-version, dan replication antar-platform. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication.html))

---

## 5. Prerequisites

### Publisher-side config

```conf id="hp8w3l"
wal_level = logical
max_wal_senders = 10
max_replication_slots = 10
```

Untuk logical replication, server yang menjalankan `CREATE PUBLICATION` adalah publisher, dan server yang menjalankan `CREATE SUBSCRIPTION` adalah subscriber. Sebuah server juga bisa menjadi publisher dan subscriber sekaligus. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-replication.html))

### Role replication

```sql id="x6afpw"
CREATE ROLE repl_user WITH LOGIN REPLICATION PASSWORD 'change_me';
```

### `pg_hba.conf`

```conf id="nd7kdr"
host    replication    repl_user    10.0.0.20/32    scram-sha-256
host    mydb           repl_user    10.0.0.20/32    scram-sha-256
```

### Table requirement

Untuk `UPDATE` dan `DELETE` yang direplikasi secara efisien, table sebaiknya punya primary key atau replica identity yang jelas.

```sql id="46g2me"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT case_file_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id);
```

---

## 6. Publication

Publication adalah definisi data/change set yang diterbitkan oleh publisher.

### Basic publication untuk table tertentu

```sql id="b21r0r"
CREATE PUBLICATION pub_case_core
FOR TABLE
    case_mgmt.case_file,
    case_mgmt.case_task,
    audit.case_event;
```

### Publication untuk semua table

```sql id="c5cz1d"
CREATE PUBLICATION pub_all_tables
FOR ALL TABLES;
```

### Publication untuk operasi tertentu

```sql id="xgy5mw"
CREATE PUBLICATION pub_case_insert_update
FOR TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
WITH (publish = 'insert, update');
```

| Publish Action | Efek |
|---|---|
| `insert` | Replicate insert. |
| `update` | Replicate update. |
| `delete` | Replicate delete. |
| `truncate` | Replicate truncate, dengan caveat FK/subscription. |

**Rule:** publication harus merepresentasikan contract data movement. Jangan asal `FOR ALL TABLES` kalau downstream hanya butuh subset.

---

## 7. Row Filter

Row filter membatasi row yang dipublish.

```sql id="mbe1j9"
CREATE PUBLICATION pub_open_cases
FOR TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE (status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'ESCALATED'));
```

Contoh untuk outbox:

```sql id="rjknz9"
CREATE PUBLICATION pub_published_outbox
FOR TABLE integration.outbox_message
WHERE (status = 'PUBLISHED');
```

**Catatan penting:** jika table dalam publication punya `WHERE` clause, row yang expression-nya `false` atau `NULL` tidak dipublish. Jika subscriber memakai beberapa publication yang mempublish table sama dengan filter berbeda, row dapat dipublish jika memenuhi salah satu filter; publication tanpa filter atau `FOR ALL TABLES` dapat membuat row selalu dipublish. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createsubscription.html))

---

## 8. Column List

Column list membatasi column yang dipublish.

```sql id="uvqq42"
CREATE PUBLICATION pub_case_public_projection
FOR TABLE case_mgmt.case_file (
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    updated_at
);
```

Use case:

| Use Case | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Hide sensitive column | Subscriber tidak menerima data tertentu. |
| Reporting projection | Subscriber hanya butuh field tertentu. |
| Reduce replication volume | Mengurangi payload perubahan. |
| Schema decoupling | Subscriber bisa punya table subset. |

**Trade-off:** column list meningkatkan decoupling, tetapi menambah governance. DDL tetap harus dijaga manual antara publisher dan subscriber.

---

## 9. Subscription

Subscription adalah sisi subscriber yang connect ke publisher dan subscribe ke publication.

```sql id="l1ymg3"
CREATE SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core
CONNECTION 'host=publisher-db port=5432 dbname=mydb user=repl_user password=change_me'
PUBLICATION pub_case_core;
```

`CREATE SUBSCRIPTION` membuat logical-replication subscription. Secara normal, command ini juga membuat replication slot di publisher, dan logical replication worker akan mulai mereplikasi saat transaction pembuatan subscription commit, kecuali subscription dibuat disabled. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createsubscription.html))

### Subscription dengan parameter eksplisit

```sql id="wfgbrs"
CREATE SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core
CONNECTION 'host=publisher-db port=5432 dbname=mydb user=repl_user password=change_me'
PUBLICATION pub_case_core
WITH (
    create_slot = true,
    enabled = true,
    copy_data = true,
    slot_name = 'sub_case_core_slot'
);
```

| Parameter | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `create_slot` | Buat replication slot di publisher. |
| `enabled` | Subscription langsung aktif atau tidak. |
| `copy_data` | Copy data awal atau hanya perubahan baru. |
| `slot_name` | Nama slot publisher. |
| `connect` | Apakah command connect ke publisher saat create. |
| `disable_on_error` | Disable subscription otomatis jika worker error. |
| `run_as_owner` | Mode execution privilege di subscriber. |
| `origin` | Filter perubahan berdasarkan origin. |
| `failover` | Menandai slot subscription agar bisa disinkronkan ke standby untuk resume setelah failover. |

Beberapa parameter penting punya default: `create_slot` default `true`, `enabled` default `true`, `slot_name` default memakai nama subscription, dan `failover` default `false`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createsubscription.html))

---

## 10. Initial Table Synchronization

Saat logical replication table mulai, PostgreSQL biasanya mengambil snapshot data table di publisher, menyalinnya ke subscriber, lalu mengirim perubahan yang terjadi setelah initial copy secara terus-menerus. Subscriber menerapkan data dalam urutan yang sama seperti publisher, sehingga transactional consistency dijamin untuk publication dalam satu subscription. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication.html))

```text id="6r7l0x"
CREATE SUBSCRIPTION copy_data=true
   ↓
Subscriber creates table sync worker
   ↓
Snapshot table data copied from publisher
   ↓
WAL changes after snapshot streamed
   ↓
Apply worker keeps subscriber updated
```

### `copy_data = true`

```sql id="nmm5m4"
CREATE SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core
CONNECTION 'host=publisher-db dbname=mydb user=repl_user password=change_me'
PUBLICATION pub_case_core
WITH (copy_data = true);
```

Cocok jika subscriber kosong dan butuh data awal.

### `copy_data = false`

```sql id="eok6te"
CREATE SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core
CONNECTION 'host=publisher-db dbname=mydb user=repl_user password=change_me'
PUBLICATION pub_case_core
WITH (copy_data = false);
```

Cocok jika data awal sudah diseed manual, misalnya via dump/restore, dan hanya ingin apply perubahan baru.

---

## 11. Refresh Publication

Jika table baru ditambahkan ke publication, subscriber perlu refresh agar tahu table baru.

```sql id="z0qcks"
ALTER PUBLICATION pub_case_core
ADD TABLE case_mgmt.case_comment;
```

Di subscriber:

```sql id="2w1m8q"
ALTER SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core
REFRESH PUBLICATION;
```

Jika tidak ingin copy data awal untuk table baru:

```sql id="asjou2"
ALTER SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core
REFRESH PUBLICATION WITH (copy_data = false);
```

**Rule:** perubahan publication adalah perubahan contract. Treat seperti migration: rencanakan schema subscriber, permission, initial data, dan monitoring.

---

## 12. Replication Identity

Replication identity menentukan informasi lama yang ditulis ke WAL untuk mengidentifikasi row saat `UPDATE` atau `DELETE` pada logical replication.

```sql id="i5r3fs"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file REPLICA IDENTITY DEFAULT;
```

PostgreSQL `ALTER TABLE ... REPLICA IDENTITY` mengubah informasi yang ditulis ke WAL untuk mengidentifikasi row yang di-update atau di-delete. Efeknya hanya relevan ketika logical replication digunakan. `DEFAULT` memakai old values dari primary key; jika tidak ada primary key, behavior sama seperti `NOTHING`. `FULL` mencatat old values dari semua column. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

| Mode | Arti | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|---|
| `DEFAULT` | Pakai primary key lama | Default terbaik untuk hampir semua table. |
| `USING INDEX index_name` | Pakai unique index tertentu | Jika tidak ingin primary key atau memakai natural key unique. |
| `FULL` | Catat semua old column values | Table tanpa key, tapi mahal. |
| `NOTHING` | Tidak catat old row identity | `UPDATE`/`DELETE` logical replication bermasalah/tidak berguna. |

### Replica identity using index

Index harus unique, bukan partial, bukan deferrable, dan hanya mencakup column `NOT NULL`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

```sql id="j1wkgu"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_case_file_case_number_not_null
ON case_mgmt.case_file (case_number);

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
REPLICA IDENTITY USING INDEX uq_case_file_case_number_not_null;
```

### Replica identity full

```sql id="7fk1a3"
ALTER TABLE audit.case_event
REPLICA IDENTITY FULL;
```

**Hati-hati:** `FULL` bisa membesarkan WAL dan memperlambat replication, terutama jika row besar atau punya TOAST-heavy column seperti `jsonb` besar.

---

## 13. DDL Limitation

Logical replication **tidak mereplikasi schema dan DDL commands**. Initial schema bisa disalin manual dengan `pg_dump --schema-only`, lalu perubahan schema berikutnya harus dijaga manual. Jika data yang direplikasi tidak cocok dengan schema subscriber, replication akan error sampai schema diperbaiki. PostgreSQL menyarankan banyak intermittent error dapat dihindari dengan menerapkan additive schema changes ke subscriber terlebih dahulu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication-restrictions.html))

### Safe schema rollout pattern

```text id="iyd86d"
1. Add column on subscriber first
2. Add column on publisher
3. Update publication column list if needed
4. Deploy writes on publisher
5. Validate replication
6. Remove old column only after subscriber no longer needs it
```

### Schema-only copy

```bash id="23mbhf"
pg_dump --schema-only --schema=case_mgmt mydb > case_mgmt_schema.sql

psql subscriber_db < case_mgmt_schema.sql
```

**Rule:** logical replication requires schema governance. Jangan menganggap subscriber otomatis mengikuti DDL publisher.

---

## 14. Sequence Limitation

Sequence data **tidak direplikasi** sebagai sequence. Nilai dari serial/identity column yang tersimpan di row tentu ikut direplikasi sebagai data table, tetapi object sequence di subscriber tetap pada state sendiri. Jika subscriber hanya read-only, ini biasanya tidak masalah. Jika subscriber akan dipakai untuk switchover/failover/write, sequence harus disesuaikan ke nilai terbaru. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication-restrictions.html))

### Sync sequence after cutover

```sql id="t96c6y"
SELECT setval(
    'case_mgmt.case_file_id_seq',
    (SELECT max(id) FROM case_mgmt.case_file),
    true
);
```

Untuk identity column, cek sequence terkait:

```sql id="tiz3am"
SELECT pg_get_serial_sequence('case_mgmt.case_file', 'id');
```

**Rule:** sebelum menjadikan subscriber sebagai writer, audit semua sequence/identity generator.

---

## 15. Object Limitation

Logical replication punya beberapa batasan penting:

| Object / Operation | Status |
|---|---|
| Table | Didukung |
| Partitioned table | Didukung dengan caveat |
| View | Tidak direplikasi sebagai target relation |
| Materialized view | Tidak direplikasi sebagai target relation |
| Foreign table | Tidak direplikasi sebagai target relation |
| Large object | Tidak direplikasi |
| DDL | Tidak direplikasi |
| Sequence state | Tidak direplikasi |
| `TRUNCATE` | Didukung, tetapi hati-hati dengan FK dan table set subscription |

Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyatakan logical replication hanya didukung oleh table, termasuk partitioned table; attempts mereplikasi view, materialized view, atau foreign table akan error. Large objects juga tidak direplikasi. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication-restrictions.html))

---

## 16. Conflict Behavior

Logical replication menerapkan perubahan di subscriber mirip DML normal. Jika incoming data melanggar constraint, replication berhenti; ini disebut conflict. Untuk `UPDATE` atau `DELETE`, row yang hilang di subscriber juga dianggap conflict, tetapi tidak menghasilkan error dan operasi tersebut dilewati. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication-conflicts.html))

| Conflict | Contoh | Behavior |
|---|---|---|
| `insert_exists` | INSERT dari publisher melanggar unique constraint di subscriber | Error sampai diperbaiki manual. |
| `update_exists` | UPDATE membuat value melanggar unique constraint | Error sampai diperbaiki manual. |
| `update_missing` | Row yang mau di-update tidak ada di subscriber | Dilewati. |
| `delete_missing` | Row yang mau di-delete tidak ada di subscriber | Dilewati. |
| Exclusion/check/FK conflict | Constraint violation | Bisa menghentikan apply worker. |
| Local writes di subscriber | Bisa ditimpa/menimbulkan conflict | Hindari kecuali desain sadar conflict. |

Conflict statistics dikumpulkan di `pg_stat_subscription_stats`, dan beberapa detail origin/commit timestamp membutuhkan `track_commit_timestamp` aktif di subscriber. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication-conflicts.html))

---

## 17. Conflict Prevention Rules

| Rule | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Subscriber sebaiknya read-only untuk replicated tables | Menghindari conflict dari local writes. |
| Jangan generate ID/sequence lokal di table replicated | Bisa bentrok dengan row publisher. |
| Samakan constraint penting | Agar data invalid tidak diam-diam masuk. |
| Terapkan additive DDL di subscriber dulu | Menghindari apply error saat publisher mulai kirim column baru. |
| Gunakan primary key/replica identity jelas | `UPDATE`/`DELETE` butuh row identity. |
| Pisahkan ownership data per node jika multi-source | Hindari dua publisher menulis key space sama. |
| Monitor subscription stats | Conflict harus cepat terlihat. |

---

## 18. Monitoring Logical Replication

### Subscription status di subscriber

```sql id="g3rsxe"
SELECT
    subid,
    subname,
    pid,
    relid::regclass AS relation,
    received_lsn,
    latest_end_lsn,
    latest_end_time
FROM pg_stat_subscription;
```

### Subscription conflict/error stats

```sql id="xo64yy"
SELECT
    subid,
    subname,
    apply_error_count,
    sync_error_count,
    stats_reset
FROM pg_stat_subscription_stats;
```

### Replication slot di publisher

```sql id="c0a6fe"
SELECT
    slot_name,
    slot_type,
    database,
    active,
    restart_lsn,
    confirmed_flush_lsn,
    wal_status,
    safe_wal_size
FROM pg_replication_slots
ORDER BY slot_name;
```

### WAL retained by logical slot

```sql id="rg3f61"
SELECT
    slot_name,
    active,
    pg_size_pretty(
        pg_wal_lsn_diff(pg_current_wal_lsn(), restart_lsn)
    ) AS retained_wal
FROM pg_replication_slots
WHERE slot_type = 'logical'
  AND restart_lsn IS NOT NULL;
```

**Risk:** logical slot yang tidak dikonsumsi dapat menahan WAL dan membuat disk penuh. Treat logical slot sama seriusnya dengan physical replication slot.

---

## 19. Operational Control

### Disable subscription

```sql id="t1cmyc"
ALTER SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core DISABLE;
```

### Enable subscription

```sql id="uqobdp"
ALTER SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core ENABLE;
```

### Change connection

```sql id="clqqd3"
ALTER SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core
CONNECTION 'host=new-publisher dbname=mydb user=repl_user password=change_me';
```

### Add publication to subscription

```sql id="07892r"
ALTER SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core
SET PUBLICATION pub_case_core, pub_case_extra;
```

### Drop subscription

```sql id="lmfcl2"
DROP SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core;
```

**Important:** drop subscription biasanya juga mencoba drop replication slot terkait di publisher jika reachable. Kalau publisher tidak reachable, perlu handling manual agar slot tidak orphan.

---

## 20. Resync Table Pattern

Jika satu table subscriber corrupt/out-of-sync, pendekatan aman biasanya resync table.

```text id="7fqi5f"
1. Disable subscription
2. Stop writes to affected table or define consistency window
3. Truncate/reload table on subscriber
4. Refresh/resync publication/subscription
5. Enable subscription
6. Validate counts/checksums/business queries
```

Contoh sederhana:

```sql id="rk193e"
ALTER SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core DISABLE;

TRUNCATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_file;

ALTER SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core ENABLE;

ALTER SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core REFRESH PUBLICATION WITH (copy_data = true);
```

**Hati-hati:** jangan truncate subscriber table sembarangan jika ada FK atau local-only data. Untuk data kritikal, lakukan side-by-side rebuild lalu swap.

---

## 21. CDC Pattern dengan Logical Replication

Logical replication dapat dipakai sebagai CDC internal PostgreSQL-to-PostgreSQL, terutama untuk reporting, integration, atau downstream processing.

```text id="7ix1ii"
OLTP Publisher
   ↓ publication: integration.outbox_message
Reporting / Integration Subscriber
   ↓ downstream processing/reporting
```

### Pattern A - Replicate business tables

```sql id="sn9lhv"
CREATE PUBLICATION pub_reporting
FOR TABLE
    case_mgmt.case_file,
    case_mgmt.case_task,
    audit.case_event;
```

Cocok untuk read model/reporting. Risiko: schema coupling lebih besar.

### Pattern B - Replicate outbox table

```sql id="z7g3cm"
CREATE PUBLICATION pub_outbox
FOR TABLE integration.outbox_message
WHERE (status = 'PUBLISHED');
```

Cocok untuk event-driven integration. Lebih eksplisit karena yang direplikasi adalah event/outbox, bukan seluruh state table.

### Pattern C - Replicate append-only audit log

```sql id="8yetko"
CREATE PUBLICATION pub_case_event
FOR TABLE audit.case_event
WITH (publish = 'insert');
```

Cocok untuk immutable event stream. Lebih mudah daripada update-heavy table karena conflict dan replica identity lebih sederhana.

---

## 22. Logical Replication untuk Migration / Upgrade

Logical replication sering dipakai untuk migrasi online antar major version:

```text id="zuz15x"
1. Prepare target database schema
2. Create publication on old/source
3. Create subscription on new/target with copy_data=true
4. Wait initial sync complete
5. Monitor lag until near-zero
6. Stop writes on source
7. Let subscriber catch up
8. Validate data
9. Sync sequences
10. Cut application to target
```

### Cutover checklist

| Item | Check |
|---|---|
| Schema target | Sudah compatible dengan publisher. |
| Extensions | Sudah tersedia di target. |
| Publications/subscriptions | Active dan caught up. |
| Lag | Mendekati nol sebelum write freeze. |
| Constraints | Sama atau lebih ketat sesuai desain. |
| Sequences | Di-set ke nilai aman setelah catch-up. |
| Roles/privileges | Sudah dipindah. |
| Application endpoint | Siap cutover. |
| Rollback | Jelas apakah rollback ke source masih mungkin. |

---

## 23. Partitioned Table Notes

Logical replication mendukung table termasuk partitioned table, tetapi ada caveat. Secara default, saat replication antar partitioned tables, perubahan biasanya berasal dari leaf partitions di publisher, sehingga partition di publisher harus juga ada sebagai target valid di subscriber. Publication dapat memakai `publish_via_partition_root` agar perubahan direplikasi memakai identity dan schema partitioned root table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication-restrictions.html))

```sql id="73a3jk"
CREATE PUBLICATION pub_case_event_partitioned
FOR TABLE audit.case_event_partitioned
WITH (publish_via_partition_root = true);
```

**Rule:** untuk partitioned table, dokumentasikan apakah contract replikasi berbasis root table atau leaf partition. Ini memengaruhi schema subscriber dan maintenance partition.

---

## 24. Security Model

| Area | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Replication user | Pakai role khusus, bukan superuser. |
| Network access | Batasi `pg_hba.conf` hanya dari subscriber. |
| Password/auth | Gunakan SCRAM/certificate sesuai policy. |
| Publication scope | Jangan publish semua table jika tidak perlu. |
| Sensitive columns | Gunakan column list atau projection table. |
| Subscriber writes | Batasi privilege write pada replicated tables. |
| Connection string | Jangan expose password di script/log. |
| Ownership | Hindari `run_as_owner = true` kecuali paham risikonya. |

`CREATE SUBSCRIPTION` membutuhkan privilege `pg_create_subscription` dan `CREATE` privilege pada database saat ini. Parameter `run_as_owner=false` adalah default dan umumnya lebih aman karena worker bertindak sebagai owner table masing-masing, bukan selalu sebagai subscription owner. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createsubscription.html))

---

## 25. Failure Modes

| Gejala | Kemungkinan | Langkah Awal |
|---|---|---|
| Subscription tidak jalan | Connection/auth/publication/slot error | Cek logs, `pg_stat_subscription`. |
| WAL di publisher membesar | Logical slot tidak aktif/lagging | Cek `pg_replication_slots`. |
| Apply worker error unique violation | `insert_exists`/`update_exists` conflict | Resolve data conflict manual. |
| Table baru tidak tereplikasi | Subscriber belum refresh publication | `ALTER SUBSCRIPTION ... REFRESH PUBLICATION`. |
| DDL publisher bikin subscription error | Schema subscriber belum compatible | Apply DDL di subscriber. |
| DELETE tidak sampai | Replica identity tidak cukup | Set primary key/replica identity. |
| Sequence duplicate setelah cutover | Sequence state tidak disync | `setval()` sequence target. |
| Subscriber data out-of-sync | Local writes/conflict/skipped missing row | Resync table atau rebuild subscriber. |
| Partition replication error | Leaf/root schema mismatch | Review `publish_via_partition_root`. |

---

## 26. Common Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Menganggap logical replication mereplikasi DDL | Apply worker error setelah schema berubah | Schema migration manual, subscriber dulu untuk additive change. |
| Menganggap sequence ikut aman untuk failover | Duplicate key saat subscriber jadi writer | Sync sequence sebelum cutover. |
| Table tanpa primary key direplikasi dengan update/delete | Update/delete tidak reliable/mahal | Tambah PK atau replica identity. |
| `REPLICA IDENTITY FULL` di row besar | WAL membesar drastis | Gunakan PK/unique index. |
| Subscriber menerima local writes di replicated table | Conflict atau overwrite | Jadikan replicated table read-only. |
| Slot logical tidak dimonitor | Disk penuh di publisher | Alert retained WAL. |
| Publication terlalu luas | Data sensitif/volume tinggi ikut keluar | Scope table/column/filter. |
| Initial sync dianggap instant | Load besar, lock/resource pressure | Rencanakan window dan monitor sync. |
| Conflict diselesaikan tanpa root cause | Error berulang | Fix ownership/key/schema/proses write. |
| Logical replication dipakai sebagai satu-satunya backup | Bad delete tetap replicated | Tetap butuh backup + PITR. |

---

## 27. Decision Matrix

| Problem | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Need reporting copy subset table | Publication table subset + subscriber read-only |
| Need near-online major upgrade | Logical replication migration pattern |
| Need CDC event stream | Replicate append-only outbox/audit table |
| Need cross-platform data movement | Logical replication |
| Need DR/failover entire cluster | Physical replication + PITR, bukan hanya logical |
| Need selective sensitive data replication | Column list + row filter |
| Need update/delete replication | Primary key / replica identity |
| Need subscriber writable after cutover | Sync sequences + validate constraints + freeze writes during cutover |
| Need avoid conflicts | Single-writer ownership per replicated table/keyspace |

---

## 28. Minimal Setup Example

### Publisher

```sql id="413hkb"
CREATE ROLE repl_user WITH LOGIN REPLICATION PASSWORD 'change_me';

CREATE PUBLICATION pub_case_core
FOR TABLE
    case_mgmt.case_file,
    case_mgmt.case_task,
    audit.case_event;
```

Config:

```conf id="kkz425"
wal_level = logical
max_wal_senders = 10
max_replication_slots = 10
```

### Subscriber

Pastikan schema/table sudah ada.

```bash id="do7f4w"
pg_dump --schema-only --schema=case_mgmt --schema=audit source_db > schema.sql
psql target_db < schema.sql
```

Buat subscription:

```sql id="hiz3si"
CREATE SUBSCRIPTION sub_case_core
CONNECTION 'host=publisher-db port=5432 dbname=source_db user=repl_user password=change_me'
PUBLICATION pub_case_core
WITH (
    copy_data = true,
    create_slot = true,
    enabled = true,
    slot_name = 'sub_case_core_slot'
);
```

Monitor:

```sql id="u6h103"
SELECT * FROM pg_stat_subscription;

SELECT * FROM pg_stat_subscription_stats;
```

---

## 29. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Logical vs physical replication | Bisa memilih replication model yang tepat. |
| 2 | Publication | Bisa membatasi table, operation, row, dan column. |
| 3 | Subscription | Bisa membuat subscriber dan memahami slot/initial copy. |
| 4 | Replication identity | Bisa memastikan `UPDATE`/`DELETE` aman. |
| 5 | DDL dan sequence limitation | Bisa membuat migration/cutover plan realistis. |
| 6 | Conflict handling | Bisa mencegah dan menangani apply error. |
| 7 | Monitoring slot dan lag | Bisa mencegah WAL disk penuh dan detect stalled apply. |
| 8 | CDC/outbox pattern | Bisa memakai logical replication untuk integration. |
| 9 | Migration/cutover pattern | Bisa merancang near-online migration antar versi/platform. |

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjut paling tepat: **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Security & Access Control: Role, User, Group Role, Privilege, Schema Permission, Default Privilege, `pg_hba.conf`, SCRAM, SSL/TLS, Row-Level Security, dan Audit Posture**. Ini tetap PostgreSQL-only dan penting untuk production hardening setelah memahami storage, backup, replication, dan data movement.

---

**Sources:**

- [PostgreSQL: Documentation: 18: Chapter 29. Logical Replication](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication.html)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 12:59:27 AM

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Security & Access Control

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk security layer di database:

- role, user, group role
- privilege dan ownership
- schema permission
- default privilege
- `pg_hba.conf`
- authentication method
- SCRAM password auth
- SSL/TLS
- row-level security
- predefined roles
- audit posture dan hardening checklist

PostgreSQL security perlu dipahami sebagai kombinasi dari **authentication**, **authorization**, **object ownership**, **privilege inheritance**, **network access rule**, dan **row-level policy**. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menempatkan user/database management dan security sebagai bagian dari server administration yang wajib dipahami untuk production. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/admin.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="d4njg7"
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Security))
    Identity
      Role
      Login Role
      Group Role
      Role Attributes
      Membership
    Authentication
      pg_hba.conf
      local
      host
      hostssl
      SCRAM
      Certificate
      Peer
      LDAP/GSS/SSPI
    Authorization
      Ownership
      GRANT
      REVOKE
      PUBLIC
      Privilege Inheritance
    Object Boundary
      Database
      Schema
      Table
      Sequence
      Function
      View
      Column
    Default Privileges
      Future Tables
      Future Sequences
      Future Functions
      Per Schema Defaults
    Row Security
      RLS Enable
      Policy
      USING
      WITH CHECK
      FORCE RLS
      BYPASSRLS
    Transport Security
      SSL/TLS
      Server Cert
      Client Cert
      hostssl
      sslmode
    Audit Posture
      Least Privilege
      Privilege Review
      Sensitive Roles
      Dangerous Grants
      Logging
```

**Core idea:** PostgreSQL tidak punya pemisahan keras antara “user” dan “group” seperti beberapa DB lain. Semuanya adalah **role**. Role bisa diberi `LOGIN` sehingga bisa menjadi user login, atau dibuat tanpa `LOGIN` sehingga berfungsi sebagai group role.

---

## 3. Identity Model: Role, User, Group Role

PostgreSQL memakai konsep **role** untuk identity dan authorization. Role dapat memiliki attribute seperti `LOGIN`, `SUPERUSER`, `CREATEDB`, `CREATEROLE`, `REPLICATION`, `BYPASSRLS`, dan membership ke role lain. Secara default, role mewarisi privilege dari role yang menjadi membership-nya, kecuali inheritance diubah. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/role-attributes.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="jyao3m"
-- Login role / user
CREATE ROLE app_user LOGIN PASSWORD 'change_me';

-- Group role / permission role
CREATE ROLE app_readonly NOLOGIN;

-- Grant membership
GRANT app_readonly TO app_user;
```

| Role Type | Contoh | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| Login role | `app_user`, `reporting_user` | Dipakai untuk connect ke database. |
| Group role | `case_reader`, `case_writer` | Mengelompokkan privilege. |
| Owner role | `case_owner` | Memiliki schema/table/function. |
| Admin role | `db_admin_limited` | Operasional terbatas. |
| Replication role | `replicator` | Koneksi replication. |
| Migration role | `schema_migrator` | Membuat/mengubah object schema. |

**Rule senior:** jangan grant privilege langsung ke user individual jika sistem punya banyak account. Grant ke group role, lalu masukkan login role ke group role.

---

## 4. Role Attributes

```sql id="z0a5xa"
CREATE ROLE app_writer
    LOGIN
    PASSWORD 'change_me'
    NOSUPERUSER
    NOCREATEDB
    NOCREATEROLE
    NOREPLICATION
    NOBYPASSRLS;
```

| Attribute | Arti | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| `LOGIN` | Role bisa connect sebagai user. | Jangan diberikan ke group role murni. |
| `SUPERUSER` | Bypass hampir semua security. | Sangat berbahaya; hindari untuk app. |
| `CREATEDB` | Bisa membuat database. | Tidak perlu untuk app runtime. |
| `CREATEROLE` | Bisa membuat/mengubah role. | Privilege escalation risk. |
| `REPLICATION` | Bisa membuat replication connection. | Bisa membaca WAL/data movement. |
| `BYPASSRLS` | Bisa bypass row-level security. | Jangan untuk app user yang harus dibatasi RLS. |
| `INHERIT` | Mewarisi privilege role membership. | Default; mudah tapi perlu governance. |
| `NOINHERIT` | Tidak otomatis mewarisi privilege. | Bagus untuk privilege elevation eksplisit. |

### Pattern: login role + group role

```sql id="u2f8gr"
CREATE ROLE case_reader NOLOGIN;
CREATE ROLE case_writer NOLOGIN;

CREATE ROLE svc_case_api LOGIN PASSWORD 'change_me';

GRANT case_reader TO svc_case_api;
GRANT case_writer TO svc_case_api;
```

### Pattern: admin role dengan explicit elevation

```sql id="5qgjcg"
CREATE ROLE maintenance_admin NOLOGIN;
CREATE ROLE alice LOGIN PASSWORD 'change_me' NOINHERIT;

GRANT maintenance_admin TO alice;
```

Saat perlu elevate:

```sql id="4h77nh"
SET ROLE maintenance_admin;
```

---

## 5. Ownership vs Privilege

PostgreSQL membedakan **owner** dan **privilege recipient**.

| Konsep | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Owner | Role yang memiliki object; bisa `ALTER`, `DROP`, `GRANT`, `REVOKE`. |
| Privilege | Hak tertentu pada object, misalnya `SELECT`, `INSERT`, `UPDATE`. |
| Superuser | Bisa melewati mayoritas boundary. |
| `PUBLIC` | Pseudo-role yang berarti semua role. |

Biasanya hanya owner object atau superuser yang bisa grant/revoke privilege object. PostgreSQL juga mendukung `WITH GRANT OPTION`, yaitu penerima privilege dapat meneruskan privilege tersebut ke role lain. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-priv.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="x1j2pr"
CREATE ROLE case_owner NOLOGIN;
CREATE ROLE case_reader NOLOGIN;
CREATE ROLE case_writer NOLOGIN;

ALTER SCHEMA case_mgmt OWNER TO case_owner;
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file OWNER TO case_owner;
```

**Rule:** owner role sebaiknya **bukan** role runtime aplikasi. Aplikasi tidak perlu memiliki table; aplikasi hanya perlu privilege yang sesuai.

---

## 6. Privilege Model

`GRANT` memberikan privilege pada database object atau membership role. `REVOKE` mencabut privilege. PostgreSQL privilege berlaku pada object tertentu seperti database, schema, table, sequence, function, dan lainnya. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-grant.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Database privilege

```sql id="jfbkg5"
GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE appdb TO app_runtime;
GRANT TEMPORARY ON DATABASE appdb TO app_runtime;
```

### Schema privilege

```sql id="uq3l31"
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA case_mgmt TO case_reader;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA case_mgmt TO case_writer;
```

### Table privilege

```sql id="vpwyal"
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE case_mgmt.case_file TO case_reader;

GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE
ON TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
TO case_writer;
```

### Sequence privilege

```sql id="yv8eal"
GRANT USAGE, SELECT
ON SEQUENCE case_mgmt.case_file_id_seq
TO case_writer;
```

### Function privilege

```sql id="d6f4dz"
GRANT EXECUTE
ON FUNCTION case_mgmt.close_case(bigint)
TO case_writer;
```

---

## 7. Common Privileges by Object

| Object | Privilege Umum | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Database | `CONNECT`, `CREATE`, `TEMPORARY` | `CONNECT` diperlukan untuk masuk database. |
| Schema | `USAGE`, `CREATE` | `USAGE` untuk mengakses object dalam schema; `CREATE` untuk membuat object. |
| Table/View | `SELECT`, `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, `TRUNCATE`, `REFERENCES`, `TRIGGER` | `TRUNCATE` dan `TRIGGER` sering terlalu kuat untuk app runtime. |
| Sequence | `USAGE`, `SELECT`, `UPDATE` | Identity/sequence insert sering butuh `USAGE`. |
| Function/Procedure | `EXECUTE` | Hati-hati default `EXECUTE` ke `PUBLIC`. |
| Type/Domain | `USAGE` | Dibutuhkan untuk memakai type tertentu. |

PostgreSQL memberi beberapa default privilege ke `PUBLIC`, misalnya `CONNECT` dan `TEMPORARY` pada database, `EXECUTE` pada function/procedure, dan `USAGE` pada language serta data type/domain. Default ini bisa dicabut oleh owner. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-priv.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Schema Permission

Schema adalah namespace. Untuk memakai object dalam schema yang tidak dimiliki role, role membutuhkan `USAGE` pada schema dan privilege pada object terkait. Secara default, user tidak bisa mengakses object di schema yang tidak mereka miliki; owner schema harus memberikan `USAGE`. PostgreSQL juga mencatat bahwa `public` secara default memberi privilege tertentu ke semua user, sehingga sering perlu di-hardening. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-schemas.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="wbab6k"
-- Harden public schema
REVOKE CREATE ON SCHEMA public FROM PUBLIC;

-- Jika perlu, revoke usage juga tergantung policy
-- REVOKE USAGE ON SCHEMA public FROM PUBLIC;
```

### Recommended schema boundary

```sql id="jv91mv"
CREATE SCHEMA case_mgmt AUTHORIZATION case_owner;
CREATE SCHEMA audit AUTHORIZATION audit_owner;
CREATE SCHEMA integration AUTHORIZATION integration_owner;

GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA case_mgmt TO case_reader, case_writer;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA audit TO audit_reader;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA integration TO integration_worker;
```

**Rule:** `USAGE ON SCHEMA` tidak otomatis memberi akses table. Role tetap butuh `SELECT`, `INSERT`, dan seterusnya pada object.

---

## 9. Default Privileges

`ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES` mengatur privilege untuk object yang dibuat **di masa depan**. Ia tidak memengaruhi object yang sudah ada. Privilege dapat diatur global dalam database saat ini atau hanya untuk schema tertentu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-alterdefaultprivileges.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Default privilege untuk future tables

```sql id="p8t7xb"
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
FOR ROLE case_owner
IN SCHEMA case_mgmt
GRANT SELECT ON TABLES TO case_reader;
```

```sql id="45n15s"
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
FOR ROLE case_owner
IN SCHEMA case_mgmt
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON TABLES TO case_writer;
```

### Default privilege untuk future sequences

```sql id="sbag9p"
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
FOR ROLE case_owner
IN SCHEMA case_mgmt
GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON SEQUENCES TO case_writer;
```

### Default privilege untuk future functions

```sql id="070tkq"
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
FOR ROLE case_owner
IN SCHEMA case_mgmt
REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTIONS FROM PUBLIC;
```

**Pitfall besar:** default privileges berlaku berdasarkan **role yang membuat object**. Kalau migration kadang dijalankan oleh `postgres`, kadang oleh `schema_migrator`, dan kadang oleh `case_owner`, hasil privilege bisa berbeda.

---

## 10. Existing Object Grants vs Future Object Grants

```sql id="7f5r6d"
-- Existing objects
GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA case_mgmt TO case_reader;
GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA case_mgmt TO case_writer;

-- Future objects
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
FOR ROLE case_owner
IN SCHEMA case_mgmt
GRANT SELECT ON TABLES TO case_reader;
```

| Kebutuhan | Command |
|---|---|
| Grant ke table yang sudah ada | `GRANT ... ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA ...` |
| Grant ke sequence yang sudah ada | `GRANT ... ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA ...` |
| Grant ke function yang sudah ada | `GRANT ... ON ALL FUNCTIONS IN SCHEMA ...` |
| Grant ke object yang akan dibuat nanti | `ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES` |

**Rule:** setiap setup privilege production biasanya butuh dua bagian: existing objects dan future objects.

---

## 11. `pg_hba.conf`

Client authentication dikontrol oleh file `pg_hba.conf`, biasanya berada di data directory cluster. File ini dibaca saat startup dan saat server menerima reload/SIGHUP; setelah diedit pada sistem aktif, lakukan reload dengan `pg_ctl reload`, `SELECT pg_reload_conf()`, atau mekanisme setara. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/auth-pg-hba-conf.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Format umum:

```conf id="xnvm5r"
# TYPE  DATABASE        USER            ADDRESS             METHOD
local   all             postgres                            peer
host    appdb           svc_case_api    10.10.0.0/16        scram-sha-256
hostssl appdb           reporting_user  10.20.1.10/32       scram-sha-256
```

| Field | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `TYPE` | Jenis koneksi: `local`, `host`, `hostssl`, `hostnossl`, dll. |
| `DATABASE` | Database target. |
| `USER` | Role/user target. |
| `ADDRESS` | CIDR client address untuk TCP/IP. |
| `METHOD` | Authentication method. |

### Reload config

```sql id="lt4mi3"
SELECT pg_reload_conf();
```

### View parsed HBA

```sql id="fv5hz6"
SELECT *
FROM pg_hba_file_rules
ORDER BY line_number;
```

**Rule:** urutan `pg_hba.conf` penting. PostgreSQL mengevaluasi dari atas ke bawah dan memakai rule pertama yang match.

---

## 12. Authentication Methods

| Method | Kapan Dipakai | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `scram-sha-256` | Password auth modern | Prefer untuk password auth. |
| `md5` | Legacy compatibility | Hindari untuk desain baru jika SCRAM tersedia. |
| `password` | Clear-text password | Jangan pakai tanpa TLS; biasanya hindari. |
| `peer` | Local Unix socket mapping OS user | Cocok untuk local admin di Linux. |
| `cert` | Client certificate auth | Cocok untuk mutual TLS / cert-based auth. |
| `gss` / `sspi` | Enterprise SSO/Kerberos/Windows | Sesuai environment. |
| `trust` | No password | Jangan untuk production TCP. |
| `reject` | Explicit deny | Berguna untuk rule blocking. |

`trust` authentication hanya cocok jika semua user dari host yang diizinkan benar-benar dipercaya. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyebut `trust` jarang masuk akal untuk TCP/IP selain localhost. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/auth-trust.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 13. SCRAM Password Authentication

Password database PostgreSQL terpisah dari password OS. Password role disimpan di catalog `pg_authid`; jika role tidak punya password, password authentication selalu gagal. Password dapat diatur dengan `CREATE ROLE`, `ALTER ROLE`, atau `\password`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/auth-password.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Set password encryption

```conf id="bwdgxd"
password_encryption = 'scram-sha-256'
```

### Create user with password

```sql id="2bsr4h"
CREATE ROLE svc_case_api
LOGIN
PASSWORD 'change_me'
NOSUPERUSER
NOCREATEDB
NOCREATEROLE
NOREPLICATION
NOBYPASSRLS;
```

### Rotate password

```sql id="jwnjgc"
ALTER ROLE svc_case_api PASSWORD 'new_secret';
```

### HBA rule

```conf id="jkelgo"
hostssl appdb svc_case_api 10.10.0.0/16 scram-sha-256
```

**Rule:** gunakan `scram-sha-256` untuk password auth modern, pastikan client library mendukung, dan jangan mengirim password auth lewat koneksi non-TLS pada network tidak tepercaya.

---

## 14. SSL/TLS

PostgreSQL memakai istilah `ssl` untuk transfer encryption, walaupun konteks modernnya adalah TLS; SSL protocol lama sudah deprecated. Parameter `ssl` default-nya `off`, dan file seperti `ssl_cert_file`, `ssl_key_file`, serta `ssl_ca_file` mengatur sertifikat server dan CA untuk verifikasi client certificate. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-connection.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Server config

```conf id="ecjp2t"
ssl = on
ssl_cert_file = 'server.crt'
ssl_key_file = 'server.key'
ssl_ca_file = 'root.crt'
```

### HBA require TLS

```conf id="9b5lbb"
hostssl appdb svc_case_api 10.10.0.0/16 scram-sha-256
```

### Certificate authentication

```conf id="dq2g9f"
hostssl appdb cert_user 10.10.0.0/16 cert
```

PostgreSQL mendukung dua pendekatan client certificate: memakai `cert` authentication method pada `hostssl`, atau menggabungkan auth method lain dengan verifikasi client certificate melalui `clientcert=verify-ca` atau `clientcert=verify-full`. `verify-full` juga memastikan Common Name certificate cocok dengan username atau mapping yang berlaku. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ssl-tcp.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15. `PUBLIC` Hardening

`PUBLIC` adalah pseudo-role yang berarti semua role. Ini sering jadi sumber privilege yang terlalu luas.

### Revoke dangerous defaults

```sql id="83x13w"
REVOKE CREATE ON SCHEMA public FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON DATABASE appdb FROM PUBLIC;
```

Lalu grant eksplisit:

```sql id="qei1pb"
GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE appdb TO app_runtime;
GRANT TEMPORARY ON DATABASE appdb TO app_runtime;
```

### Revoke execute on functions from public

```sql id="0t29vl"
REVOKE EXECUTE ON ALL FUNCTIONS IN SCHEMA public FROM PUBLIC;
```

Future functions:

```sql id="qb1kis"
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
IN SCHEMA public
REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTIONS FROM PUBLIC;
```

**Rule:** review semua privilege `PUBLIC` saat database baru dibuat. Default yang nyaman untuk development sering terlalu longgar untuk production.

---

## 16. Row-Level Security / RLS

Selain privilege SQL standar via `GRANT`, PostgreSQL mendukung **row-level security** untuk membatasi row mana yang bisa dibaca, diinsert, diupdate, atau dihapus oleh user tertentu. Policy RLS bekerja per table dan per user/role. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-rowsecurity.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Enable RLS

```sql id="87qa71"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
```

### Policy untuk tenant isolation

```sql id="f7d0dp"
CREATE POLICY case_file_tenant_policy
ON case_mgmt.case_file
USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::bigint)
WITH CHECK (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::bigint);
```

### Set tenant context

```sql id="8naufq"
SET app.tenant_id = '1001';

SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_file;
```

| Clause | Berlaku Untuk | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `USING` | `SELECT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE` visibility | Row mana yang terlihat/tertarget. |
| `WITH CHECK` | `INSERT`, `UPDATE` new row | Row baru/hasil update boleh atau tidak. |

### Force RLS for owner

```sql id="c0kc9t"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
```

**Important:** table owner dan role dengan `BYPASSRLS` dapat melewati RLS kecuali policy/design memperhitungkan hal ini. Jangan pakai owner role sebagai runtime role aplikasi.

---

## 17. RLS Pattern: Case Assignment Visibility

Contoh: user hanya boleh melihat case yang assigned ke role/subject mereka.

```sql id="0xeob8"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_file (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    assigned_user text,
    status text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY case_file_assignee_read_policy
ON case_mgmt.case_file
FOR SELECT
USING (
    assigned_user = current_user
    OR pg_has_role(current_user, 'case_supervisor', 'member')
);
```

**Interpretasi:** RLS bagus untuk guardrail data-access di database, tetapi jangan jadikan RLS sebagai satu-satunya tempat business workflow authorization yang kompleks. Untuk workflow state transition, tetap perlu constraint, transaction pattern, dan domain logic.

---

## 18. Column-Level Privilege

PostgreSQL mendukung grant pada column tertentu.

```sql id="wilv53"
GRANT SELECT (id, case_number, status, created_at)
ON case_mgmt.case_file
TO case_reader_limited;
```

```sql id="mrz1pk"
GRANT UPDATE (status, updated_at)
ON case_mgmt.case_file
TO case_status_writer;
```

| Use Case | Column Privilege Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Hide sensitive column sederhana | Ya |
| Allow update hanya field tertentu | Ya |
| Complex masking / derived projection | Lebih baik view |
| Dynamic per-row masking | RLS/view/function pattern |

---

## 19. View-Based Access Boundary

Untuk read-only projection, view sering lebih mudah diaudit daripada grant langsung ke table penuh.

```sql id="qz1e7y"
CREATE VIEW case_mgmt.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    created_at
FROM case_mgmt.case_file;

REVOKE ALL ON case_mgmt.case_file FROM reporting_user;
GRANT SELECT ON case_mgmt.v_case_summary TO reporting_user;
```

### Security barrier view

```sql id="jn1ck8"
CREATE VIEW case_mgmt.v_case_summary_secure
WITH (security_barrier = true)
AS
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE status <> 'SEALED';
```

**Rule:** view bisa menjadi projection/security boundary, tetapi ownership, RLS, function volatility, dan `security_barrier` perlu dipahami jika datanya sensitif.

---

## 20. Function Security: `SECURITY INVOKER` vs `SECURITY DEFINER`

| Mode | Eksekusi Sebagai | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| `SECURITY INVOKER` | Caller/current user | Default; lebih aman. |
| `SECURITY DEFINER` | Owner function | Bisa menjadi privilege escalation jika salah. |

```sql id="tosv5f"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.close_case(p_case_id bigint)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = case_mgmt, pg_temp
AS $$
    UPDATE case_file
    SET status = 'CLOSED'
    WHERE id = p_case_id;
$$;
```

**Hardening untuk `SECURITY DEFINER`:**

| Checklist | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Set `search_path` eksplisit | Mencegah object hijacking. |
| Owner function bukan superuser | Mengurangi blast radius. |
| Grant `EXECUTE` hanya ke role perlu | Jangan biarkan `PUBLIC`. |
| Validasi input | Hindari dynamic SQL injection. |
| Audit function body | Ini boundary privilege escalation. |

---

## 21. Predefined Roles

PostgreSQL menyediakan predefined roles untuk capability umum tertentu. Administrator dapat `GRANT` predefined role ke user/role lain, misalnya `pg_signal_backend`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/predefined-roles.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```sql id="6rb3au"
GRANT pg_read_all_stats TO monitoring_user;
GRANT pg_signal_backend TO limited_admin;
```

| Predefined Role | Kegunaan Umum | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| `pg_read_all_stats` | Monitoring statistics semua user. | Bisa melihat metadata/statistik sensitif. |
| `pg_read_all_settings` | Baca semua settings. | Bisa expose config sensitif. |
| `pg_signal_backend` | Signal session lain. | Bisa mengganggu workload. |
| `pg_monitor` | Bundle monitoring privileges. | Jangan beri ke app runtime. |
| `pg_read_server_files` | Baca file server. | Sangat sensitif. |
| `pg_write_server_files` | Tulis file server. | Sangat sensitif. |
| `pg_execute_server_program` | Eksekusi program server. | Sangat berbahaya. |

**Rule:** predefined roles bukan “role biasa”. Treat sebagai privileged capabilities dan review seperti admin access.

---

## 22. Replication Security

Role untuk replication connection harus memiliki `REPLICATION` atau superuser, dan role tersebut harus bisa login serta diizinkan di `pg_hba.conf`. Untuk initial copy logical replication, role juga membutuhkan `SELECT` pada published table jika bukan superuser. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication-security.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="tmij89"
CREATE ROLE repl_user
LOGIN
REPLICATION
PASSWORD 'change_me'
NOSUPERUSER
NOCREATEDB
NOCREATEROLE
NOBYPASSRLS;
```

```conf id="jr7svu"
hostssl replication repl_user 10.20.0.10/32 scram-sha-256
hostssl appdb       repl_user 10.20.0.10/32 scram-sha-256
```

**Security note:** PostgreSQL documentation notes there are currently no privileges on publications; a subscription that can connect may access a publication. If you need to hide data, control publication scope, connection access, and avoid exposing the same data via another publication. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/logical-replication-security.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 23. Audit Posture

PostgreSQL core menyediakan logs, catalog views, role/privilege catalogs, dan extension ecosystem. Untuk audit posture, fokus minimum adalah:

| Area | Yang Diaudit |
|---|---|
| Role inventory | Role mana punya `LOGIN`, `SUPERUSER`, `CREATEROLE`, `BYPASSRLS`, `REPLICATION`. |
| Membership | Siapa member role privileged. |
| Object ownership | Owner schema/table/function bukan app runtime. |
| Grants | Privilege langsung ke user vs group role. |
| `PUBLIC` privilege | Default privilege terlalu luas. |
| HBA rules | Network/method terlalu luas. |
| TLS usage | `hostssl`, cert, cipher policy. |
| RLS | Table sensitif sudah enable/force policy. |
| Function security | `SECURITY DEFINER` review. |
| Replication slots/users | Data exfiltration/retention risk. |

### Role attribute audit

```sql id="cuxr43"
SELECT
    rolname,
    rolcanlogin,
    rolsuper,
    rolcreatedb,
    rolcreaterole,
    rolreplication,
    rolbypassrls,
    rolvaliduntil
FROM pg_roles
ORDER BY rolname;
```

### Role membership audit

```sql id="bzxb92"
SELECT
    member_role.rolname AS member,
    parent_role.rolname AS granted_role,
    admin_option
FROM pg_auth_members m
JOIN pg_roles member_role ON member_role.oid = m.member
JOIN pg_roles parent_role ON parent_role.oid = m.roleid
ORDER BY granted_role, member;
```

### Object owner audit

```sql id="xxrqaa"
SELECT
    n.nspname AS schema_name,
    c.relname AS object_name,
    c.relkind,
    r.rolname AS owner
FROM pg_class c
JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace
JOIN pg_roles r ON r.oid = c.relowner
WHERE n.nspname NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')
ORDER BY n.nspname, c.relname;
```

---

## 24. Privilege Inspection Queries

### Table privileges

```sql id="l4u7xs"
SELECT
    table_schema,
    table_name,
    grantee,
    privilege_type,
    is_grantable
FROM information_schema.table_privileges
WHERE table_schema NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')
ORDER BY table_schema, table_name, grantee, privilege_type;
```

### Schema privileges

```sql id="o5m82h"
SELECT
    nspname AS schema_name,
    rolname AS role_name,
    has_schema_privilege(rolname, nspname, 'USAGE') AS has_usage,
    has_schema_privilege(rolname, nspname, 'CREATE') AS has_create
FROM pg_namespace
CROSS JOIN pg_roles
WHERE nspname NOT LIKE 'pg_%'
  AND nspname <> 'information_schema'
ORDER BY schema_name, role_name;
```

### Function execute grants

```sql id="fn1jqk"
SELECT
    routine_schema,
    routine_name,
    grantee,
    privilege_type
FROM information_schema.routine_privileges
WHERE routine_schema NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')
ORDER BY routine_schema, routine_name, grantee;
```

### Default privileges

```sql id="ej4dpd"
SELECT
    defaclrole::regrole AS owner_role,
    defaclnamespace::regnamespace AS schema_name,
    defaclobjtype AS object_type,
    defaclacl AS privileges
FROM pg_default_acl
ORDER BY owner_role, schema_name, object_type;
```

---

## 25. Production Role Design Pattern

### Roles

```sql id="k3k4o3"
CREATE ROLE case_owner NOLOGIN;
CREATE ROLE case_reader NOLOGIN;
CREATE ROLE case_writer NOLOGIN;
CREATE ROLE case_migrator LOGIN PASSWORD 'change_me';
CREATE ROLE svc_case_api LOGIN PASSWORD 'change_me';
CREATE ROLE reporting_user LOGIN PASSWORD 'change_me';
```

### Ownership

```sql id="ogqxlg"
CREATE SCHEMA case_mgmt AUTHORIZATION case_owner;

ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
FOR ROLE case_owner
IN SCHEMA case_mgmt
GRANT SELECT ON TABLES TO case_reader;

ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
FOR ROLE case_owner
IN SCHEMA case_mgmt
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON TABLES TO case_writer;
```

### Runtime grants

```sql id="1h73b5"
GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE appdb TO svc_case_api, reporting_user;

GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA case_mgmt TO case_reader, case_writer;

GRANT case_reader TO reporting_user;
GRANT case_reader, case_writer TO svc_case_api;
```

### Migration ownership control

```sql id="pvtyby"
GRANT case_owner TO case_migrator;
```

Migration session:

```sql id="uf8k7n"
SET ROLE case_owner;

CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_file (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    status text NOT NULL
);
```

**Why:** object dibuat dengan owner konsisten, default privileges berjalan konsisten, app runtime bukan owner.

---

## 26. Least Privilege Matrix

| Role | Database | Schema | Table | Sequence | Function | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `case_owner` | As needed | Owner | Owner | Owner | Owner | Tidak dipakai runtime app. |
| `case_reader` | `CONNECT` | `USAGE` | `SELECT` | Usually none | selected `EXECUTE` | Reporting/read-only. |
| `case_writer` | `CONNECT` | `USAGE` | `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE` | `USAGE, SELECT` | selected `EXECUTE` | App write path. |
| `case_migrator` | `CONNECT` | via `SET ROLE case_owner` | DDL via owner role | DDL via owner role | DDL via owner role | Dipakai migration only. |
| `monitoring_user` | `CONNECT` | minimal | stats views | none | none | Bisa diberi `pg_monitor` sesuai risk. |
| `repl_user` | replication/appdb as needed | minimal | `SELECT` for logical copy | none | none | `REPLICATION`, HBA restricted. |

---

## 27. Security Hardening Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Superuser | Tidak ada app/runtime user dengan `SUPERUSER`. |
| Role attributes | Review `CREATEROLE`, `REPLICATION`, `BYPASSRLS`. |
| Ownership | App runtime bukan owner table/schema/function. |
| Group roles | Privilege diberikan ke group role, bukan user satu-satu. |
| Public schema | `REVOKE CREATE ON SCHEMA public FROM PUBLIC`. |
| Public database | Review `CONNECT`, `TEMPORARY`, `CREATE`. |
| Functions | Revoke unwanted `EXECUTE` from `PUBLIC`. |
| Default privileges | Set untuk future tables/sequences/functions. |
| HBA | Rule paling sempit: database, user, CIDR, method. |
| TLS | Pakai `hostssl` untuk network tidak tepercaya. |
| Password | Prefer `scram-sha-256`; rotate credential. |
| RLS | Enable/force untuk table yang butuh row isolation. |
| Security definer | Audit owner, search_path, grants. |
| Replication | Restrict `REPLICATION` role dan monitor slot. |
| Monitoring | Audit roles/grants secara berkala. |

---

## 28. Common Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| App user menjadi owner table | App bisa `DROP/ALTER`, RLS bisa bypass oleh owner | Pisahkan owner role dan runtime role. |
| Grant langsung ke user individual | Sulit audit dan revoke | Pakai group role. |
| Lupa `USAGE ON SCHEMA` | Table grant ada tapi query tetap gagal | Grant schema usage. |
| Lupa sequence privilege | Insert ke identity/sequence gagal | Grant `USAGE` pada sequence. |
| Mengandalkan `ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES` untuk object lama | Object lama tetap tidak berubah | Gunakan `GRANT ... ON ALL ...`. |
| `PUBLIC` dibiarkan default | Access terlalu luas | Revoke dan grant eksplisit. |
| `trust` di TCP production | Semua matching host bisa login tanpa password | Gunakan SCRAM/cert, restrict CIDR. |
| HBA rule luas di atas rule sempit | Rule sempit tidak pernah dipakai | Urutkan dari spesifik ke umum. |
| `SECURITY DEFINER` tanpa `SET search_path` | Privilege escalation via object hijack | Set search_path eksplisit. |
| Role dengan `BYPASSRLS` dipakai aplikasi | RLS tidak efektif | Jangan grant `BYPASSRLS` ke app. |
| Publication terlalu luas | Data sensitif tereplikasi | Batasi publication/table/column/filter. |
| Monitoring user terlalu powerful | Exposure metadata/query | Grant minimum predefined role. |

---

## 29. Incident Response Patterns

| Incident | Tindakan Awal |
|---|---|
| Credential leaked | `ALTER ROLE ... PASSWORD`, revoke sessions, rotate app secret. |
| User diberi privilege salah | `REVOKE`, audit membership, cek grant option chain. |
| App accidentally owns objects | `ALTER ... OWNER TO owner_role`, review grants. |
| RLS leak suspected | Check role attributes, table owner, `BYPASSRLS`, policies. |
| Unauthorized connection | Review `pg_hba.conf`, logs, role password, CIDR rules. |
| Function privilege escalation | Revoke execute, review `SECURITY DEFINER`, fix search_path. |
| Replication role misuse | Disable role, drop suspicious slot, audit publication scope. |
| PUBLIC privilege too broad | Revoke default, re-grant explicit roles, test app behavior. |

### Kill sessions after credential incident

```sql id="v849vd"
SELECT pg_terminate_backend(pid)
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE usename = 'svc_case_api'
  AND pid <> pg_backend_pid();
```

### Disable login temporarily

```sql id="26aogq"
ALTER ROLE svc_case_api NOLOGIN;
```

---

## 30. Minimal Secure Setup Example

```sql id="sfhi1u"
-- Owner and permission roles
CREATE ROLE case_owner NOLOGIN;
CREATE ROLE case_reader NOLOGIN;
CREATE ROLE case_writer NOLOGIN;

-- Runtime users
CREATE ROLE svc_case_api LOGIN PASSWORD 'change_me' NOSUPERUSER NOCREATEDB NOCREATEROLE NOREPLICATION NOBYPASSRLS;
CREATE ROLE reporting_user LOGIN PASSWORD 'change_me' NOSUPERUSER NOCREATEDB NOCREATEROLE NOREPLICATION NOBYPASSRLS;

-- Schema
CREATE SCHEMA case_mgmt AUTHORIZATION case_owner;

-- Harden public
REVOKE CREATE ON SCHEMA public FROM PUBLIC;

-- Database access
GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE appdb TO svc_case_api, reporting_user;

-- Schema access
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA case_mgmt TO case_reader, case_writer;

-- Membership
GRANT case_reader TO reporting_user;
GRANT case_reader, case_writer TO svc_case_api;

-- Existing objects, if any
GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA case_mgmt TO case_reader;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA case_mgmt TO case_writer;
GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA case_mgmt TO case_writer;

-- Future objects
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
FOR ROLE case_owner
IN SCHEMA case_mgmt
GRANT SELECT ON TABLES TO case_reader;

ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
FOR ROLE case_owner
IN SCHEMA case_mgmt
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON TABLES TO case_writer;

ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES
FOR ROLE case_owner
IN SCHEMA case_mgmt
GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON SEQUENCES TO case_writer;
```

`pg_hba.conf`:

```conf id="ei8fza"
# TYPE    DATABASE    USER             ADDRESS          METHOD
local     all         postgres                          peer
hostssl   appdb       svc_case_api     10.10.0.0/16     scram-sha-256
hostssl   appdb       reporting_user   10.20.1.10/32    scram-sha-256
hostssl   replication repl_user        10.30.1.10/32    scram-sha-256
```

---

## 31. Decision Matrix

| Problem | PostgreSQL Feature |
|---|---|
| Banyak user butuh privilege sama | Group role |
| App perlu connect tapi tidak boleh own object | Login role + group privilege |
| Schema object tidak bisa diakses walau table grant ada | `GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA` |
| Future tables tidak otomatis accessible | `ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES` |
| Need restrict by source network | `pg_hba.conf` CIDR |
| Need password auth modern | `scram-sha-256` |
| Need encrypted network connection | `ssl=on` + `hostssl` |
| Need client cert auth | `cert` auth or `clientcert=verify-full` |
| Need tenant/row isolation | RLS policy |
| Need hide specific columns | Column grants or view |
| Need controlled privileged operation | Carefully audited `SECURITY DEFINER` function |
| Need monitor database safely | Predefined role such as `pg_monitor` / `pg_read_all_stats` with review |

---

## 32. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Role model | Paham user/group/owner sama-sama role. |
| 2 | Object privilege | Bisa membedakan database/schema/table/sequence/function grants. |
| 3 | Ownership | Bisa mencegah app runtime menjadi owner object. |
| 4 | Default privilege | Bisa mengatur privilege object masa depan. |
| 5 | `pg_hba.conf` | Bisa membaca dan harden connection rules. |
| 6 | SCRAM + TLS | Bisa membuat auth dan transport security yang layak production. |
| 7 | RLS | Bisa membuat row-level boundary untuk tenant/assignment. |
| 8 | Function security | Bisa menghindari privilege escalation via `SECURITY DEFINER`. |
| 9 | Audit queries | Bisa review role, grants, ownership, dan risky capability. |

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjut paling tepat: **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Administration & Operations: Configuration, `postgresql.conf`, `ALTER SYSTEM`, Connection Limits, Memory Settings, Logging, `pg_stat_activity`, Maintenance Commands, dan Operational Runbook**.
