# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Administration & Operations

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk operasi database production:

- konfigurasi server
- `postgresql.conf`, `postgresql.auto.conf`, `ALTER SYSTEM`
- connection limit
- memory settings
- logging
- session/activity monitoring
- operational commands
- maintenance runbook
- incident triage

PostgreSQL administration bukan hanya “mengubah parameter”, tetapi mengelola **lifecycle cluster, konfigurasi, resource, observability, connection pressure, long-running session, lock, maintenance, dan failure response** secara defensible.

PostgreSQL menyediakan banyak configuration parameter yang mengubah perilaku database system; parameter ini dikelompokkan dalam dokumentasi server configuration, termasuk connection/authentication, resource consumption, WAL, query planning, logging, statistics, autovacuum, dan client defaults. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="postgres-admin-ops"
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Administration & Operations))
    Configuration
      postgresql.conf
      postgresql.auto.conf
      ALTER SYSTEM
      SET
      ALTER ROLE
      ALTER DATABASE
      Reload vs Restart
    Connection Management
      max_connections
      reserved_connections
      superuser_reserved_connections
      idle sessions
      idle in transaction
    Resource Management
      shared_buffers
      work_mem
      maintenance_work_mem
      temp_buffers
      effective_cache_size
      huge_pages
    Logging
      log_destination
      logging_collector
      log_line_prefix
      log_min_duration_statement
      log_lock_waits
      log_checkpoints
      log_connections
      log_disconnections
    Monitoring
      pg_stat_activity
      pg_stat_database
      pg_stat_user_tables
      pg_stat_user_indexes
      pg_locks
      pg_stat_statements
    Maintenance
      VACUUM
      ANALYZE
      REINDEX
      CHECKPOINT
      pg_reload_conf
      pg_terminate_backend
    Runbooks
      Slow Query
      Lock Wait
      Connection Exhaustion
      Disk Full
      WAL Growth
      Bloat
      Replication Lag
```

**Core idea:** PostgreSQL operation harus dipisahkan menjadi tiga level:

| Level | Contoh | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Session-level | `SET work_mem = '64MB'` | Hanya berlaku di session saat ini. |
| Database/role-level | `ALTER DATABASE`, `ALTER ROLE` | Bisa memengaruhi banyak session baru. |
| Cluster-level | `postgresql.conf`, `ALTER SYSTEM` | Memengaruhi seluruh cluster; perlu governance. |

---

## 3. Configuration Sources

PostgreSQL configuration dapat datang dari beberapa sumber. Urutan dan scope penting karena parameter yang sama bisa diset di beberapa tempat.

| Source | Scope | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Server command line | Cluster process | `postgres -c shared_buffers=...` |
| `postgresql.conf` | Cluster default | File konfigurasi utama. |
| `postgresql.auto.conf` | Cluster override | Ditulis oleh `ALTER SYSTEM`. |
| `ALTER DATABASE ... SET` | Database default | Berlaku untuk session baru ke database tertentu. |
| `ALTER ROLE ... SET` | Role default | Berlaku untuk session baru role tertentu. |
| `SET` | Current session | Berlaku hanya session saat ini. |
| `SET LOCAL` | Current transaction | Berlaku sampai transaction selesai. |

`ALTER SYSTEM` adalah cara SQL untuk mengubah konfigurasi global cluster; command ini menulis setting ke `postgresql.auto.conf`, yang dibaca bersama `postgresql.conf`. `RESET` atau set ke `DEFAULT` akan menghapus entry dari `postgresql.auto.conf`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altersystem.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Inspect Configuration

### Show one parameter

```sql id="show-param"
SHOW max_connections;

SHOW shared_buffers;

SHOW log_min_duration_statement;
```

### Show all parameters

```sql id="show-all"
SHOW ALL;
```

### Query `pg_settings`

```sql id="pg-settings"
SELECT
    name,
    setting,
    unit,
    context,
    vartype,
    source,
    pending_restart,
    short_desc
FROM pg_settings
WHERE name IN (
    'max_connections',
    'shared_buffers',
    'work_mem',
    'maintenance_work_mem',
    'effective_cache_size',
    'log_min_duration_statement',
    'idle_in_transaction_session_timeout'
)
ORDER BY name;
```

| Column | Makna |
|---|---|
| `setting` | Nilai aktif. |
| `unit` | Unit parameter, jika ada. |
| `context` | Kapan parameter bisa berubah. |
| `source` | Dari mana nilai berasal. |
| `pending_restart` | `true` jika butuh restart agar efektif. |

---

## 5. Reload vs Restart

Tidak semua parameter bisa berubah langsung.

| Jenis Perubahan | Contoh | Aksi |
|---|---|---|
| Session-level | `work_mem` | `SET`, tidak perlu reload. |
| SIGHUP/reload | logging tertentu, beberapa timeout | `SELECT pg_reload_conf()` |
| Restart-required | `max_connections`, `shared_buffers` | Restart PostgreSQL |
| Superuser-only / privileged SET | Parameter sensitif | Butuh privilege sesuai |

`SET` mengubah runtime parameter untuk session saat ini saja. Beberapa parameter dapat diubah on-the-fly, tetapi ada juga yang hanya bisa diubah saat server atau session start. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-set.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Reload config

```sql id="reload-conf"
SELECT pg_reload_conf();
```

### Check pending restart

```sql id="pending-restart"
SELECT
    name,
    setting,
    source,
    pending_restart
FROM pg_settings
WHERE pending_restart;
```

**Rule:** setelah mengubah config, selalu validasi apakah setting aktif atau masih `pending_restart`.

---

## 6. `ALTER SYSTEM` Usage

### Set global parameter

```sql id="alter-system-set"
ALTER SYSTEM SET log_min_duration_statement = '500ms';

SELECT pg_reload_conf();
```

### Reset one parameter

```sql id="alter-system-reset"
ALTER SYSTEM RESET log_min_duration_statement;

SELECT pg_reload_conf();
```

### Reset all `ALTER SYSTEM` overrides

```sql id="alter-system-reset-all"
ALTER SYSTEM RESET ALL;

SELECT pg_reload_conf();
```

### Inspect source

```sql id="config-source"
SELECT
    name,
    setting,
    source,
    sourcefile,
    sourceline
FROM pg_settings
WHERE source IN ('configuration file', 'override');
```

**Operational rule:** `ALTER SYSTEM` nyaman untuk emergency tuning, tetapi untuk production governance, perubahan config sebaiknya tetap terdokumentasi dan versioned. Jangan biarkan `postgresql.auto.conf` menjadi sumber konfigurasi “misterius”.

---

## 7. `ALTER DATABASE` dan `ALTER ROLE` Defaults

### Per-database default

```sql id="alter-db-set"
ALTER DATABASE appdb
SET statement_timeout = '30s';
```

### Per-role default

```sql id="alter-role-set"
ALTER ROLE reporting_user
SET statement_timeout = '5min';

ALTER ROLE reporting_user
SET work_mem = '128MB';
```

### Per-role-per-database default

```sql id="alter-role-db-set"
ALTER ROLE reporting_user
IN DATABASE appdb
SET statement_timeout = '2min';
```

`ALTER DATABASE` dapat mengubah beberapa setting per database, dan hanya database owner atau superuser yang dapat mengubah setting tersebut. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-alterdatabase.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

**Rule:** gunakan `ALTER ROLE` / `ALTER DATABASE` untuk workload class yang berbeda, misalnya reporting user boleh `statement_timeout` lebih panjang daripada API user.

---

## 8. Connection Management

`max_connections` menentukan jumlah maksimum concurrent connection ke server. Default biasanya 100, dan parameter ini hanya bisa diubah saat server start. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-connection.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="conn-settings"
SELECT
    name,
    setting,
    unit,
    context,
    pending_restart
FROM pg_settings
WHERE name IN (
    'max_connections',
    'superuser_reserved_connections',
    'reserved_connections'
);
```

### Current connection usage

```sql id="connection-usage"
SELECT
    count(*) AS total_connections,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE state = 'active') AS active,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE state = 'idle') AS idle,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE state = 'idle in transaction') AS idle_in_transaction
FROM pg_stat_activity;
```

### By database/user/application

```sql id="conn-by-user"
SELECT
    datname,
    usename,
    application_name,
    state,
    count(*) AS connections
FROM pg_stat_activity
GROUP BY datname, usename, application_name, state
ORDER BY connections DESC;
```

### Connection pressure decision

| Gejala | Kemungkinan | Tindakan |
|---|---|---|
| Too many connections | Pool leak / pool terlalu besar / idle connection banyak | Cek `pg_stat_activity`, turunkan pool, terminate idle jika darurat. |
| Banyak `idle in transaction` | Aplikasi membuka transaction tapi tidak commit/rollback | Set timeout, fix aplikasi/proses. |
| Active query banyak dan lambat | Query bottleneck atau lock wait | Cek wait event, locks, query plan. |
| Superuser tidak bisa login | Connection slots penuh | Gunakan reserved connections dengan benar. |

---

## 9. Session State dan Wait Event

`pg_stat_activity` adalah view utama untuk melihat session, query aktif, state, wait event, dan transaction age. PostgreSQL collection untuk currently executing command diaktifkan default, dan informasi ini terlihat ke superuser, role dengan privilege `pg_read_all_stats`, atau owner session terkait. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-statistics.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="pg-stat-activity"
SELECT
    pid,
    datname,
    usename,
    application_name,
    client_addr,
    state,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    now() - xact_start AS xact_age,
    now() - query_start AS query_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE state <> 'idle'
ORDER BY query_start ASC;
```

| State | Makna |
|---|---|
| `active` | Sedang menjalankan query. |
| `idle` | Koneksi terbuka tapi tidak sedang query. |
| `idle in transaction` | Transaction terbuka tapi tidak sedang query. Berbahaya jika lama. |
| `idle in transaction (aborted)` | Transaction error dan belum rollback. |
| `disabled` | Tracking disabled untuk backend tersebut. |

### Long transactions

```sql id="long-xact"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    state,
    now() - xact_start AS xact_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE xact_start IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY xact_start ASC
LIMIT 20;
```

**Operational rule:** long transaction bisa menahan vacuum cleanup, memperbesar bloat, dan membuat lock bertahan lama. Ini salah satu hal pertama yang harus dicek saat storage membesar atau vacuum tidak efektif.

---

## 10. Timeout Settings

Timeout adalah guardrail production. Tanpa timeout, query atau transaction buruk bisa menahan resource terlalu lama.

| Parameter | Fungsi | Rekomendasi Umum |
|---|---|---|
| `statement_timeout` | Maksimum durasi statement | Set untuk API/user role. |
| `lock_timeout` | Maksimum menunggu lock | Pendek untuk request path. |
| `idle_in_transaction_session_timeout` | Maksimum idle dalam transaction | Wajib untuk mencegah zombie transaction. |
| `idle_session_timeout` | Maksimum idle session | Gunakan hati-hati, bisa ganggu pool. |
| `deadlock_timeout` | Waktu sebelum deadlock check/log lock wait | Biasanya server-level tuning. |

```sql id="timeouts-role"
ALTER ROLE svc_api
SET statement_timeout = '15s';

ALTER ROLE svc_api
SET lock_timeout = '2s';

ALTER ROLE svc_api
SET idle_in_transaction_session_timeout = '30s';
```

### Per-transaction override

```sql id="set-local-timeout"
BEGIN;

SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '5min';
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';

-- maintenance/update terbatas

COMMIT;
```

---

## 11. Memory Settings

`shared_buffers` menentukan jumlah memory yang digunakan PostgreSQL untuk shared memory buffers. Default biasanya 128 MB, tetapi nilai yang jauh lebih tinggi dari minimum biasanya dibutuhkan untuk performa yang baik. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-resource.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Parameter | Scope | Fungsi | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| `shared_buffers` | Cluster | Cache page PostgreSQL | Butuh restart; terlalu besar bisa mengurangi OS cache. |
| `work_mem` | Per operation | Sort/hash/join memory | Dikalikan per query node dan connection; mudah overcommit. |
| `maintenance_work_mem` | Per maintenance op | VACUUM, CREATE INDEX, ALTER TABLE FK, dll. | Terlalu besar saat banyak maintenance paralel. |
| `autovacuum_work_mem` | Autovacuum worker | Memory autovacuum | Bisa override maintenance memory untuk autovacuum. |
| `temp_buffers` | Per session | Temporary table buffer | Per session. |
| `effective_cache_size` | Planner estimate | Estimasi cache OS+DB | Bukan alokasi memory nyata. |

### Inspect memory settings

```sql id="memory-settings"
SELECT
    name,
    setting,
    unit,
    context,
    source,
    pending_restart
FROM pg_settings
WHERE name IN (
    'shared_buffers',
    'work_mem',
    'maintenance_work_mem',
    'autovacuum_work_mem',
    'temp_buffers',
    'effective_cache_size'
)
ORDER BY name;
```

### `work_mem` warning

```text id="work-mem-warning"
work_mem bukan total memory per connection.
Satu query bisa memakai beberapa sort/hash node.
Banyak concurrent query bisa membuat total memory jauh lebih besar dari nilai work_mem.
```

**Rule:** jangan menaikkan `work_mem` global hanya karena satu report query lambat. Lebih aman set per role, per session, atau per transaction untuk workload tertentu.

---

## 12. WAL dan Checkpoint Operational Settings

| Parameter | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `wal_level` | Level informasi WAL: minimal/replica/logical. |
| `max_wal_size` | Soft limit WAL sebelum checkpoint otomatis. |
| `min_wal_size` | WAL yang dipertahankan untuk reuse. |
| `checkpoint_timeout` | Interval maksimum antar checkpoint. |
| `checkpoint_completion_target` | Sebaran durasi checkpoint agar tidak spike. |
| `archive_mode` | Enable WAL archiving. |
| `archive_command` / `archive_library` | Mekanisme archive WAL. |
| `synchronous_commit` | Durability/replication commit behavior. |

PostgreSQL WAL configuration mencakup checkpoint, archiving, synchronous commit, dan recovery-related setting. Misalnya `remote_apply` pada `synchronous_commit` menunggu standby menerima, menerapkan WAL, dan membuat perubahan visible di standby, tetapi menyebabkan commit delay lebih besar. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-wal.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Inspect WAL settings

```sql id="wal-settings"
SELECT
    name,
    setting,
    unit,
    context,
    source,
    pending_restart
FROM pg_settings
WHERE name IN (
    'wal_level',
    'max_wal_size',
    'min_wal_size',
    'checkpoint_timeout',
    'checkpoint_completion_target',
    'archive_mode',
    'archive_command',
    'synchronous_commit'
)
ORDER BY name;
```

---

## 13. Logging

PostgreSQL mendukung beberapa destinasi log: `stderr`, `csvlog`, `jsonlog`, dan `syslog`; di Windows juga ada `eventlog`. Default-nya log ke `stderr`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-logging.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Parameter | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `log_destination` | Tujuan log: `stderr`, `csvlog`, `jsonlog`, `syslog`. |
| `logging_collector` | Capture log `stderr` ke file. |
| `log_directory` | Directory log. |
| `log_filename` | Pattern nama file log. |
| `log_rotation_age` | Rotasi berdasarkan waktu. |
| `log_rotation_size` | Rotasi berdasarkan ukuran. |
| `log_line_prefix` | Prefix log line: timestamp, pid, user, db, app, dll. |
| `log_min_messages` | Level server message yang dicatat. |
| `log_min_duration_statement` | Log statement yang durasinya melewati threshold. |
| `log_lock_waits` | Log session yang menunggu lock lebih lama dari `deadlock_timeout`. |
| `log_checkpoints` | Log checkpoint activity. |
| `log_connections` / `log_disconnections` | Log connect/disconnect. |

### Practical logging baseline

```conf id="logging-baseline"
log_destination = 'stderr'
logging_collector = on
log_directory = 'log'
log_filename = 'postgresql-%Y-%m-%d_%H%M%S.log'

log_line_prefix = '%m [%p] user=%u db=%d app=%a client=%h '
log_min_duration_statement = '500ms'
log_lock_waits = on
log_checkpoints = on
```

### Inspect logging config

```sql id="logging-settings"
SELECT
    name,
    setting,
    unit,
    context,
    source
FROM pg_settings
WHERE name LIKE 'log_%'
ORDER BY name;
```

**Rule:** logging harus cukup detail untuk incident, tetapi tidak boleh membanjiri disk. Log slow query, lock wait, checkpoint, dan connection event sesuai kebutuhan operasional.

---

## 14. Statistics and Monitoring Views

PostgreSQL menyediakan chapter khusus untuk monitoring database activity; dokumentasi menyatakan administrator sering perlu tahu “apa yang sedang dilakukan sistem”, dan PostgreSQL menyediakan beberapa tools untuk memonitor aktivitas serta menganalisis performa. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/monitoring.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| View | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `pg_stat_activity` | Session/query aktif. |
| `pg_stat_database` | Statistik per database. |
| `pg_stat_user_tables` | Scan, tuple, vacuum/analyze per table. |
| `pg_stat_user_indexes` | Pemakaian index. |
| `pg_locks` | Lock aktif/requested. |
| `pg_stat_bgwriter` | Background writer/checkpoint stats. |
| `pg_stat_wal` | WAL statistics. |
| `pg_stat_replication` | Replication sender status. |
| `pg_stat_archiver` | WAL archiving status. |
| `pg_stat_progress_vacuum` | Progress vacuum. |
| `pg_stat_progress_create_index` | Progress create index. |

### Database-level health

```sql id="db-health"
SELECT
    datname,
    numbackends,
    xact_commit,
    xact_rollback,
    blks_read,
    blks_hit,
    tup_returned,
    tup_fetched,
    tup_inserted,
    tup_updated,
    tup_deleted,
    deadlocks,
    temp_files,
    temp_bytes
FROM pg_stat_database
WHERE datname NOT IN ('template0', 'template1')
ORDER BY datname;
```

### Cache hit ratio approximation

```sql id="cache-hit"
SELECT
    datname,
    blks_hit,
    blks_read,
    round(
        100.0 * blks_hit / NULLIF(blks_hit + blks_read, 0),
        2
    ) AS cache_hit_percent
FROM pg_stat_database
WHERE datname NOT IN ('template0', 'template1')
ORDER BY cache_hit_percent ASC NULLS LAST;
```

**Caution:** cache hit ratio bukan KPI tunggal. Query buruk bisa punya cache hit tinggi tetapi tetap lambat.

---

## 15. `pg_stat_statements`

`pg_stat_statements` melacak planning dan execution statistics untuk SQL statement yang dijalankan server. Modul ini berguna untuk menemukan query dengan total cost tertinggi, latency rata-rata tinggi, atau frekuensi besar. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgstatstatements.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Enable extension

Biasanya perlu:

```conf id="pgss-conf"
shared_preload_libraries = 'pg_stat_statements'
```

Lalu restart PostgreSQL, kemudian:

```sql id="pgss-enable"
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_stat_statements;
```

### Top total time

```sql id="pgss-total"
SELECT
    calls,
    total_exec_time,
    mean_exec_time,
    max_exec_time,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Top mean latency

```sql id="pgss-mean"
SELECT
    calls,
    mean_exec_time,
    max_exec_time,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
WHERE calls >= 10
ORDER BY mean_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

| Metric | Interpretasi |
|---|---|
| `total_exec_time` tinggi | Beban total besar. |
| `mean_exec_time` tinggi | Query individual lambat. |
| `calls` tinggi | Query sering; optimasi kecil berdampak besar. |
| `rows` tinggi | Mungkin over-fetching. |
| `max_exec_time` tinggi | Ada outlier/lock/I/O spike. |

---

## 16. Lock Monitoring

### Current locks

```sql id="locks-current"
SELECT
    locktype,
    relation::regclass AS relation_name,
    mode,
    granted,
    pid
FROM pg_locks
ORDER BY granted, relation_name, mode;
```

### Blocked vs blocker

```sql id="blocked-blocker"
SELECT
    blocked.pid AS blocked_pid,
    blocked.usename AS blocked_user,
    blocked.query AS blocked_query,
    blocker.pid AS blocker_pid,
    blocker.usename AS blocker_user,
    blocker.query AS blocker_query
FROM pg_stat_activity blocked
JOIN pg_locks blocked_locks
    ON blocked_locks.pid = blocked.pid
JOIN pg_locks blocker_locks
    ON blocker_locks.locktype = blocked_locks.locktype
   AND blocker_locks.database IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.database
   AND blocker_locks.relation IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.relation
   AND blocker_locks.page IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.page
   AND blocker_locks.tuple IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.tuple
   AND blocker_locks.virtualxid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.virtualxid
   AND blocker_locks.transactionid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.transactionid
   AND blocker_locks.classid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.classid
   AND blocker_locks.objid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.objid
   AND blocker_locks.objsubid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.objsubid
   AND blocker_locks.pid <> blocked_locks.pid
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocker
    ON blocker.pid = blocker_locks.pid
WHERE NOT blocked_locks.granted
  AND blocker_locks.granted;
```

### Safer terminate pattern

```sql id="terminate-blocker"
SELECT pg_terminate_backend(:pid);
```

**Rule:** jangan langsung terminate session hanya karena terlihat blocking. Pahami apakah blocker sedang menjalankan migration, vacuum, batch critical, atau transaction aplikasi yang bisa diulang.

---

## 17. Maintenance Commands

| Command | Fungsi | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| `VACUUM` | Membersihkan dead tuple agar space reusable. | Bisa I/O heavy. |
| `VACUUM ANALYZE` | Vacuum + update statistics. | I/O + planner stats change. |
| `ANALYZE` | Update planner statistics. | Relatif ringan. |
| `REINDEX` | Rebuild index. | Bisa blocking jika bukan concurrent. |
| `REINDEX CONCURRENTLY` | Rebuild index dengan blocking lebih rendah. | Lebih lama/kompleks. |
| `CHECKPOINT` | Paksa checkpoint. | Bisa I/O spike; jangan rutin sembarangan. |
| `DISCARD` | Reset session state tertentu. | Untuk session/pool management. |

### Common maintenance snippets

```sql id="maintenance-snippets"
VACUUM (VERBOSE, ANALYZE) case_mgmt.case_file;

ANALYZE case_mgmt.case_file;

REINDEX INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_case_file_status_created;
```

---

## 18. Operational Readiness Queries

### Largest relations

```sql id="largest-relations"
SELECT
    relid::regclass AS relation,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_table_size(relid)) AS table_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_indexes_size(relid)) AS indexes_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size(relid)) AS total_size
FROM pg_statio_user_tables
ORDER BY pg_total_relation_size(relid) DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Dead tuple suspects

```sql id="dead-tuples"
SELECT
    schemaname,
    relname,
    n_live_tup,
    n_dead_tup,
    last_vacuum,
    last_autovacuum,
    last_analyze,
    last_autoanalyze
FROM pg_stat_user_tables
ORDER BY n_dead_tup DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Unused / suspicious large indexes

```sql id="index-usage"
SELECT
    schemaname,
    relname,
    indexrelname,
    idx_scan,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_relation_size(indexrelid)) AS index_size
FROM pg_stat_user_indexes
ORDER BY pg_relation_size(indexrelid) DESC
LIMIT 30;
```

### Temporary file pressure

```sql id="temp-pressure"
SELECT
    datname,
    temp_files,
    pg_size_pretty(temp_bytes) AS temp_bytes
FROM pg_stat_database
ORDER BY temp_bytes DESC;
```

High temp file usage sering menunjukkan sort/hash spill, `work_mem` terlalu kecil untuk workload tertentu, query buruk, atau report query yang perlu index/rewrite.

---

## 19. Runbook: Slow Query

### Step 1 - Identify query

```sql id="slow-query-identify"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    now() - query_start AS query_age,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE state = 'active'
ORDER BY query_start ASC;
```

### Step 2 - Check wait

| Wait Type | Interpretasi |
|---|---|
| `Lock` | Menunggu lock. Cek blocker. |
| `IO` | I/O bottleneck atau baca besar. |
| `Client` | Menunggu client kirim/terima data. |
| `LWLock` | Internal contention; perlu investigasi lebih dalam. |
| null tapi active lama | CPU/query execution. |

### Step 3 - Use `EXPLAIN`

```sql id="explain-slow"
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT ...;
```

### Step 4 - Decide action

| Temuan | Tindakan |
|---|---|
| Row estimate salah | `ANALYZE`, extended statistics. |
| Seq scan besar tidak selective | Index/rewrite query. |
| Sort spill | Index order atau `work_mem` scoped. |
| Lock wait | Resolve blocker, fix transaction design. |
| Over-fetching | Kurangi select list/pagination. |

---

## 20. Runbook: Connection Exhaustion

### Step 1 - Count by state

```sql id="conn-exhaustion-count"
SELECT
    state,
    count(*)
FROM pg_stat_activity
GROUP BY state
ORDER BY count(*) DESC;
```

### Step 2 - Count by app/user

```sql id="conn-exhaustion-app"
SELECT
    usename,
    application_name,
    client_addr,
    state,
    count(*)
FROM pg_stat_activity
GROUP BY usename, application_name, client_addr, state
ORDER BY count(*) DESC;
```

### Step 3 - Find idle-in-transaction

```sql id="idle-in-xact"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    now() - xact_start AS xact_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE state = 'idle in transaction'
ORDER BY xact_start ASC;
```

### Emergency terminate idle-in-transaction older than threshold

```sql id="kill-idle-xact"
SELECT pg_terminate_backend(pid)
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE state = 'idle in transaction'
  AND now() - xact_start > interval '10 minutes'
  AND pid <> pg_backend_pid();
```

**Root cause:** biasanya bukan `max_connections` terlalu kecil, melainkan connection pool terlalu besar, connection leak, atau transaction lifecycle buruk.

---

## 21. Runbook: Lock Wait / Migration Stuck

### Step 1 - Find blocked sessions

```sql id="blocked-sessions"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    now() - query_start AS wait_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE wait_event_type = 'Lock'
ORDER BY query_start ASC;
```

### Step 2 - Find blockers

Gunakan query blocked-vs-blocker dari section lock monitoring.

### Step 3 - Decision

| Situation | Action |
|---|---|
| Blocker adalah idle transaction lama | Terminate blocker setelah validasi. |
| Blocker adalah migration penting | Tunggu atau pause traffic, tergantung risk. |
| Banyak API blocked oleh DDL | Rollback DDL jika aman. |
| DDL menunggu lock terlalu lama | Gunakan `lock_timeout` untuk migration berikutnya. |

### Safer migration guardrail

```sql id="migration-guardrail"
BEGIN;

SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '5min';

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN external_ref text;

COMMIT;
```

---

## 22. Runbook: Disk Full

### Step 1 - Largest objects

```sql id="disk-largest"
SELECT
    relid::regclass AS relation,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size(relid)) AS total_size
FROM pg_statio_user_tables
ORDER BY pg_total_relation_size(relid) DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Step 2 - WAL pressure

```sql id="wal-archiver"
SELECT
    archived_count,
    last_archived_wal,
    last_archived_time,
    failed_count,
    last_failed_wal,
    last_failed_time
FROM pg_stat_archiver;
```

```sql id="slot-wal"
SELECT
    slot_name,
    slot_type,
    active,
    restart_lsn,
    pg_size_pretty(
        pg_wal_lsn_diff(pg_current_wal_lsn(), restart_lsn)
    ) AS retained_wal
FROM pg_replication_slots
WHERE restart_lsn IS NOT NULL;
```

### Step 3 - Temp files

```sql id="temp-files"
SELECT
    datname,
    temp_files,
    pg_size_pretty(temp_bytes) AS temp_bytes
FROM pg_stat_database
ORDER BY temp_bytes DESC;
```

### Emergency decision

| Cause | Action |
|---|---|
| WAL archive failing | Fix archive destination/command; do not delete random WAL manually. |
| Inactive replication slot retaining WAL | Drop obsolete slot only after confirming not needed. |
| Temp file explosion | Cancel offending query, review sort/hash/report. |
| Table/index bloat | Plan maintenance; emergency delete may not free disk immediately. |
| Logs too large | Rotate/compress/delete old logs carefully. |

---

## 23. Runbook: Autovacuum / Bloat Concern

### Table vacuum status

```sql id="autovac-status"
SELECT
    relname,
    n_live_tup,
    n_dead_tup,
    last_autovacuum,
    last_autoanalyze,
    autovacuum_count,
    autoanalyze_count
FROM pg_stat_user_tables
ORDER BY n_dead_tup DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Long transaction blocking cleanup

```sql id="vacuum-blocker"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    state,
    now() - xact_start AS xact_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE xact_start IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY xact_start ASC;
```

### Vacuum progress

```sql id="vacuum-progress"
SELECT
    pid,
    datname,
    relid::regclass AS relation,
    phase,
    heap_blks_total,
    heap_blks_scanned,
    heap_blks_vacuumed,
    index_vacuum_count
FROM pg_stat_progress_vacuum;
```

**Decision:** jika autovacuum “lama”, jangan langsung kill. Cek apakah ia sedang mencegah wraparound, membersihkan table besar, atau terhambat long transaction.

---

## 24. Runbook: Bad Configuration Change

### Check config sources

```sql id="bad-config-source"
SELECT
    name,
    setting,
    source,
    sourcefile,
    sourceline,
    pending_restart
FROM pg_settings
WHERE source <> 'default'
ORDER BY name;
```

### Reset `ALTER SYSTEM` override

```sql id="reset-bad-config"
ALTER SYSTEM RESET bad_parameter_name;

SELECT pg_reload_conf();
```

### If restart required

```sql id="pending-restart-check"
SELECT
    name,
    setting,
    pending_restart
FROM pg_settings
WHERE pending_restart;
```

**Rule:** setiap emergency config change harus punya rollback command. Jangan hanya mencatat nilai baru; catat nilai lama dan source-nya.

---

## 25. Operational Command Pack

### Reload config

```sql id="cmd-reload"
SELECT pg_reload_conf();
```

### Cancel query, keep connection

```sql id="cmd-cancel"
SELECT pg_cancel_backend(:pid);
```

### Terminate connection

```sql id="cmd-terminate"
SELECT pg_terminate_backend(:pid);
```

### Current WAL LSN

```sql id="cmd-wal-lsn"
SELECT pg_current_wal_lsn();
```

### Check recovery mode

```sql id="cmd-recovery"
SELECT pg_is_in_recovery();
```

### Database size

```sql id="cmd-db-size"
SELECT
    datname,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_database_size(datname)) AS size
FROM pg_database
ORDER BY pg_database_size(datname) DESC;
```

### Relation size

```sql id="cmd-relation-size"
SELECT
    pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size('case_mgmt.case_file')) AS total_size;
```

---

## 26. Operational Baseline Settings

Ini bukan angka final, tetapi kategori setting yang harus diputuskan eksplisit.

| Area | Parameter | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| Connections | `max_connections` | Disesuaikan dengan pooling dan memory. |
| Memory | `shared_buffers` | Cluster-level; butuh restart. |
| Memory | `work_mem` | Jangan terlalu besar global. |
| Maintenance | `maintenance_work_mem` | Untuk index/vacuum/maintenance. |
| Timeout | `statement_timeout` | Berbeda untuk API/reporting/migration. |
| Timeout | `lock_timeout` | Penting untuk API/migration. |
| Timeout | `idle_in_transaction_session_timeout` | Guardrail long transaction. |
| Logging | `log_min_duration_statement` | Slow query visibility. |
| Logging | `log_lock_waits` | Lock wait visibility. |
| Logging | `log_checkpoints` | Checkpoint visibility. |
| Stats | `shared_preload_libraries` | Untuk `pg_stat_statements` jika dipakai. |
| Autovacuum | per-table options | Untuk table besar/update-heavy. |
| WAL | `max_wal_size`, archiving | Disesuaikan backup/PITR/replication. |

---

## 27. Production Review Checklist

| Category | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Config governance | Perubahan `postgresql.conf` / `ALTER SYSTEM` tercatat. |
| Restart awareness | `pending_restart` dicek setelah perubahan. |
| Connection | Connection count, pool sizing, reserved slots jelas. |
| Timeouts | API/reporting/migration punya timeout berbeda. |
| Logging | Slow query, lock wait, checkpoint terlihat. |
| Monitoring | Query pack tersedia untuk activity, locks, size, vacuum, WAL. |
| `pg_stat_statements` | Enabled jika perlu workload visibility. |
| Vacuum | Autovacuum tidak dimatikan; table hot punya tuning jika perlu. |
| Disk | Largest relation, WAL, temp file, log growth dimonitor. |
| Replication | Slot/WAL retention dimonitor jika ada replication. |
| Backup | Backup dan restore drill tetap ada; ops bukan pengganti backup. |
| Runbook | Incident connection/lock/disk/WAL/slow query punya langkah jelas. |

---

## 28. Common Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Naikkan `max_connections` tanpa hitung memory | Memory pressure, context switching, OOM risk | Perbaiki pooling dan query lifecycle dulu. |
| `work_mem` dinaikkan global besar | Memory overcommit saat banyak concurrent query | Set scoped per role/session/query. |
| Tidak cek `pending_restart` | Mengira config aktif padahal belum | Query `pg_settings`. |
| `ALTER SYSTEM` dipakai terus tanpa governance | Config drift | Versioned config + audit `postgresql.auto.conf`. |
| Tidak ada `statement_timeout` | Query runaway | Set per role/database. |
| Tidak ada `idle_in_transaction_session_timeout` | Vacuum blocked, lock tertahan | Set untuk app roles. |
| Logging terlalu minim | Incident sulit ditelusuri | Enable slow query/lock/checkpoint logs. |
| Logging terlalu verbose | Disk penuh | Threshold dan retention. |
| Langsung kill session tanpa analisis | Bisa rollback transaksi penting | Identifikasi blocker dan business impact. |
| Menghapus WAL manual saat disk penuh | Corruption/replication/PITR rusak | Cek archiver/slot/root cause. |
| Autovacuum dimatikan | Bloat dan wraparound risk | Tune, jangan disable. |
| Tidak monitor temp files | Sort/hash spill tidak terlihat | Monitor `temp_bytes`. |

---

## 29. Decision Matrix

| Problem | First Place to Look | Likely Action |
|---|---|---|
| Query lambat | `pg_stat_activity`, `pg_stat_statements`, `EXPLAIN` | Rewrite/index/analyze/timeout. |
| Banyak lock wait | `pg_locks`, `pg_stat_activity` | Resolve blocker, fix transaction/migration. |
| Connection habis | `pg_stat_activity` grouped by app/user | Pool tuning, terminate idle-in-xact. |
| Disk membesar | relation size, WAL, temp, logs | Identify source, not random delete. |
| WAL menumpuk | `pg_stat_archiver`, `pg_replication_slots` | Fix archive/slot. |
| Table bloat | `pg_stat_user_tables`, vacuum history | Vacuum/autovacuum tuning/reindex/rewrite. |
| Planner salah | `ANALYZE`, `pg_stats`, explain estimates | Analyze/statistics/index. |
| Config tidak berubah | `pg_settings.pending_restart` | Reload/restart as required. |
| API sering timeout | activity/wait event/logs | Query/lock/pool investigation. |
| Maintenance mengganggu traffic | activity/locks/progress views | Use concurrent ops/window/timeout guardrail. |

---

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Configuration model | Paham `postgresql.conf`, `ALTER SYSTEM`, `SET`, reload vs restart. |
| 2 | Connection management | Bisa membaca connection pressure dan idle transaction. |
| 3 | Memory settings | Bisa membedakan `shared_buffers`, `work_mem`, maintenance memory. |

| 9 | Operational governance | Bisa menjaga config, access, backup, monitoring, dan runbook tetap konsisten. |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 1:05:42 AM

Saya lanjutkan ke partitioning PostgreSQL. Fokusnya: kapan partitioning benar-benar berguna, bagaimana desain range/list/hash, pruning, index strategy, retention, dan risiko maintenance pada table besar.

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Partitioning, Pruning, Index Strategy, Retention, dan Maintenance Pattern

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk table partitioning:

- declarative partitioning
- range, list, hash partition
- default partition
- partition pruning
- partition-wise maintenance
- index strategy
- unique constraint limitation
- retention via detach/drop partition
- operational pitfalls untuk table besar

PostgreSQL partitioning memecah satu logical table menjadi beberapa physical table kecil yang disebut **partitions**. Query tetap diarahkan ke parent table, tetapi PostgreSQL dapat membaca hanya partition yang relevan jika predicate query cocok dengan partition key. PostgreSQL documentation current menjelaskan partitioning sebagai teknik untuk membagi table besar menjadi bagian fisik yang lebih kecil, dan menyebut **partition pruning** sebagai optimisasi query untuk menghindari scan partition yang tidak relevan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Partitioning))
    Partitioned Table
      Parent Table
      Child Partitions
      Partition Key
      Partition Bound
    Strategies
      Range
      List
      Hash
    Query Optimization
      Partition Pruning
      Constraint Exclusion Legacy
      Runtime Pruning
    Indexing
      Partitioned Index
      Local Index
      Unique Constraint Limitation
      Per Partition Index
    Maintenance
      Create Future Partition
      Attach Partition
      Detach Partition
      Drop Old Partition
      Analyze Parent
      Vacuum Partitions
    Operations
      Retention
      Bulk Load
      Archive
      Reindex Partition
      Monitoring
    Risks
      Too Many Partitions
      Missing Future Partition
      Default Partition Trap
      Query Predicate Mismatch
      Global Uniqueness
```

**Core idea:** partitioning bukan pengganti index. Partitioning terutama membantu ketika data besar bisa dipisahkan secara natural berdasarkan key tertentu, misalnya waktu, tenant, region, atau hash bucket. Index tetap dibutuhkan di dalam partition untuk lookup yang selective.

---

## 3. Kapan Partitioning Layak Dipakai?

| Kondisi | Partitioning Cocok? | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Table sangat besar dan query sering filter berdasarkan waktu | Ya | Partition pruning bisa scan hanya range waktu relevan. |
| Retention period jelas, misalnya hapus data > 2 tahun | Ya | Drop/detach partition jauh lebih murah daripada delete massal. |
| Bulk load data periodik per periode | Ya | Bisa load ke partition terpisah lalu attach. |
| Audit/event log append-only | Ya | Range partition by `created_at` umum. |
| Outbox kecil tapi update-heavy | Belum tentu | Index + autovacuum tuning sering cukup. |
| Query selalu lookup by primary key saja | Belum tentu | B-tree index mungkin lebih efektif. |
| Data kecil/sedang | Tidak | Kompleksitas tidak sebanding. |
| Ingin “mempercepat semua query” | Tidak | Query tanpa partition key tetap bisa scan banyak partition. |
| Butuh global unique constraint tanpa partition key | Hati-hati | PostgreSQL punya batasan unique di partitioned table. |

**Rule:** partitioning valid jika ada **partition key yang muncul konsisten di query dan operasi maintenance**. Tanpa itu, partitioning hanya menambah kompleksitas.

---

## 4. Jenis Partitioning

PostgreSQL declarative partitioning mendukung tiga strategi utama: **range**, **list**, dan **hash**. `CREATE TABLE ... PARTITION BY` mendefinisikan parent partitioned table; partition key dapat berupa column atau expression, dengan batasan khusus untuk setiap strategi. ([Postgres Pro](https://postgrespro.com/docs/postgresql/current/sql-createtable?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Strategy | Partition Key | Cocok Untuk | Contoh |
|---|---|---|---|
| `RANGE` | Range nilai berurutan | Time-series, audit log, event log, retention period | per bulan berdasarkan `created_at` |
| `LIST` | Set nilai diskret | Region, country, category, tenant group kecil | per `region_code` |
| `HASH` | Hash modulus/remainder | Distribusi merata jika tidak ada range natural | bucket berdasarkan `tenant_id` |

---

## 5. Range Partitioning

Range partitioning adalah pilihan paling umum untuk data time-based.

### Parent table

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (created_at, id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

### Monthly partitions

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event_2026_07
PARTITION OF audit.case_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+07')
TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+07');

CREATE TABLE audit.case_event_2026_08
PARTITION OF audit.case_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+07')
TO ('2026-09-01 00:00:00+07');
```

### Insert

```sql
INSERT INTO audit.case_event (case_id, event_type, payload, created_at)
VALUES (1001, 'CASE_OPENED', '{"source":"SYSTEM"}', now());
```

PostgreSQL routes row ke partition yang cocok berdasarkan partition bound. Jika tidak ada partition yang cocok dan tidak ada default partition, insert akan gagal.

---

## 6. List Partitioning

List partitioning cocok untuk value diskret dengan jumlah manageable.

```sql
CREATE TABLE compliance.case_region (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    case_number text NOT NULL,
    region_code text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
) PARTITION BY LIST (region_code);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE compliance.case_region_apac
PARTITION OF compliance.case_region
FOR VALUES IN ('ID', 'SG', 'MY', 'TH', 'VN');

CREATE TABLE compliance.case_region_emea
PARTITION OF compliance.case_region
FOR VALUES IN ('GB', 'DE', 'FR', 'NL');

CREATE TABLE compliance.case_region_amer
PARTITION OF compliance.case_region
FOR VALUES IN ('US', 'CA', 'BR');
```

**Cocok jika:** partition values relatif stabil.  
**Tidak cocok jika:** value terus bertambah liar, misalnya one partition per customer tanpa governance.

---

## 7. Hash Partitioning

Hash partitioning cocok ketika tidak ada range natural, tetapi ingin membagi data besar secara merata.

```sql
CREATE TABLE tenant_event (
    tenant_id bigint NOT NULL,
    event_id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    payload jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, event_id)
) PARTITION BY HASH (tenant_id);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p0
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 0);

CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p1
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 1);

CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p2
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 2);

CREATE TABLE tenant_event_p3
PARTITION OF tenant_event
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 3);
```

| Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|
| Distribusi data lebih merata. | Retention time-based lebih sulit. |
| Query equality by partition key bisa prune ke partition tertentu. | Query range waktu tanpa hash key tetap scan banyak partition. |
| Mengurangi ukuran index per partition. | Mengubah jumlah bucket tidak trivial. |

---

## 8. Default Partition

Default partition menampung row yang tidak cocok dengan partition bound lain.

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event_default
PARTITION OF audit.case_event
DEFAULT;
```

| Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Insert tidak gagal saat partition masa depan lupa dibuat. | Data bisa diam-diam masuk default partition. |
| Bisa menjadi safety net operasional. | Default partition bisa membesar dan merusak pruning/performa. |
| Berguna saat key values tidak terduga. | Harus ada monitoring ketat. |

**Rule:** default partition boleh dipakai sebagai guardrail, bukan tempat tinggal permanen. Harus ada alert jika default partition menerima row.

```sql
SELECT count(*)
FROM audit.case_event_default;
```

---

## 9. Partition Pruning

Partition pruning adalah optimisasi PostgreSQL untuk melewati partition yang tidak mungkin berisi row yang dibutuhkan query. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyebut partition pruning dapat meningkatkan performa query pada declaratively partitioned table, dan dikontrol oleh `enable_partition_pruning`, yang default-nya aktif. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-partitioning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Query yang bisa prune

```sql
SELECT count(*)
FROM audit.case_event
WHERE created_at >= timestamptz '2026-07-01 00:00:00+07'
  AND created_at <  timestamptz '2026-08-01 00:00:00+07';
```

### Query yang buruk untuk pruning

```sql
SELECT count(*)
FROM audit.case_event
WHERE date(created_at) = date '2026-07-01';
```

Kenapa buruk: predicate membungkus partition key dengan function, sehingga planner lebih sulit membuktikan partition mana yang relevan.

### Cek pruning via `EXPLAIN`

```sql
EXPLAIN
SELECT *
FROM audit.case_event
WHERE created_at >= timestamptz '2026-07-01 00:00:00+07'
  AND created_at <  timestamptz '2026-07-02 00:00:00+07';
```

Yang diharapkan: plan hanya menyentuh partition yang relevan, bukan semua partition.

---

## 10. Predicate Design untuk Partition Pruning

| Partition Key | Predicate Baik | Predicate Kurang Baik |
|---|---|---|
| `created_at` range | `created_at >= ... AND created_at < ...` | `date(created_at) = ...` |
| `tenant_id` hash | `tenant_id = 42` | `tenant_id::text = '42'` |
| `region_code` list | `region_code IN ('ID','SG')` | `lower(region_code) = 'id'` |
| composite range | predicate mencakup leading partition expression | predicate hanya field non-partition |
| monthly partition | half-open interval `[start, next_start)` | `BETWEEN` dengan boundary ambigu |

**Rule:** tulis query dengan predicate langsung pada partition key. Untuk waktu, gunakan half-open interval: `>= start AND < next_start`.

---

## 11. Index Strategy pada Partitioned Table

Partitioned table tidak menyimpan data sendiri; data ada di child partitions. Index pada parent partitioned table membuat **partitioned index**, dan index fisik dibuat/terhubung di masing-masing partition.

### Create index on parent

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_case_created
ON audit.case_event (case_id, created_at DESC);
```

Ini membuat index untuk partitions yang ada dan membantu mengatur index di partition baru.

### Per-partition index

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_2026_07_case_created
ON audit.case_event_2026_07 (case_id, created_at DESC);
```

| Approach | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|
| Index parent | Default pilihan untuk index seragam di semua partition. |
| Index per partition | Saat index berbeda per partition, maintenance khusus, atau attach index manual. |
| Partial index per partition | Untuk subset tertentu di partition tertentu. |
| BRIN per time partition | Untuk append-only besar dengan physical order time-correlated. |

**Rule:** partitioning mengurangi ukuran index per partition, tetapi tidak menghilangkan kebutuhan index.

---

## 12. Unique Constraint dan Primary Key Limitation

Pada partitioned table, unique constraint atau primary key harus mencakup semua partition key. Tujuannya agar PostgreSQL bisa menjamin uniqueness tanpa global index lintas partition.

```sql
-- Valid: partition key created_at termasuk dalam primary key
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (created_at, id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

Problem jika ingin global unique `id` saja:

```sql
-- Konsep ini bermasalah untuk partitioned table by created_at
PRIMARY KEY (id)
```

### Design choices

| Kebutuhan | Pilihan |
|---|---|
| Uniqueness cukup dalam partition | Include partition key pada PK/unique. |
| Butuh globally unique ID | Gunakan UUID/random/time-ordered ID dan enforce di aplikasi/generator, atau partition by ID-compatible key. |
| Butuh unique business key global | Pertimbangkan non-partitioned lookup table kecil untuk key registry. |
| Butuh FK ke partitioned table | Desain PK harus realistis untuk FK consumer. |

**Rule:** desain primary key partitioned table harus dipikirkan dari awal. Mengubahnya setelah table besar jauh lebih mahal.

---

## 13. Partition Key Selection

| Candidate Key | Cocok Jika | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| `created_at` / `event_time` | Data append-only, retention time-based, query by period | Query by ID saja tetap perlu index dan bisa scan partition jika waktu tidak diketahui. |
| `tenant_id` | Tenant isolation dan query selalu membawa tenant | Hot tenant bisa membuat skew. |
| `region_code` | Region-based routing/reporting | Region tidak seimbang. |
| `status` | Hampir tidak pernah cocok | Status sering berubah dan cardinality kecil; menyebabkan row movement/update overhead. |
| Hash of tenant/user | Distribusi merata | Retention natural sulit. |
| Composite `(tenant_id, created_at)` | Query selalu tenant + time | Lebih kompleks; partition count bisa meledak jika salah desain. |

**Anti-pattern:** partition by `status` untuk workflow table. Status berubah, jumlah status kecil, dan query biasanya butuh index + state transition, bukan partition movement.

---

## 14. Partition Count dan Granularity

| Granularity | Cocok Untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Daily | Volume harian sangat besar, retention harian | Terlalu banyak partition jika retention panjang. |
| Monthly | Audit/event umum, retention bulanan | Partition bisa terlalu besar jika volume ekstrem. |
| Quarterly | Volume sedang, retention jangka panjang | Query harian tetap scan partition besar. |
| Yearly | Volume rendah/sedang | Maintenance lebih mudah tapi pruning kurang tajam. |
| Hash 8/16/32 buckets | Tenant distribution | Rebalancing sulit. |

**Rule praktis:** pilih partition size yang membuat maintenance nyaman, bukan hanya query cepat. Misalnya partition bulanan untuk audit log sering lebih operasional dibanding partition harian jika volume tidak ekstrem.

---

## 15. Retention Pattern: Drop Old Partition

Untuk data time-series, salah satu keuntungan terbesar partitioning adalah retention.

```sql
DROP TABLE audit.case_event_2024_01;
```

Ini jauh lebih murah daripada:

```sql
DELETE FROM audit.case_event
WHERE created_at < timestamptz '2024-02-01 00:00:00+07';
```

| Delete Massal | Drop Partition |
|---|---|
| Membuat dead tuple. | Menghapus object fisik langsung. |
| Butuh vacuum. | Tidak perlu vacuum untuk row yang hilang. |
| Bisa lama dan lock/IO heavy. | Biasanya jauh lebih cepat. |
| WAL besar untuk row delete. | Lebih ringan secara operasional. |

**Rule:** jika retention adalah requirement utama, desain partitioning harus align dengan retention boundary.

---

## 16. Retention Pattern: Detach lalu Archive

Jika data lama perlu diarsipkan sebelum dihapus:

```sql
ALTER TABLE audit.case_event
DETACH PARTITION audit.case_event_2024_01;
```

Setelah detach, partition menjadi table biasa.

```sql
ALTER TABLE audit.case_event_2024_01
SET SCHEMA archive;
```

Kemudian bisa dump/archive/drop:

```bash
pg_dump -t archive.case_event_2024_01 mydb > case_event_2024_01.sql
```

Lalu drop jika sudah aman:

```sql
DROP TABLE archive.case_event_2024_01;
```

**Pattern:** detach → validate/archive → drop. Ini lebih aman untuk compliance/forensic retention daripada langsung drop.

---

## 17. Creating Future Partitions

Jangan menunggu hari pertama bulan baru baru membuat partition.

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event_2026_09
PARTITION OF audit.case_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-09-01 00:00:00+07')
TO ('2026-10-01 00:00:00+07');
```

### Checklist future partition

| Item | Check |
|---|---|
| Partition bound benar | Tidak overlap, tidak gap. |
| Timezone boundary konsisten | Gunakan UTC atau policy eksplisit. |
| Index terbentuk | Parent index atau index manual. |
| Privilege/default privilege | Partition owner/grant benar. |
| Autovacuum options | Inherited atau diset sesuai kebutuhan. |
| Monitoring default partition | Pastikan tidak ada row nyasar. |

---

## 18. Attach Existing Table as Partition

Useful untuk bulk load.

### Step 1 - Create staging table

```sql
CREATE TABLE staging.case_event_2026_10
(LIKE audit.case_event INCLUDING DEFAULTS INCLUDING CONSTRAINTS);
```

### Step 2 - Load data

```sql
COPY staging.case_event_2026_10 (case_id, event_type, payload, created_at)
FROM '/tmp/case_event_2026_10.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);
```

### Step 3 - Validate bound with check constraint

```sql
ALTER TABLE staging.case_event_2026_10
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_event_2026_10_bound
CHECK (
    created_at >= timestamptz '2026-10-01 00:00:00+07'
    AND created_at < timestamptz '2026-11-01 00:00:00+07'
);
```

### Step 4 - Attach

```sql
ALTER TABLE audit.case_event
ATTACH PARTITION staging.case_event_2026_10
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01 00:00:00+07')
TO ('2026-11-01 00:00:00+07');
```

**Rule:** pre-validate bound dengan check constraint agar attach tidak perlu scan mahal untuk membuktikan semua row cocok.

---

## 19. Partitioned Table dan Foreign Key

Partitioning bisa dipakai dengan FK, tetapi desainnya harus hati-hati:

| Concern | Catatan |
|---|---|
| FK ke partitioned table | Perlu key yang valid dan unik sesuai partitioning limitation. |
| FK dari partitioned table | Umum, tapi index child partitions tetap penting. |
| Dropping old partition | Bisa gagal jika ada FK reference. |
| Cascading delete | Bisa sangat mahal dan berbahaya. |
| Audit/event table | Biasanya hindari FK keras jika retention/archive perlu bebas. |

**Rule:** untuk audit/event log jangka panjang, FK keras ke core table kadang kurang cocok karena retention lifecycle berbeda. Gunakan logical reference jika audit harus tetap hidup setelah parent berubah/hilang.

---

## 20. Partitioning dan Autovacuum/Analyze

Setiap partition adalah table fisik sendiri. Vacuum dan analyze terjadi pada partition, bukan pada parent sebagai data holder. Namun statistics parent tetap penting untuk query yang masuk lewat parent.

```sql
ANALYZE audit.case_event;
```

Untuk partition besar/hot:

```sql
ALTER TABLE audit.case_event_2026_07 SET (
    autovacuum_vacuum_scale_factor = 0.02,
    autovacuum_analyze_scale_factor = 0.01
);
```

| Table Type | Maintenance |
|---|---|
| Append-only historical partition | Vacuum ringan, analyze setelah load. |
| Current hot partition | Autovacuum/analyze lebih aktif. |
| Detached archive partition | Bisa freeze/analyze/dump/drop sesuai policy. |
| Parent partitioned table | Manual analyze kadang penting untuk planner. |

---

## 21. Partitioning dan Query Pattern

### Good: query includes partition key

```sql
SELECT *
FROM audit.case_event
WHERE created_at >= timestamptz '2026-07-01 00:00:00+07'
  AND created_at <  timestamptz '2026-07-08 00:00:00+07'
  AND case_id = 1001
ORDER BY created_at DESC;
```

Index:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_case_created
ON audit.case_event (case_id, created_at DESC);
```

### Risky: query lacks partition key

```sql
SELECT *
FROM audit.case_event
WHERE case_id = 1001
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

Ini bisa menyentuh banyak partition jika planner tidak bisa prune berdasarkan `created_at`.

### Better if query normally wants recent events

```sql
SELECT *
FROM audit.case_event
WHERE case_id = 1001
  AND created_at >= now() - interval '90 days'
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

**Rule:** API/query contract untuk table partitioned sebaiknya menyertakan partition key jika memungkinkan.

---

## 22. Timezone dan Boundary

Untuk range partition berbasis waktu, boundary harus konsisten.

```sql
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00')
```

atau dengan policy lokal eksplisit:

```sql
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+07')
TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+07')
```

| Pilihan | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| UTC boundary | Konsisten lintas region, umum untuk event time | Reporting lokal butuh konversi. |
| Local timezone boundary | Sesuai kalender bisnis lokal | DST/region multi-timezone bisa rumit. |
| `date` partition key | Boundary sederhana untuk kalender | Kehilangan jam/time precision. |

**Rule:** untuk audit/event, biasanya gunakan `timestamptz` dan boundary UTC atau policy timezone bisnis yang eksplisit.

---

## 23. Multi-Level Partitioning

Partition dapat dipartition lagi.

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event (
    tenant_id bigint NOT NULL,
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

Monthly partition yang dipartition lagi by hash tenant:

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event_2026_07
PARTITION OF audit.case_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00')
PARTITION BY HASH (tenant_id);
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event_2026_07_p0
PARTITION OF audit.case_event_2026_07
FOR VALUES WITH (MODULUS 4, REMAINDER 0);
```

| Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Bisa gabungkan time retention + tenant distribution. | Partition count cepat meledak. |
| Query tenant+time bisa sangat targeted. | Maintenance lebih kompleks. |
| Mengurangi hot partition size. | DDL/index/monitoring makin rumit. |

**Rule:** multi-level partitioning hanya jika single-level tidak cukup. Jangan mulai dari desain paling kompleks.

---

## 24. Monitoring Partitioned Tables

### List partitions

```sql
SELECT
    parent.relname AS parent,
    child.relname AS partition
FROM pg_inherits
JOIN pg_class parent ON pg_inherits.inhparent = parent.oid
JOIN pg_class child ON pg_inherits.inhrelid = child.oid
WHERE parent.relname = 'case_event'
ORDER BY child.relname;
```

### Partition sizes

```sql
SELECT
    child.oid::regclass AS partition,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size(child.oid)) AS total_size
FROM pg_inherits
JOIN pg_class parent ON pg_inherits.inhparent = parent.oid
JOIN pg_class child ON pg_inherits.inhrelid = child.oid
WHERE parent.oid = 'audit.case_event'::regclass
ORDER BY pg_total_relation_size(child.oid) DESC;
```

### Row estimates per partition

```sql
SELECT
    child.oid::regclass AS partition,
    child.reltuples::bigint AS estimated_rows
FROM pg_inherits
JOIN pg_class parent ON pg_inherits.inhparent = parent.oid
JOIN pg_class child ON pg_inherits.inhrelid = child.oid
WHERE parent.oid = 'audit.case_event'::regclass
ORDER BY child.relname;
```

### Find default partition count

```sql
SELECT count(*)
FROM audit.case_event_default;
```

---

## 25. Maintenance Runbook: Monthly Time Partition

### A. Create next month partition

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event_2026_09
PARTITION OF audit.case_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-09-01 00:00:00+00')
TO ('2026-10-01 00:00:00+00');
```

### B. Confirm index exists

```sql
SELECT
    schemaname,
    tablename,
    indexname
FROM pg_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'audit'
  AND tablename = 'case_event_2026_09';
```

### C. Analyze new partition after data arrives

```sql
ANALYZE audit.case_event_2026_09;
```

### D. Retire old partition

```sql
ALTER TABLE audit.case_event
DETACH PARTITION audit.case_event_2024_07;
```

### E. Archive/drop

```bash
pg_dump -t audit.case_event_2024_07 mydb > case_event_2024_07.sql
```

```sql
DROP TABLE audit.case_event_2024_07;
```

---

## 26. Operational Runbook: Missing Partition Incident

### Symptom

```text
ERROR: no partition of relation "case_event" found for row
```

### Immediate actions

1. Identify missing bound.
2. Create correct future/current partition.
3. Re-run failed insert/job if safe.
4. Check whether default partition exists and captured data.
5. Add monitoring to prevent recurrence.

### Create missing partition

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event_2026_10
PARTITION OF audit.case_event
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01 00:00:00+00')
TO ('2026-11-01 00:00:00+00');
```

### If rows went to default partition

Move them carefully:

```sql
INSERT INTO audit.case_event
SELECT *
FROM audit.case_event_default
WHERE created_at >= timestamptz '2026-10-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND created_at <  timestamptz '2026-11-01 00:00:00+00';

DELETE FROM audit.case_event_default
WHERE created_at >= timestamptz '2026-10-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND created_at <  timestamptz '2026-11-01 00:00:00+00';
```

**Caution:** lakukan dalam batch jika default partition besar.

---

## 27. Partitioning Pattern untuk Audit/Event Log

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event (
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    actor text,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    PRIMARY KEY (created_at, id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_case_created
ON audit.case_event (case_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_type_created
ON audit.case_event (event_type, created_at DESC);
```

| Design | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Partition by `created_at` | Retention dan query by time. |
| PK includes `created_at` | Memenuhi uniqueness rule partitioned table. |
| Index `case_id, created_at` | Timeline per case. |
| Index `event_type, created_at` | Query event tertentu per periode. |
| JSONB payload | Flexible audit context. |
| Avoid heavy FK if retention differs | Audit lifecycle sering lebih panjang dari core entity. |

---

## 28. Partitioning Pattern untuk Outbox

Outbox sering update-heavy, jadi partitioning harus hati-hati.

### Option A - no partition, partial index

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_outbox_pending
ON integration.outbox_message (created_at ASC, id ASC)
WHERE status = 'PENDING';
```

Ini sering cukup.

### Option B - range partition by created_at untuk retention

```sql
CREATE TABLE integration.outbox_message (
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    aggregate_type text NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id bigint NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    published_at timestamptz,
    PRIMARY KEY (created_at, id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

**Trade-off:** jika row berpindah status tapi tidak berpindah partition, aman. Jangan partition by `status` karena update status akan menyebabkan row movement antar partition dan menambah overhead.

---

## 29. Partitioning Pattern untuk Case History

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_status_history (
    changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    old_status text,
    new_status text NOT NULL,
    changed_by text NOT NULL,
    reason text,
    PRIMARY KEY (changed_at, id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (changed_at);
```

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_status_history_case_changed
ON case_mgmt.case_status_history (case_id, changed_at DESC);
```

**Cocok karena:** history append-only, query sering by case dan time, retention bisa periodik.

---

## 30. Common Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Partition tanpa query predicate partition key | Query tetap scan banyak partition | Pastikan query membawa partition key. |
| Terlalu banyak partition | Planning/maintenance overhead | Pilih granularity realistis. |
| Lupa create future partition | Insert gagal atau masuk default partition | Automasi dan alert. |
| Default partition dibiarkan membesar | Pruning/performa buruk | Monitor dan kosongkan default. |
| Partition by mutable column seperti `status` | Row movement dan write overhead | Partition by stable key. |
| Mengira partitioning mengganti index | Query tetap lambat | Buat index sesuai query pattern. |
| Global unique key tidak dirancang | PK/unique gagal dibuat | Include partition key atau desain key registry. |
| Drop partition tanpa cek FK/retention | Data hilang/constraint error | Detach-archive-drop runbook. |
| Boundary timezone tidak konsisten | Row masuk partition salah | Standardisasi UTC/policy timezone. |
| Tidak analyze partition/parent | Planner estimate buruk | `ANALYZE` partition dan parent sesuai kebutuhan. |
| Multi-level partition terlalu awal | Kompleksitas tinggi | Mulai single-level kecuali ada bukti. |

---

## 31. Decision Matrix

| Problem | Partitioning Choice |
|---|---|
| Audit/event table besar, retention bulanan | Range partition by `created_at` monthly. |
| Case history besar, query by case and period | Range by `changed_at` + index `(case_id, changed_at)`. |
| Tenant data sangat besar, query selalu tenant | Hash/list by `tenant_id`, atau composite time+tenant jika perlu. |
| Region-isolated reporting | List by `region_code`. |
| Outbox pending query lambat | Coba partial index dulu; partition by time hanya untuk retention. |
| Need delete old data efficiently | Range partition aligned with retention boundary. |
| Need global unique business key | Jangan mengandalkan partition unique kecuali key include partition key; gunakan registry table jika perlu. |
| Need bulk load monthly data | Load staging table, validate check constraint, attach partition. |
| Need archive old data | Detach partition, dump/archive, drop. |

---

## 32. Review Checklist Sebelum Partitioning

| Checklist | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Partition key | Apakah muncul di mayoritas query penting? |
| Retention | Apakah boundary partition align dengan delete/archive policy? |
| Granularity | Berapa partition setelah 1, 3, 5 tahun? |
| Query pattern | Apakah API/report menyertakan partition key? |
| Index | Index apa yang dibutuhkan di setiap partition? |
| PK/Unique | Apakah partition key masuk constraint yang harus unique? |
| FK | Apakah FK lifecycle cocok dengan detach/drop partition? |
| Default partition | Apakah perlu safety net? Bagaimana monitoringnya? |
| Future partition | Siapa/apa yang membuat partition berikutnya? |
| Analyze/Vacuum | Apakah maintenance per partition jelas? |
| Archive | Apakah detach/drop punya approval dan runbook? |
| Observability | Bisa melihat size/row/lag/default partition? |

---

## 33. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Declarative partitioning | Paham parent vs child partition. |
| 2 | Range/list/hash | Bisa memilih strategi sesuai data. |
| 3 | Partition pruning | Bisa menulis query yang benar-benar prune. |
| 4 | Index per partition | Bisa mendesain index yang tetap dibutuhkan. |
| 5 | Unique/PK limitation | Bisa mendesain key sejak awal. |
| 6 | Retention via detach/drop | Bisa menghapus/archive data lama tanpa delete massal. |
| 7 | Future partition ops | Bisa mencegah missing partition incident. |
| 8 | Monitoring | Bisa membaca size, row estimate, default partition, dan maintenance state. |
| 9 | Production pattern | Bisa menerapkan ke audit log, event log, outbox, dan history table. |
