# Sisa Materi PostgreSQL yang Direkomendasikan

| Urutan | Materi | Fokus |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | **PL/pgSQL, Functions, Procedures, Triggers, Event Triggers** | Server-side programming, procedural logic, audit trigger, validation trigger, exception handling, dynamic SQL. |
| 2 | **PostgreSQL Advanced SQL Querying** | Window function lanjutan, recursive CTE, `LATERAL`, grouping sets, set-returning functions, advanced aggregation. |
| 3 | **Full Text Search** | `tsvector`, `tsquery`, dictionary, ranking, GIN index, search design tanpa external search engine. |
| 4 | **JSONB Advanced Patterns** | JSON path, indexing strategy, generated column from JSONB, validation, semi-structured schema governance. |
| 5 | **PostgreSQL Extension Ecosystem** | `pg_stat_statements`, `pg_trgm`, `btree_gist`, `uuid-ossp`, `pgcrypto`, FDW, extension governance. |
| 6 | **Foreign Data Wrapper / FDW** | `postgres_fdw`, remote table access, pushdown, cross-database query, security, performance caveat. |
| 7 | **PostgreSQL Import/Export & Bulk Loading** | `COPY`, `\copy`, staging table, validation, batch load, CSV handling, error isolation. |
| 8 | **PostgreSQL Schema Migration Strategy** | Lock-aware DDL, expand-contract, backward-compatible migration, rollback/forward-fix, large table migration. |
| 9 | **PostgreSQL Observability & Incident Debugging** | Slow query, lock wait, deadlock, bloat, WAL growth, replication lag, autovacuum issue, practical runbooks. |
| 10 | **PostgreSQL System Catalogs & Introspection** | `pg_class`, `pg_attribute`, `pg_namespace`, `pg_constraint`, `pg_index`, dependency analysis. |
| 11 | **PostgreSQL Tablespace, Physical Layout, and Capacity Planning** | Tablespace, disk layout, growth estimation, index/table size, capacity risk. |
| 12 | **PostgreSQL High-Volume Data Lifecycle** | Retention, archival, purge, partition lifecycle, audit/history/event table design. |
| 13 | **PostgreSQL HA/DR Operational Playbook** | Failover drill, restore drill, timeline, rewind, RPO/RTO, split-brain prevention. |
| 14 | **PostgreSQL Anti-Patterns & Production Smells** | Over-indexing, long transactions, unbounded JSONB, bad pagination, missing constraints, wrong isolation assumptions. |
| 15 | **PostgreSQL Design Patterns for Case Management / Regulatory Systems** | Case lifecycle, audit trail, state transition, assignment queue, escalation, SLA, outbox, history model. |

# Rekomendasi Urutan Lanjut

Urutan paling natural berikutnya:

1. **PL/pgSQL, Functions, Procedures, Triggers, Event Triggers**
2. **Advanced SQL Querying**
3. **Full Text Search**
4. **JSONB Advanced Patterns**
5. **Extension Ecosystem**
6. **Import/Export & Bulk Loading**
7. **Schema Migration Strategy**
8. **Observability & Incident Debugging**
9. **System Catalogs & Introspection**
10. **PostgreSQL Design Patterns for Case Management / Regulatory Systems**

Materi berikutnya yang paling cocok untuk dilanjutkan sekarang: **PL/pgSQL, Functions, Procedures, Triggers, Event Triggers, Exception Handling, Dynamic SQL, dan Server-Side Programming Pattern**.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 1:10:27 AM

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL PL/pgSQL, Functions, Procedures, Triggers, Event Triggers, Dynamic SQL, dan Server-Side Programming Pattern

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk logic yang hidup di database:

- SQL functions
- PL/pgSQL functions
- procedures
- anonymous `DO` blocks
- trigger functions
- data-change triggers
- event triggers
- exception handling
- dynamic SQL
- `SECURITY DEFINER`
- audit/validation/automation patterns

PL/pgSQL adalah procedural language bawaan PostgreSQL untuk membuat function, procedure, dan trigger logic. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL current menempatkan PL/pgSQL dalam Chapter 41, mencakup struktur function, statements, control structures, cursors, transaction management, error handling, trigger functions, dan plan caching. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="plpgsql-map"
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Server-Side Programming))
    Functions
      SQL Function
      PLpgSQL Function
      Scalar Return
      Table Return
      Set Return
      Volatility
      Security Invoker
      Security Definer
    Procedures
      CALL
      Transaction Control
      Maintenance Procedure
    Anonymous Blocks
      DO
      One-time Admin Script
      Migration Helper
    Triggers
      BEFORE
      AFTER
      INSTEAD OF
      ROW
      STATEMENT
      Constraint Trigger
    Event Triggers
      DDL Command Start
      DDL Command End
      SQL Drop
      Table Rewrite
    PLpgSQL Language
      Variables
      IF
      CASE
      LOOP
      FOR
      SELECT INTO
      RETURN QUERY
      RAISE
      EXCEPTION
      EXECUTE Dynamic SQL
    Patterns
      Audit Trigger
      Validation Trigger
      Updated At Trigger
      Soft Guardrail
      DDL Guardrail
      Batch Procedure
```

**Core idea:** server-side programming di PostgreSQL berguna jika logic harus dekat dengan data, harus atomic dalam transaction yang sama, harus enforce invariant lintas client, atau dipakai untuk operasi internal database. Tetapi jangan memindahkan seluruh application domain logic ke database tanpa alasan kuat, karena trigger/function bisa menjadi hidden coupling.

---

## 3. Function vs Procedure vs Trigger vs Event Trigger

| Bentuk | Dipanggil Dengan | Return | Cocok Untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| SQL function | `SELECT func(...)` | Ya | Wrapper query sederhana, computed expression, helper deterministic. | Bisa inline/optimal tergantung bentuk. |
| PL/pgSQL function | `SELECT func(...)` atau dipakai di SQL | Ya | Logic procedural, branching, validation, multi-step query. | Perlu label volatility/security dengan benar. |
| Procedure | `CALL proc(...)` | Tidak seperti function biasa; bisa punya `OUT` | Maintenance/batch operation. | Procedure dibuat dengan `CREATE PROCEDURE`; prosedur dipanggil via `CALL`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createprocedure.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| `DO` block | `DO $$ ... $$` | Tidak | One-off script, migration helper, admin automation. | `DO` dieksekusi sebagai anonymous code block satu kali; default language adalah `plpgsql`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-do.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Trigger function | Otomatis oleh DML | `trigger` | Audit, validation, derived column, guardrail. | Trigger function dibuat tanpa argumen dan return `trigger`; argumen dari `CREATE TRIGGER` masuk via `TG_ARGV`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-trigger.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Event trigger function | Otomatis oleh DDL event | `event_trigger` | DDL audit, DDL guardrail. | Event trigger bersifat global di database dan menangkap DDL events, bukan DML table tertentu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/event-triggers.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |

---

## 4. SQL Function

SQL function cocok untuk wrapper query sederhana.

```sql id="sql-func-basic"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.is_terminal_status(p_status text)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
AS $$
    SELECT p_status IN ('CLOSED', 'CANCELLED', 'REJECTED')
$$;
```

Pemakaian:

```sql id="sql-func-use"
SELECT case_mgmt.is_terminal_status('CLOSED');
```

### Kapan pakai SQL function

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| Expression kecil | Branching kompleks |
| Helper deterministic | Banyak statement dengan side effect |
| Wrapper query simple | Error handling kompleks |
| Function untuk index expression/check | Dynamic SQL |

---

## 5. PL/pgSQL Function Basic

Function PL/pgSQL dibuat dengan `CREATE FUNCTION ... LANGUAGE plpgsql`. Dokumentasi PL/pgSQL menunjukkan function didefinisikan ke server melalui `CREATE FUNCTION`, dengan body function ditulis sebagai string literal; dollar-quoting biasanya paling nyaman. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-structure.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="plpgsql-basic"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.normalize_case_number(p_case_number text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
IMMUTABLE
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_normalized text;
BEGIN
    v_normalized := upper(trim(p_case_number));

    IF v_normalized = '' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'case_number cannot be blank';
    END IF;

    RETURN v_normalized;
END;
$$;
```

Pemakaian:

```sql id="plpgsql-basic-use"
SELECT case_mgmt.normalize_case_number(' case-2026-001 ');
```

---

## 6. Function Attributes: Volatility, Strictness, Security, Parallel

Function attributes bukan dekorasi; planner dan security model memakai metadata ini.

| Attribute | Arti | Default / Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `IMMUTABLE` | Hasil selalu sama untuk input sama; tidak membaca state database. | Cocok untuk pure function. |
| `STABLE` | Hasil stabil dalam satu statement/table scan, tetapi bisa berubah antar statement. | Cocok untuk lookup/config/current setting. |
| `VOLATILE` | Bisa berubah kapan saja atau punya side effect. | Default jika tidak ditentukan. |
| `STRICT` / `RETURNS NULL ON NULL INPUT` | Jika argumen ada `NULL`, function tidak dieksekusi dan langsung return `NULL`. | Bagus untuk helper sederhana. |
| `SECURITY INVOKER` | Jalan dengan privilege caller. | Default. |
| `SECURITY DEFINER` | Jalan dengan privilege owner function. | Powerful, berisiko privilege escalation. |
| `PARALLEL SAFE/RESTRICTED/UNSAFE` | Memberi tahu planner apakah function aman di parallel query. | Default `PARALLEL UNSAFE`. |

PostgreSQL menggunakan volatility (`IMMUTABLE`, `STABLE`, `VOLATILE`) untuk memberi tahu optimizer tentang perilaku function; jika tidak ditentukan, default-nya `VOLATILE`. Function dengan side effect harus diklasifikasikan `VOLATILE` agar tidak dioptimasi secara salah. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createfunction.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Example: strict immutable helper

```sql id="strict-func"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.safe_upper(p_value text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
STRICT
AS $$
    SELECT upper(trim(p_value))
$$;
```

---

## 7. Return Scalar, Composite, Table, dan Set

### Return scalar

```sql id="return-scalar"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.priority_label(p_priority integer)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
AS $$
    SELECT CASE
        WHEN p_priority >= 100 THEN 'CRITICAL'
        WHEN p_priority >= 50 THEN 'HIGH'
        WHEN p_priority >= 10 THEN 'MEDIUM'
        ELSE 'LOW'
    END
$$;
```

### Return table

```sql id="return-table"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.find_open_cases(p_limit integer DEFAULT 50)
RETURNS TABLE (
    id bigint,
    case_number text,
    priority integer,
    created_at timestamptz
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT c.id, c.case_number, c.priority, c.created_at
    FROM case_mgmt.case_file c
    WHERE c.status = 'OPEN'
    ORDER BY c.priority DESC, c.created_at ASC
    LIMIT p_limit;
END;
$$;
```

PL/pgSQL set-returning functions dapat memakai `RETURN NEXT` atau `RETURN QUERY`, lalu final `RETURN` tanpa argumen untuk mengakhiri function. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-control-structures.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. `SELECT INTO`, `FOUND`, dan `STRICT`

PL/pgSQL `SELECT ... INTO` memasukkan hasil query ke variable, bukan membuat table seperti `SELECT INTO` SQL biasa.

```sql id="select-into"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.get_case_status(p_case_id bigint)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_status text;
BEGIN
    SELECT status
    INTO v_status
    FROM case_mgmt.case_file
    WHERE id = p_case_id;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'case % not found', p_case_id
            USING ERRCODE = 'P0002';
    END IF;

    RETURN v_status;
END;
$$;
```

### `INTO STRICT`

```sql id="select-into-strict"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.get_case_number_strict(p_case_id bigint)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_case_number text;
BEGIN
    SELECT case_number
    INTO STRICT v_case_number
    FROM case_mgmt.case_file
    WHERE id = p_case_id;

    RETURN v_case_number;
END;
$$;
```

| Pattern | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `SELECT INTO var` | Ambil satu row; jika tidak ada, variable jadi null dan `FOUND = false`. |
| `SELECT INTO STRICT var` | Harus tepat satu row; jika 0 atau >1 akan error. |
| `FOUND` | Boolean status statement PL/pgSQL terakhir yang relevan. |

---

## 9. Control Flow

### `IF`

```sql id="if-example"
IF p_priority >= 100 THEN
    v_label := 'CRITICAL';
ELSIF p_priority >= 50 THEN
    v_label := 'HIGH';
ELSE
    v_label := 'NORMAL';
END IF;
```

### `CASE`

```sql id="case-example"
v_action := CASE p_status
    WHEN 'OPEN' THEN 'PROCESS'
    WHEN 'CLOSED' THEN 'IGNORE'
    ELSE 'REVIEW'
END;
```

### `LOOP`

```sql id="loop-example"
LOOP
    EXIT WHEN v_count >= 10;
    v_count := v_count + 1;
END LOOP;
```

### `FOR record IN SELECT`

```sql id="for-select"
FOR v_row IN
    SELECT id, case_number
    FROM case_mgmt.case_file
    WHERE status = 'OPEN'
LOOP
    RAISE NOTICE 'open case: %, %', v_row.id, v_row.case_number;
END LOOP;
```

PL/pgSQL control structures mencakup conditional, loops, return statement, dan set-returning constructs seperti `RETURN QUERY`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-control-structures.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Procedures dan Transaction Control

Procedure dibuat dengan `CREATE PROCEDURE` dan dipanggil dengan `CALL`. Berbeda dari function, procedure dirancang untuk operation-oriented command. PostgreSQL juga punya transaction management di PL/pgSQL, tetapi transaction control punya batasan konteks pemanggilan; dokumentasi PL/pgSQL menyebut transaction commands tidak boleh dipakai dalam cursor loop yang digerakkan command non-read-only, misalnya `UPDATE ... RETURNING`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createprocedure.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="procedure-basic"
CREATE PROCEDURE maintenance.close_stale_draft_cases(p_before timestamptz)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_updated_count bigint;
BEGIN
    UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
    SET status = 'CANCELLED',
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE status = 'DRAFT'
      AND created_at < p_before;

    GET DIAGNOSTICS v_updated_count = ROW_COUNT;

    RAISE NOTICE 'cancelled stale draft cases: %', v_updated_count;
END;
$$;
```

Pemakaian:

```sql id="procedure-call"
CALL maintenance.close_stale_draft_cases(now() - interval '90 days');
```

### Function vs Procedure

| Kriteria | Function | Procedure |
|---|---|---|
| Dipakai dalam `SELECT` | Ya | Tidak |
| Return value langsung | Ya | Tidak seperti function biasa |
| Cocok untuk expression/query | Ya | Tidak |
| Cocok untuk maintenance command | Bisa, tapi procedure lebih semantic | Ya |
| Transaction control | Lebih terbatas | Lebih relevan, tetap tergantung konteks `CALL` |

---

## 11. Anonymous `DO` Block

`DO` menjalankan anonymous code block satu kali, seperti transient function tanpa parameter dan return `void`; jika language tidak disebut, default-nya `plpgsql`. Jika `DO` dieksekusi di dalam transaction block, procedural code tidak boleh menjalankan transaction control statement. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-do.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="do-block"
DO $$
DECLARE
    r record;
BEGIN
    FOR r IN
        SELECT schemaname, tablename
        FROM pg_tables
        WHERE schemaname = 'case_mgmt'
    LOOP
        RAISE NOTICE 'table: %.%', r.schemaname, r.tablename;
    END LOOP;
END;
$$;
```

### Kapan pakai `DO`

| Cocok | Hindari |
|---|---|
| One-off migration helper | Business logic permanen |
| Grant/revoke massal | Logic yang perlu test/reuse |
| Catalog inspection script | API-facing behavior |
| Data repair terkontrol | Trigger tersembunyi |

---

## 12. Dynamic SQL dengan `EXECUTE`

Dynamic SQL dipakai ketika nama table, column, schema, atau command shape harus dibentuk saat runtime. PL/pgSQL menyediakan `EXECUTE` untuk command dinamis; normal plan caching PL/pgSQL tidak bekerja untuk command yang berubah-ubah sehingga dynamic command ditangani oleh `EXECUTE`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-statements.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="dynamic-execute-basic"
CREATE FUNCTION maintenance.count_rows(p_schema text, p_table text)
RETURNS bigint
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_count bigint;
BEGIN
    EXECUTE format('SELECT count(*) FROM %I.%I', p_schema, p_table)
    INTO v_count;

    RETURN v_count;
END;
$$;
```

### Dynamic SQL dengan parameter data

```sql id="dynamic-execute-using"
CREATE FUNCTION maintenance.count_status_rows(
    p_schema text,
    p_table text,
    p_status text
)
RETURNS bigint
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_count bigint;
BEGIN
    EXECUTE format(
        'SELECT count(*) FROM %I.%I WHERE status = $1',
        p_schema,
        p_table
    )
    INTO v_count
    USING p_status;

    RETURN v_count;
END;
$$;
```

### `format()` specifier penting

| Specifier | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `%I` | Identifier aman: schema/table/column name. |
| `%L` | Literal SQL aman. |
| `%s` | Raw string; gunakan hati-hati. |
| `USING` | Parameter value aman untuk dynamic query. |

**Rule:** identifier tidak bisa diparameterkan dengan `USING`, jadi pakai `format('%I', identifier)`. Value data sebaiknya pakai `USING`, bukan string concatenation.

---

## 13. Error Handling dan `RAISE`

PL/pgSQL mendukung `RAISE` untuk message dan error. Level umum: `DEBUG`, `LOG`, `INFO`, `NOTICE`, `WARNING`, dan `EXCEPTION`. PL/pgSQL juga mendukung assertion via `ASSERT`, yang mengangkat `ASSERT_FAILURE` jika kondisi false/null. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-errors-and-messages.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="raise-example"
RAISE NOTICE 'processing case id=%', p_case_id;

RAISE EXCEPTION 'case % not found', p_case_id
    USING ERRCODE = 'P0002',
          HINT = 'Check whether the case was already archived.';
```

### Exception block

```sql id="exception-block"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.try_close_case(p_case_id bigint)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
    SET status = 'CLOSED',
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = p_case_id
      AND status = 'IN_REVIEW';

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RETURN false;
    END IF;

    RETURN true;

EXCEPTION
    WHEN unique_violation THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'unexpected unique conflict while closing case %', p_case_id;
    WHEN others THEN
        RAISE;
END;
$$;
```

### Error handling rule

| Rule | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Jangan swallow error tanpa alasan | Menyembunyikan data corruption/bug. |
| Gunakan SQLSTATE jika perlu | Lebih stabil dari teks error. |
| Pakai `RAISE EXCEPTION ... USING HINT/DETAIL` | Membantu debugging. |
| `WHEN others` sebaiknya re-raise kecuali benar-benar recoverable | Menghindari false success. |

---

## 14. Trigger Function Basic

Trigger function PL/pgSQL dibuat dengan `CREATE FUNCTION`, tidak punya argumen formal, dan return type `trigger`. Saat dipanggil sebagai trigger, PostgreSQL menyediakan special variables seperti `NEW`, `OLD`, dan `TG_*`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-trigger.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="trigger-function-basic"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.set_updated_at()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at := now();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
```

Trigger:

```sql id="trigger-basic"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_case_file_set_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON case_mgmt.case_file
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION case_mgmt.set_updated_at();
```

`CREATE TRIGGER` membuat trigger baru atau, dengan `CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER`, mengganti definisi trigger yang ada; trigger diasosiasikan dengan table, view, atau foreign table dan menjalankan function saat operation tertentu terjadi. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createtrigger.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15. Trigger Timing dan Level

| Timing | Level | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|---|
| `BEFORE` | Row | Ubah/validasi `NEW` sebelum ditulis. |
| `AFTER` | Row | Audit/log setelah perubahan berhasil secara logical. |
| `INSTEAD OF` | Row pada view | Membuat view updatable secara custom. |
| `BEFORE` | Statement | Guardrail sebelum operasi massal. |
| `AFTER` | Statement | Summary/audit sekali per statement. |
| Constraint trigger | Row, biasanya `AFTER` | Constraint yang bisa deferrable. |

### Trigger variable penting

| Variable | Arti |
|---|---|
| `NEW` | Row baru untuk `INSERT`/`UPDATE`. |
| `OLD` | Row lama untuk `UPDATE`/`DELETE`. |
| `TG_OP` | Operation: `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, `TRUNCATE`. |
| `TG_TABLE_SCHEMA` | Schema table trigger. |
| `TG_TABLE_NAME` | Nama table trigger. |
| `TG_NAME` | Nama trigger. |
| `TG_WHEN` | `BEFORE`, `AFTER`, atau `INSTEAD OF`. |
| `TG_LEVEL` | `ROW` atau `STATEMENT`. |
| `TG_ARGV[]` | Argumen trigger dari `CREATE TRIGGER`. |

---

## 16. Pattern: Updated At Trigger

```sql id="updated-at-table"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_file (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    status text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

```sql id="updated-at-func"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.touch_updated_at()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at := now();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
```

```sql id="updated-at-trigger"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_case_file_touch_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON case_mgmt.case_file
FOR EACH ROW
WHEN (OLD IS DISTINCT FROM NEW)
EXECUTE FUNCTION case_mgmt.touch_updated_at();
```

**Catatan:** trigger seperti ini praktis, tetapi bisa mengejutkan saat ada update yang “tidak mengubah data” tetap mengubah `updated_at`. `WHEN (OLD IS DISTINCT FROM NEW)` membantu mengurangi noise.

---

## 17. Pattern: Audit Trigger

### Audit table

```sql id="audit-table"
CREATE TABLE audit.row_change_log (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    table_schema text NOT NULL,
    table_name text NOT NULL,
    operation text NOT NULL,
    row_pk jsonb,
    old_row jsonb,
    new_row jsonb,
    changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    changed_by text NOT NULL DEFAULT current_user
);
```

### Trigger function

```sql id="audit-func"
CREATE FUNCTION audit.log_row_change()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_pk jsonb;
BEGIN
    IF TG_OP IN ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') THEN
        v_pk := jsonb_build_object('id', to_jsonb(OLD)->'id');
    ELSE
        v_pk := jsonb_build_object('id', to_jsonb(NEW)->'id');
    END IF;

    INSERT INTO audit.row_change_log (
        table_schema,
        table_name,
        operation,
        row_pk,
        old_row,
        new_row
    )
    VALUES (
        TG_TABLE_SCHEMA,
        TG_TABLE_NAME,
        TG_OP,
        v_pk,
        CASE WHEN TG_OP IN ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') THEN to_jsonb(OLD) ELSE NULL END,
        CASE WHEN TG_OP IN ('INSERT', 'UPDATE') THEN to_jsonb(NEW) ELSE NULL END
    );

    IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
        RETURN OLD;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
```

### Attach trigger

```sql id="audit-trigger"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_case_file_audit
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON case_mgmt.case_file
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.log_row_change();
```

### Audit trigger trade-off

| Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|
| Semua client otomatis diaudit. | Write amplification. |
| Atomic dengan perubahan data. | Audit table bisa sangat besar. |
| Sulit dilewati tanpa disable trigger. | Payload JSONB bisa mahal. |
| Cocok untuk forensic trail. | Trigger tersembunyi dari aplikasi. |

---

## 18. Pattern: Validation Trigger

Gunakan trigger jika invariant tidak bisa ditulis dengan `CHECK`, FK, unique, exclusion constraint, atau transaction pattern.

```sql id="validation-func"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.validate_case_close()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status = 'CLOSED' AND NEW.closed_at IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'closed_at is required when status is CLOSED'
            USING ERRCODE = '23514';
    END IF;

    IF OLD.status = 'CLOSED' AND NEW.status <> 'CLOSED' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'closed case cannot be reopened by direct update'
            USING ERRCODE = '23514';
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
```

```sql id="validation-trigger"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_case_file_validate_close
BEFORE UPDATE ON case_mgmt.case_file
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION case_mgmt.validate_case_close();
```

**Rule:** prefer declarative constraints dulu. Trigger adalah opsi saat rule butuh procedural logic atau cross-table check yang tidak bisa dimodelkan dengan constraint biasa.

---

## 19. Pattern: Prevent Dangerous Delete

```sql id="prevent-delete-func"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.prevent_closed_case_delete()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF OLD.status = 'CLOSED' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'closed cases cannot be deleted: case_id=%', OLD.id
            USING ERRCODE = '23000';
    END IF;

    RETURN OLD;
END;
$$;
```

```sql id="prevent-delete-trigger"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_case_file_prevent_closed_delete
BEFORE DELETE ON case_mgmt.case_file
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION case_mgmt.prevent_closed_case_delete();
```

**Caution:** trigger delete guard bisa mengganggu retention/purge process. Jika ada legal purge/admin path, desain role/procedure khusus daripada disable trigger sembarangan.

---

## 20. Statement-Level Trigger dengan Transition Tables

Untuk audit mass update summary, statement-level trigger bisa lebih murah daripada row-level audit detail.

```sql id="transition-audit-table"
CREATE TABLE audit.case_status_bulk_change (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    operation text NOT NULL,
    affected_count bigint NOT NULL,
    changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

```sql id="transition-func"
CREATE FUNCTION audit.log_case_status_bulk_update()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_count bigint;
BEGIN
    SELECT count(*) INTO v_count FROM new_rows;

    INSERT INTO audit.case_status_bulk_change(operation, affected_count)
    VALUES (TG_OP, v_count);

    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;
```

```sql id="transition-trigger"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_case_file_bulk_update
AFTER UPDATE ON case_mgmt.case_file
REFERENCING NEW TABLE AS new_rows
FOR EACH STATEMENT
EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.log_case_status_bulk_update();
```

**Kapan cocok:** audit agregat, bukan forensic row-by-row.

---

## 21. Event Triggers untuk DDL Audit/Guardrail

Event triggers menangkap DDL events secara database-wide. Berbeda dari regular trigger yang melekat ke table dan menangkap DML, event trigger global pada database dan menangkap DDL event. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/event-triggers.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Event trigger function

Event trigger function harus return `event_trigger`; return type ini hanya sinyal bahwa function dipanggil sebagai event trigger dan function tidak mengembalikan nilai. Jika beberapa event trigger ada untuk event yang sama, trigger akan fire dalam urutan alfabetis berdasarkan nama trigger. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/event-trigger-definition.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="event-audit-table"
CREATE TABLE audit.ddl_audit_log (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    tag text NOT NULL,
    username text NOT NULL DEFAULT current_user,
    database_name text NOT NULL DEFAULT current_database(),
    occurred_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

```sql id="event-trigger-func"
CREATE FUNCTION audit.log_ddl_command()
RETURNS event_trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO audit.ddl_audit_log(tag)
    VALUES (tg_tag);
END;
$$;
```

```sql id="event-trigger"
CREATE EVENT TRIGGER trg_log_ddl_command
ON ddl_command_end
EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.log_ddl_command();
```

`CREATE EVENT TRIGGER` membuat event trigger baru; ketika event yang ditentukan terjadi dan `WHEN` condition terpenuhi, trigger function akan dieksekusi. Filter yang didukung saat ini memakai `TAG` untuk membatasi command tag tertentu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createeventtrigger.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 22. Event Trigger: DDL Guardrail

Contoh menolak `DROP TABLE` di database tertentu.

```sql id="ddl-guard-func"
CREATE FUNCTION audit.prevent_drop_table()
RETURNS event_trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF tg_tag = 'DROP TABLE' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'DROP TABLE is not allowed in this database';
    END IF;
END;
$$;
```

```sql id="ddl-guard-trigger"
CREATE EVENT TRIGGER trg_prevent_drop_table
ON ddl_command_start
WHEN TAG IN ('DROP TABLE')
EXECUTE FUNCTION audit.prevent_drop_table();
```

### Event trigger use cases

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| Audit DDL | Enforce row-level data rule |
| Prevent dangerous DDL | Business workflow transition |
| Track schema changes | High-frequency DML audit |
| Migration governance | Per-table validation |

---

## 23. `SECURITY DEFINER` Function

`SECURITY DEFINER` membuat function berjalan dengan privilege owner function, bukan caller. Ini powerful untuk controlled privileged operations, tetapi juga salah satu sumber privilege escalation terbesar. PostgreSQL docs secara eksplisit menyarankan `search_path` aman untuk `SECURITY DEFINER`, menaruh trusted schema lalu `pg_temp` terakhir, agar object yang dibuat user tidak dapat menutupi object yang dimaksud function. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createfunction.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="security-definer"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.close_case_as_system(p_case_id bigint)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = case_mgmt, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE case_file
    SET status = 'CLOSED',
        closed_at = now(),
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = p_case_id
      AND status = 'IN_REVIEW';

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'case % cannot be closed', p_case_id;
    END IF;
END;
$$;
```

Grant minimal:

```sql id="security-definer-grant"
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION case_mgmt.close_case_as_system(bigint) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION case_mgmt.close_case_as_system(bigint) TO case_operator;
```

### Security checklist

| Checklist | Wajib? |
|---|---:|
| Owner bukan superuser jika tidak perlu | Ya |
| `SET search_path = trusted_schema, pg_temp` | Ya |
| Revoke `EXECUTE` from `PUBLIC` | Ya |
| Grant ke role spesifik | Ya |
| Hindari dynamic SQL raw | Ya |
| Audit function body | Ya |
| Jangan return data sensitif tidak perlu | Ya |

---

## 24. Plan Caching dan Function Performance

PL/pgSQL dapat cache plans untuk SQL statement statis dalam function. Dynamic SQL via `EXECUTE` tidak memakai plan caching PL/pgSQL yang sama karena command shape bisa berubah. Ini alasan mengapa dynamic SQL perlu dipakai hanya saat memang perlu, misalnya dynamic table/column/schema. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-statements.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Pattern | Performance Implication |
|---|---|
| Static SQL dalam PL/pgSQL | Bisa mendapat plan caching. |
| Dynamic SQL `EXECUTE` | Direncanakan saat runtime. |
| Function dipanggil per row besar | Bisa mahal jika melakukan query internal berulang. |
| Volatility salah | Planner bisa salah optimasi atau tidak bisa optimasi. |
| Trigger row-level pada table hot | Write amplification. |

**Rule:** jangan memanggil function PL/pgSQL yang query database di setiap row dari query besar tanpa memahami biayanya.

---

## 25. Server-Side Programming Decision Matrix

| Problem | Pilihan Terbaik |
|---|---|
| Field derived sederhana | Generated column atau SQL expression |
| Invariant satu row | `CHECK` constraint |
| Referential integrity | FK |
| Unique business key | Unique constraint |
| No-overlap interval | Exclusion constraint |
| Atomic state transition | Conditional `UPDATE ... WHERE ... RETURNING` |
| Audit semua perubahan table | Row-level `AFTER` trigger |
| Set `updated_at` otomatis | Row-level `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger |
| DDL audit | Event trigger |
| One-off admin loop | `DO` block |
| Maintenance batch reusable | Procedure |
| Controlled privileged operation | `SECURITY DEFINER` function, dengan hardening |
| Complex report query | View/materialized view/function, tergantung freshness/performance |

---

## 26. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Semua business logic dipindah ke trigger | Hidden coupling, sulit test/debug | Gunakan trigger hanya untuk invariant/audit yang harus dekat data. |
| Trigger melakukan call eksternal | Transaction lambat/fragile | Jangan lakukan network call dari DB trigger. |
| Dynamic SQL dengan string concatenation raw | SQL injection / object hijack | Pakai `format('%I')`, `%L`, dan `USING`. |
| `SECURITY DEFINER` tanpa `search_path` | Privilege escalation | Set trusted schema dan `pg_temp` terakhir. |
| Function side-effect diberi `IMMUTABLE` | Wrong query result/optimizer bug-like behavior | Label `VOLATILE`. |
| Row-level trigger di table high-write tanpa ukuran biaya | Write latency/bloat/audit growth | Benchmark, batch, partition audit. |
| Exception ditelan lalu return sukses | Data corruption tersembunyi | Re-raise kecuali recoverable. |
| Trigger audit menyimpan full JSON row untuk semua update | Storage meledak | Filter column penting atau audit delta. |
| Event trigger terlalu agresif | Migration legitimate gagal | Scope dengan `WHEN TAG`, role check, runbook override. |
| Function/procedure tidak schema-qualified | search_path ambiguity | Gunakan schema-qualified names. |

---

## 27. Catalog Introspection untuk Functions dan Triggers

### List functions

```sql id="list-functions"
SELECT
    n.nspname AS schema_name,
    p.proname AS function_name,
    pg_get_function_identity_arguments(p.oid) AS args,
    l.lanname AS language,
    p.provolatile,
    p.prosecdef AS security_definer
FROM pg_proc p
JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = p.pronamespace
JOIN pg_language l ON l.oid = p.prolang
WHERE n.nspname NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')
ORDER BY n.nspname, p.proname;
```

### List triggers

```sql id="list-triggers"
SELECT
    event_object_schema,
    event_object_table,
    trigger_name,
    action_timing,
    event_manipulation,
    action_orientation,
    action_statement
FROM information_schema.triggers
WHERE trigger_schema NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')
ORDER BY event_object_schema, event_object_table, trigger_name;
```

### Disabled triggers

```sql id="disabled-triggers"
SELECT
    n.nspname AS schema_name,
    c.relname AS table_name,
    t.tgname AS trigger_name,
    t.tgenabled
FROM pg_trigger t
JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = t.tgrelid
JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace
WHERE NOT t.tgisinternal
ORDER BY n.nspname, c.relname, t.tgname;
```

---

## 28. Testing Patterns

### Test function behavior in transaction

```sql id="test-transaction"
BEGIN;

SELECT case_mgmt.close_case_as_system(1001);

SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE id = 1001;

ROLLBACK;
```

### Test trigger

```sql id="test-trigger"
BEGIN;

UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
SET status = 'IN_REVIEW'
WHERE id = 1001;

SELECT *
FROM audit.row_change_log
WHERE row_pk @> '{"id":1001}'::jsonb
ORDER BY changed_at DESC;

ROLLBACK;
```

### Test error path

```sql id="test-error"
BEGIN;

SELECT case_mgmt.close_case_as_system(-1);

ROLLBACK;
```

**Rule:** test successful path, no-op path, conflict path, permission path, and rollback behavior.

---

## 29. Production Review Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Function volatility | `IMMUTABLE/STABLE/VOLATILE` sesuai behavior. |
| Security | `SECURITY DEFINER` hardened atau hindari. |
| Search path | Function penting pakai schema-qualified name atau `SET search_path`. |
| Grants | `EXECUTE` tidak terbuka ke `PUBLIC` jika sensitif. |
| Trigger cost | Row-level trigger tidak terlalu mahal untuk hot table. |
| Audit growth | Audit table punya retention/partitioning. |
| Error handling | Tidak swallow error penting. |
| Dynamic SQL | Identifier/value escaped aman. |
| Transaction behavior | Procedure/DO tidak melanggar batasan transaction context. |
| Observability | `RAISE NOTICE` tidak spam production; logging cukup. |
| Migration impact | Trigger/function change kompatibel dengan deployment. |
| Ownership | Owner function/table jelas dan bukan app runtime sembarangan. |

---

## 30. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | SQL function vs PL/pgSQL function | Bisa memilih bentuk function yang tepat. |
| 2 | Function attributes | Bisa memberi volatility/security/strictness yang benar. |
| 3 | PL/pgSQL basics | Bisa memakai variable, `IF`, loop, `SELECT INTO`, `RETURN QUERY`. |
| 4 | Procedures dan `DO` | Bisa membuat maintenance operation dan one-off script. |
| 5 | Dynamic SQL | Bisa membuat command dinamis yang aman. |
| 6 | Error handling | Bisa membuat error yang jelas tanpa menyembunyikan bug. |
| 7 | Data-change triggers | Bisa membuat audit/validation/timestamp trigger. |
| 8 | Event triggers | Bisa membuat DDL audit/guardrail. |
| 9 | Security definer | Bisa membuat privileged function tanpa membuka privilege escalation. |
| 10 | Production review | Bisa audit function/trigger dari sisi performance, security, dan maintainability. |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 1:13:43 AM

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Advanced SQL Querying

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk query SQL lanjutan:

- window functions
- window frame
- recursive CTE
- `LATERAL`
- grouping sets, `ROLLUP`, `CUBE`
- set-returning functions
- ordered-set aggregates
- aggregate `FILTER`
- advanced query pattern untuk workflow, audit, reporting, dan analytics

PostgreSQL menyediakan fitur query lanjutan seperti `WITH`/CTE, recursive query, table expressions, `LATERAL`, window functions, grouping sets, dan ordered-set aggregate sebagai bagian dari SQL language. `WITH` sendiri dapat berisi auxiliary statement berupa `SELECT`, `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, atau `MERGE`, dan berlaku hanya untuk satu primary statement. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-with.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Advanced Querying))
    Window Functions
      PARTITION BY
      ORDER BY
      Frame
      Ranking
      Lag Lead
      Running Aggregate
    CTE
      WITH
      Recursive CTE
      Materialized
      Not Materialized
      Data Modifying CTE
    LATERAL
      Per Row Subquery
      Top N per Group
      JSON Expansion
      Function Expansion
    Aggregation
      FILTER
      GROUPING SETS
      ROLLUP
      CUBE
      Ordered Set Aggregate
    Set Returning
      generate_series
      unnest
      jsonb_each
      WITH ORDINALITY
    Workflow Patterns
      Latest Status
      Transition Timeline
      SLA Buckets
      Assignment Ranking
      Hierarchy Traversal
```

**Core idea:** advanced SQL bukan sekadar “query kompleks”. Tujuannya adalah mengekspresikan pola yang sering muncul di sistem nyata: ranking, latest-state extraction, timeline diff, hierarchy traversal, bucketing, report subtotal, dan per-row expansion tanpa memecah logic menjadi banyak round-trip.

---

## 3. Window Functions

Window function menghitung nilai lintas sekumpulan row yang berhubungan dengan row saat ini, tanpa menggabungkan row menjadi satu seperti `GROUP BY`. PostgreSQL menyatakan window function melakukan kalkulasi atas “virtual table” yang dihasilkan setelah `FROM`, `WHERE`, `GROUP BY`, dan `HAVING`. Window function hanya boleh muncul di select list dan `ORDER BY` query. 

```sql
SELECT
    case_id,
    status,
    changed_at,
    row_number() OVER (
        PARTITION BY case_id
        ORDER BY changed_at DESC
    ) AS status_rank
FROM case_mgmt.case_status_history;
```

| Komponen | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `OVER (...)` | Menandai function sebagai window function. |
| `PARTITION BY` | Membagi row ke grup logis. |
| `ORDER BY` | Urutan dalam partition. |
| Frame | Subset row sekitar current row yang dipakai function tertentu. |

---

## 4. Ranking Functions

```sql
SELECT
    assignee_id,
    case_id,
    priority,
    created_at,
    row_number() OVER (
        PARTITION BY assignee_id
        ORDER BY priority DESC, created_at ASC
    ) AS queue_position,
    rank() OVER (
        PARTITION BY assignee_id
        ORDER BY priority DESC
    ) AS priority_rank,
    dense_rank() OVER (
        PARTITION BY assignee_id
        ORDER BY priority DESC
    ) AS dense_priority_rank
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE status = 'OPEN';
```

| Function | Behavior | Use Case |
|---|---|---|
| `row_number()` | Nomor unik per row. | Queue position, dedup pick one row. |
| `rank()` | Ranking dengan gap saat tie. | Competition rank. |
| `dense_rank()` | Ranking tanpa gap. | Bucket rank yang stabil. |
| `percent_rank()` | Posisi relatif 0-1. | Percentile-ish view. |
| `cume_dist()` | Cumulative distribution. | SLA distribution/reporting. |
| `ntile(n)` | Bagi row ke n bucket. | Sampling, prioritization bucket. |

---

## 5. `lag()` dan `lead()` untuk Timeline Diff

`lag()` mengambil nilai dari row sebelumnya dalam window; `lead()` mengambil nilai dari row sesudahnya.

```sql
SELECT
    case_id,
    old_status,
    new_status,
    changed_at,
    lag(new_status) OVER (
        PARTITION BY case_id
        ORDER BY changed_at
    ) AS previous_new_status,
    lag(changed_at) OVER (
        PARTITION BY case_id
        ORDER BY changed_at
    ) AS previous_changed_at,
    changed_at
      - lag(changed_at) OVER (
            PARTITION BY case_id
            ORDER BY changed_at
        ) AS duration_since_previous_change
FROM case_mgmt.case_status_history
ORDER BY case_id, changed_at;
```

**Use case:** menghitung durasi antar status, mendeteksi transisi tidak valid, audit lifecycle, SLA dwell time.

---

## 6. Latest Row per Group

### Pattern A - `row_number()`

```sql
WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT
        h.*,
        row_number() OVER (
            PARTITION BY case_id
            ORDER BY changed_at DESC, id DESC
        ) AS rn
    FROM case_mgmt.case_status_history h
)
SELECT *
FROM ranked
WHERE rn = 1;
```

### Pattern B - PostgreSQL-specific `DISTINCT ON`

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT ON (case_id)
    case_id,
    new_status,
    changed_at
FROM case_mgmt.case_status_history
ORDER BY case_id, changed_at DESC, id DESC;
```

| Pattern | Kelebihan | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `row_number()` | Standard-ish, fleksibel. | Bagus untuk top-N juga. |
| `DISTINCT ON` | Ringkas dan sangat praktis di PostgreSQL. | PostgreSQL-specific; `ORDER BY` harus dirancang hati-hati. |

Index yang cocok:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_status_history_case_changed
ON case_mgmt.case_status_history (case_id, changed_at DESC, id DESC);
```

---

## 7. Running Total dan Running Count

```sql
SELECT
    date_trunc('day', created_at)::date AS event_date,
    count(*) AS daily_events,
    sum(count(*)) OVER (
        ORDER BY date_trunc('day', created_at)::date
    ) AS running_events
FROM audit.case_event
GROUP BY date_trunc('day', created_at)::date
ORDER BY event_date;
```

**Pattern penting:** aggregate biasa (`count(*)`) dipakai dulu per group, lalu window aggregate (`sum(count(*)) OVER`) menghitung running total lintas hasil group.

---

## 8. Window Frame: `ROWS`, `RANGE`, `GROUPS`

Window frame menentukan baris mana yang dihitung relatif terhadap current row.

```sql
SELECT
    case_id,
    changed_at,
    new_status,
    count(*) OVER (
        PARTITION BY case_id
        ORDER BY changed_at
        ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW
    ) AS transition_number
FROM case_mgmt.case_status_history;
```

| Frame Type | Makna |
|---|---|
| `ROWS` | Berdasarkan jumlah row fisik relatif. |
| `RANGE` | Berdasarkan nilai ordering; peer dengan nilai sama ikut. |
| `GROUPS` | Berdasarkan peer group dalam ordering. |

### Moving average contoh

```sql
SELECT
    event_date,
    daily_count,
    avg(daily_count) OVER (
        ORDER BY event_date
        ROWS BETWEEN 6 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW
    ) AS seven_day_avg
FROM audit.daily_case_event_count
ORDER BY event_date;
```

**Pitfall:** `last_value()` sering mengejutkan karena default frame biasanya berakhir di current row, bukan akhir partition. Untuk “nilai terakhir seluruh partition”, tentukan frame eksplisit.

```sql
last_value(new_status) OVER (
    PARTITION BY case_id
    ORDER BY changed_at
    ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING
)
```

---

## 9. Aggregate `FILTER`

`FILTER` membuat conditional aggregate lebih bersih daripada banyak `CASE WHEN`.

```sql
SELECT
    count(*) AS total_cases,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'OPEN') AS open_cases,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'ESCALATED') AS escalated_cases,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE sla_due_at < now() AND status <> 'CLOSED') AS overdue_cases
FROM case_mgmt.case_file;
```

Untuk reporting per assignee:

```sql
SELECT
    assigned_user_id,
    count(*) AS total,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'OPEN') AS open_count,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'IN_REVIEW') AS review_count,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'ESCALATED') AS escalated_count
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
GROUP BY assigned_user_id;
```

---

## 10. Grouping Sets, `ROLLUP`, dan `CUBE`

PostgreSQL mendukung `GROUPING SETS`, `ROLLUP`, dan `CUBE` dalam `GROUP BY`. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menjelaskan bahwa `CUBE` dan `ROLLUP` dapat dipakai langsung dalam `GROUP BY` atau nested di dalam `GROUPING SETS`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-table-expressions.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### `GROUPING SETS`

```sql
SELECT
    status,
    assigned_user_id,
    count(*) AS total
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
GROUP BY GROUPING SETS (
    (status, assigned_user_id),
    (status),
    (assigned_user_id),
    ()
)
ORDER BY status, assigned_user_id;
```

Ini setara dengan membuat beberapa aggregation level dalam satu query.

### `ROLLUP`

`ROLLUP(status, severity)` menghasilkan subtotal hierarkis:

```sql
SELECT
    status,
    severity,
    count(*) AS total
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
GROUP BY ROLLUP (status, severity)
ORDER BY status, severity;
```

Output conceptual:

| status | severity | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `OPEN` | `HIGH` | Detail per status + severity |
| `OPEN` | `NULL` | Subtotal per status |
| `NULL` | `NULL` | Grand total |

### `CUBE`

`CUBE(status, severity)` menghasilkan semua kombinasi grouping:

```sql
SELECT
    status,
    severity,
    count(*) AS total
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
GROUP BY CUBE (status, severity)
ORDER BY status, severity;
```

| Construct | Cocok Untuk |
|---|---|
| `GROUPING SETS` | Report level custom. |
| `ROLLUP` | Hierarki natural: year → month → day, status → severity. |
| `CUBE` | Semua kombinasi dimensi kecil. |

---

## 11. `GROUPING()` untuk Membedakan `NULL`

Subtotal dari `ROLLUP`/`CUBE` memakai `NULL`, yang bisa ambigu dengan nilai `NULL` asli. Gunakan `GROUPING()`.

```sql
SELECT
    CASE WHEN GROUPING(status) = 1 THEN 'ALL_STATUS' ELSE status END AS status_label,
    CASE WHEN GROUPING(severity) = 1 THEN 'ALL_SEVERITY' ELSE severity END AS severity_label,
    count(*) AS total
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
GROUP BY ROLLUP (status, severity)
ORDER BY GROUPING(status), status, GROUPING(severity), severity;
```

| `GROUPING(col)` | Makna |
|---:|---|
| `0` | Column ikut grouping normal. |
| `1` | Column di-aggregate sebagai subtotal/all. |

---

## 12. Ordered-Set Aggregates

Ordered-set aggregate memakai `WITHIN GROUP`.

```sql
SELECT
    percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY extract(epoch FROM resolved_at - created_at)) AS median_resolution_seconds,
    percentile_cont(0.95) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY extract(epoch FROM resolved_at - created_at)) AS p95_resolution_seconds
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE status = 'CLOSED'
  AND resolved_at IS NOT NULL;
```

| Function | Use Case |
|---|---|
| `percentile_cont(fraction)` | Continuous percentile/interpolated. |
| `percentile_disc(fraction)` | Discrete percentile dari nilai aktual. |
| `mode()` | Nilai yang paling sering muncul. |

**Use case:** median SLA resolution, p95 queue time, p99 processing duration.

---

## 13. Recursive CTE

Recursive CTE dipakai untuk hierarchy/tree/graph traversal. PostgreSQL recursive query dievaluasi secara iteratif: non-recursive term menghasilkan working table awal, lalu recursive term diulang selama masih menghasilkan row baru. Dokumentasi resmi menjelaskan bahwa `WITH RECURSIVE` memungkinkan self-reference di CTE. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-with.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Organization hierarchy

```sql
WITH RECURSIVE org_tree AS (
    SELECT
        id,
        parent_id,
        name,
        1 AS depth,
        ARRAY[id] AS path
    FROM org_unit
    WHERE parent_id IS NULL

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        child.id,
        child.parent_id,
        child.name,
        parent.depth + 1,
        parent.path || child.id
    FROM org_unit child
    JOIN org_tree parent
        ON child.parent_id = parent.id
)
SELECT *
FROM org_tree
ORDER BY path;
```

### Case escalation chain

```sql
WITH RECURSIVE escalation_chain AS (
    SELECT
        id,
        previous_escalation_id,
        case_id,
        escalated_to,
        escalated_at,
        1 AS depth
    FROM case_mgmt.case_escalation
    WHERE id = :starting_escalation_id

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        e.id,
        e.previous_escalation_id,
        e.case_id,
        e.escalated_to,
        e.escalated_at,
        c.depth + 1
    FROM case_mgmt.case_escalation e
    JOIN escalation_chain c
        ON e.id = c.previous_escalation_id
)
SELECT *
FROM escalation_chain
ORDER BY depth;
```

---

## 14. Recursive CTE Cycle Protection

Recursive query bisa infinite jika ada cycle. Gunakan path tracking.

```sql
WITH RECURSIVE org_tree AS (
    SELECT
        id,
        parent_id,
        name,
        ARRAY[id] AS path,
        false AS is_cycle
    FROM org_unit
    WHERE parent_id IS NULL

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        child.id,
        child.parent_id,
        child.name,
        parent.path || child.id,
        child.id = ANY(parent.path) AS is_cycle
    FROM org_unit child
    JOIN org_tree parent
        ON child.parent_id = parent.id
    WHERE NOT parent.is_cycle
)
SELECT *
FROM org_tree;
```

**Rule:** recursive CTE untuk graph/hierarchy production harus punya cycle defense, depth limit, atau data constraint yang menjamin acyclic structure.

---

## 15. `LATERAL`

`LATERAL` membuat subquery di `FROM` bisa mereferensikan column dari item `FROM` sebelumnya. PostgreSQL table expression docs membahas `LATERAL` dalam `FROM` sebagai mekanisme agar subquery/function dievaluasi per row dari sumber di sebelah kirinya. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-table-expressions.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Latest event per case

```sql
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.case_number,
    latest.event_type,
    latest.created_at AS latest_event_at
FROM case_mgmt.case_file c
LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT e.event_type, e.created_at
    FROM audit.case_event e
    WHERE e.case_id = c.id
    ORDER BY e.created_at DESC, e.id DESC
    LIMIT 1
) latest ON true
WHERE c.status = 'OPEN';
```

Index:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_case_created
ON audit.case_event (case_id, created_at DESC, id DESC);
```

### Top 3 tasks per assignee

```sql
SELECT
    u.id AS assignee_id,
    u.display_name,
    t.id AS task_id,
    t.priority,
    t.created_at
FROM identity.app_user u
LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT id, priority, created_at
    FROM case_mgmt.case_task t
    WHERE t.assignee_id = u.id
      AND t.status = 'READY'
    ORDER BY priority DESC, created_at ASC
    LIMIT 3
) t ON true;
```

**Pattern:** `LATERAL` sangat cocok untuk “top N per parent row” tanpa window ranking seluruh table jika parent set kecil dan index mendukung.

---

## 16. `CROSS JOIN LATERAL` dengan JSONB Expansion

```sql
SELECT
    e.id AS event_id,
    kv.key,
    kv.value
FROM audit.case_event e
CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_each_text(e.payload) AS kv(key, value)
WHERE e.created_at >= now() - interval '1 day';
```

### Array expansion

```sql
SELECT
    c.id AS case_id,
    tag
FROM case_mgmt.case_file c
CROSS JOIN LATERAL unnest(c.tags) AS tag;
```

**Caution:** expansion bisa memperbesar row count drastis. Selalu filter table utama sebelum expand jika memungkinkan.

---

## 17. Set-Returning Functions

Set-returning functions menghasilkan banyak row.

### `generate_series`

```sql
SELECT d::date AS report_date
FROM generate_series(
    date '2026-07-01',
    date '2026-07-31',
    interval '1 day'
) AS d;
```

### Fill missing dates in report

```sql
WITH dates AS (
    SELECT d::date AS report_date
    FROM generate_series(
        date '2026-07-01',
        date '2026-07-31',
        interval '1 day'
    ) AS d
),
daily AS (
    SELECT
        created_at::date AS report_date,
        count(*) AS total_cases
    FROM case_mgmt.case_file
    WHERE created_at >= date '2026-07-01'
      AND created_at <  date '2026-08-01'
    GROUP BY created_at::date
)
SELECT
    dates.report_date,
    coalesce(daily.total_cases, 0) AS total_cases
FROM dates
LEFT JOIN daily USING (report_date)
ORDER BY dates.report_date;
```

### `WITH ORDINALITY`

```sql
SELECT
    value,
    ordinal_position
FROM unnest(ARRAY['OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'ESCALATED', 'CLOSED'])
WITH ORDINALITY AS s(value, ordinal_position);
```

**Use case:** preserve ordering dari array/list literal.

---

## 18. Data-Modifying CTE

CTE bisa dipakai untuk mutation yang hasilnya dipakai statement berikutnya dalam satu statement.

```sql
WITH changed AS (
    UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
    SET status = 'ESCALATED',
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = :case_id
      AND status = 'OPEN'
    RETURNING id, case_number, status
)
INSERT INTO audit.case_event (
    case_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    created_at
)
SELECT
    id,
    'CASE_ESCALATED',
    jsonb_build_object(
        'case_number', case_number,
        'new_status', status
    ),
    now()
FROM changed
RETURNING case_id;
```

**Pattern:** atomic mutation + audit event dalam satu SQL statement.

---

## 19. CTE Materialization: `MATERIALIZED` dan `NOT MATERIALIZED`

PostgreSQL mendukung kontrol materialisasi CTE dengan `MATERIALIZED` dan `NOT MATERIALIZED`. Ini berguna saat planner behavior penting: materialisasi bisa menghindari recomputation, tetapi bisa juga menghalangi pushdown predicate.

```sql
WITH recent_cases AS MATERIALIZED (
    SELECT *
    FROM case_mgmt.case_file
    WHERE created_at >= now() - interval '30 days'
)
SELECT *
FROM recent_cases
WHERE status = 'OPEN';
```

```sql
WITH recent_cases AS NOT MATERIALIZED (
    SELECT *
    FROM case_mgmt.case_file
    WHERE created_at >= now() - interval '30 days'
)
SELECT *
FROM recent_cases
WHERE status = 'OPEN';
```

| Option | Gunakan Jika |
|---|---|
| `MATERIALIZED` | CTE dipakai berulang atau ingin snapshot intermediate eksplisit. |
| `NOT MATERIALIZED` | Ingin planner bisa inline/push predicate. |
| Default | Biarkan jika tidak punya evidence dari `EXPLAIN`. |

---

## 20. Anti-Join dan Semi-Join Patterns

### `EXISTS`

```sql
SELECT c.*
FROM case_mgmt.case_file c
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM case_mgmt.case_task t
    WHERE t.case_id = c.id
      AND t.status = 'READY'
);
```

### `NOT EXISTS`

```sql
SELECT c.*
FROM case_mgmt.case_file c
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM case_mgmt.case_task t
    WHERE t.case_id = c.id
      AND t.status <> 'DONE'
);
```

**Rule:** prefer `EXISTS`/`NOT EXISTS` untuk existence check. Hindari `NOT IN` jika subquery bisa menghasilkan `NULL`, karena SQL three-valued logic bisa membuat hasil mengejutkan.

---

## 21. Query Pattern: State Transition Timeline

```sql
WITH transitions AS (
    SELECT
        case_id,
        old_status,
        new_status,
        changed_at,
        lag(new_status) OVER (
            PARTITION BY case_id
            ORDER BY changed_at
        ) AS previous_status,
        lead(changed_at) OVER (
            PARTITION BY case_id
            ORDER BY changed_at
        ) AS next_changed_at
    FROM case_mgmt.case_status_history
)
SELECT
    case_id,
    new_status AS status,
    changed_at AS entered_at,
    coalesce(next_changed_at, now()) AS exited_at,
    coalesce(next_changed_at, now()) - changed_at AS dwell_time
FROM transitions
ORDER BY case_id, entered_at;
```

**Use case:** menghitung waktu yang dihabiskan case di setiap status.

---

## 22. Query Pattern: SLA Buckets

```sql
SELECT
    CASE
        WHEN sla_due_at IS NULL THEN 'NO_SLA'
        WHEN status = 'CLOSED' THEN 'CLOSED'
        WHEN sla_due_at < now() THEN 'OVERDUE'
        WHEN sla_due_at < now() + interval '24 hours' THEN 'DUE_24H'
        ELSE 'ON_TRACK'
    END AS sla_bucket,
    count(*) AS total
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
GROUP BY sla_bucket
ORDER BY total DESC;
```

Dengan `FILTER` per assignee:

```sql
SELECT
    assigned_user_id,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE sla_due_at < now() AND status <> 'CLOSED') AS overdue,
    count(*) FILTER (
        WHERE sla_due_at >= now()
          AND sla_due_at < now() + interval '24 hours'
          AND status <> 'CLOSED'
    ) AS due_24h,
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'CLOSED') AS closed
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
GROUP BY assigned_user_id;
```

---

## 23. Query Pattern: Dedup Event by Business Key

```sql
WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT
        *,
        row_number() OVER (
            PARTITION BY external_event_id
            ORDER BY received_at DESC, id DESC
        ) AS rn
    FROM integration.inbound_event
)
SELECT *
FROM ranked
WHERE rn = 1;
```

Untuk menandai duplicate:

```sql
WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT
        id,
        row_number() OVER (
            PARTITION BY external_event_id
            ORDER BY received_at ASC, id ASC
        ) AS rn
    FROM integration.inbound_event
)
UPDATE integration.inbound_event e
SET duplicate = true
FROM ranked r
WHERE e.id = r.id
  AND r.rn > 1;
```

---

## 24. Query Pattern: Gap Detection

Cari tanggal tanpa event.

```sql
WITH dates AS (
    SELECT d::date AS event_date
    FROM generate_series(
        current_date - interval '30 days',
        current_date,
        interval '1 day'
    ) AS d
),
actual AS (
    SELECT created_at::date AS event_date, count(*) AS total
    FROM audit.case_event
    WHERE created_at >= current_date - interval '30 days'
    GROUP BY created_at::date
)
SELECT
    dates.event_date,
    coalesce(actual.total, 0) AS total
FROM dates
LEFT JOIN actual USING (event_date)
WHERE coalesce(actual.total, 0) = 0
ORDER BY dates.event_date;
```

**Use case:** monitoring ingestion gap, missing daily batch, audit stream interruption.

---

## 25. Query Pattern: Hierarchical Permission Expansion

```sql
WITH RECURSIVE role_tree AS (
    SELECT
        role_id,
        parent_role_id,
        role_name,
        ARRAY[role_id] AS path
    FROM security.role_hierarchy
    WHERE role_id = :root_role_id

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        child.role_id,
        child.parent_role_id,
        child.role_name,
        parent.path || child.role_id
    FROM security.role_hierarchy child
    JOIN role_tree parent
        ON child.parent_role_id = parent.role_id
    WHERE NOT child.role_id = ANY(parent.path)
)
SELECT *
FROM role_tree;
```

**Use case:** expand inherited ACL/group role di model authorization internal.

---

## 26. Performance Rules

| Pattern | Risk | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| Window function dengan `PARTITION BY` besar | Sort besar/temp file | Index sesuai partition/order, filter lebih awal. |
| Recursive CTE tanpa batas | Infinite/very expensive traversal | Cycle protection, depth limit. |
| `LATERAL` pada parent set besar | Nested loop mahal | Pastikan parent filtered dan inner indexed. |
| JSON/array expansion sebelum filter | Row explosion | Filter base rows dulu. |
| `CUBE` dengan banyak dimensi | Kombinasi grouping meledak | Batasi dimensi; gunakan grouping sets eksplisit. |
| `DISTINCT ON` tanpa order deterministik | Hasil tidak stabil | Tambah tie-breaker seperti `id DESC`. |
| Function di predicate partition/index key | Pruning/index tidak optimal | Rewrite predicate range/equality langsung. |
| CTE materialized tanpa perlu | Predicate pushdown terhambat | Cek `EXPLAIN`, gunakan `NOT MATERIALIZED` jika tepat. |

---

## 27. Common Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Menganggap window function mengurangi row seperti `GROUP BY` | Hasil lebih banyak dari perkiraan | Pahami window tidak collapse row. |
| `last_value()` tanpa frame eksplisit | Menghasilkan current row, bukan last partition | Set frame `UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING`. |
| Recursive CTE tanpa cycle check | Query tidak selesai / sangat mahal | Track path atau depth. |
| `LATERAL` dipakai tanpa index inner query | Lambat per-row lookup | Index inner table. |
| `ROLLUP` subtotal `NULL` disalahartikan | Report salah | Gunakan `GROUPING()`. |
| `NOT IN` dengan `NULL` | Hasil kosong/membingungkan | Prefer `NOT EXISTS`. |
| `generate_series` besar tanpa batas | Row explosion | Batasi range. |
| `CUBE` banyak dimension | Output combinatorial | Gunakan `GROUPING SETS` spesifik. |
| `DISTINCT ON` tidak cocok urutan | Ambil row salah | `ORDER BY` harus selaras. |
| Query analytics langsung ke OLTP hot table | Beban produksi | Materialized view/replica/partition/reporting strategy. |

---

## 28. Decision Matrix

| Need | Query Feature |
|---|---|
| Latest row per group | `row_number()` or `DISTINCT ON` |
| Previous/next state | `lag()` / `lead()` |
| Running total | Window aggregate |
| Median/p95 | Ordered-set aggregate |
| Conditional counts | Aggregate `FILTER` |
| Hierarchy traversal | Recursive CTE |
| Top N child rows per parent | `LATERAL` |
| Expand JSON object/array | `jsonb_each`, `jsonb_array_elements`, `LATERAL` |
| Fill missing dates | `generate_series` + `LEFT JOIN` |
| Multi-level subtotal | `ROLLUP` |
| All dimension combinations | `CUBE` |
| Custom subtotal levels | `GROUPING SETS` |
| Atomic update + audit insert | Data-modifying CTE |

---

## 29. Review Checklist

| Checklist | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Correctness | Apakah query menghasilkan row cardinality yang diharapkan? |
| Determinism | Apakah `ORDER BY` punya tie-breaker? |
| NULL semantics | Apakah `NULL` ditangani eksplisit? |
| Index support | Apakah predicate/order/join punya index yang masuk akal? |
| Partition pruning | Jika partitioned, apakah query membawa partition key? |
| Row explosion | Apakah JSON/array expansion dibatasi? |
| Recursive safety | Apakah ada cycle/depth guard? |
| Aggregation level | Apakah subtotal dibedakan dengan `GROUPING()`? |
| Performance evidence | Sudah cek `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)`? |
| Operational impact | Apakah query analytics aman dijalankan di OLTP primary? |

---

## 30. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Window function basics | Bisa ranking, latest row, previous/next row. |
| 2 | Window frames | Bisa running total dan moving windows dengan benar. |
| 3 | Aggregate `FILTER` | Bisa membuat conditional report ringkas. |
| 4 | `GROUPING SETS`, `ROLLUP`, `CUBE` | Bisa membuat subtotal multi-level. |
| 5 | Ordered-set aggregates | Bisa menghitung median/p95/p99 SLA. |
| 6 | Recursive CTE | Bisa traversal hierarchy dan graph sederhana. |
| 7 | `LATERAL` | Bisa top-N per parent dan per-row expansion. |
| 8 | Set-returning functions | Bisa generate date series, expand arrays/JSON. |
| 9 | Data-modifying CTE | Bisa atomic mutation + audit pattern. |
| 10 | Performance review | Bisa mencegah query advanced menjadi production hazard. |

Lanjut paling tepat: **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Full Text Search: `tsvector`, `tsquery`, dictionary, ranking, highlighting, GIN index, generated column, dan search design tanpa external search engine**.
