## Response:
7/13/2026, 1:15:49 AM

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Full Text Search

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk pencarian teks bawaan PostgreSQL:

- `tsvector`
- `tsquery`
- `to_tsvector`
- `to_tsquery`, `plainto_tsquery`, `phraseto_tsquery`, `websearch_to_tsquery`
- text search configuration
- dictionary, stemming, stop words
- ranking dengan `ts_rank` / `ts_rank_cd`
- highlighting dengan `ts_headline`
- GIN/GiST index
- generated column untuk search vector
- weighted search
- search design untuk case management, audit, document metadata, dan internal search

PostgreSQL Full Text Search berada di Chapter 12 dokumentasi SQL Language. Dokumentasi membagi topik ini ke introduction, table/index, controlling text search, additional features, parsers, dictionaries, configuration, testing/debugging, dan preferred index types. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/textsearch.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="fts-map"
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Full Text Search))
    Document
      title
      body
      metadata
      structured fields
    tsvector
      lexemes
      positions
      weights
      normalized terms
    tsquery
      terms
      boolean operators
      phrase operators
      prefix matching
      web style query
    Configuration
      parser
      dictionaries
      stop words
      stemming
      language
    Search
      @@ match
      ranking
      highlighting
      filtering
    Index
      GIN
      GiST
      expression index
      generated column
    Patterns
      case search
      audit search
      document search
      weighted search
      autocomplete-ish
      pg_trgm hybrid
```

**Core idea:** PostgreSQL Full Text Search tidak mencari string mentah seperti `LIKE '%word%'`. Ia mengubah dokumen menjadi **lexeme vector** (`tsvector`) dan query user menjadi **query expression** (`tsquery`), lalu mencocokkan keduanya dengan operator `@@`.

---

## 3. Basic Concept: Document, Lexeme, Vector, Query

| Konsep | Bentuk | Makna |
|---|---|---|
| Document | `text` atau gabungan beberapa field | Teks yang ingin dicari. |
| Token | kata mentah hasil parsing | Misalnya `running`, `cases`, `closed`. |
| Lexeme | token yang sudah dinormalisasi | Misalnya `cases` → `case`, tergantung dictionary. |
| `tsvector` | daftar lexeme + posisi + optional weight | Representasi dokumen yang searchable. |
| `tsquery` | ekspresi query | Representasi pencarian user. |
| Match operator | `@@` | Apakah vector/text cocok dengan query. |

Untuk implementasi FTS, PostgreSQL membutuhkan fungsi untuk membuat `tsvector` dari dokumen, fungsi untuk membuat `tsquery` dari input user, fungsi ranking untuk mengurutkan hasil, dan fungsi display/highlight agar hasil mudah dibaca. PostgreSQL menyediakan semua fungsi tersebut. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/textsearch-controls.html))

---

## 4. `to_tsvector`: Dokumen → Search Vector

```sql id="to-tsvector-basic"
SELECT to_tsvector(
    'english',
    'A closed case was escalated after repeated violations.'
);
```

Contoh hasil kira-kira:

```text id="to-tsvector-result"
'case':3 'close':2 'escal':5 'repeat':7 'violat':8
```

`to_tsvector` memecah teks menjadi token, mengurangi token menjadi lexeme, membuang stop words, dan mengembalikan `tsvector` yang berisi lexeme beserta posisi dalam dokumen. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL memberi contoh bahwa kata seperti `a`, `on`, dan `it` dapat dibuang sebagai stop words, sedangkan bentuk plural seperti `rats` dapat dinormalisasi menjadi `rat`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/textsearch-controls.html))

### Dengan bahasa/config berbeda

```sql id="to-tsvector-config"
SELECT to_tsvector('simple', 'closed cases escalated');

SELECT to_tsvector('english', 'closed cases escalated');
```

| Config | Efek Umum |
|---|---|
| `simple` | Minimal stemming; lebih literal. |
| `english` | Stop words dan stemming bahasa Inggris. |
| custom config | Bisa mengatur parser/dictionary sendiri. |

**Rule:** pilih text search configuration sesuai bahasa dokumen. Untuk data multilingual, satu config global sering tidak cukup.

---

## 5. `tsquery`: User Query → Search Expression

PostgreSQL menyediakan beberapa fungsi untuk membuat `tsquery`.

| Function | Input Style | Cocok Untuk |
|---|---|---|
| `to_tsquery` | Syntax tsquery eksplisit | Advanced/internal query. |
| `plainto_tsquery` | Plain words | Search box sederhana. |
| `phraseto_tsquery` | Phrase-like input | Pencarian frasa. |
| `websearch_to_tsquery` | Web-search style | Search box user-facing. |

### `to_tsquery`

```sql id="to-tsquery"
SELECT to_tsquery('english', 'case & escalat');
```

`to_tsquery` cocok jika input sudah memakai operator tsquery seperti `&`, `|`, `!`, `<->`.

### `plainto_tsquery`

```sql id="plainto-tsquery"
SELECT plainto_tsquery('english', 'closed escalated case');
```

Cocok untuk input user polos. Biasanya kata-kata dianggap harus match bersama.

### `phraseto_tsquery`

```sql id="phraseto-tsquery"
SELECT phraseto_tsquery('english', 'closed case');
```

Cocok jika urutan kata penting.

### `websearch_to_tsquery`

```sql id="websearch-tsquery"
SELECT websearch_to_tsquery('english', '"closed case" OR escalation -draft');
```

Cocok untuk search box yang ingin terasa seperti mesin pencari biasa.

---

## 6. Operator Utama

PostgreSQL menyediakan operator text search khusus. Yang paling penting adalah `@@`, yaitu match antara `tsvector` dan `tsquery`. Dokumentasi fungsi/operator text search menjelaskan bahwa `tsvector @@ tsquery` atau `tsquery @@ tsvector` menghasilkan boolean apakah vector cocok dengan query. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-textsearch.html))

```sql id="match-operator"
SELECT
    to_tsvector('english', 'closed case escalated')
    @@
    plainto_tsquery('english', 'closed case') AS matched;
```

| Operator | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `@@` | Match `tsvector` dengan `tsquery`. |
| `&&` | AND dua `tsquery`. |
| `||` | OR dua `tsquery`. |
| `!!` | NOT / negasi query. |
| `<->` | Phrase / followed-by operator. |
| `@>` | Query containment. |
| `<@` | Query contained-by. |

Contoh:

```sql id="tsquery-operators"
SELECT to_tsquery('english', 'case') && to_tsquery('english', 'closed');

SELECT to_tsquery('english', 'case') || to_tsquery('english', 'complaint');

SELECT to_tsquery('english', 'closed') <-> to_tsquery('english', 'case');
```

---

## 7. Basic Table Search

Misal table:

```sql id="case-doc-table"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_document (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    title text NOT NULL,
    summary text,
    body text,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Query dasar:

```sql id="basic-table-search"
SELECT
    id,
    case_id,
    title
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE to_tsvector(
        'english',
        coalesce(title, '') || ' ' ||
        coalesce(summary, '') || ' ' ||
        coalesce(body, '')
      )
      @@ plainto_tsquery('english', 'escalated violation');
```

**Penting:** `to_tsvector(NULL)` menghasilkan `NULL`, sehingga PostgreSQL documentation menyarankan memakai `coalesce` ketika field bisa `NULL`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/textsearch-controls.html))

---

## 8. Generated Column untuk Search Vector

Untuk production, jangan hitung `to_tsvector(...)` di setiap query besar jika datanya sering dicari. Simpan search vector sebagai generated column.

```sql id="generated-search-vector"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_document
ADD COLUMN search_vector tsvector
GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
    setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(title, '')), 'A') ||
    setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(summary, '')), 'B') ||
    setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(body, '')), 'D')
) STORED;
```

Index:

```sql id="generated-search-index"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_document_search_vector
ON case_mgmt.case_document
USING gin (search_vector);
```

Search:

```sql id="generated-search-query"
SELECT
    id,
    case_id,
    title
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', 'escalated violation');
```

**Rule:** generated `tsvector` column cocok untuk search field gabungan yang sering dipakai dan perlu index stabil.

---

## 9. Weighted Search

`setweight` memberi bobot `A`, `B`, `C`, atau `D` pada bagian dokumen. Biasanya title lebih penting daripada body.

```sql id="weighted-vector"
SELECT
    setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(title, '')), 'A') ||
    setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(summary, '')), 'B') ||
    setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(body, '')), 'D')
FROM case_mgmt.case_document;
```

Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menjelaskan `setweight` dapat memberi label weight `A`, `B`, `C`, atau `D` pada entry `tsvector`, umumnya untuk membedakan bagian dokumen seperti title versus body; informasi ini kemudian bisa dipakai dalam ranking. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/textsearch-controls.html))

### Ranking dengan bobot

```sql id="weighted-rank"
SELECT
    id,
    title,
    ts_rank(
        ARRAY[0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 1.0],
        search_vector,
        websearch_to_tsquery('english', :q)
    ) AS rank
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', :q)
ORDER BY rank DESC, created_at DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

Array weight urutannya adalah bobot untuk `D`, `C`, `B`, `A`.

---

## 10. Ranking: `ts_rank` dan `ts_rank_cd`

PostgreSQL menyediakan ranking function untuk mengurutkan hasil search berdasarkan relevansi.

```sql id="ts-rank-basic"
SELECT
    id,
    title,
    ts_rank(search_vector, plainto_tsquery('english', 'closed case')) AS rank
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE search_vector @@ plainto_tsquery('english', 'closed case')
ORDER BY rank DESC;
```

```sql id="ts-rank-cd"
SELECT
    id,
    title,
    ts_rank_cd(search_vector, plainto_tsquery('english', 'closed case')) AS rank
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE search_vector @@ plainto_tsquery('english', 'closed case')
ORDER BY rank DESC;
```

| Function | Intuisi |
|---|---|
| `ts_rank` | Ranking berbasis frekuensi/weight match. |
| `ts_rank_cd` | Cover density ranking; cenderung mempertimbangkan kedekatan term. |

### Ranking + recency

```sql id="rank-recency"
SELECT
    id,
    title,
    ts_rank(search_vector, q.query) AS text_rank,
    created_at,
    ts_rank(search_vector, q.query)
      + 0.05 / greatest(1, extract(day FROM now() - created_at)) AS final_rank
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
CROSS JOIN websearch_to_tsquery('english', :q) AS q(query)
WHERE search_vector @@ q.query
ORDER BY final_rank DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

**Rule:** ranking PostgreSQL adalah relevansi lexical, bukan semantic relevance. Untuk ranking bisnis, gabungkan dengan recency, status, permission, atau priority jika perlu.

---

## 11. Highlighting: `ts_headline`

`ts_headline` membuat snippet teks dengan term yang match diberi marker.

```sql id="ts-headline-basic"
SELECT
    id,
    title,
    ts_headline(
        'english',
        body,
        websearch_to_tsquery('english', :q),
        'StartSel=<mark>, StopSel=</mark>, MaxFragments=2, MinWords=5, MaxWords=20'
    ) AS snippet
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', :q)
LIMIT 20;
```

**Use case:** hasil search internal yang menunjukkan konteks kenapa dokumen match.

**Caution:** `ts_headline` bekerja pada teks asli, bukan hanya `tsvector`; jangan jalankan pada body sangat besar untuk banyak row tanpa limit/ranking terlebih dahulu.

---

## 12. GIN dan GiST Index

PostgreSQL mendukung dua jenis index untuk FTS: **GIN** dan **GiST**. Index tidak wajib untuk full text search, tetapi jika column sering dicari, index biasanya diinginkan. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyatakan GIN adalah preferred text search index type; GIN menyimpan entry per lexeme dengan compressed list lokasi match, sedangkan GiST bersifat lossy dan perlu recheck row aktual untuk menghilangkan false matches. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/textsearch-indexes.html))

### GIN index

```sql id="gin-index"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_document_search_vector
ON case_mgmt.case_document
USING gin (search_vector);
```

### Expression GIN index

```sql id="expression-gin-index"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_document_search_expr
ON case_mgmt.case_document
USING gin (
    to_tsvector(
        'english',
        coalesce(title, '') || ' ' || coalesce(body, '')
    )
);
```

Query harus cocok dengan expression:

```sql id="expression-gin-query"
SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE to_tsvector(
        'english',
        coalesce(title, '') || ' ' || coalesce(body, '')
      )
      @@ plainto_tsquery('english', 'violation');
```

### GiST index

```sql id="gist-index"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_document_search_gist
ON case_mgmt.case_document
USING gist (search_vector);
```

| Index | Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|---|
| GIN | Umumnya terbaik untuk FTS lookup, precise inverted index. | Update/index maintenance bisa lebih berat. |
| GiST | Bisa lebih flexible, supports included columns, signature-based. | Lossy, false positive recheck, sering kurang cocok untuk FTS umum. |
| No index | Simple untuk table kecil. | Seq scan untuk search rutin. |

---

## 13. Search Configuration

Text search configuration menentukan parser dan dictionary chain. Efeknya besar terhadap hasil.

```sql id="config-example"
SELECT to_tsvector('english', 'violations were repeatedly reported');

SELECT to_tsvector('simple', 'violations were repeatedly reported');
```

| Area | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Stop words | Kata umum bisa dibuang. |
| Stemming | Bentuk kata dinormalisasi. |
| Dictionary | Menentukan lexeme hasil token. |
| Language | Search bahasa Inggris berbeda dari bahasa lain. |
| Custom config | Bisa menyesuaikan domain-specific vocabulary. |

**Rule:** untuk data campuran Indonesia/Inggris, `english` bisa buruk untuk kata Indonesia. `simple` lebih predictable tetapi tidak punya stemming bahasa. Untuk sistem production multilingual, simpan `language_code` dan buat `tsvector` berdasarkan config yang sesuai, atau gunakan strategi terpisah per bahasa.

---

## 14. Inspect dan Debug Text Search

### Lihat parsing token

```sql id="ts-debug"
SELECT *
FROM ts_debug('english', 'Closed cases were escalated repeatedly.');
```

### Cek dictionary/config

```sql id="ts-configs"
SELECT cfgname
FROM pg_ts_config
ORDER BY cfgname;
```

### Cek dictionaries

```sql id="ts-dicts"
SELECT dictname
FROM pg_ts_dict
ORDER BY dictname;
```

### Statistik lexeme dokumen

```sql id="ts-stat"
SELECT *
FROM ts_stat(
    'SELECT search_vector FROM case_mgmt.case_document'
)
ORDER BY nentry DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

**Use case:** memahami kenapa kata tertentu tidak match, apakah stop word dibuang, apakah stemming sesuai, dan apakah vector terlalu noise-heavy.

---

## 15. Query Builder Pattern: Aman untuk User Input

Untuk search box user-facing, default aman:

```sql id="websearch-safe"
SELECT
    id,
    title,
    ts_rank(search_vector, q.query) AS rank
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
CROSS JOIN websearch_to_tsquery('english', :search_text) AS q(query)
WHERE search_vector @@ q.query
ORDER BY rank DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

Hindari membangun `to_tsquery` langsung dari input user mentah:

```sql id="bad-to-tsquery"
-- risk: user harus tahu syntax tsquery, mudah error
WHERE search_vector @@ to_tsquery('english', :raw_user_input)
```

| Input Type | Function |
|---|---|
| User search box biasa | `websearch_to_tsquery` |
| Plain text internal | `plainto_tsquery` |
| Phrase search | `phraseto_tsquery` |
| Advanced admin syntax | `to_tsquery` |

---

## 16. Prefix Search

PostgreSQL `tsquery` mendukung prefix matching dengan `:*`.

```sql id="prefix-query"
SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE search_vector @@ to_tsquery('english', 'escalat:*');
```

**Use case:** user mengetik awal kata seperti `escal...`.

**Caution:** prefix search bukan full autocomplete system. Untuk substring/fuzzy/autocomplete yang lebih toleran typo, pertimbangkan `pg_trgm`.

---

## 17. Phrase Search

```sql id="phrase-search"
SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE search_vector @@ phraseto_tsquery('english', 'closed case');
```

Atau explicit:

```sql id="phrase-explicit"
SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE search_vector @@ to_tsquery('english', 'closed <-> case');
```

Operator `<->` membentuk phrase query yang match jika dua query cocok pada lexeme berturut-turut. Dokumentasi text search operator menyebut `<->` sebagai phrase query / followed-by operator. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-textsearch.html))

---

## 18. Hybrid Search: FTS + Structured Filter

Search production biasanya tidak hanya text match; harus ada filter status, tenant, date, permission, dan visibility.

```sql id="hybrid-search"
SELECT
    d.id,
    d.case_id,
    d.title,
    ts_rank(d.search_vector, q.query) AS rank
FROM case_mgmt.case_document d
CROSS JOIN websearch_to_tsquery('english', :q) AS q(query)
WHERE d.search_vector @@ q.query
  AND d.created_at >= :from_time
  AND d.created_at <  :to_time
  AND d.visibility = 'INTERNAL'
  AND d.case_id = :case_id
ORDER BY rank DESC, d.created_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

Index strategy:

```sql id="hybrid-indexes"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_document_search_vector
ON case_mgmt.case_document
USING gin (search_vector);

CREATE INDEX idx_case_document_case_created
ON case_mgmt.case_document (case_id, created_at DESC);
```

**Rule:** GIN index menyelesaikan text match; B-tree index tetap dibutuhkan untuk structured filters yang penting.

---

## 19. Pattern: Case Search

```sql id="case-search-table"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_file_search (
    case_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL,
    title text NOT NULL,
    complainant_name text,
    subject_name text,
    status text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    search_vector tsvector GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        setweight(to_tsvector('simple', coalesce(case_number, '')), 'A') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(title, '')), 'A') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('simple', coalesce(complainant_name, '')), 'B') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('simple', coalesce(subject_name, '')), 'B')
    ) STORED
);
```

```sql id="case-search-index"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_search_vector
ON case_mgmt.case_file_search
USING gin (search_vector);

CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_search_status_created
ON case_mgmt.case_file_search (status, created_at DESC);
```

Query:

```sql id="case-search-query"
SELECT
    case_id,
    case_number,
    title,
    status,
    ts_rank(search_vector, q.query) AS rank
FROM case_mgmt.case_file_search
CROSS JOIN websearch_to_tsquery('english', :q) AS q(query)
WHERE search_vector @@ q.query
  AND status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'ESCALATED')
ORDER BY rank DESC, created_at DESC
LIMIT 25;
```

**Design note:** `case_number` dan nama orang sering lebih cocok dengan `simple` atau trigram daripada `english`, karena stemming bahasa Inggris bisa tidak relevan.

---

## 20. Pattern: Audit/Event Search

```sql id="audit-search-table"
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event_search (
    event_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    actor text,
    message text,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    search_vector tsvector GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        setweight(to_tsvector('simple', coalesce(event_type, '')), 'A') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('simple', coalesce(actor, '')), 'B') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(message, '')), 'C') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('simple', coalesce(payload::text, '')), 'D')
    ) STORED
);
```

```sql id="audit-search-index"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_search_vector
ON audit.case_event_search
USING gin (search_vector);

CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_search_case_created
ON audit.case_event_search (case_id, created_at DESC);
```

**Caution:** indexing `payload::text` bisa sangat noisy dan besar. Promote field penting dari JSONB ke column normal jika sering dicari.

---

## 21. Pattern: Document Search with Highlight

```sql id="document-search-highlight"
WITH q AS (
    SELECT websearch_to_tsquery('english', :q) AS query
)
SELECT
    d.id,
    d.title,
    ts_rank(d.search_vector, q.query) AS rank,
    ts_headline(
        'english',
        d.body,
        q.query,
        'StartSel=<mark>, StopSel=</mark>, MaxFragments=3, MinWords=5, MaxWords=25'
    ) AS snippet
FROM case_mgmt.case_document d
CROSS JOIN q
WHERE d.search_vector @@ q.query
ORDER BY rank DESC, d.created_at DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

**Rule:** compute highlight hanya setelah text match + limit/ranking jika body besar. Jika langsung highlight ribuan row, query bisa mahal.

---

## 22. Pattern: Multi-Language Search

### Option A - satu config simple

```sql id="multilang-simple"
search_vector tsvector GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
    to_tsvector('simple', coalesce(title, '') || ' ' || coalesce(body, ''))
) STORED
```

Kelebihan: predictable untuk campuran bahasa.  
Kekurangan: tidak ada stemming/stop word bahasa spesifik.

### Option B - separate columns per language

```sql id="multilang-separate"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_document
ADD COLUMN search_vector_en tsvector GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
    to_tsvector('english', coalesce(title, '') || ' ' || coalesce(body, ''))
) STORED;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_document
ADD COLUMN search_vector_simple tsvector GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
    to_tsvector('simple', coalesce(title, '') || ' ' || coalesce(body, ''))
) STORED;
```

### Option C - config driven by `language_code`

Generated column tidak selalu nyaman jika config dinamis kompleks. Bisa gunakan trigger/materialized search table jika perlu.

| Strategy | Cocok Untuk |
|---|---|
| `simple` | Data multilingual ringan, exact-ish search. |
| Per-language vector | Dokumen punya bahasa jelas. |
| Custom config | Domain vocabulary spesifik. |
| External search | Multilingual relevance, typo tolerance, semantic search kompleks. |

---

## 23. `pg_trgm`: Fuzzy/Similarity Companion

Full Text Search bagus untuk word/lexeme search. Untuk typo, substring, partial name, case number, atau similarity search, `pg_trgm` sering lebih cocok. `pg_trgm` menyediakan GiST dan GIN operator class untuk index text column agar similarity search cepat; index ini juga mendukung trigram-based index search untuk `LIKE`, `ILIKE`, regex, dan equality, walau tidak seefisien B-tree untuk equality sederhana. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgtrgm.html))

Enable extension:

```sql id="pg-trgm-enable"
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm;
```

Index:

```sql id="pg-trgm-index"
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_case_number_trgm
ON case_mgmt.case_file
USING gin (case_number gin_trgm_ops);
```

Similarity query:

```sql id="pg-trgm-query"
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    similarity(case_number, :q) AS score
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE case_number % :q
ORDER BY score DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

Substring search:

```sql id="pg-trgm-ilike"
SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE case_number ILIKE '%' || :q || '%';
```

| Need | Better Tool |
|---|---|
| Word search with stemming/ranking | Full Text Search |
| Typo tolerance | `pg_trgm` |
| Substring search | `pg_trgm` |
| Case number/name partial match | `pg_trgm` |
| Phrase/boolean lexical search | Full Text Search |
| Semantic meaning search | Usually external/vector search, not core FTS |

---

## 24. FTS vs `LIKE` vs `pg_trgm`

| Feature | FTS | `LIKE/ILIKE` | `pg_trgm` |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Word normalization | Ya | Tidak | Tidak |
| Stop words | Ya | Tidak | Tidak |
| Ranking | Ya | Tidak native | Similarity score |
| Phrase query | Ya | Basic pattern only | Tidak semantic |
| Typo tolerance | Terbatas | Tidak | Ya |
| Substring search | Tidak ideal | Ya, tapi lambat tanpa trigram | Ya |
| Index type | GIN/GiST on `tsvector` | B-tree untuk prefix tertentu | GIN/GiST trigram |
| Best use | Document/search box | Simple pattern | Fuzzy/name/code search |

---

## 25. Performance Rules

| Rule | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Simpan `tsvector` untuk dokumen yang sering dicari | Menghindari parsing ulang saat query. |
| Pakai GIN index untuk search vector | GIN adalah preferred index type untuk FTS. |
| Gabungkan FTS dengan structured filters | Search production biasanya butuh permission/status/date. |
| Jangan highlight sebelum limit | `ts_headline` bisa mahal. |
| Gunakan `coalesce` saat membangun vector | `NULL` bisa membuat vector gabungan jadi hilang. |
| Hindari indexing JSONB text penuh jika tidak perlu | Noise dan index besar. |
| Cek `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)` | Pastikan GIN index dipakai. |
| Gunakan `websearch_to_tsquery` untuk user input | Lebih aman/user-friendly. |
| Gunakan `pg_trgm` untuk typo/substring | FTS bukan fuzzy search engine. |
| Pertimbangkan partitioning untuk corpus sangat besar | FTS index per partition bisa lebih manageable. |

---

## 26. Monitoring dan Introspection

### Index size

```sql id="fts-index-size"
SELECT
    schemaname,
    tablename,
    indexname,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_relation_size(indexname::regclass)) AS index_size
FROM pg_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'case_mgmt'
  AND indexname LIKE '%search%';
```

### Index usage

```sql id="fts-index-usage"
SELECT
    schemaname,
    relname,
    indexrelname,
    idx_scan,
    idx_tup_read,
    idx_tup_fetch,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_relation_size(indexrelid)) AS index_size
FROM pg_stat_user_indexes
WHERE indexrelname LIKE '%search%'
ORDER BY pg_relation_size(indexrelid) DESC;
```

### Slow search query via `pg_stat_statements`

```sql id="fts-pgss"
SELECT
    calls,
    mean_exec_time,
    max_exec_time,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
WHERE query ILIKE '%@@%'
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Explain search

```sql id="fts-explain"
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT id, title
FROM case_mgmt.case_document
WHERE search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', 'closed case')
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

---

## 27. Common Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Menggunakan `LIKE '%term%'` untuk document search besar | Seq scan / lambat | FTS atau `pg_trgm` sesuai kebutuhan. |
| Memakai `to_tsquery` untuk input user mentah | Syntax error / UX buruk | `websearch_to_tsquery` atau `plainto_tsquery`. |
| Tidak memakai `coalesce` saat build vector | Field `NULL` membuat hasil tidak lengkap | `coalesce(field, '')`. |
| Search config salah bahasa | Term tidak match sesuai harapan | Pilih config sesuai bahasa/dokumen. |
| Hitung `to_tsvector` terus di query | CPU tinggi | Generated `tsvector` column + GIN index. |
| Tidak ranking hasil | Hasil terasa random | `ts_rank` / `ts_rank_cd` + business sort. |
| Ranking hanya lexical | Hasil bisnis kurang relevan | Gabungkan recency/priority/status. |
| Highlight semua row sebelum limit | Query mahal | Match/rank/limit dulu. |
| Index JSON payload mentah | Index besar/noisy | Promote field penting. |
| Mengharapkan typo tolerance dari FTS | User tidak menemukan hasil | Tambahkan `pg_trgm`. |
| GIN index dianggap cukup untuk semua filter | Structured filter tetap lambat | Tambah B-tree index sesuai filter. |
| Tidak test dengan data realistis | Ranking/index behavior misleading | Gunakan staging data volume realistis. |

---

## 28. Decision Matrix

| Problem | PostgreSQL Feature |
|---|---|
| Search dokumen teks panjang | `tsvector` + GIN + ranking |
| Search title lebih penting dari body | `setweight` |
| Search user-friendly input | `websearch_to_tsquery` |
| Search phrase | `phraseto_tsquery` atau `<->` |
| Search prefix kata | `to_tsquery('term:*')` |
| Highlight matched text | `ts_headline` |
| Search case number typo/partial | `pg_trgm` |
| Search metadata JSON kecil | Promote field atau controlled `jsonb` text vector |
| Search multilingual simple | `simple` config atau per-language vector |
| Search large audit/event corpus | Partition + per-partition GIN + structured filters |
| Need semantic/vector relevance | Pertimbangkan search/vector system lain; FTS lexical |

---

## 29. Review Checklist

| Checklist | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Search target | Field apa saja yang searchable? |
| Language | Config apa yang dipakai: `english`, `simple`, atau custom? |
| Vector storage | Expression index cukup atau butuh generated column? |
| Weight | Title/number/status/body punya bobot berbeda? |
| Query parser | User input pakai `websearch_to_tsquery` atau `plainto_tsquery`? |
| Ranking | Apakah hasil diurutkan dengan `ts_rank` + business sort? |
| Highlight | Apakah highlight hanya dilakukan setelah result dibatasi? |
| Index | Apakah GIN index ada dan dipakai? |
| Structured filters | Apakah status/date/case/permission punya index sendiri? |
| Typos | Apakah butuh `pg_trgm`? |
| Data growth | Apakah corpus perlu partitioning/retention? |
| Observability | Apakah slow search query terlihat di logs/`pg_stat_statements`? |

---

## 30. Minimal Production Pattern

```sql id="minimal-production-fts"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_document (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    title text NOT NULL,
    summary text,
    body text,
    visibility text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INTERNAL',
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    search_vector tsvector GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(title, '')), 'A') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(summary, '')), 'B') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(body, '')), 'D')
    ) STORED
);

CREATE INDEX idx_case_document_search_vector
ON case_mgmt.case_document
USING gin (search_vector);

CREATE INDEX idx_case_document_case_created
ON case_mgmt.case_document (case_id, created_at DESC);
```

```sql id="minimal-production-query"
WITH q AS (
    SELECT websearch_to_tsquery('english', :search_text) AS query
)
SELECT
    d.id,
    d.case_id,
    d.title,
    ts_rank(d.search_vector, q.query) AS rank,
    ts_headline(
        'english',
        coalesce(d.summary, d.body, d.title),
        q.query,
        'StartSel=<mark>, StopSel=</mark>, MaxFragments=2'
    ) AS snippet
FROM case_mgmt.case_document d
CROSS JOIN q
WHERE d.search_vector @@ q.query
  AND d.visibility = 'INTERNAL'
  AND d.case_id = :case_id
ORDER BY rank DESC, d.created_at DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

---

## 31. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `tsvector` dan `tsquery` | Paham representasi dokumen dan query. |
| 2 | `to_tsvector`, `plainto_tsquery`, `websearch_to_tsquery` | Bisa membuat search dasar yang aman untuk user input. |
| 3 | Match operator `@@` | Bisa menjalankan pencarian lexical. |
| 4 | Weighted vector | Bisa memberi prioritas title/body/metadata. |
| 5 | GIN index | Bisa membuat search scalable. |
| 6 | Ranking | Bisa mengurutkan hasil berdasarkan relevansi. |
| 7 | Highlighting | Bisa membuat snippet hasil search. |
| 8 | Configuration/dictionary | Bisa debug stemming, stop word, bahasa. |
| 9 | `pg_trgm` hybrid | Bisa menangani typo/substring/name/code search. |
| 10 | Production search design | Bisa menggabungkan FTS, structured filters, ranking, permissions, dan observability. |

Lanjut paling tepat: **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL JSONB Advanced Patterns: JSONPath, Operators, Indexing Strategy, Generated Columns from JSONB, Validation, Schema Governance, dan Anti-Pattern Semi-Structured Data**.

---

**Sources:**

- [PostgreSQL: Documentation: 18: Chapter 12. Full Text Search](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/textsearch.html)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 1:18:35 AM

Saya lanjutkan ke JSONB advanced. Fokusnya: operator/query JSONB, JSONPath, indexing, generated columns, validasi, governance schema semi-terstruktur, dan anti-pattern production.

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL JSONB Advanced Patterns

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk penggunaan `jsonb` tingkat lanjut:

- `json` vs `jsonb`
- JSON operators
- JSONPath
- `jsonb_path_exists`, `jsonb_path_query`, `jsonb_path_query_array`
- `JSON_TABLE`
- GIN index: `jsonb_ops` vs `jsonb_path_ops`
- expression index
- generated columns dari JSONB
- validation dengan `CHECK`
- schema governance semi-structured data
- anti-pattern JSONB di production

PostgreSQL mendukung tipe `json` dan `jsonb`; `jsonb` adalah bentuk binary yang diproses lebih lanjut sehingga umumnya lebih cocok untuk indexing dan query, sedangkan `json` lebih menjaga teks input asli. Untuk workload query/search, `jsonb` hampir selalu menjadi default pilihan. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL current juga menjelaskan detail operator class GIN untuk `jsonb`, termasuk `jsonb_ops` dan `jsonb_path_ops`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/datatype-json.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL JSONB Advanced))
    Storage Choice
      json
      jsonb
      structured columns
      hybrid model
    Operators
      extraction
      containment
      existence
      path access
      mutation
    JSONPath
      jsonpath
      predicates
      variables
      path query
      path exists
    Indexing
      GIN jsonb_ops
      GIN jsonb_path_ops
      expression index
      partial index
      generated column
    Validation
      jsonb_typeof
      CHECK
      required keys
      type checks
      enum-like checks
    Governance
      promote important fields
      payload version
      schema contract
      migration strategy
    Patterns
      webhook payload
      audit context
      event metadata
      flexible attributes
      search projection
```

**Core idea:** `jsonb` bukan tempat untuk “semua column yang belum sempat didesain”. Gunakan `jsonb` untuk data yang memang semi-terstruktur, bervariasi, jarang dipakai sebagai join key, atau perlu disimpan sebagai payload fleksibel. Field yang sering difilter, di-join, diurutkan, divalidasi, atau menjadi invariant bisnis biasanya lebih sehat dipromosikan menjadi column biasa.

---

## 3. `json` vs `jsonb`

| Aspek | `json` | `jsonb` |
|---|---|---|
| Storage | Menyimpan teks JSON input | Menyimpan bentuk binary terurai |
| Whitespace/order key | Lebih menjaga input asli | Tidak menjaga whitespace dan order object key secara semantik |
| Duplicate key | Lebih dekat ke input asli | Biasanya nilai terakhir yang efektif |
| Query operators | Ada, tapi lebih terbatas untuk indexing | Kaya operator dan indexing |
| Indexing | Tidak sekuat `jsonb` | GIN/B-tree/hash/operator class |
| Use case | Menyimpan raw payload persis | Queryable semi-structured data |

**Rule:** pakai `jsonb` untuk aplikasi yang perlu query payload. Pakai `json` hanya jika alasan utamanya adalah menyimpan dokumen JSON sebagai teks input yang lebih faithful.

---

## 4. Table Contoh

```sql
CREATE TABLE integration.inbound_event (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    source_system text NOT NULL,
    external_event_id text NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    received_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT inbound_event_payload_object
        CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object')
);
```

`jsonb_typeof` mengembalikan tipe top-level JSON sebagai teks seperti `object`, `array`, `string`, `number`, `boolean`, atau `null`; ini berguna untuk constraint dasar agar payload tidak berisi bentuk yang salah, misalnya array padahal contract mengharuskan object. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-json.html))

---

## 5. Operator Extraction

| Operator | Return | Contoh | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| `->` | `json/jsonb` | `payload -> 'customer'` | Ambil object/array/value sebagai JSON. |
| `->>` | `text` | `payload ->> 'status'` | Ambil scalar sebagai text. |
| `#>` | `json/jsonb` | `payload #> '{customer,address}'` | Path extraction sebagai JSON. |
| `#>>` | `text` | `payload #>> '{customer,address,city}'` | Path extraction sebagai text. |

```sql
SELECT
    id,
    payload -> 'customer' AS customer_json,
    payload ->> 'status' AS status_text,
    payload #>> '{customer,address,city}' AS city
FROM integration.inbound_event;
```

### Casting hasil extraction

```sql
SELECT
    id,
    (payload ->> 'amount')::numeric(18,2) AS amount,
    (payload ->> 'occurredAt')::timestamptz AS occurred_at
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload ? 'amount';
```

**Pitfall:** cast bisa gagal jika value tidak sesuai format. Untuk production, validasi dengan `CHECK`, generated column, staging table, atau data cleansing sebelum cast massal.

---

## 6. Existence Operators

| Operator | Arti |
|---|---|
| `?` | Apakah top-level key/string element ada? |
| `?|` | Apakah salah satu key dari array ada? |
| `?&` | Apakah semua key dari array ada? |

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload ? 'customerId';
```

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload ?| ARRAY['customerId', 'accountId'];
```

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload ?& ARRAY['status', 'occurredAt'];
```

Operator existence seperti `?`, `?|`, dan `?&` didukung oleh GIN operator class default `jsonb_ops`, tetapi tidak oleh `jsonb_path_ops`; ini penting saat memilih index. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/gin.html))

---

## 7. Containment: `@>` dan `<@`

Containment adalah operator paling penting untuk query `jsonb`.

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload @> '{"status": "PENDING"}'::jsonb;
```

Nested containment:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload @> '{"customer": {"segment": "ENTERPRISE"}}'::jsonb;
```

Array containment:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload @> '{"tags": ["vip"]}'::jsonb;
```

| Pattern | Cocok? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| `payload @> '{"status":"PENDING"}'` | Ya | Sangat cocok dengan GIN. |
| `payload ->> 'status' = 'PENDING'` | Bisa | Biasanya butuh expression index B-tree jika sering. |
| `payload @> '{"customer":{"id":"C001"}}'` | Ya | Cocok untuk nested object. |
| `payload ? 'status'` | Ya dengan `jsonb_ops` | Top-level existence. |

---

## 8. JSONB Mutation Functions

JSONB immutable secara nilai; update berarti membuat value baru.

### `jsonb_set`

```sql
UPDATE integration.inbound_event
SET payload = jsonb_set(
    payload,
    '{processing,status}',
    '"PROCESSED"'::jsonb,
    true
)
WHERE id = 1001;
```

### `jsonb_insert`

```sql
UPDATE integration.inbound_event
SET payload = jsonb_insert(
    payload,
    '{tags,0}',
    '"priority"'::jsonb
)
WHERE id = 1001;
```

### Delete key

```sql
UPDATE integration.inbound_event
SET payload = payload - 'debug'
WHERE payload ? 'debug';
```

### Delete nested path

```sql
UPDATE integration.inbound_event
SET payload = payload #- '{internal,tempToken}'
WHERE payload #> '{internal,tempToken}' IS NOT NULL;
```

**Rule:** update JSONB besar bisa mahal karena row baru ditulis ulang secara MVCC. Jangan sering mutate field kecil di dalam JSONB besar jika field itu sebenarnya state operasional penting.

---

## 9. JSONPath Dasar

PostgreSQL mendukung SQL/JSON path language melalui tipe `jsonpath`. Path expression memakai `$` sebagai context item, lalu accessor seperti `.key`, `[*]`, atau filter predicate. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menjelaskan JSONPath sebagai bahasa path mirip XPath untuk mengambil item dari JSON value, dan path expression selalu mulai dari `$`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-json.html))

```sql
SELECT jsonb_path_query(
    '{
       "items": [
         {"sku": "A1", "qty": 2},
         {"sku": "B2", "qty": 5}
       ]
     }'::jsonb,
    '$.items[*] ? (@.qty > 3)'
);
```

Hasil:

```text
{"sku": "B2", "qty": 5}
```

---

## 10. JSONPath Functions

| Function | Return | Use Case |
|---|---|---|
| `jsonb_path_exists` | boolean | Cek apakah ada item match path. |
| `jsonb_path_match` | boolean / null | Cek predicate JSONPath yang menghasilkan boolean tunggal. |
| `jsonb_path_query` | setof jsonb | Ambil semua item yang match. |
| `jsonb_path_query_array` | jsonb array | Ambil semua match sebagai array. |
| `jsonb_path_query_first` | jsonb | Ambil match pertama. |

Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menjelaskan bahwa `jsonb_path_query` mengembalikan semua item JSON yang dipilih oleh path, `jsonb_path_query_array` mengembalikan hasil sebagai JSON array, dan `jsonb_path_query_first` mengembalikan item pertama atau `NULL` jika tidak ada hasil. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-json.html))

### Exists

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE jsonb_path_exists(
    payload,
    '$.items[*] ? (@.qty > 10)'
);
```

### Query array

```sql
SELECT
    id,
    jsonb_path_query_array(payload, '$.items[*].sku') AS skus
FROM integration.inbound_event;
```

### Variables

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE jsonb_path_exists(
    payload,
    '$.items[*] ? (@.qty >= $minQty)',
    jsonb_build_object('minQty', 10)
);
```

---

## 11. JSON Operators vs JSONPath

| Need | Operator Biasa | JSONPath |
|---|---|---|
| Ambil top-level key | `payload -> 'key'` | Bisa, tapi overkill |
| Cek containment sederhana | `@>` | Bisa |
| Cek key exists | `?` | Bisa |
| Filter array nested by condition | Sulit | Cocok |
| Ambil banyak item nested | `jsonb_array_elements` + SQL | Cocok |
| Predicate kompleks di dokumen | Rumit | Cocok |
| Index-friendly containment | Sangat baik | Bisa dengan `@?`/`@@` tergantung pattern/index |

**Rule:** gunakan operator biasa untuk query sederhana dan index-friendly. Gunakan JSONPath saat struktur nested/array membutuhkan predicate yang lebih ekspresif.

---

## 12. `JSON_TABLE`

PostgreSQL current mendukung `JSON_TABLE`, yang membuat hasil JSONPath menjadi bentuk relasional dengan kolom. Dokumentasi menjelaskan bahwa `JSON_TABLE` menggunakan JSON path expression untuk memilih row pattern, lalu `COLUMNS` mendefinisikan schema output. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-json.html))

```sql
SELECT jt.*
FROM integration.inbound_event e,
JSON_TABLE(
    e.payload,
    '$.items[*]'
    COLUMNS (
        sku text PATH '$.sku',
        qty integer PATH '$.qty',
        price numeric PATH '$.price'
    )
) AS jt;
```

**Use case:** eksplode payload JSON menjadi rows dan columns untuk reporting, validation, atau staging transformation.

| Kapan cocok | Kapan hati-hati |
|---|---|
| Transform JSON array ke relational rows | Payload besar dan row explosion |
| Extract banyak field sekaligus | Query OLTP latency-sensitive |
| Staging import pipeline | Schema JSON tidak stabil |
| Report dari nested JSON | Butuh index/filter sebelum expand |

---

## 13. GIN Index untuk JSONB

### Default `jsonb_ops`

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_payload_gin
ON integration.inbound_event
USING gin (payload);
```

`jsonb_ops` adalah default operator class. Ia mendukung operator `@>`, `@?`, `@@`, `?`, `?|`, dan `?&`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/gin.html))

### `jsonb_path_ops`

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_payload_path_gin
ON integration.inbound_event
USING gin (payload jsonb_path_ops);
```

`jsonb_path_ops` mendukung lebih sedikit operator, yaitu `@>`, `@?`, dan `@@`, tetapi dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyatakan operator class ini biasanya jauh lebih kecil dan lebih spesifik untuk pencarian containment/path tertentu dibanding default `jsonb_ops`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/datatype-json.html))

| Operator Class | Mendukung | Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|---|---|
| `jsonb_ops` | `@>`, `@?`, `@@`, `?`, `?|`, `?&` | Fleksibel | Index bisa lebih besar |
| `jsonb_path_ops` | `@>`, `@?`, `@@` | Biasanya lebih kecil/cepat untuk containment/path | Tidak support key existence `?` |

### Decision

| Query Dominan | Index |
|---|---|
| Banyak `payload ? 'key'` | `jsonb_ops` |
| Banyak `payload @> ...` | `jsonb_path_ops` bisa lebih baik |
| Banyak mixed operator | `jsonb_ops` |
| Hanya beberapa key spesifik sering difilter | Expression index / generated column |
| Query equality/range pada scalar extracted | B-tree expression/generated column |

---

## 14. Expression Index pada JSONB

Jika query sering mengambil satu scalar key, B-tree expression index sering lebih tepat daripada GIN besar.

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_status_expr
ON integration.inbound_event ((payload ->> 'status'));
```

Query:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload ->> 'status' = 'PENDING';
```

Untuk numeric:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_amount_expr
ON integration.inbound_event (((payload ->> 'amount')::numeric));
```

Query:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE (payload ->> 'amount')::numeric >= 100000;
```

**Caution:** expression index harus cocok dengan expression query. Jika cast/query expression berbeda, planner mungkin tidak bisa memakai index.

---

## 15. Partial Index untuk JSONB

Jika hanya subset row penting:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_pending_created
ON integration.inbound_event (received_at DESC)
WHERE payload @> '{"status": "PENDING"}'::jsonb;
```

Query:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload @> '{"status": "PENDING"}'::jsonb
ORDER BY received_at DESC
LIMIT 100;
```

Partial expression index:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_customer_id
ON integration.inbound_event ((payload ->> 'customerId'))
WHERE payload ? 'customerId';
```

**Rule:** partial index cocok jika predicate sangat sering dipakai dan subset-nya kecil/stabil.

---

## 16. Generated Columns dari JSONB

Generated column adalah column yang selalu dihitung dari column lain. PostgreSQL current mendukung generated column **stored** dan **virtual**; stored dihitung saat write dan menyimpan hasilnya, sedangkan virtual dihitung saat read. Generated expression hanya boleh memakai immutable function dan tidak boleh memakai subquery. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-generated-columns.html))

```sql
ALTER TABLE integration.inbound_event
ADD COLUMN customer_id text
GENERATED ALWAYS AS (payload ->> 'customerId') STORED;

ALTER TABLE integration.inbound_event
ADD COLUMN payload_status text
GENERATED ALWAYS AS (payload ->> 'status') STORED;
```

Index:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_customer_id
ON integration.inbound_event (customer_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_payload_status_received
ON integration.inbound_event (payload_status, received_at DESC);
```

Query:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload_status = 'PENDING'
ORDER BY received_at DESC;
```

### Generated column vs expression index

| Aspek | Generated Column | Expression Index |
|---|---|---|
| Bisa dibaca langsung | Ya | Tidak sebagai column |
| Bisa diberi privilege terpisah | Ya | Tidak |
| Query lebih readable | Ya | Sedang |
| Storage ekstra | Stored: ya | Index storage saja |
| Cocok untuk field penting | Ya | Cocok untuk optimization-only |
| Bisa jadi FK/constraint target | Lebih praktis | Tidak langsung |

PostgreSQL juga mencatat generated columns memiliki privilege terpisah dari base column, sehingga bisa mengizinkan role membaca generated column tanpa membaca base column, dengan catatan keamanan tertentu untuk virtual generated column. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-generated-columns.html))

---

## 17. Promote Field dari JSONB ke Column

Promote field jika field tersebut:

| Kondisi | Lebih Baik Jadi Column? |
|---|---:|
| Sering dipakai `WHERE` | Ya |
| Sering dipakai `JOIN` | Ya |
| Sering dipakai `ORDER BY` | Ya |
| Perlu FK | Ya |
| Perlu unique constraint | Ya |
| Perlu strict type | Ya |
| Perlu NOT NULL | Ya |
| Hanya metadata display jarang | Tidak harus |
| Struktur berbeda-beda per event type | Bisa tetap JSONB |
| Raw webhook payload | Tetap JSONB + beberapa promoted columns |

Pattern hybrid:

```sql
CREATE TABLE integration.inbound_event (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    source_system text NOT NULL,
    external_event_id text NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    customer_id text GENERATED ALWAYS AS (payload ->> 'customerId') STORED,
    payload_status text GENERATED ALWAYS AS (payload ->> 'status') STORED,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    received_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT inbound_event_payload_object
        CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object')
);
```

---

## 18. Validation dengan `CHECK`

### Top-level object

```sql
ALTER TABLE integration.inbound_event
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_payload_is_object
CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object');
```

### Required keys

```sql
ALTER TABLE integration.inbound_event
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_payload_required_keys
CHECK (payload ?& ARRAY['customerId', 'status', 'occurredAt']);
```

### Enum-like value

```sql
ALTER TABLE integration.inbound_event
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_payload_status_valid
CHECK (
    payload ->> 'status' IN ('PENDING', 'PROCESSED', 'FAILED')
);
```

### Type validation

```sql
ALTER TABLE integration.inbound_event
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_payload_customer_id_string
CHECK (
    jsonb_typeof(payload -> 'customerId') = 'string'
);
```

### Numeric validation

```sql
ALTER TABLE integration.inbound_event
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_payload_amount_number
CHECK (
    NOT (payload ? 'amount')
    OR jsonb_typeof(payload -> 'amount') = 'number'
);
```

**Rule:** `CHECK` constraint bagus untuk validasi ringan dan stabil. Untuk validasi schema JSON kompleks, pertimbangkan staging pipeline, domain-specific validation function, atau validasi di layer ingest sebelum insert.

---

## 19. JSON Schema Governance

Tambahkan versi payload agar evolusi schema eksplisit.

```sql
CREATE TABLE integration.partner_payload (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    partner_code text NOT NULL,
    payload_version integer NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    received_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT payload_is_object
        CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object'),

    CONSTRAINT payload_version_supported
        CHECK (payload_version IN (1, 2, 3))
);
```

Pattern query:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM integration.partner_payload
WHERE partner_code = 'PARTNER_A'
  AND payload_version = 2
  AND payload @> '{"status": "ACTIVE"}'::jsonb;
```

| Governance Need | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Schema berubah antar partner | `partner_code` + `payload_version` |
| Field penting lintas versi | Promote menjadi column |
| Field versi tertentu | Validasi conditional dengan `CHECK` |
| Backward compatibility | Support parser per version |
| Deprecation | Track count per version, migrate/reject old version |
| Debugging | Simpan raw payload immutable |

---

## 20. Conditional Validation by Event Type

```sql
ALTER TABLE integration.inbound_event
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_opened_payload
CHECK (
    event_type <> 'CASE_OPENED'
    OR (
        payload ?& ARRAY['caseNumber', 'openedBy']
        AND jsonb_typeof(payload -> 'caseNumber') = 'string'
        AND jsonb_typeof(payload -> 'openedBy') = 'string'
    )
);
```

```sql
ALTER TABLE integration.inbound_event
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_payment_received_payload
CHECK (
    event_type <> 'PAYMENT_RECEIVED'
    OR (
        payload ?& ARRAY['amount', 'currency']
        AND jsonb_typeof(payload -> 'amount') = 'number'
        AND payload ->> 'currency' IN ('IDR', 'USD', 'SGD')
    )
);
```

**Caution:** terlalu banyak conditional check pada JSONB bisa membuat table sulit diubah. Jika rule makin kompleks, pisahkan table per event family atau gunakan staging + validation procedure.

---

## 21. JSONB Array Query Pattern

Table:

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_file (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    attributes jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb
);
```

Payload contoh:

```json
{
  "tags": ["vip", "regulatory", "urgent"],
  "risk": {"score": 87, "level": "HIGH"}
}
```

### Contains tag

```sql
SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE attributes @> '{"tags": ["vip"]}'::jsonb;
```

### Expand tags

```sql
SELECT
    c.id,
    tag.value AS tag
FROM case_mgmt.case_file c
CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements_text(c.attributes -> 'tags') AS tag(value);
```

### Any tag with filter

```sql
SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_file c
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM jsonb_array_elements_text(c.attributes -> 'tags') AS tag(value)
    WHERE tag.value IN ('urgent', 'regulatory')
);
```

**Rule:** jika tag digunakan untuk query high-volume, pertimbangkan normalized join table atau generated/search projection. JSONB array nyaman, tetapi tidak selalu paling baik untuk relational relationship.

---

## 22. JSONB to Relational Projection

### Projection view

```sql
CREATE VIEW integration.v_inbound_event_projected AS
SELECT
    id,
    source_system,
    external_event_id,
    event_type,
    payload ->> 'customerId' AS customer_id,
    payload ->> 'status' AS payload_status,
    (payload ->> 'occurredAt')::timestamptz AS occurred_at,
    received_at
FROM integration.inbound_event;
```

### Materialized projection table pattern

```sql
CREATE TABLE integration.inbound_event_projection (
    inbound_event_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
    customer_id text,
    payload_status text,
    occurred_at timestamptz,
    source_system text NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    received_at timestamptz NOT NULL
);
```

```sql
INSERT INTO integration.inbound_event_projection (
    inbound_event_id,
    customer_id,
    payload_status,
    occurred_at,
    source_system,
    event_type,
    received_at
)
SELECT
    id,
    payload ->> 'customerId',
    payload ->> 'status',
    NULLIF(payload ->> 'occurredAt', '')::timestamptz,
    source_system,
    event_type,
    received_at
FROM integration.inbound_event;
```

| Projection Type | Kapan |
|---|---|
| View | Ringan, data kecil/sedang, transform sederhana |
| Generated column | Field penting per row, selalu sinkron |
| Materialized projection table | Reporting/search berat, transform kompleks |
| Normalized child table | Array/object menjadi relational relationship |

---

## 23. JSONB dengan Partitioning

JSONB sering muncul di event/audit table besar. Kombinasi umum:

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event (
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    PRIMARY KEY (created_at, id)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);
```

Index parent:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_case_created
ON audit.case_event (case_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_case_event_payload_gin
ON audit.case_event
USING gin (payload jsonb_path_ops);
```

**Rule:** untuk table partitioned, pastikan query membawa partition key seperti `created_at`, bukan hanya filter JSONB. GIN index membantu match payload, tetapi pruning partition mengurangi jumlah index/table yang perlu disentuh.

---

## 24. JSONB dan TOAST / Storage Cost

JSONB besar bisa masuk TOAST. Ini membuat row utama tetap lebih kecil, tetapi akses ke field besar bisa memicu I/O tambahan. Pattern yang sehat:

```sql
-- Untuk list/report ringan
SELECT id, source_system, event_type, received_at
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE received_at >= now() - interval '1 day'
ORDER BY received_at DESC
LIMIT 100;

-- Ambil payload hanya saat detail view
SELECT id, payload
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE id = 1001;
```

**Rule:** hindari `SELECT *` pada table dengan payload JSONB besar. Ambil payload hanya saat dibutuhkan.

---

## 25. JSONB Update dan MVCC Cost

```sql
UPDATE integration.inbound_event
SET payload = jsonb_set(payload, '{processing,retryCount}', '3'::jsonb, true)
WHERE id = 1001;
```

Walaupun hanya satu key berubah, PostgreSQL tetap membuat versi row baru. Pada table update-heavy, JSONB besar bisa menyebabkan:

| Efek | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Dead tuple | MVCC membuat row version lama |
| WAL besar | Perubahan JSONB dapat menghasilkan WAL besar |
| TOAST churn | Large value bisa menyebabkan write tambahan |
| Index maintenance | GIN/expression index ikut update |
| Bloat | Jika update sering dan vacuum kalah cepat |

**Design rule:** JSONB cocok untuk payload immutable atau mostly-read. Untuk state yang sering berubah, gunakan column biasa.

---

## 26. JSONB Index Strategy Matrix

| Query | Index yang Cocok |
|---|---|
| `payload @> '{"status":"PENDING"}'` | GIN `jsonb_path_ops` atau `jsonb_ops` |
| `payload ? 'customerId'` | GIN `jsonb_ops` |
| `payload ->> 'status' = 'PENDING'` | B-tree expression/generated column |
| `(payload ->> 'amount')::numeric > 1000` | B-tree expression/generated numeric column |
| `payload @? '$.items[*] ? (@.qty > 10)'` | GIN JSONB, cek `EXPLAIN` |
| Search exact nested object | GIN containment |
| Frequent sort by JSON scalar | Generated column + B-tree |
| Frequent join by JSON scalar | Promote/generated column + B-tree |
| Rare ad-hoc JSON exploration | Maybe no index or broad GIN |
| Many different JSON predicates | Broad GIN `jsonb_ops`, watch size |

---

## 27. Pattern: Webhook Inbox

```sql
CREATE TABLE integration.webhook_inbox (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    provider text NOT NULL,
    external_event_id text NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    payload_version integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    received_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    processed_at timestamptz,
    processing_status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',

    CONSTRAINT webhook_payload_object
        CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object'),

    CONSTRAINT webhook_unique_event
        UNIQUE (provider, external_event_id)
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_webhook_pending
ON integration.webhook_inbox (received_at ASC, id ASC)
WHERE processing_status = 'PENDING';

CREATE INDEX idx_webhook_payload_gin
ON integration.webhook_inbox
USING gin (payload jsonb_path_ops);
```

**Design:** operational status (`processing_status`, `processed_at`) menjadi column biasa karena sering berubah. Raw payload tetap JSONB dan sebaiknya immutable.

---

## 28. Pattern: Flexible Case Attributes

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_file (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    status text NOT NULL,
    priority integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    attributes jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,

    CONSTRAINT case_attributes_object
        CHECK (jsonb_typeof(attributes) = 'object')
);
```

Index untuk ad-hoc containment:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_attributes_gin
ON case_mgmt.case_file
USING gin (attributes);
```

Expression index untuk attribute penting:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_external_ref
ON case_mgmt.case_file ((attributes ->> 'externalRef'))
WHERE attributes ? 'externalRef';
```

**Rule:** flexible attributes boleh, tetapi jika attribute mulai jadi filter utama, promote ke column atau generated column.

---

## 29. Pattern: Audit Context

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit.case_event (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    actor text,
    context jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT context_object
        CHECK (jsonb_typeof(context) = 'object')
);
```

Contoh context:

```json
{
  "ip": "10.10.1.20",
  "userAgent": "internal-service",
  "requestId": "req-123",
  "source": "case-api"
}
```

Query:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM audit.case_event
WHERE context @> '{"source": "case-api"}'::jsonb
  AND created_at >= now() - interval '7 days';
```

**Design:** audit context cocok untuk JSONB karena field bisa berubah antar event type dan biasanya tidak semua field perlu schema ketat.

---

## 30. Pattern: JSONB Staging to Typed Table

### Staging raw

```sql
CREATE TABLE staging.raw_partner_case (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    partner_code text NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    loaded_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object')
);
```

### Target typed

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.partner_case_import (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    partner_code text NOT NULL,
    external_case_id text NOT NULL,
    case_number text NOT NULL,
    opened_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    priority integer NOT NULL,
    raw_payload jsonb NOT NULL
);
```

### Transform

```sql
INSERT INTO case_mgmt.partner_case_import (
    partner_code,
    external_case_id,
    case_number,
    opened_at,
    priority,
    raw_payload
)
SELECT
    partner_code,
    payload ->> 'externalCaseId',
    payload ->> 'caseNumber',
    (payload ->> 'openedAt')::timestamptz,
    (payload ->> 'priority')::integer,
    payload
FROM staging.raw_partner_case
WHERE payload ?& ARRAY['externalCaseId', 'caseNumber', 'openedAt', 'priority'];
```

**Rule:** untuk import/integration, staging JSONB + typed target table sering lebih aman daripada menyimpan semuanya sebagai JSONB di table final.

---

## 31. Monitoring JSONB Index dan Size

### Table/index size

```sql
SELECT
    relid::regclass AS relation,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_table_size(relid)) AS table_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_indexes_size(relid)) AS indexes_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size(relid)) AS total_size
FROM pg_statio_user_tables
WHERE relid::regclass::text LIKE 'integration.%'
ORDER BY pg_total_relation_size(relid) DESC;
```

### Index usage

```sql
SELECT
    schemaname,
    relname,
    indexrelname,
    idx_scan,
    idx_tup_read,
    idx_tup_fetch,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_relation_size(indexrelid)) AS index_size
FROM pg_stat_user_indexes
WHERE indexrelname ILIKE '%payload%'
   OR indexrelname ILIKE '%json%'
ORDER BY pg_relation_size(indexrelid) DESC;
```

### Slow JSONB queries

```sql
SELECT
    calls,
    mean_exec_time,
    max_exec_time,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
WHERE query ILIKE '%jsonb%'
   OR query ILIKE '%@>%'
   OR query ILIKE '%->>%'
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

---

## 32. `EXPLAIN` Checklist untuk JSONB Query

```sql
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT *
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload @> '{"status": "PENDING"}'::jsonb;
```

Yang dicek:

| Signal | Interpretasi |
|---|---|
| `Bitmap Index Scan` pada GIN | Index dipakai |
| `Seq Scan` besar | Index tidak ada/tidak cocok/selectivity buruk |
| Rows estimate jauh salah | Statistics/predicate sulit dipahami planner |
| Banyak heap blocks read | Data besar/buffer miss |
| Recheck banyak | Normal untuk beberapa index, tapi bisa mahal |
| Sort besar | Butuh B-tree index untuk sort/filter tambahan |
| Filter setelah index | GIN hanya bantu sebagian predicate |

---

## 33. Common Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Semua field disimpan di JSONB | Constraint, join, index, analytics sulit | Promote field penting ke column |
| JSONB dipakai untuk state sering berubah | WAL/bloat/update cost tinggi | Gunakan column biasa |
| GIN broad index dibuat tanpa kebutuhan | Index besar dan write lambat | Gunakan expression/generated/partial index |
| Pakai `jsonb_path_ops` lalu butuh `?` | Index tidak support operator | Pilih `jsonb_ops` |
| Cast JSON scalar langsung tanpa validasi | Query gagal saat data buruk | `CHECK`, staging, generated typed column |
| Query `payload ->> 'x'` tanpa expression index | Seq scan | Buat expression/generated B-tree |
| Index expression tidak sama dengan query | Index tidak dipakai | Samakan expression persis |
| `SELECT *` dari payload besar | I/O dan network besar | Ambil column spesifik |
| JSONB array untuk relational many-to-many | Integrity/query sulit | Normalize child table |
| Tidak versioning payload | Evolusi schema kacau | Tambah `payload_version` |
| GIN index dianggap menyelesaikan semua | Sort/join/range tetap lambat | Gabungkan dengan B-tree/generated columns |
| Tidak monitor index size | Storage membengkak | Review `pg_stat_user_indexes` dan relation size |

---

## 34. Decision Matrix

| Problem | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Raw webhook payload | `jsonb` immutable + operational columns |
| Query status/customer from JSON often | Generated column + B-tree |
| Ad-hoc containment search | GIN `jsonb_ops` |
| Heavy containment search only | GIN `jsonb_path_ops` |
| Need key existence `?` | GIN `jsonb_ops` |
| Need range/sort on JSON number/date | Generated typed column |
| Need validate required keys | `CHECK` with `?&` and `jsonb_typeof` |
| Need nested array filtering | JSONPath or lateral expansion |
| Need report nested JSON as rows | `JSON_TABLE` / `jsonb_array_elements` |
| Need enforce FK/unique on JSON field | Promote/generated column, then constraint |
| Need mutable workflow state | Real columns, not JSONB |
| Need flexible optional metadata | JSONB object |

---

## 35. Review Checklist

| Checklist | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Data ownership | Apakah payload raw, metadata, atau domain state? |
| Query pattern | Field JSON mana yang sering difilter/sort/join? |
| Type safety | Apakah field penting punya type validation? |
| Constraint | Apakah required key dan enum value divalidasi? |
| Index | Apakah operator query cocok dengan index operator class? |
| Generated columns | Apakah scalar penting sudah dipromosikan? |
| Payload size | Apakah payload besar dan sering `SELECT *`? |
| Update pattern | Apakah JSONB sering di-update? |
| Versioning | Apakah ada `payload_version` untuk evolusi schema? |
| Partitioning | Apakah table event besar perlu partition by time? |
| Observability | Apakah size/index usage/slow JSON query dimonitor? |
| Governance | Apakah ada rule kapan field harus keluar dari JSONB? |

---

## 36. Minimal Production Pattern

```sql
CREATE TABLE integration.inbound_event (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,

    source_system text NOT NULL,
    external_event_id text NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,

    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    payload_version integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

    customer_id text GENERATED ALWAYS AS (payload ->> 'customerId') STORED,
    payload_status text GENERATED ALWAYS AS (payload ->> 'status') STORED,

    received_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT inbound_event_unique_external
        UNIQUE (source_system, external_event_id),

    CONSTRAINT inbound_event_payload_object
        CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = 'object'),

    CONSTRAINT inbound_event_payload_required
        CHECK (payload ?& ARRAY['customerId', 'status']),

    CONSTRAINT inbound_event_payload_status_valid
        CHECK (payload ->> 'status' IN ('PENDING', 'PROCESSED', 'FAILED'))
);

CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_status_received
ON integration.inbound_event (payload_status, received_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_customer_id
ON integration.inbound_event (customer_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_inbound_event_payload_gin
ON integration.inbound_event
USING gin (payload jsonb_path_ops);
```

Query:

```sql
SELECT
    id,
    source_system,
    external_event_id,
    event_type,
    customer_id,
    payload_status,
    received_at
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE payload_status = 'PENDING'
  AND customer_id = 'CUST-001'
ORDER BY received_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

Detail payload:

```sql
SELECT payload
FROM integration.inbound_event
WHERE id = 1001;
```

---

## 37. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `json` vs `jsonb` | Bisa memilih storage type yang tepat. |
| 2 | Extraction operators | Bisa mengambil scalar/object nested. |
| 3 | Containment/existence | Bisa menulis predicate JSONB index-friendly. |
| 4 | JSONPath | Bisa query nested array/object kompleks. |
| 5 | `JSON_TABLE` / lateral expansion | Bisa mengubah JSON menjadi rows/columns. |
| 6 | GIN operator class | Bisa memilih `jsonb_ops` vs `jsonb_path_ops`. |
| 7 | Expression/generated index | Bisa mengoptimalkan scalar JSON penting. |
| 8 | Validation | Bisa enforce minimal schema dengan `CHECK`. |
| 9 | Governance | Bisa menentukan kapan field harus dipromosikan. |
| 10 | Production review | Bisa menghindari bloat, WAL besar, index salah, dan schema drift. |

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjut paling tepat: **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Extension Ecosystem: `pg_stat_statements`, `pg_trgm`, `btree_gist`, `pgcrypto`, `uuid-ossp`, FDW, Extension Governance, Security, dan Upgrade Strategy**.

---

**Sources:**

- [PostgreSQL: Documentation: 18: 8.14. JSON Types](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/datatype-json.html)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 1:22:35 AM

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Extension Ecosystem

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk extension ecosystem:

- konsep extension
- `CREATE EXTENSION`, `ALTER EXTENSION`, `DROP EXTENSION`
- trusted vs non-trusted extension
- schema placement
- extension inventory
- `pg_stat_statements`
- `pg_trgm`
- `btree_gist`
- `btree_gin`
- `pgcrypto`
- `uuid-ossp`
- `unaccent`
- `postgres_fdw`
- extension governance, security, backup/restore, dan upgrade strategy

PostgreSQL extension adalah mekanisme packaging untuk menambahkan SQL object seperti function, type, operator, index support method, atau foreign data wrapper ke database. `CREATE EXTENSION` memuat extension ke **database saat ini**, bukan otomatis ke semua database dalam cluster. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createextension.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Extension Ecosystem))
    Extension Lifecycle
      Available
      Installed
      Versioned
      Upgraded
      Dropped
    Installation
      CREATE EXTENSION
      SCHEMA
      VERSION
      CASCADE
      Trusted
      Superuser Required
    Observability
      pg_stat_statements
      pgstattuple
      pg_buffercache
      amcheck
    Search
      pg_trgm
      unaccent
      Full Text Search Support
    Indexing
      btree_gist
      btree_gin
      bloom
    Security Crypto
      pgcrypto
      uuid-ossp
    Integration
      postgres_fdw
      file_fdw
      dblink
    Governance
      Allowlist
      Ownership
      Schema Placement
      Upgrade Plan
      Backup Restore
      Managed Service Support
```

**Core idea:** extension memperluas PostgreSQL, tetapi juga memperluas **attack surface, operational dependency, upgrade complexity, dan restore dependency**. Extension harus diperlakukan seperti bagian dari database architecture, bukan sekadar command tambahan.

---

## 3. Extension Lifecycle

| State | Makna | Cara Cek |
|---|---|---|
| Available | Extension files/control script tersedia di server. | `pg_available_extensions` |
| Installed | Extension sudah dibuat dalam database tertentu. | `pg_extension` |
| Versioned | Extension punya installed version. | `pg_extension.extversion` |
| Upgradable | Ada versi baru yang bisa dipakai. | `pg_available_extension_versions` |
| Dropped | Extension dan object member-nya dihapus. | `DROP EXTENSION` |

```sql
SELECT
    name,
    default_version,
    installed_version,
    comment
FROM pg_available_extensions
ORDER BY name;
```

```sql
SELECT
    extname,
    extversion,
    extnamespace::regnamespace AS schema_name,
    extowner::regrole AS owner
FROM pg_extension
ORDER BY extname;
```

PostgreSQL menyediakan view `pg_available_extensions` dan `pg_available_extension_versions` untuk melihat extension yang tersedia untuk dimuat. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createextension.html))

---

## 4. `CREATE EXTENSION`

Syntax dasar:

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm;
```

Dengan schema eksplisit:

```sql
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS extensions;

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm
WITH SCHEMA extensions;
```

Dengan versi eksplisit:

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS hstore
WITH VERSION '1.8';
```

| Clause | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `IF NOT EXISTS` | Tidak error jika extension sudah ada, tetapi tetap harus dicek apakah extension yang ada sesuai ekspektasi. |
| `SCHEMA` | Meletakkan object extension ke schema tertentu, jika extension tersebut relocatable. |
| `VERSION` | Memilih versi extension tertentu. |
| `CASCADE` | Menginstall extension dependency yang dibutuhkan. |

`CREATE EXTENSION` menjalankan script extension, membuat object seperti function/type/operator/index support method, dan mencatat object mana saja yang menjadi member extension agar bisa di-drop sebagai satu unit. User yang menjalankan command biasanya menjadi owner extension. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createextension.html))

---

## 5. Trusted vs Non-Trusted Extension

Tidak semua extension boleh diinstall oleh non-superuser. PostgreSQL membedakan extension yang dianggap **trusted** dan extension yang membutuhkan privilege lebih tinggi. Trusted extension dapat diinstall oleh user yang punya `CREATE` privilege pada database saat ini; banyak extension lain tetap membutuhkan superuser atau privilege khusus. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/contrib.html))

| Extension | Trusted? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| `pg_trgm` | Ya | Similarity/fuzzy search berbasis trigram. |
| `pgcrypto` | Ya | Fungsi cryptographic; butuh OpenSSL saat build. |
| `btree_gist` | Ya | GiST operator class dengan behavior B-tree. |
| `btree_gin` | Ya | GIN operator class dengan behavior B-tree. |
| `uuid-ossp` | Ya | UUID generation tambahan. |
| `unaccent` | Ya | Dictionary untuk menghapus accent/diacritic. |
| `postgres_fdw` | Umumnya lebih privileged | FDW untuk akses remote PostgreSQL; setup server/mapping butuh governance ketat. |

**Rule:** buat allowlist extension per environment. Jangan memberi user biasa kemampuan install extension bebas di production.

---

## 6. Schema Placement Strategy

Default object extension sering masuk ke `public` jika schema tidak diatur. Untuk production, schema khusus sering lebih rapi.

```sql
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS extensions;

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm WITH SCHEMA extensions;
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist WITH SCHEMA extensions;
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto WITH SCHEMA extensions;
```

| Strategy | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Install di `public` | Simple, umum di banyak tutorial | `public` sering terlalu longgar; search path risk. |
| Install di `extensions` | Lebih rapi dan terkontrol | Beberapa extension tidak relocatable atau object perlu schema-qualified. |
| Install di application schema | Dekat dengan object aplikasi | Bisa mencampur ownership/dependency. |
| Install di `template1` | Otomatis tersedia untuk database baru | Bisa menyebarkan extension yang tidak semua DB butuhkan. |

PostgreSQL memungkinkan extension object diletakkan ke schema pilihan dengan `CREATE EXTENSION ... SCHEMA`, jika extension tersebut mengizinkan relokasi; extension name sendiri tetap unik database-wide dan tidak dianggap berada di schema tertentu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createextension.html))

---

## 7. Inventory dan Dependency

### List installed extensions

```sql
SELECT
    extname,
    extversion,
    extnamespace::regnamespace AS schema_name,
    extowner::regrole AS owner
FROM pg_extension
ORDER BY extname;
```

### Object anggota extension

```sql
SELECT
    e.extname,
    d.classid::regclass AS catalog,
    d.objid,
    d.objid::regprocedure AS object_if_function
FROM pg_extension e
JOIN pg_depend d
    ON d.refobjid = e.oid
WHERE e.extname = 'pg_trgm';
```

### Extensions available but not installed

```sql
SELECT
    name,
    default_version,
    comment
FROM pg_available_extensions
WHERE installed_version IS NULL
ORDER BY name;
```

### Extension versions

```sql
SELECT
    name,
    version,
    installed,
    superuser,
    trusted,
    relocatable,
    schema
FROM pg_available_extension_versions
ORDER BY name, version;
```

**Rule:** inventory extension harus masuk production review, sama seperti schema migration dan role grants.

---

## 8. `pg_stat_statements`

`pg_stat_statements` adalah extension observability paling penting untuk workload SQL. Modul ini melacak planning dan execution statistics dari SQL statement yang dijalankan server. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgstatstatements.html))

### Enable

Biasanya perlu `shared_preload_libraries`, lalu restart:

```conf
shared_preload_libraries = 'pg_stat_statements'
```

Setelah restart, di database target:

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_stat_statements;
```

Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyatakan `pg_stat_statements` harus dimuat lewat `shared_preload_libraries` karena membutuhkan shared memory tambahan, sehingga penambahan atau penghapusan module ini memerlukan restart server. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgstatstatements.html))

### Query top total time

```sql
SELECT
    calls,
    round(total_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS total_exec_ms,
    round(mean_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS mean_exec_ms,
    round(max_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS max_exec_ms,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Query top mean latency

```sql
SELECT
    calls,
    round(mean_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS mean_exec_ms,
    round(max_exec_time::numeric, 2) AS max_exec_ms,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
WHERE calls >= 10
ORDER BY mean_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Reset stats

```sql
SELECT pg_stat_statements_reset();
```

| Metric | Arti |
|---|---|
| `calls` | Jumlah eksekusi normalized query. |
| `total_exec_time` | Total waktu execution. |
| `mean_exec_time` | Rata-rata latency execution. |
| `max_exec_time` | Worst observed execution latency. |
| `rows` | Total rows yang dikembalikan/diproses. |
| `query` | Representative normalized query text. |

**Rule:** `pg_stat_statements` adalah starting point untuk performance investigation. Setelah menemukan query kandidat, lanjutkan dengan `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)`.

---

## 9. `pg_trgm`

`pg_trgm` menyediakan function/operator untuk similarity text berdasarkan trigram, plus operator class index untuk mempercepat pencarian string mirip. Extension ini trusted dan dapat diinstall oleh non-superuser yang punya `CREATE` privilege pada database. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgtrgm.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Enable

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm;
```

### Similarity search

```sql
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    similarity(case_number, 'CASE-2026-001') AS score
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE case_number % 'CASE-2026-001'
ORDER BY score DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Index untuk fuzzy/substring search

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_case_number_trgm
ON case_mgmt.case_file
USING gin (case_number gin_trgm_ops);
```

### `ILIKE` dengan trigram

```sql
SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE case_number ILIKE '%2026-001%';
```

| Use Case | Cocok |
|---|---|
| Typo-tolerant search | Ya |
| Partial case number search | Ya |
| Name search | Ya |
| `LIKE '%term%'` skala besar | Ya, dengan GIN/GiST trigram |
| Semantic meaning search | Tidak |
| Full document lexical ranking | Lebih cocok FTS |

**Rule:** gunakan Full Text Search untuk word/lexeme search, gunakan `pg_trgm` untuk typo, substring, case number, name, dan fuzzy matching.

---

## 10. `unaccent`

`unaccent` adalah text search dictionary yang menghapus accent/diacritic dari lexeme. Dictionary ini bersifat filtering dictionary, sehingga output-nya diteruskan ke dictionary berikutnya. Ini berguna untuk accent-insensitive search. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/unaccent.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Enable

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS unaccent;
```

### Function usage

```sql
SELECT unaccent('Hôtel déjà vu');
```

### Search pattern

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_person_name_unaccent_trgm
ON identity.person
USING gin (unaccent(lower(full_name)) gin_trgm_ops);
```

Query:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM identity.person
WHERE unaccent(lower(full_name)) ILIKE unaccent(lower('%jose%'));
```
