**Caution:** expression index harus cocok dengan expression di query. Jika index memakai `unaccent(lower(full_name))`, query juga harus memakai bentuk yang sama agar planner bisa memanfaatkan index.

---

## 11. `btree_gist`

`btree_gist` menyediakan GiST operator class yang meniru behavior B-tree untuk banyak tipe data umum, seperti integer, numeric, timestamp, date, text, inet, uuid, bool, dan enum. Extension ini sering dipakai untuk exclusion constraint yang mencampur equality dan range overlap. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/btree-gist.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Enable

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist;
```

### No-overlap schedule pattern

```sql
CREATE TABLE booking.room_reservation (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    room_id bigint NOT NULL,
    reserved_period tstzrange NOT NULL,

    EXCLUDE USING gist (
        room_id WITH =,
        reserved_period WITH &&
    )
);
```

| Use Case | Kenapa `btree_gist` |
|---|---|
| Prevent overlapping booking per resource | Butuh equality pada `room_id` + range overlap. |
| Validity period per entity | `entity_id =` + `valid_period &&`. |
| Assignment period no-overlap | `assignee_id =` + time range overlap. |
| Complex exclusion constraint | GiST dapat menggabungkan operator family berbeda. |

**Rule:** gunakan `btree_gist` saat butuh exclusion constraint dengan scalar equality + range operator. Untuk equality lookup biasa, B-tree normal tetap pilihan default.

---

## 12. `btree_gin`

`btree_gin` menyediakan GIN operator class dengan behavior B-tree untuk berbagai tipe scalar. Ini berguna ketika perlu multicolumn GIN index yang mencampur scalar dan tipe composite/search seperti array/jsonb/tsvector. `btree_gin` bukan pengganti B-tree biasa untuk equality/range query umum. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/btree-gin.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Enable

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gin;
```

### Example: combine scalar + array

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_label (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id bigint NOT NULL,
    labels text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
);

CREATE INDEX idx_case_label_tenant_labels_gin
ON case_mgmt.case_label
USING gin (tenant_id, labels);
```

| Use Case | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Multicolumn GIN dengan scalar + array | Ya |
| Equality lookup scalar biasa | Tidak, gunakan B-tree |
| Range sorting scalar | Tidak |
| Search composite values | Ya, jika operator/query cocok |

---

## 13. `pgcrypto`

`pgcrypto` menyediakan cryptographic functions untuk PostgreSQL. Modul ini trusted, bisa diinstall oleh non-superuser dengan `CREATE` privilege pada database, tetapi membutuhkan OpenSSL saat PostgreSQL dibangun. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgcrypto.html))

### Enable

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;
```

### Hashing

```sql
SELECT encode(digest('hello', 'sha256'), 'hex');
```

`digest()` mendukung algoritma seperti `md5`, `sha1`, `sha224`, `sha256`, `sha384`, dan `sha512`, serta algoritma lain yang didukung OpenSSL. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgcrypto.html))

### HMAC

```sql
SELECT encode(
    hmac('payload', 'secret-key', 'sha256'),
    'hex'
);
```

Dokumentasi menjelaskan `hmac()` mirip `digest()`, tetapi hash hanya bisa dihitung ulang jika key diketahui, sehingga membantu mencegah payload diubah lalu hash-nya ikut dimanipulasi. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgcrypto.html))

### Password hashing

```sql
SELECT crypt('my_password', gen_salt('bf'));
```

### Random UUID / bytes

```sql
SELECT gen_random_uuid();

SELECT gen_random_bytes(32);
```

| Use Case | Function |
|---|---|
| Hash data | `digest()` |
| Message authentication | `hmac()` |
| Password hashing | `crypt()` + `gen_salt()` |
| Random bytes | `gen_random_bytes()` |
| Random UUID | `gen_random_uuid()` |

**Security rule:** database crypto tidak otomatis membuat sistem aman. Key management, access control, logging, backup encryption, dan application threat model tetap harus dirancang. Jangan menyimpan secret key di SQL function body atau table yang bisa dibaca banyak role.

---

## 14. `uuid-ossp`

`uuid-ossp` menyediakan function untuk menghasilkan UUID dengan beberapa algoritma standar. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL current menyatakan extension ini hanya diperlukan untuk kebutuhan khusus di luar kemampuan UUID generation bawaan core PostgreSQL. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/uuid-ossp.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Enable

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";
```

### Usage

```sql
SELECT uuid_generate_v4();

SELECT uuid_generate_v1();

SELECT uuid_nil();
```

### Built-in UUID functions sebagai default modern

```sql
SELECT gen_random_uuid();

SELECT uuidv4();

SELECT uuidv7();
```

PostgreSQL 18 menyediakan built-in `uuidv7()` untuk UUID timestamp-ordered, dan dokumentasi UUID functions mencatat `uuid-ossp` sebagai penyedia tambahan untuk algoritma UUID lain. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/release-18.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Need | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Random UUID sederhana | Built-in `gen_random_uuid()` atau `uuidv4()` |
| Time-ordered UUID | Built-in `uuidv7()` |
| Algoritma khusus dari `uuid-ossp` | `"uuid-ossp"` |
| New schema design PG18+ | Prefer built-in jika cukup |

**Rule:** jangan install `uuid-ossp` hanya karena tutorial lama. Cek dulu apakah built-in UUID functions sudah memenuhi kebutuhan.

---

## 15. `postgres_fdw`

`postgres_fdw` adalah foreign data wrapper untuk mengakses data di server PostgreSQL eksternal. Dibanding `dblink`, `postgres_fdw` memberi syntax lebih transparan dan lebih standards-compliant, serta bisa memberi performa lebih baik dalam banyak kasus. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Enable

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS postgres_fdw;
```

### Create foreign server

```sql
CREATE SERVER remote_core_db
FOREIGN DATA WRAPPER postgres_fdw
OPTIONS (
    host 'remote-db.example.internal',
    port '5432',
    dbname 'coredb'
);
```

`CREATE SERVER` mendefinisikan foreign server yang biasanya berisi connection information untuk foreign data wrapper; user-specific connection information disediakan lewat user mapping. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createserver.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### User mapping

```sql
CREATE USER MAPPING FOR reporting_user
SERVER remote_core_db
OPTIONS (
    user 'remote_readonly',
    password 'change_me'
);
```

### Import schema

```sql
IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA public
LIMIT TO (customer, account)
FROM SERVER remote_core_db
INTO foreign_core;
```

### Query foreign table

```sql
SELECT *
FROM foreign_core.customer
WHERE customer_id = 'CUST-001';
```

| Use Case | Cocok |
|---|---|
| Read data remote PostgreSQL | Ya |
| Lightweight cross-db reporting | Ya |
| Migration bridge | Ya |
| OLTP tight coupling antar DB | Hati-hati |
| Distributed transaction kompleks | Hindari jika tidak benar-benar paham |
| High-volume join remote-local | Hati-hati; cek pushdown dan network cost |

**Rule:** FDW membuat remote database terasa lokal, tetapi latency, transaction semantics, pushdown, security, dan failure mode tetap remote.

---

## 16. Diagnostic / Admin Extensions Worth Knowing

| Extension | Fungsi | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `amcheck` | Verifikasi consistency B-tree/table/index tertentu | Berguna untuk corruption investigation. |
| `pgstattuple` | Melihat tuple-level stats dan bloat indikatif | Bisa mahal pada relation besar. |
| `pg_visibility` | Inspect visibility map | Berguna untuk index-only scan/vacuum investigation. |
| `pg_buffercache` | Inspect shared buffer cache | Diagnostic advanced. |
| `pg_prewarm` | Preload relation ke buffer cache / OS cache | Gunakan hati-hati, tidak mengganti tuning/query design. |
| `pg_walinspect` | Inspect WAL records | Advanced WAL debugging. |
| `pgrowlocks` | Lihat row locks pada table | Diagnostic lock advanced. |
| `pageinspect` | Inspect database page low-level | Advanced; risk interpretasi salah. |
| `pg_surgery` | Low-level relation surgery | Last resort; dokumentasi menyebut unsafe dan bisa corrupt database. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgsurgery.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |

**Rule:** diagnostic extensions bisa sangat powerful. Batasi akses, dokumentasikan use case, dan jangan menjalankan low-level tools di production tanpa alasan kuat.

---

## 17. Extension Governance Model

### Recommended allowlist

| Category | Extension | Production Justification |
|---|---|---|
| Observability | `pg_stat_statements` | Hampir selalu berguna untuk workload analysis. |
| Search | `pg_trgm`, `unaccent` | Name/code/fuzzy/accent-insensitive search. |
| Constraint/index | `btree_gist` | Exclusion constraint no-overlap. |
| Crypto/utility | `pgcrypto` | Hash/HMAC/random utilities dengan governance. |
| UUID | `uuid-ossp` | Hanya jika built-in UUID functions tidak cukup. |
| Integration | `postgres_fdw` | Cross-PostgreSQL access dengan security review. |
| Diagnostic | `amcheck`, `pgstattuple`, `pg_visibility` | Admin/DBA tooling; not app dependency. |

### Governance checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Business reason | Kenapa extension diperlukan? |
| Environment support | Tersedia di dev/test/prod/DR? |
| Managed service support | Didukung oleh provider/on-prem package? |
| Privilege | Siapa boleh `CREATE EXTENSION`? |
| Schema | Object extension diletakkan di schema mana? |
| Ownership | Owner extension siapa? |
| Security | Extension membuka file/network/crypto capability? |
| Backup/restore | Extension tersedia sebelum restore? |
| Upgrade | Ada breaking behavior atau reindex requirement? |
| Observability | Apakah extension punya storage/shared memory overhead? |
| Rollback | Bisa drop tanpa menghancurkan object dependency penting? |

---

## 18. Security Posture

| Risk | Contoh | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Privilege escalation | Extension function powerful dipakai role luas | Batasi grants dan install ownership. |
| Search path attack | Extension object di schema tidak aman | Gunakan schema trusted dan `search_path` eksplisit. |
| Data exfiltration | FDW membuka remote access | Batasi server/user mapping dan network. |
| Secret leakage | Password FDW/user mapping atau crypto key terekspos | Privilege ketat, vault/external secret management bila applicable. |
| Uncontrolled install | User dengan `CREATE` install trusted extension bebas | Revoke `CREATE` pada database/schema dari role umum. |
| Dangerous diagnostic | Low-level page/surgery tools | Admin-only, break-glass process. |

### Revoke broad create

```sql
REVOKE CREATE ON DATABASE appdb FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE CREATE ON SCHEMA public FROM PUBLIC;
```

### Dedicated extension owner

```sql
CREATE ROLE extension_owner NOLOGIN;

ALTER EXTENSION pg_trgm OWNER TO extension_owner;
ALTER EXTENSION pgcrypto OWNER TO extension_owner;
```

---

## 19. Backup / Restore Considerations

Logical dump biasanya menyertakan `CREATE EXTENSION`, tetapi restore akan gagal jika extension files tidak tersedia di target server. Physical backup membawa cluster files, tetapi target runtime tetap harus compatible dengan extension binary/library jika extension memakai native code.

| Backup/Restore Concern | Mitigasi |
|---|---|
| Extension tidak tersedia di target | Install package/extension files sebelum restore. |
| Version mismatch | Pin/test extension version. |
| Managed service tidak support extension | Cek support matrix sebelum migrasi. |
| Extension installed di `template1` tanpa disadari | Inventory source DB dan target DB. |
| FDW user mapping secret | Review dump/restore handling dan access. |
| `pg_stat_statements` missing preload | Restore schema bisa ada, tetapi module tidak aktif optimal sampai config/restart. |

### Pre-restore check

```sql
SELECT
    name,
    default_version,
    installed_version
FROM pg_available_extensions
WHERE name IN (
    'pg_stat_statements',
    'pg_trgm',
    'btree_gist',
    'pgcrypto',
    'uuid-ossp',
    'postgres_fdw',
    'unaccent'
)
ORDER BY name;
```

---

## 20. Upgrade Strategy

### Extension upgrade inventory

```sql
SELECT
    e.extname,
    e.extversion AS installed_version,
    a.default_version
FROM pg_extension e
JOIN pg_available_extensions a
    ON a.name = e.extname
ORDER BY e.extname;
```

### Upgrade extension

```sql
ALTER EXTENSION pg_trgm UPDATE;
```

Atau ke versi spesifik:

```sql
ALTER EXTENSION hstore UPDATE TO '1.8';
```

### Post-upgrade checks

```sql
SELECT
    extname,
    extversion
FROM pg_extension
ORDER BY extname;
```

PostgreSQL 18 release notes mencatat bahwa cluster yang default collation provider-nya berbeda dari libc dapat mengalami perubahan behavior pada beberapa full-text search function dan `pg_trgm`; saat upgrade dengan `pg_upgrade`, direkomendasikan reindex index terkait full-text search dan `pg_trgm` setelah upgrade. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/release-18.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

**Rule:** extension upgrade harus diuji di staging dengan data realistis. Untuk extension yang berhubungan dengan index/operator/collation/search, siapkan rencana `REINDEX`.

---

## 21. Patterns by Problem

### 21.1 Slow query investigation

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_stat_statements;
```

```sql
SELECT
    calls,
    total_exec_time,
    mean_exec_time,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

Use: workload top offenders.

---

### 21.2 Fuzzy case number / name search

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm;

CREATE INDEX idx_case_file_case_number_trgm
ON case_mgmt.case_file
USING gin (case_number gin_trgm_ops);
```

```sql
SELECT *
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE case_number % 'CASE-2026-001'
ORDER BY similarity(case_number, 'CASE-2026-001') DESC;
```

Use: typo/partial match.

---

### 21.3 No-overlap assignment period

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist;

CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_assignment_period (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id bigint NOT NULL,
    assignee_id bigint NOT NULL,
    active_period tstzrange NOT NULL,

    EXCLUDE USING gist (
        case_id WITH =,
        assignee_id WITH =,
        active_period WITH &&
    )
);
```

Use: prevent overlapping active assignment.

---

### 21.4 HMAC signature verification

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;

SELECT encode(
    hmac(payload::text, 'secret-key', 'sha256'),
    'hex'
)
FROM integration.webhook_inbox
WHERE id = 1001;
```

Use: verify payload authenticity. Key management tetap harus di luar desain SQL yang mudah terbaca banyak role.

---

### 21.5 Remote reporting with `postgres_fdw`

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS postgres_fdw;

CREATE SERVER core_readonly
FOREIGN DATA WRAPPER postgres_fdw
OPTIONS (host 'core-db', dbname 'coredb', port '5432');

CREATE USER MAPPING FOR reporting_user
SERVER core_readonly
OPTIONS (user 'reporting_ro', password 'change_me');

IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA public
LIMIT TO (case_file)
FROM SERVER core_readonly
INTO foreign_core;
```

Use: reporting/read-only bridge. Jangan jadikan ini tight OLTP dependency tanpa review.

---

## 22. Extension Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Install extension langsung di production tanpa staging | Restore/upgrade/security risk | Test dan allowlist. |
| Semua extension masuk `public` tanpa governance | Search path dan privilege sprawl | Dedicated `extensions` schema jika cocok. |
| `pg_stat_statements` dibuat tapi lupa `shared_preload_libraries` | Tidak aktif sesuai ekspektasi | Config + restart + verify. |
| `uuid-ossp` dipakai untuk random UUID padahal built-in cukup | Dependency tidak perlu | Prefer built-in `gen_random_uuid()`, `uuidv4()`, `uuidv7()` jika cukup. |
| `pg_trgm` index dibuat pada semua text column | Index bloat/write overhead | Buat hanya untuk query nyata. |
| `btree_gist` dipakai untuk equality biasa | Index kurang tepat | B-tree biasa. |
| `pgcrypto` dipakai menyimpan key di DB | Secret exposure | External key management / privilege ketat. |
| FDW dipakai untuk join besar remote-local | Latency dan plan buruk | ETL/materialized copy/logical replication. |
| Drop extension dengan `CASCADE` tanpa review | Object dependency ikut hilang | Pakai `RESTRICT`, cek dependency. |
| Extension version tidak dicatat dalam migration | Environment drift | Versioned migration/inventory. |

---

## 23. `DROP EXTENSION` Safety

```sql
DROP EXTENSION pg_trgm RESTRICT;
```

`DROP EXTENSION` menghapus extension dari database. Jika ada object yang bergantung secara eksplisit pada extension, `DROP EXTENSION` dapat menyebabkan object tersebut ikut terhapus jika memakai `CASCADE`; command membutuhkan ownership extension. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-dropextension.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Dependency check before drop

```sql
SELECT
    dependent_ns.nspname AS dependent_schema,
    dependent_view.relname AS dependent_object,
    source_ns.nspname AS source_schema,
    source_proc.proname AS source_function
FROM pg_depend d
JOIN pg_rewrite r
    ON r.oid = d.objid
JOIN pg_class dependent_view
    ON dependent_view.oid = r.ev_class
JOIN pg_namespace dependent_ns
    ON dependent_ns.oid = dependent_view.relnamespace
JOIN pg_proc source_proc
    ON source_proc.oid = d.refobjid
JOIN pg_namespace source_ns
    ON source_ns.oid = source_proc.pronamespace
WHERE source_proc.proname ILIKE '%unaccent%';
```

**Rule:** hindari `DROP EXTENSION ... CASCADE` kecuali sudah tahu object apa yang akan ikut hilang.

---

## 24. Minimal Production Extension Baseline

Untuk banyak PostgreSQL production system, baseline yang realistis:

```sql
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS extensions;

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_stat_statements;
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm WITH SCHEMA extensions;
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist WITH SCHEMA extensions;
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto WITH SCHEMA extensions;
```

Tambahan sesuai kebutuhan:

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS unaccent WITH SCHEMA extensions;
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS postgres_fdw;
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp" WITH SCHEMA extensions;
```

| Extension | Default Recommendation |
|---|---|
| `pg_stat_statements` | Hampir selalu berguna untuk observability. |
| `pg_trgm` | Install jika ada fuzzy/substring search. |
| `btree_gist` | Install jika ada exclusion constraint. |
| `pgcrypto` | Install jika butuh hash/HMAC/random utility. |
| `unaccent` | Install jika search perlu accent-insensitive. |
| `postgres_fdw` | Install hanya setelah security/network review. |
| `uuid-ossp` | Install hanya jika built-in UUID functions tidak cukup. |

---

## 25. Operational Checklist

| Area | Query / Action |
|---|---|
| Available extension | `SELECT * FROM pg_available_extensions` |
| Installed extension | `SELECT * FROM pg_extension` |
| Extension versions | `SELECT * FROM pg_available_extension_versions` |
| Shared preload | `SHOW shared_preload_libraries` |
| Extension owner | `SELECT extname, extowner::regrole FROM pg_extension` |
| Extension schema | `SELECT extname, extnamespace::regnamespace FROM pg_extension` |
| Upgrade check | Compare `extversion` vs `default_version` |
| Dependency check | `pg_depend` / `DROP ... RESTRICT` |
| Restore readiness | Verify target has extension available |
| Security review | Check who has `CREATE` on database/schema |

### Review `CREATE` privilege

```sql
SELECT
    datname,
    datacl
FROM pg_database
WHERE datname = current_database();
```

```sql
SELECT
    nspname,
    nspacl
FROM pg_namespace
WHERE nspname IN ('public', 'extensions');
```

---

## 26. Decision Matrix

| Problem | Extension / Feature |
|---|---|
| Find top slow/heavy queries | `pg_stat_statements` |
| Fuzzy search by name/case number | `pg_trgm` |
| Accent-insensitive search | `unaccent` |
| Prevent overlapping time ranges per entity | `btree_gist` + exclusion constraint |
| Hash/HMAC/random bytes | `pgcrypto` |
| Random/time-ordered UUID in PostgreSQL 18+ | Built-in UUID functions first |
| Special UUID algorithms | `uuid-ossp` |
| Query remote PostgreSQL | `postgres_fdw` |
| Cross-database ad-hoc access | `postgres_fdw` or avoid with ETL |
| Inspect bloat / tuple stats | `pgstattuple` |
| Verify index/table consistency | `amcheck` |
| Inspect visibility map | `pg_visibility` |
| Low-level page debugging | `pageinspect`, admin-only |
| Emergency corrupted tuple surgery | `pg_surgery`, last resort only |

---

## 27. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `CREATE EXTENSION` lifecycle | Paham extension per database, owner, schema, version. |
| 2 | Trusted extension model | Bisa membatasi siapa boleh install extension. |
| 3 | Extension inventory | Bisa audit installed/available versions. |
| 4 | `pg_stat_statements` | Bisa menemukan query paling mahal. |
| 5 | `pg_trgm` + `unaccent` | Bisa membangun fuzzy/accent-insensitive search. |
| 6 | `btree_gist` / `btree_gin` | Bisa memilih extension index support yang tepat. |
| 7 | `pgcrypto` / UUID | Bisa memakai crypto/UUID utility tanpa dependency berlebihan. |
| 8 | `postgres_fdw` | Bisa memahami remote table access dan risikonya. |
| 9 | Diagnostic extensions | Bisa memilih tool admin tanpa merusak production. |
| 10 | Governance & upgrade | Bisa menjaga extension tetap aman saat backup, restore, dan upgrade. |

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjut paling tepat: **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Foreign Data Wrapper / FDW Deep Dive: `postgres_fdw`, Foreign Server, User Mapping, Foreign Table, Import Foreign Schema, Pushdown, Transaction Behavior, Security, dan Performance Caveats**.

---

**Sources:**

- [PostgreSQL: Documentation: 18: CREATE EXTENSION](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createextension.html)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 1:26:42 AM

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Foreign Data Wrapper / `postgres_fdw` Deep Dive

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk Foreign Data Wrapper, khususnya `postgres_fdw`:

- konsep FDW
- `postgres_fdw`
- foreign server
- user mapping
- foreign table
- `IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA`
- read/write foreign table
- query pushdown
- cost estimation
- local statistics
- connection management
- transaction behavior
- security
- performance caveats
- operational runbook

`postgres_fdw` adalah extension PostgreSQL untuk mengakses data yang tersimpan di PostgreSQL server lain. Dibanding `dblink`, `postgres_fdw` menyediakan syntax yang lebih transparan, lebih sesuai SQL/MED, dan dalam banyak kasus dapat memberi performa lebih baik. Setup dasarnya: install extension, buat foreign server, buat user mapping, lalu buat/import foreign table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL FDW / postgres_fdw))
    Local PostgreSQL
      Extension postgres_fdw
      Foreign Server
      User Mapping
      Foreign Table
      Local Planner
    Remote PostgreSQL
      Remote Database
      Remote Schema
      Remote Table/View
      Remote User
      Remote Transaction
    Query Execution
      Pushdown
      Local Filter
      Remote SQL
      Network Transfer
      Recheck
    Operations
      Import Schema
      Analyze Foreign Table
      Monitor Connections
      Disconnect
      Explain Verbose
    Risks
      Latency
      Credentials
      Privilege Leakage
      Stale Statistics
      Partial Pushdown
      Transaction Surprise
      Cross-DB Coupling
```

**Core idea:** foreign table terlihat seperti table lokal, tetapi execution-nya tetap remote. Planner lokal harus memutuskan bagian query mana yang dikirim ke remote server dan bagian mana yang dieksekusi lokal. Jadi FDW adalah **database federation**, bukan magic local table.

---

## 3. Kapan `postgres_fdw` Cocok?

| Use Case | Cocok? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Read-only cross-database lookup kecil/sedang | Ya | Misalnya reference data dari DB lain. |
| Reporting ringan dari remote PostgreSQL | Ya | Pastikan filter dipushdown. |
| Migration bridge sementara | Ya | Berguna saat cutover bertahap. |
| Admin/ops data access antar DB | Ya | Dengan role dan mapping terbatas. |
| Cross-database ETL sederhana | Ya | Untuk volume terkendali. |
| High-throughput OLTP antar service DB | Hati-hati | Latency dan coupling tinggi. |
| Join besar remote-local | Hati-hati | Bisa transfer data besar ke lokal. |
| Pengganti replication/CDC | Tidak ideal | Logical replication/ETL sering lebih tepat. |
| Pengganti backup | Tidak | FDW bukan backup/restore strategy. |
| Distributed transaction kuat | Tidak ideal | `postgres_fdw` tidak mendukung prepare remote transaction untuk two-phase commit. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html)) |

---

## 4. Komponen Utama

| Komponen | Dibuat Dengan | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| Extension | `CREATE EXTENSION postgres_fdw` | Mengaktifkan wrapper. |
| Foreign server | `CREATE SERVER` | Menyimpan connection info remote, kecuali user/password. |
| User mapping | `CREATE USER MAPPING` | Mapping local role ke remote credential. |
| Foreign table | `CREATE FOREIGN TABLE` | Definisi table lokal yang menunjuk remote table/view. |
| Imported schema | `IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA` | Membuat foreign table definitions otomatis dari remote schema. |
| Remote user | `CREATE ROLE` di remote | Role yang benar-benar menjalankan query di remote. |

`CREATE SERVER` mendefinisikan foreign server dan biasanya menyimpan connection information untuk FDW; informasi spesifik user disimpan melalui user mapping. Membuat foreign server membutuhkan `USAGE` privilege pada foreign-data wrapper. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createserver.html))

---

## 5. Minimal Setup

### 5.1 Remote database: buat role khusus

Di remote PostgreSQL:

```sql
CREATE ROLE fdw_readonly LOGIN PASSWORD 'change_me';

GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE coredb TO fdw_readonly;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO fdw_readonly;
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.customer TO fdw_readonly;
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.account TO fdw_readonly;
```

**Rule:** remote user harus least-privilege. Jangan pakai remote superuser untuk FDW.

---

### 5.2 Local database: install extension

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS postgres_fdw;
```

---

### 5.3 Buat foreign server

```sql
CREATE SERVER coredb_server
FOREIGN DATA WRAPPER postgres_fdw
OPTIONS (
    host 'core-db.internal',
    port '5432',
    dbname 'coredb'
);
```

Pada `postgres_fdw`, connection options foreign server memakai opsi libpq, tetapi `user` dan `password` tidak boleh disimpan di server options; keduanya diletakkan di user mapping. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

---

### 5.4 Buat user mapping

```sql
CREATE USER MAPPING FOR reporting_user
SERVER coredb_server
OPTIONS (
    user 'fdw_readonly',
    password 'change_me'
);
```

`CREATE USER MAPPING` memetakan local user ke foreign server dan biasanya menyimpan remote user/password. Owner foreign server bisa membuat mapping untuk user mana pun; user biasa bisa membuat mapping untuk dirinya sendiri jika sudah punya `USAGE` pada foreign server. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createusermapping.html))

---

### 5.5 Buat schema lokal untuk foreign tables

```sql
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS foreign_core;
```

---

### 5.6 Import foreign schema

```sql
IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA public
LIMIT TO (customer, account)
FROM SERVER coredb_server
INTO foreign_core;
```

`IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA` membuat foreign table lokal yang merepresentasikan table/view di foreign server. Secara default ia mengimpor semua table dan view pada remote schema, tetapi bisa dibatasi dengan `LIMIT TO` atau `EXCEPT`. User butuh `USAGE` pada foreign server dan `CREATE` pada target schema. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-importforeignschema.html))

---

## 6. Manual `CREATE FOREIGN TABLE`

Jika tidak mau import otomatis:

```sql
CREATE FOREIGN TABLE foreign_core.customer (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    customer_number text NOT NULL,
    display_name text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL
)
SERVER coredb_server
OPTIONS (
    schema_name 'public',
    table_name 'customer'
);
```

Column lokal harus kompatibel dengan remote table. PostgreSQL docs merekomendasikan foreign table column dideklarasikan dengan tipe data dan collation yang sama dengan remote column; mismatch tipe/collation bisa menyebabkan anomali karena kondisi query dapat diinterpretasikan berbeda di remote. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

---

## 7. Read Query

```sql
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.customer_number,
    c.display_name
FROM foreign_core.customer c
WHERE c.status = 'ACTIVE'
ORDER BY c.created_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

Query ke foreign table dapat terlihat seperti query lokal biasa, tetapi PostgreSQL akan membuat remote SQL dan mengambil hasil dari remote server.

### Cek SQL yang dikirim ke remote

```sql
EXPLAIN (VERBOSE, COSTS)
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.customer_number,
    c.display_name
FROM foreign_core.customer c
WHERE c.status = 'ACTIVE'
ORDER BY c.created_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

`EXPLAIN VERBOSE` dapat dipakai untuk melihat query yang benar-benar dikirim ke remote server. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

---

## 8. Write Query

`postgres_fdw` bisa memodifikasi remote table dengan `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, `COPY`, dan `TRUNCATE`, jika remote user dalam user mapping punya privilege yang sesuai. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

```sql
INSERT INTO foreign_core.customer (
    id,
    customer_number,
    display_name,
    status,
    created_at
)
VALUES (
    1001,
    'CUST-1001',
    'Acme Corp',
    'ACTIVE',
    now()
);
```

```sql
UPDATE foreign_core.customer
SET status = 'INACTIVE'
WHERE id = 1001;
```

```sql
DELETE FROM foreign_core.customer
WHERE id = 1001;
```

### Caveat `ON CONFLICT`

`postgres_fdw` saat ini tidak mendukung `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`; `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` didukung jika tidak memakai unique index inference specification. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

---

## 9. `IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA` Options dan Caveat

```sql
IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA public
LIMIT TO (customer, account)
FROM SERVER coredb_server
INTO foreign_core
OPTIONS (
    import_default 'false',
    import_generated 'true',
    import_not_null 'true'
);
```

| Option | Default | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| `import_default` | `false` | Hati-hati: default seperti `nextval()` bisa berbeda lokal vs remote. |
| `import_generated` | `true` | Import bisa gagal jika generated expression memakai function/operator yang tidak ada lokal. |
| `import_not_null` | `true` | `NOT NULL` ikut diimpor. |
| Check constraint | Tidak otomatis | Harus dibuat manual jika benar-benar perlu. |

PostgreSQL docs menyatakan constraint selain `NOT NULL` tidak pernah otomatis diimpor, karena constraint expression bisa dievaluasi berbeda di local dan remote sehingga berisiko menghasilkan optimisasi query yang salah. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

---

## 10. Query Pushdown

`postgres_fdw` mencoba mengurangi data transfer dengan mengirim predicate/filter dan query operation tertentu ke remote server.

```sql
EXPLAIN (VERBOSE)
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.display_name
FROM foreign_core.customer c
WHERE c.status = 'ACTIVE'
  AND c.created_at >= timestamptz '2026-01-01 00:00:00+00';
```

Yang diinginkan di plan:

```text
Remote SQL: SELECT id, display_name FROM public.customer
            WHERE ((status = 'ACTIVE') AND (created_at >= ...))
```

`postgres_fdw` mengoptimalkan remote query dengan mengirim `WHERE` clause ke remote server dan tidak mengambil column yang tidak diperlukan. Tetapi clause tidak dikirim ke remote kecuali memakai type/operator/function built-in atau extension yang diset pada opsi server `extensions`, dan function/operator harus `IMMUTABLE`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

---

## 11. Join Pushdown

Join antara foreign tables pada **foreign server yang sama** bisa dikirim sebagai join remote.

```sql
EXPLAIN (VERBOSE)
SELECT
    c.customer_number,
    a.account_number
FROM foreign_core.customer c
JOIN foreign_core.account a
    ON a.customer_id = c.id
WHERE c.status = 'ACTIVE';
```

`postgres_fdw` dapat mengirim join antar foreign table pada server yang sama ke remote server, kecuali planner menilai lebih efisien mengambil row secara terpisah atau table terkait memakai user mapping berbeda. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

### Remote-local join

```sql
SELECT
    l.case_id,
    c.customer_number
FROM case_mgmt.case_file l
JOIN foreign_core.customer c
    ON c.id = l.customer_id
WHERE l.status = 'OPEN';
```

Ini sering lebih berisiko karena salah satu sisi harus dibawa ke sisi lain secara efektif. Filter local harus dipersempit dulu.

---

## 12. Aggregate, Sort, Limit Pushdown

Pattern yang biasanya ingin dipushdown:

```sql
EXPLAIN (VERBOSE)
SELECT
    status,
    count(*) AS total
FROM foreign_core.customer
GROUP BY status;
```

```sql
EXPLAIN (VERBOSE)
SELECT *
FROM foreign_core.customer
WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

**Rule:** selalu cek `EXPLAIN VERBOSE`. Jangan mengasumsikan `GROUP BY`, `ORDER BY`, atau `LIMIT` pasti dieksekusi remote dalam semua kondisi.

---

## 13. Cost Estimation

`postgres_fdw` punya opsi estimasi biaya:

```sql
ALTER SERVER coredb_server
OPTIONS (
    ADD use_remote_estimate 'true'
);
```

Atau saat create:

```sql
CREATE SERVER coredb_server
FOREIGN DATA WRAPPER postgres_fdw
OPTIONS (
    host 'core-db.internal',
    dbname 'coredb',
    port '5432',
    use_remote_estimate 'true',
    fdw_startup_cost '100',
    fdw_tuple_cost '0.2'
);
```

| Option | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `use_remote_estimate` | Jika `true`, local server meminta remote `EXPLAIN` untuk estimasi biaya. |
| `fdw_startup_cost` | Overhead setup remote query. Default `100`. |
| `fdw_tuple_cost` | Extra cost per tuple transfer. Default `0.2`. |

Jika `use_remote_estimate` `true`, `postgres_fdw` meminta remote `EXPLAIN` untuk row count dan cost, lalu menambahkan startup/tuple cost. Jika `false`, estimasi dilakukan lokal dan bisa kurang akurat kecuali ada statistik lokal pada foreign table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

---

## 14. `ANALYZE` Foreign Table

```sql
ANALYZE foreign_core.customer;
```

`ANALYZE` pada foreign table mengambil data remote untuk membangun statistik lokal. Ini bisa mengurangi overhead planning, tetapi statistik lokal bisa cepat stale jika remote table sering berubah. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

### Cek statistik

```sql
SELECT
    schemaname,
    tablename,
    attname,
    n_distinct,
    most_common_vals,
    most_common_freqs
FROM pg_stats
WHERE schemaname = 'foreign_core'
  AND tablename = 'customer';
```

| Remote Table Pattern | Strategy |
|---|---|
| Data relatif stabil | `ANALYZE` lokal bisa berguna. |
| Data sering berubah | Pertimbangkan `use_remote_estimate`. |
| Query jarang | Tidak perlu over-tune. |
| Query sering dan latency sensitif | Test `use_remote_estimate` vs local stats. |

---

## 15. Connection Management

`postgres_fdw` membuka koneksi ke foreign server saat query pertama menggunakan foreign table server tersebut. Secara default koneksi ini disimpan dan digunakan ulang dalam session yang sama. Jika beberapa user mapping dipakai, koneksi dibuat per user mapping. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

```sql
SELECT *
FROM postgres_fdw_get_connections();
```

```sql
SELECT *
FROM postgres_fdw_get_connections(true);
```

Disconnect server tertentu:

```sql
SELECT postgres_fdw_disconnect('coredb_server');
```

Disconnect semua:

```sql
SELECT postgres_fdw_disconnect_all();
```

### `keep_connections`

```sql
ALTER SERVER coredb_server
OPTIONS (ADD keep_connections 'off');
```

`keep_connections` default-nya `on`; jika `off`, koneksi ke foreign server akan dibuang di akhir transaction. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

| Setting | Kapan |
|---|---|
| `keep_connections = on` | Query FDW sering, ingin reuse koneksi. |
| `keep_connections = off` | Ingin mengurangi koneksi idle di remote, dengan biaya reconnect. |

---

## 16. Transaction Behavior

Ketika query lokal menyentuh remote table, `postgres_fdw` membuka remote transaction yang terkait dengan local transaction. Remote transaction commit/abort mengikuti commit/abort local transaction, dan savepoint juga dikelola dengan remote savepoint. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

```sql
BEGIN;

SELECT *
FROM foreign_core.customer
WHERE id = 1001;

UPDATE foreign_core.customer
SET status = 'INACTIVE'
WHERE id = 1001;

COMMIT;
```

### Isolation surprise

Jika local transaction bukan `SERIALIZABLE`, remote transaction memakai `REPEATABLE READ`; jika local transaction `SERIALIZABLE`, remote juga memakai `SERIALIZABLE`. Konsekuensinya, successive queries dalam satu local transaction bisa melihat snapshot remote yang sama, bahkan jika local transaction `READ COMMITTED`. Ini bisa mengejutkan karena berbeda dari ekspektasi `READ COMMITTED` lokal. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

**Rule:** jangan membuat transaksi FDW panjang. Remote snapshot, remote locks, dan koneksi bisa tertahan lebih lama dari yang disadari.

---

## 17. Two-Phase Commit Caveat

`postgres_fdw` saat ini tidak mendukung prepare remote transaction untuk two-phase commit. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

| Implikasi | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Tidak ada distributed atomic commit kuat lintas banyak DB | Jangan desain critical workflow seolah-olah FDW = XA transaction. |
| Kegagalan network/remote bisa membuat recovery rumit | Butuh idempotency/reconciliation. |
| Untuk integrasi reliable | Outbox, logical replication, atau ETL terkontrol sering lebih aman. |

---

## 18. Asynchronous Execution

`postgres_fdw` mendukung asynchronous execution untuk menjalankan beberapa bagian dari `Append` node secara concurrent, dikontrol oleh opsi `async_capable`. Default-nya `false`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

```sql
ALTER SERVER shard_server_1
OPTIONS (ADD async_capable 'true');

ALTER SERVER shard_server_2
OPTIONS (ADD async_capable 'true');
```

| Cocok Untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|
| Partitioned foreign tables ke beberapa server | Bisa mengurangi serial waiting. |
| Query union/append dari banyak remote | Bisa membantu jika latency dominan. |
| Banyak table pada server sama dan user mapping sama | Terbatas, karena koneksi ke server sama dijalankan sequential untuk konsistensi. |

---

## 19. Parallel Commit / Abort

Jika satu local transaction menyentuh banyak foreign server, commit/abort remote default-nya dilakukan serial. `parallel_commit` dan `parallel_abort` bisa mempercepat, tetapi juga punya risiko overhead di server dengan banyak remote transaction. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

```sql
ALTER SERVER coredb_server
OPTIONS (
    ADD parallel_commit 'true',
    ADD parallel_abort 'true'
);
```

**Rule:** aktifkan hanya setelah ada bukti bottleneck commit/abort multi-server. Jangan default-kan tanpa test.

---

## 20. Updatability dan Truncatability

Secara default, foreign table `postgres_fdw` dianggap updatable dan truncatable. Ini dapat dioverride di server atau table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

```sql
ALTER SERVER coredb_server
OPTIONS (ADD updatable 'false');
```

Per foreign table:

```sql
ALTER FOREIGN TABLE foreign_core.customer
OPTIONS (ADD updatable 'false');
```

Untuk mencegah `TRUNCATE`:

```sql
ALTER SERVER coredb_server
OPTIONS (ADD truncatable 'false');
```

| Pattern | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Reporting foreign schema | Set `updatable=false`, `truncatable=false`. |
| Migration bridge write | Boleh true, tapi role remote harus minimal. |
| Production critical remote table | Hindari write via FDW kecuali jelas ownership-nya. |

---

## 21. Security Model

### Local privileges

```sql
GRANT USAGE ON FOREIGN SERVER coredb_server TO reporting_user;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA foreign_core TO reporting_user;
GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA foreign_core TO reporting_user;
```

### Remote privileges

Remote user dalam user mapping harus punya privilege di remote object:

```sql
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.customer TO fdw_readonly;
```

### Jangan pakai `PUBLIC` user mapping

```sql
-- Hindari kecuali benar-benar paham risikonya
CREATE USER MAPPING FOR PUBLIC
SERVER coredb_server
OPTIONS (user 'remote_public', password '...');
```

### Credential caveat

Hati-hati dengan opsi `password_required=false`. PostgreSQL docs memperingatkan agar tidak mengaturnya pada role `public`, karena bisa memungkinkan user tak berprivilege mengeksploitasi hak autentikasi user OS yang menjalankan PostgreSQL dan berpotensi eskalasi privilege. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

---

## 22. SCRAM Pass-Through

`postgres_fdw` punya opsi `use_scram_passthrough` agar koneksi FDW memakai SCRAM-hashed secrets, bukan menyimpan password plaintext di catalog. Opsi ini mensyaratkan remote meminta `scram-sha-256`, incoming client session juga memakai SCRAM, dan local/remote memiliki SCRAM secret yang identik untuk user terkait. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

```sql
ALTER SERVER coredb_server
OPTIONS (ADD use_scram_passthrough 'true');
```

**Rule:** ini menarik untuk mengurangi plaintext password di catalog, tetapi setup-nya ketat. Uji di staging sebelum production.

---

## 23. Remote Execution Environment

Remote session yang dibuka `postgres_fdw` memakai `search_path` terbatas ke `pg_catalog`; parameter seperti `TimeZone` diset ke UTC, `DateStyle` ke ISO, dan `IntervalStyle` ke `postgres`. Ini mengurangi ambiguity, tetapi bisa mengejutkan jika remote view/trigger/function bergantung pada `search_path`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

**Rule:** function yang mungkin dieksekusi remote harus schema-qualified atau punya `SET search_path` eksplisit.

---

## 24. Performance Rules

| Rule | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Selalu cek `EXPLAIN VERBOSE` | Untuk memastikan filter/join/limit dipushdown. |
| Select column spesifik, jangan `SELECT *` | Mengurangi network transfer. |
| Filter remote table seawal mungkin | Mengurangi row yang dikirim ke lokal. |
| Hindari join besar remote-local | Data transfer bisa meledak. |
| Samakan tipe dan collation | Menghindari semantic anomaly dan pushdown issue. |
| Gunakan `use_remote_estimate` untuk query kompleks | Estimasi remote bisa lebih akurat, tapi ada overhead. |
| `ANALYZE` foreign table untuk stats lokal | Berguna jika remote data relatif stabil. |
| Batasi transaction panjang | Remote transaction/snapshot/koneksi tertahan. |
| Set remote index di remote server | Pushdown tetap membutuhkan index remote agar cepat. |
| Treat FDW sebagai network call | Latency, timeout, failure mode harus dipikirkan. |

---

## 25. Monitoring Query FDW

### Foreign server inventory

```sql
SELECT
    srvname,
    srvowner::regrole AS owner,
    srvfdw::regclass AS fdw_oid,
    srvoptions
FROM pg_foreign_server
ORDER BY srvname;
```

### Foreign tables

```sql
SELECT
    foreign_table_schema,
    foreign_table_name,
    foreign_server_name
FROM information_schema.foreign_tables
ORDER BY foreign_table_schema, foreign_table_name;
```

### User mappings

```sql
SELECT
    srv.srvname,
    um.umuser::regrole AS local_user,
    um.umoptions
FROM pg_user_mapping um
JOIN pg_foreign_server srv
    ON srv.oid = um.umserver
ORDER BY srv.srvname, local_user;
```

**Security note:** user mapping options bisa mengandung credential. Batasi akses catalog dan jangan expose hasil query ini sembarangan.

---

## 26. Monitoring Open FDW Connections

```sql
SELECT *
FROM postgres_fdw_get_connections(true);
```

Kolom penting:

| Column | Makna |
|---|---|
| `server_name` | Foreign server tujuan. |
| `user_name` | User mapping yang dipakai. |
| `valid` | Koneksi masih valid. |
| `used_in_xact` | Sedang dipakai dalam transaction lokal. |
| `closed` | Terdeteksi tertutup. |
| `remote_backend_pid` | PID backend remote. |

Jika `postgres_fdw_get_connections(true)` menemukan connection yang tertutup dan `used_in_xact=true`, transaction lokal sebaiknya di-rollback segera daripada dilanjutkan sampai commit gagal. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html))

---

## 27. Troubleshooting: Query Lambat

### Step 1 - `EXPLAIN VERBOSE`

```sql
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, VERBOSE, BUFFERS)
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.display_name
FROM foreign_core.customer c
WHERE c.status = 'ACTIVE'
ORDER BY c.created_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

### Step 2 - Cek remote SQL

Cari `Remote SQL` di plan.

| Temuan | Interpretasi |
|---|---|
| Predicate muncul di `Remote SQL` | Filter dipushdown. |
| Predicate tidak muncul | Filter dieksekusi lokal; data transfer bisa besar. |
| `SELECT *` remote | Query mengambil terlalu banyak column. |
| Join tidak dipushdown | Mungkin beda server/user mapping atau ada local expression. |
| Limit/order tidak dipushdown | Remote mengirim terlalu banyak row. |

### Step 3 - Cek remote index

Di remote DB:

```sql
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT id, display_name
FROM public.customer
WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

Index remote yang mungkin cocok:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_customer_status_created
ON public.customer (status, created_at DESC);
```

---

## 28. Troubleshooting: Connection / Auth Error

Checklist:

| Area | Cek |
|---|---|
| Network | Host/port reachable dari local PostgreSQL server. |
| Remote PostgreSQL | `listen_addresses`, firewall, TLS policy. |
| Remote `pg_hba.conf` | Mengizinkan koneksi dari host local DB. |
| User mapping | Remote user/password benar. |
| Local privilege | Local user punya `USAGE ON FOREIGN SERVER`. |
| Remote privilege | Remote user punya `SELECT/INSERT/...` pada remote object. |
| SSL | `sslmode`, cert, CA, hostname verification. |
| Password storage | Plain password vs SCRAM pass-through. |

---

## 29. Troubleshooting: Schema Drift

Remote schema berubah, foreign table lokal bisa stale.

### Cek foreign table definition

```sql
SELECT
    column_name,
    data_type,
    is_nullable
FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'foreign_core'
  AND table_name = 'customer'
ORDER BY ordinal_position;
```

### Re-import controlled

```sql
DROP FOREIGN TABLE foreign_core.customer;

IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA public
LIMIT TO (customer)
FROM SERVER coredb_server
INTO foreign_core;
```

**Rule:** treat remote schema changes like contract changes. Jangan biarkan foreign table silently drift.

---

## 30. Pattern: Read-Only Reference Data

```sql
CREATE SERVER refdata_server
FOREIGN DATA WRAPPER postgres_fdw
OPTIONS (
    host 'refdata-db.internal',
    port '5432',
    dbname 'refdb',
    use_remote_estimate 'true'
);

CREATE USER MAPPING FOR app_reader
SERVER refdata_server
OPTIONS (
    user 'refdata_readonly',
    password 'change_me'
);

CREATE SCHEMA foreign_refdata;

IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA public
LIMIT TO (country, currency, product_catalog)
FROM SERVER refdata_server
INTO foreign_refdata;

ALTER SERVER refdata_server
OPTIONS (
    ADD updatable 'false',
    ADD truncatable 'false'
);
```

Use case: reference lookup/reporting. Untuk hot path latency-sensitive, pertimbangkan local cached table atau replication.

---

## 31. Pattern: Migration Bridge

```text
Old DB
  ↓ FDW
New DB
  ↓
Validate data
  ↓
Backfill local tables
  ↓
Cutover
  ↓
Drop FDW dependency
```

Contoh backfill:

```sql
INSERT INTO case_mgmt.customer_snapshot (
    customer_id,
    customer_number,
    display_name,
    status,
    captured_at
)
SELECT
    id,
    customer_number,
    display_name,
    status,
    now()
FROM foreign_core.customer
WHERE status = 'ACTIVE';
```

**Rule:** migration bridge harus sementara. Setelah cutover, hapus dependency FDW jika tidak lagi diperlukan.

---

## 32. Pattern: Remote Reporting Snapshot

Daripada report berat langsung join remote-local setiap saat:

```sql
CREATE TABLE reporting.customer_daily_snapshot AS
SELECT
    c.id AS customer_id,
    c.customer_number,
    c.display_name,
    c.status,
    current_date AS snapshot_date
FROM foreign_core.customer c;
```

Refresh terkontrol:

```sql
TRUNCATE reporting.customer_daily_snapshot;

INSERT INTO reporting.customer_daily_snapshot
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.customer_number,
    c.display_name,
    c.status,
    current_date
FROM foreign_core.customer c;
```

**Rule:** untuk report berat berulang, materialize/snapshot sering lebih stabil daripada live FDW query.

---

## 33. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| FDW dipakai di request path high-QPS | Latency remote ikut latency API | Cache/snapshot/replication. |
| Join besar remote-local | Data transfer besar | Pushdown, snapshot, ETL. |
| Remote user superuser | Security blast radius besar | Remote least-privilege role. |
| `CREATE USER MAPPING FOR PUBLIC` sembarangan | Credential shared terlalu luas | Per-role mapping. |
| Menganggap FDW = distributed transaction | Inconsistency saat failure | Outbox/retry/reconciliation. |
| Tidak cek `EXPLAIN VERBOSE` | Pushdown tidak diketahui | Review remote SQL. |
| Tidak ada remote index | Pushdown tetap lambat | Index remote sesuai query. |
| Schema drift tidak dimonitor | Query gagal/hasil salah | Contract/migration sync. |
| `SELECT *` dari foreign table | Network transfer besar | Column projection. |
| Long transaction FDW | Remote snapshot/locks/koneksi tertahan | Short transaction. |
| FDW menggantikan replication untuk CDC | Lag/consistency/ownership kabur | Logical replication/CDC pipeline. |

---

## 34. Decision Matrix

| Problem | Recommended Pattern |
|---|---|
| Query small remote reference table | `postgres_fdw` read-only |
| Query remote table occasionally | `postgres_fdw` with strict filter |
| Frequent report from remote data | Snapshot/materialized local table |
| Need near-real-time copy | Logical replication, not FDW |
| Need one-time migration/backfill | FDW migration bridge |
| Need high-QPS lookup | Local copy/cache |
| Need write remote table | FDW only if ownership/transaction semantics clear |
| Need distributed workflow | Outbox/idempotency/reconciliation |
| Need remote join between two remote tables same DB | FDW can pushdown; verify with `EXPLAIN VERBOSE` |
| Need remote-local large join | Avoid; pre-stage one side |

---

## 35. Production Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Remote role | Least privilege, no superuser. |
| Local privilege | `USAGE ON FOREIGN SERVER` hanya untuk role perlu. |
| User mapping | Per-role, bukan `PUBLIC` kecuali sangat terkontrol. |
| Credentials | Rotation strategy, avoid plaintext if possible, consider SCRAM pass-through. |
| Network | TLS/firewall/pg_hba jelas. |
| Schema | Foreign table types/collations match remote. |
| Pushdown | `EXPLAIN VERBOSE` disimpan untuk query penting. |
| Remote index | Remote DB punya index untuk pushed predicates/order. |
| Statistics | `ANALYZE` foreign table atau `use_remote_estimate` dievaluasi. |
| Transactions | Tidak ada long transaction FDW. |
| Writes | Updatability/truncatability diset sesuai use case. |
| Monitoring | Foreign connections dan slow FDW queries terlihat. |
| Runbook | Auth error, schema drift, slow query, remote outage punya langkah. |
| Exit strategy | Jika FDW hanya migration bridge, kapan dihapus? |

---

## 36. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | FDW mental model | Paham foreign table tetap remote. |
| 2 | `postgres_fdw` setup | Bisa membuat extension, server, mapping, foreign table. |
| 3 | `IMPORT FOREIGN SCHEMA` | Bisa mengimpor schema remote secara terkontrol. |
| 4 | Security | Bisa desain remote role dan local mapping yang aman. |
| 5 | Pushdown | Bisa membaca `EXPLAIN VERBOSE` dan remote SQL. |
| 6 | Cost/statistics | Bisa memilih `ANALYZE` foreign table vs `use_remote_estimate`. |
| 7 | Connection/transaction behavior | Bisa menghindari long transaction dan connection surprise. |
| 8 | Performance patterns | Bisa membedakan live FDW, snapshot, cache, dan replication. |
| 9 | Troubleshooting | Bisa debug auth, schema drift, slow query, remote outage. |
| 10 | Governance | Bisa memastikan FDW tidak menjadi hidden distributed monolith. |

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjut paling tepat: **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Import/Export & Bulk Loading: `COPY`, `\copy`, CSV, staging table, validation, batch load, error isolation, transform pipeline, dan large data movement pattern**.

---

**Sources:**

- [PostgreSQL: Documentation: 18: F.38. postgres_fdw — access data stored in external PostgreSQL servers](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/postgres-fdw.html)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 1:32:10 AM

Saya lanjutkan ke import/export dan bulk loading PostgreSQL. Fokusnya: `COPY`, `\copy`, staging table, validasi, transform pipeline, error isolation, performa load besar, dan pola data movement yang aman.

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Import/Export & Bulk Loading

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk pemindahan data skala kecil sampai besar:

- `COPY`
- `\copy`
- CSV / text / binary format
- `COPY FROM STDIN` / `COPY TO STDOUT`
- `COPY FROM PROGRAM`
- staging table
- validasi data
- transform pipeline
- batch load
- error isolation
- `ON_ERROR`, `REJECT_LIMIT`
- progress monitoring
- bulk-load performance pattern
- export untuk audit/reporting/migration

PostgreSQL menyediakan `COPY` untuk memindahkan data antara table dan file sistem. `COPY TO` menyalin data table/query ke file, sedangkan `COPY FROM` memuat data dari file ke table dan menambahkan data ke row yang sudah ada. `COPY TO` juga bisa mengekspor hasil `SELECT`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="copy-map"
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Import Export Bulk Loading))
    Import
      COPY FROM
      psql copy
      STDIN
      File
      Program
      CSV
      Binary
    Export
      COPY TO
      SELECT TO
      STDOUT
      CSV HEADER
      Audit Extract
    Staging
      Raw Table
      Text Columns
      Validation
      Typed Target
      Reject Table
      Transform SQL
    Performance
      COPY over INSERT
      Single Transaction
      Drop Create Index
      Analyze After Load
      Maintenance Work Mem
      WAL Volume
    Error Handling
      ON_ERROR
      REJECT_LIMIT
      LOG_VERBOSITY
      Staging Validation
      Batch Isolation
    Operations
      Progress View
      Row Counts
      Checksums
      Constraints
      Idempotency
      Rollback
```

**Core idea:** untuk data besar, jangan langsung “insert satu-satu ke final table”. Gunakan alur **raw → staging → validate → transform → target → analyze → verify**. `COPY` adalah mekanisme load cepat; staging dan validasi adalah mekanisme keselamatan.

---

## 3. `COPY` vs `\copy`

| Aspek | `COPY` SQL | `\copy` psql |
|---|---|---|
| Dieksekusi oleh | PostgreSQL server | Client `psql` |
| File path dilihat dari | Server database | Mesin client yang menjalankan `psql` |
| Perlu file accessible oleh | OS user PostgreSQL server | User OS client |
| Cocok untuk | Server-side batch/load terkontrol | Import/export dari laptop/bastion/CI |
| Efisiensi | Bisa lebih efisien untuk file server-side | Data lewat koneksi client-server |
| Syntax | SQL command | Meta-command `psql` |

Jangan bingungkan `COPY` dengan `\copy`. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menjelaskan bahwa `\copy` menjalankan `COPY FROM STDIN` atau `COPY TO STDOUT`, lalu membaca/menulis file yang accessible dari client `psql`; sedangkan `COPY` dengan nama file membaca/menulis dari sudut pandang server PostgreSQL. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

---

## 4. Basic Import: `COPY FROM`

### CSV dengan header

```sql id="copy-from-csv"
COPY staging.raw_case_import (
    external_case_id,
    case_number,
    opened_at,
    priority,
    status
)
FROM '/data/import/cases.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
);
```

### CSV dengan delimiter custom

```sql id="copy-from-delimiter"
COPY staging.raw_case_import
FROM '/data/import/cases_pipe.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true,
    DELIMITER '|',
    QUOTE '"',
    ESCAPE '"'
);
```

### NULL marker

```sql id="copy-null"
COPY staging.raw_case_import
FROM '/data/import/cases.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true,
    NULL ''
);
```

Jika column list diberikan pada `COPY FROM`, setiap field input dipetakan sesuai urutan column list tersebut. Column table yang tidak ada di column list akan menerima default value. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

---

## 5. Basic Export: `COPY TO`

### Export table ke CSV

```sql id="copy-to-csv"
COPY case_mgmt.case_file
TO '/data/export/case_file.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
);
```

### Export hasil query

```sql id="copy-to-select"
COPY (
    SELECT
        id,
        case_number,
        status,
        created_at
    FROM case_mgmt.case_file
    WHERE created_at >= timestamptz '2026-01-01 00:00:00+00'
    ORDER BY created_at
)
TO '/data/export/cases_2026.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
);
```

`COPY TO` bisa menyalin isi plain table atau hasil `SELECT`. Untuk relation seperti partitioned table atau view, gunakan bentuk `COPY (SELECT ...) TO` agar hasil query yang diekspor sesuai kebutuhan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

---

## 6. `\copy` dari Client

### Import dari file client

```bash id="psql-copy-import"
psql -d appdb -c "\copy staging.raw_case_import FROM '/local/path/cases.csv' WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true)"
```

### Export ke file client

```bash id="psql-copy-export"
psql -d appdb -c "\copy (SELECT id, case_number, status FROM case_mgmt.case_file) TO '/local/path/cases.csv' WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true)"
```

Dalam `psql`, `\copy ... from stdin` membaca data dari input yang sama dengan command sampai menemukan baris `\.` atau EOF. Dokumentasi `psql` juga mencatat bahwa `\copy` mirip syntax SQL `COPY`, tetapi seluruh sisa baris dianggap argumen `\copy`, sehingga parsing-nya berbeda dari meta-command psql biasa. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/app-psql.html))

**Rule:** gunakan `\copy` saat file ada di client machine. Gunakan `COPY` saat file memang ada di server database dan permission/path sudah dikontrol.

---

## 7. Format: Text, CSV, Binary

| Format | Option | Cocok Untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Text | default / `FORMAT text` | Simple tab-separated internal export | Escape rules berbeda dari CSV. |
| CSV | `FORMAT csv` | Interop dengan spreadsheet, data exchange, ETL | Paling umum untuk import/export manual. |
| Binary | `FORMAT binary` | PostgreSQL-to-PostgreSQL high fidelity | Tidak portable untuk semua tooling. |

### CSV recommended baseline

```sql id="copy-csv-baseline"
COPY staging.raw_case_import
FROM '/data/import/cases.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true,
    ENCODING 'UTF8',
    NULL '',
    QUOTE '"',
    ESCAPE '"'
);
```

`COPY` mendukung opsi seperti `FORMAT`, `DELIMITER`, `NULL`, `HEADER`, `QUOTE`, `ESCAPE`, `ENCODING`, `ON_ERROR`, `REJECT_LIMIT`, dan `LOG_VERBOSITY`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

---

## 8. Server-Side File Security dan Privilege

```sql id="copy-server-file"
COPY staging.raw_case_import
FROM '/var/lib/postgresql/import/cases.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);
```

Server-side `COPY` dengan nama file membutuhkan file accessible oleh OS user yang menjalankan PostgreSQL, dan path file dilihat dari sudut pandang server, bukan client. PostgreSQL merekomendasikan nama file `COPY` ditulis sebagai absolute path; untuk `COPY TO`, absolute path bahkan enforced. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

| Risiko | Mitigasi |
|---|---|
| File path salah karena path client/server berbeda | Pakai absolute path dan pahami lokasi eksekusi. |
| Permission OS tidak cukup | Pastikan file readable/writable oleh PostgreSQL server user. |
| Data sensitif diekspor ke lokasi salah | Gunakan directory terkontrol dan audit. |
| `COPY PROGRAM` dieksekusi server-side | Batasi privilege sangat ketat. |

---

## 9. `COPY FROM PROGRAM` / `COPY TO PROGRAM`

### Load dari program output

```sql id="copy-from-program"
COPY staging.raw_case_import
FROM PROGRAM 'gzip -dc /data/import/cases.csv.gz'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
);
```

### Export ke gzip

```sql id="copy-to-program"
COPY (
    SELECT *
    FROM audit.case_event
    WHERE created_at >= timestamptz '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
      AND created_at <  timestamptz '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
)
TO PROGRAM 'gzip > /data/export/case_event_2026_07.csv.gz'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
);
```

Ketika `PROGRAM` digunakan, PostgreSQL server menjalankan command tersebut dan membaca dari standard output program atau menulis ke standard input program. Command harus executable oleh OS user PostgreSQL dan ditentukan dari sudut pandang server. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

**Security rule:** perlakukan `COPY PROGRAM` seperti kemampuan eksekusi command di server database. Jangan berikan ke role aplikasi.

---

## 10. Staging Table Pattern

Jangan load file eksternal langsung ke table final jika data belum dipercaya.

### Raw staging: semua text

```sql id="staging-raw"
CREATE TABLE staging.raw_case_import (
    load_id uuid NOT NULL,
    line_number bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    external_case_id text,
    case_number text,
    opened_at_text text,
    priority_text text,
    status_text text,
    raw_loaded_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### Load ke staging

```sql id="load-raw"
COPY staging.raw_case_import (
    load_id,
    external_case_id,
    case_number,
    opened_at_text,
    priority_text,
    status_text
)
FROM '/data/import/cases.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true,
    NULL ''
);
```

### Validate sebelum transform

```sql id="validate-staging"
SELECT *
FROM staging.raw_case_import
WHERE load_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
  AND (
      case_number IS NULL
      OR opened_at_text IS NULL
      OR priority_text !~ '^[0-9]+$'
      OR status_text NOT IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'CLOSED')
  );
```

**Rule:** staging dengan text columns membuat data buruk bisa masuk dulu untuk dianalisis. Transform ke tipe final dilakukan setelah validasi eksplisit.

---

## 11. Transform dari Staging ke Target

Target typed table:

```sql id="target-table"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.partner_case_import (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    external_case_id text NOT NULL,
    case_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    opened_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    priority integer NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    imported_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Transform:

```sql id="transform-staging"
INSERT INTO case_mgmt.partner_case_import (
    external_case_id,
    case_number,
    opened_at,
    priority,
    status
)
SELECT
    external_case_id,
    upper(trim(case_number)),
    opened_at_text::timestamptz,
    priority_text::integer,
    status_text
FROM staging.raw_case_import
WHERE load_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
  AND case_number IS NOT NULL
  AND opened_at_text IS NOT NULL
  AND priority_text ~ '^[0-9]+$'
  AND status_text IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'CLOSED');
```

### Capture rejected rows

```sql id="reject-table"
CREATE TABLE staging.case_import_reject (
    load_id uuid NOT NULL,
    line_number bigint NOT NULL,
    reason text NOT NULL,
    raw_row jsonb NOT NULL,
    rejected_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

```sql id="insert-rejects"
INSERT INTO staging.case_import_reject (
    load_id,
    line_number,
    reason,
    raw_row
)
SELECT
    load_id,
    line_number,
    CASE
        WHEN case_number IS NULL THEN 'case_number missing'
        WHEN opened_at_text IS NULL THEN 'opened_at missing'
        WHEN priority_text !~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN 'priority invalid'
        WHEN status_text NOT IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'CLOSED') THEN 'status invalid'
        ELSE 'unknown validation error'
    END,
    to_jsonb(r)
FROM staging.raw_case_import r
WHERE load_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
  AND (
      case_number IS NULL
      OR opened_at_text IS NULL
      OR priority_text !~ '^[0-9]+$'
      OR status_text NOT IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'CLOSED')
  );
```

---

## 12. Idempotent Load Pattern

Gunakan `load_id`, natural key, dan conflict handling.

```sql id="idempotent-target"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.imported_case (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    source_system text NOT NULL,
    external_case_id text NOT NULL,
    case_number text NOT NULL,
    opened_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    imported_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT uq_imported_case_source_external
        UNIQUE (source_system, external_case_id)
);
```

```sql id="idempotent-upsert"
INSERT INTO case_mgmt.imported_case (
    source_system,
    external_case_id,
    case_number,
    opened_at,
    status
)
SELECT
    'PARTNER_A',
    external_case_id,
    upper(trim(case_number)),
    opened_at_text::timestamptz,
    status_text
FROM staging.raw_case_import
WHERE load_id = :load_id
ON CONFLICT (source_system, external_case_id)
DO UPDATE SET
    case_number = EXCLUDED.case_number,
    opened_at = EXCLUDED.opened_at,
    status = EXCLUDED.status;
```

**Rule:** setiap bulk import production harus bisa dijalankan ulang dengan aman, atau minimal punya prosedur rollback/cleanup yang jelas.

---

## 13. `COPY ON_ERROR` dan `REJECT_LIMIT`

PostgreSQL `COPY` default-nya gagal saat menemukan error. Untuk kasus “best-effort load”, `ON_ERROR` dapat dipakai untuk perilaku lain. PostgreSQL 18 menambahkan `REJECT_LIMIT` untuk membatasi jumlah invalid rows yang boleh diabaikan saat `ON_ERROR = 'ignore'`, dan menambahkan `LOG_VERBOSITY silent` untuk menekan output baris yang diabaikan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

```sql id="copy-on-error"
COPY staging.raw_case_import
FROM '/data/import/cases.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true,
    ON_ERROR ignore,
    REJECT_LIMIT 100,
    LOG_VERBOSITY verbose
);
```

| Option | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `ON_ERROR stop` | Default; berhenti saat error. |
| `ON_ERROR ignore` | Abaikan row yang error, sesuai dukungan versi. |
| `REJECT_LIMIT n` | Batas jumlah row invalid yang boleh diabaikan. |
| `LOG_VERBOSITY` | Mengatur seberapa detail log untuk row yang diabaikan. |

**Practical rule:** untuk data penting, staging validation masih lebih aman daripada langsung `ON_ERROR ignore`, karena staging memberi audit trail row buruk. Pakai `ON_ERROR ignore` untuk file sangat besar ketika toleransi reject jelas dan monitoring reject count aktif.

---

## 14. Progress Monitoring

PostgreSQL menyediakan `pg_stat_progress_copy` untuk backend yang sedang menjalankan `COPY`. View ini menampilkan command, tipe I/O, bytes processed, total bytes jika tersedia, tuples processed, tuples excluded oleh `WHERE`, dan tuples skipped saat `ON_ERROR` bukan `stop`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/progress-reporting.html))

```sql id="copy-progress"
SELECT
    pid,
    datname,
    relid::regclass AS relation,
    command,
    type,
    bytes_processed,
    bytes_total,
    round(100.0 * bytes_processed / NULLIF(bytes_total, 0), 2) AS pct,
    tuples_processed,
    tuples_excluded,
    tuples_skipped
FROM pg_stat_progress_copy;
```

| Column | Interpretasi |
|---|---|
| `command` | `COPY FROM` atau `COPY TO`. |
| `type` | `FILE`, `PROGRAM`, `PIPE`, atau `CALLBACK`. |
| `bytes_processed` | Byte yang sudah diproses. |
| `bytes_total` | Ukuran file sumber jika tersedia. |
| `tuples_processed` | Row yang diproses. |
| `tuples_excluded` | Row tidak diproses karena `WHERE` pada `COPY`. |
| `tuples_skipped` | Row malformed yang diskip oleh mode error handling. |

---

## 15. `COPY FROM ... WHERE`

Untuk load sebagian data dari file, `COPY FROM` mendukung filter `WHERE`.

```sql id="copy-where"
COPY staging.raw_case_import (
    external_case_id,
    case_number,
    opened_at_text,
    priority_text,
    status_text
)
FROM '/data/import/cases.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
)
WHERE status_text IS NOT NULL;
```

**Use case:** filtering ringan saat load.  
**Caution:** jangan jadikan `COPY WHERE` sebagai pengganti validasi lengkap jika rules kompleks.

---

## 16. Bulk Load Performance Rules

PostgreSQL documentation untuk populating database menyarankan beberapa prinsip: gunakan `COPY` dibanding banyak `INSERT`, pertimbangkan satu transaction besar untuk atomicity, buat/drop index sesuai kebutuhan, handle foreign key constraints, naikkan `maintenance_work_mem` saat build index, naikkan `max_wal_size`, dan jalankan `ANALYZE` setelah load. Dokumentasi juga menyatakan `COPY` hampir selalu lebih cepat daripada `INSERT` untuk banyak row, bahkan jika `INSERT` memakai prepared statement dan batching. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/populate.html))

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Gunakan `COPY`, bukan row-by-row `INSERT` | Lebih sedikit overhead parsing/planning/execution. |
| Load ke staging tanpa banyak index | Index maintenance saat load bisa mahal. |
| Build index setelah load besar | Sering lebih cepat daripada maintain index per row. |
| Jalankan `ANALYZE` setelah load | Planner butuh statistics baru. |
| Validasi data sebelum target final | Menghindari partial dirty data. |
| Load dalam transaction jika atomicity penting | Bisa rollback semua jika ada kegagalan. |
| Hindari trigger/FK berat saat raw load jika bisa | Validasi bisa dilakukan setelah staging. |
| Perhatikan WAL volume | Load besar bisa membebani disk/replication/backup. |

---

## 17. Index Strategy saat Bulk Load

### Option A - target kosong: create index setelah load

```sql id="bulk-index-after"
CREATE TABLE staging.big_import (
    id bigint,
    case_number text,
    status text,
    created_at timestamptz
);

COPY staging.big_import
FROM '/data/import/big_import.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);

CREATE INDEX idx_big_import_case_number
ON staging.big_import (case_number);

ANALYZE staging.big_import;
```

### Option B - target existing: load staging lalu insert target

```sql id="bulk-stage-insert-target"
COPY staging.raw_case_import
FROM '/data/import/cases.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);

INSERT INTO case_mgmt.case_file (...)
SELECT ...
FROM staging.raw_case_import
WHERE ...;
```

`CREATE INDEX` performance dipengaruhi `maintenance_work_mem`; nilai yang lebih besar bisa mengurangi waktu build index selama tidak melebihi memory yang tersedia dan menyebabkan swapping. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 18. Constraint Strategy

| Constraint | Saat Load Raw | Saat Transform ke Target |
|---|---|---|
| `NOT NULL` | Bisa longgar di staging | Tegakkan di target. |
| `CHECK` | Minimal basic format di staging | Tegakkan domain rule di target. |
| FK | Hindari di raw staging | Tegakkan setelah data referensi siap. |
| UNIQUE | Bisa di staging jika perlu dedup | Tegakkan di target dengan `ON CONFLICT`. |
| RLS | Hindari direct `COPY FROM` ke table RLS | Gunakan staging/`INSERT` sesuai policy. |

`COPY FROM` saat ini tidak didukung untuk table dengan row-level security; dokumentasi menyarankan menggunakan equivalent `INSERT` statements jika RLS aktif. `COPY TO` tetap menerapkan relevant `SELECT` policies. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

---

## 19. Transaction Pattern

### Atomic load

```sql id="atomic-load"
BEGIN;

COPY staging.raw_case_import
FROM '/data/import/cases.csv'
WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true);

INSERT INTO case_mgmt.partner_case_import (...)
SELECT ...
FROM staging.raw_case_import
WHERE ...;

ANALYZE case_mgmt.partner_case_import;

COMMIT;
```

Kelebihan: jika gagal, rollback semua.

### Stepwise load with audit

```sql id="stepwise-load"
INSERT INTO staging.load_run (
    load_id,
    source_file,
    started_at,
    status
)
VALUES (
    :load_id,
    '/data/import/cases.csv',
    now(),
    'STARTED'
);
```

Lalu `COPY`, validasi, transform, update status.

```sql id="load-run-finish"
UPDATE staging.load_run
SET finished_at = now(),
    status = 'SUCCEEDED'
WHERE load_id = :load_id;
```

**Rule:** untuk file penting, catat metadata load: file name, checksum, row count, started/finished time, status, imported_by, dan error summary.

---

## 20. Load Run Metadata

```sql id="load-run-table"
CREATE TABLE staging.load_run (
    load_id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    source_system text NOT NULL,
    source_file text NOT NULL,
    file_sha256 text,
    started_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    finished_at timestamptz,
    status text NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('STARTED', 'VALIDATED', 'FAILED', 'SUCCEEDED')),
    raw_row_count bigint,
    valid_row_count bigint,
    rejected_row_count bigint,
    note text
);
```

### Verify row counts

```sql id="verify-row-counts"
UPDATE staging.load_run lr
SET
    raw_row_count = raw_count.cnt,
    rejected_row_count = reject_count.cnt,
    valid_row_count = raw_count.cnt - reject_count.cnt
FROM
    (
        SELECT load_id, count(*) AS cnt
        FROM staging.raw_case_import
        WHERE load_id = :load_id
        GROUP BY load_id
    ) raw_count,
    (
        SELECT load_id, count(*) AS cnt
        FROM staging.case_import_reject
        WHERE load_id = :load_id
        GROUP BY load_id
    ) reject_count
WHERE lr.load_id = :load_id
  AND raw_count.load_id = lr.load_id
  AND reject_count.load_id = lr.load_id;
```

---

## 21. CSV Edge Cases

| Edge Case | Risiko | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Delimiter muncul dalam value | Column shift | Gunakan CSV quote benar. |
| Newline dalam quoted field | Row count terlihat aneh di text tools | Gunakan parser CSV, bukan line count biasa. |
| Empty string vs NULL | Data semantics salah | Set `NULL` option eksplisit. |
| Encoding berbeda | Invalid byte sequence | Gunakan `ENCODING` atau konversi file. |
| Date format ambigu | Cast salah/gagal | Load text dulu, parse eksplisit. |
| Decimal separator lokal | Cast numeric gagal | Normalize staging. |
| Header mismatch | Column salah | Gunakan column list dan validasi header di pipeline. |
| Duplicate key | Load target gagal | Staging dedup / `ON CONFLICT`. |
| Extra/missing columns | COPY error | Definisikan format contract. |

---

## 22. Export Pattern: Audit Extract

```sql id="audit-export"
COPY (
    SELECT
        id,
        case_id,
        event_type,
        actor,
        created_at,
        context
    FROM audit.case_event
    WHERE created_at >= timestamptz '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
      AND created_at <  timestamptz '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
    ORDER BY created_at, id
)
TO '/data/export/audit_case_event_2026_07.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true,
    ENCODING 'UTF8'
);
```

### Export compressed

```sql id="audit-export-gzip"
COPY (
    SELECT
        id,
        case_id,
        event_type,
        actor,
        created_at,
        context
    FROM audit.case_event
    WHERE created_at >= timestamptz '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
      AND created_at <  timestamptz '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
    ORDER BY created_at, id
)
TO PROGRAM 'gzip > /data/export/audit_case_event_2026_07.csv.gz'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
);
```

**Rule:** export untuk audit harus deterministic: filter waktu jelas, timezone eksplisit, order stabil, dan manifest/checksum file disimpan.

---

## 23. Export Pattern: Client-Side Secure Extract

```bash id="client-export"
psql "dbname=appdb sslmode=require" \
  -c "\copy (
        SELECT id, case_number, status, created_at
        FROM case_mgmt.case_file
        WHERE created_at >= timestamp '2026-07-01'
          AND created_at <  timestamp '2026-08-01'
        ORDER BY created_at, id
      ) TO './case_file_2026_07.csv' WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true)"
```

**Use case:** analyst/operator tidak punya akses server filesystem, tetapi boleh melakukan export hasil query sesuai privilege DB.

---

## 24. Import Pattern: Compressed CSV dari Client

```bash id="client-gzip-import"
gzip -dc cases.csv.gz | psql -d appdb -c "\copy staging.raw_case_import FROM STDIN WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER true)"
```

Atau server-side:

```sql id="server-gzip-import"
COPY staging.raw_case_import
FROM PROGRAM 'gzip -dc /data/import/cases.csv.gz'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
);
```

| Pattern | Pilih Jika |
|---|---|
| Client pipe ke `\copy` | File ada di client/bastion/CI. |
| Server `COPY FROM PROGRAM` | File ada di server dan role berhak execute program. |
| Pre-unzip lalu `COPY` | Ingin menghindari `PROGRAM` privilege. |

---

## 25. Import Pattern: Partitioned Table

Untuk table partitioned, load langsung ke parent bisa otomatis route ke partition yang benar.

```sql id="copy-partition-parent"
COPY audit.case_event (
    created_at,
    case_id,
    event_type,
    payload
)
FROM '/data/import/case_event_2026_07.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
);
```

Namun untuk load besar per partition, sering lebih terkontrol load ke staging/partition langsung.

```sql id="copy-partition-child"
COPY audit.case_event_2026_07 (
    created_at,
    case_id,
    event_type,
    payload
)
FROM '/data/import/case_event_2026_07.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
);
```

`COPY FROM` dapat dipakai pada plain, foreign, atau partitioned tables, dan juga pada views yang memiliki `INSTEAD OF INSERT` triggers. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html))

---

## 26. Import Pattern: Staging + Attach Partition

Untuk batch besar time-series:

```sql id="partition-staging"
CREATE TABLE staging.case_event_2026_07
(LIKE audit.case_event INCLUDING DEFAULTS INCLUDING CONSTRAINTS);
```

```sql id="partition-copy-stage"
COPY staging.case_event_2026_07 (
    created_at,
    case_id,
    event_type,
    payload
)
FROM '/data/import/case_event_2026_07.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true
);
```

Validate bound:

```sql id="partition-check-bound"
ALTER TABLE staging.case_event_2026_07
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_event_2026_07_bound
CHECK (
    created_at >= timestamptz '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND created_at < timestamptz '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
);
```

Attach:

```sql id="partition-attach-after-copy"
ALTER TABLE audit.case_event
ATTACH PARTITION staging.case_event_2026_07
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00');
```

**Rule:** untuk partition besar, load dan validate di luar parent dulu, lalu attach setelah siap. Ini mengurangi risiko parent table menerima data buruk dan membuat maintenance lebih terkontrol.

---

## 27. Dedup Pattern Setelah Load

```sql id="dedup-row-number"
WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT
        load_id,
        line_number,
        external_case_id,
        row_number() OVER (
            PARTITION BY external_case_id
            ORDER BY line_number
        ) AS rn
    FROM staging.raw_case_import
    WHERE load_id = :load_id
)
INSERT INTO staging.case_import_reject (
    load_id,
    line_number,
    reason,
    raw_row
)
SELECT
```
