```sql id="dedup-row-number"
    r.load_id,
    r.line_number,
    'duplicate external_case_id in file',
    to_jsonb(raw)
FROM ranked r
JOIN staging.raw_case_import raw
    ON raw.load_id = r.load_id
   AND raw.line_number = r.line_number
WHERE r.rn > 1;
```

Load valid unique rows:

```sql id="load-deduped"
WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT
        raw.*,
        row_number() OVER (
            PARTITION BY external_case_id
            ORDER BY line_number
        ) AS rn
    FROM staging.raw_case_import raw
    WHERE load_id = :load_id
)
INSERT INTO case_mgmt.imported_case (...)
SELECT ...
FROM ranked
WHERE rn = 1;
```

---

## 28. Large Data Movement: `COPY` vs `pg_dump`

| Need | Better Tool |
|---|---|
| Export/import one table/query as CSV | `COPY` / `\copy` |
| Logical backup database/schema/table | `pg_dump` |
| Selective restore object | `pg_dump -Fc` + `pg_restore` |
| Full physical backup/PITR | `pg_basebackup` + WAL archive |
| ETL into staging | `COPY` |
| Audit extract readable by humans | `COPY TO CSV` |
| PostgreSQL-to-PostgreSQL exact logical transfer | `pg_dump` or `COPY binary` depending need |

**Rule:** `COPY` adalah data movement command, bukan backup strategy penuh. Untuk backup/restore gunakan `pg_dump`, physical backup, dan PITR sesuai kebutuhan.

---

## 29. Monitoring dan Verification Pack

### Row count staging

```sql id="count-staging"
SELECT count(*)
FROM staging.raw_case_import
WHERE load_id = :load_id;
```

### Reject count

```sql id="count-rejects"
SELECT reason, count(*)
FROM staging.case_import_reject
WHERE load_id = :load_id
GROUP BY reason
ORDER BY count(*) DESC;
```

### Target count by status

```sql id="target-count"
SELECT status, count(*)
FROM case_mgmt.imported_case
WHERE imported_at >= :load_started_at
GROUP BY status;
```

### Sample checksum-ish aggregate

```sql id="checksum-aggregate"
SELECT
    count(*) AS row_count,
    md5(string_agg(external_case_id, ',' ORDER BY external_case_id)) AS external_id_digest
FROM case_mgmt.imported_case
WHERE source_system = 'PARTNER_A';
```

### Analyze after load

```sql id="analyze-after-load"
ANALYZE case_mgmt.imported_case;
```

Setelah bulk load, jalankan `ANALYZE` agar planner punya statistics baru; dokumentasi populating database secara eksplisit memasukkan “Run ANALYZE Afterwards” sebagai salah satu langkah performa/operasional setelah load besar. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/populate.html))

---

## 30. Error Isolation Strategies

| Strategy | Cocok Untuk | Trade-off |
|---|---|---|
| `COPY` langsung ke target | Data trusted, format bersih, target kecil | Gagal saat row buruk; partial/error handling terbatas. |
| `COPY ON_ERROR ignore` | Best-effort load file besar | Row buruk diskip; perlu reject monitoring. |
| Raw staging text | Data eksternal tidak trusted | Butuh transform/validasi tambahan. |
| Batch split file | File sangat besar dan error perlu isolasi | Orkestrasi lebih kompleks. |
| Load per partition/per period | Time-series besar | Perlu partition lifecycle. |
| `ON CONFLICT` target | Idempotent import | Bisa menyembunyikan data change jika tidak diaudit. |
| Reject table | Audit dan correction loop | Butuh desain reason/raw row. |

---

## 31. Operational Runbook: Import CSV Production

### A. Prepare load metadata

```sql id="runbook-create-load"
INSERT INTO staging.load_run (
    load_id,
    source_system,
    source_file,
    status
)
VALUES (
    :load_id,
    'PARTNER_A',
    '/data/import/cases.csv',
    'STARTED'
);
```

### B. Load raw staging

```sql id="runbook-copy"
COPY staging.raw_case_import (
    load_id,
    external_case_id,
    case_number,
    opened_at_text,
    priority_text,
    status_text
)
FROM '/data/import/cases.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true,
    NULL ''
);
```

### C. Validate

```sql id="runbook-validation"
INSERT INTO staging.case_import_reject (
    load_id,
    line_number,
    reason,
    raw_row
)
SELECT
    load_id,
    line_number,
    CASE
        WHEN external_case_id IS NULL THEN 'external_case_id missing'
        WHEN case_number IS NULL THEN 'case_number missing'
        WHEN opened_at_text IS NULL THEN 'opened_at missing'
        WHEN priority_text !~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN 'priority invalid'
        WHEN status_text NOT IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'CLOSED') THEN 'status invalid'
        ELSE 'unknown'
    END,
    to_jsonb(r)
FROM staging.raw_case_import r
WHERE load_id = :load_id
  AND (
      external_case_id IS NULL
      OR case_number IS NULL
      OR opened_at_text IS NULL
      OR priority_text !~ '^[0-9]+$'
      OR status_text NOT IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'CLOSED')
  );
```

### D. Stop if reject exceeds threshold

```sql id="runbook-reject-threshold"
DO $$
DECLARE
    v_rejects bigint;
BEGIN
    SELECT count(*)
    INTO v_rejects
    FROM staging.case_import_reject
    WHERE load_id = :load_id;

    IF v_rejects > 100 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Reject count too high: %', v_rejects;
    END IF;
END;
$$;
```

### E. Transform to target

```sql id="runbook-transform"
INSERT INTO case_mgmt.imported_case (
    source_system,
    external_case_id,
    case_number,
    opened_at,
    status
)
SELECT
    'PARTNER_A',
    external_case_id,
    upper(trim(case_number)),
    opened_at_text::timestamptz,
    status_text
FROM staging.raw_case_import r
WHERE load_id = :load_id
  AND NOT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM staging.case_import_reject rej
      WHERE rej.load_id = r.load_id
        AND rej.line_number = r.line_number
  )
ON CONFLICT (source_system, external_case_id)
DO UPDATE SET
    case_number = EXCLUDED.case_number,
    opened_at = EXCLUDED.opened_at,
    status = EXCLUDED.status;
```

### F. Analyze and finalize

```sql id="runbook-finalize"
ANALYZE case_mgmt.imported_case;

UPDATE staging.load_run
SET
    finished_at = now(),
    status = 'SUCCEEDED'
WHERE load_id = :load_id;
```

---

## 32. Operational Runbook: Export CSV

### A. Define exact extract

```sql id="export-exact"
COPY (
    SELECT
        id,
        case_number,
        status,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    FROM case_mgmt.case_file
    WHERE updated_at >= timestamptz '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
      AND updated_at <  timestamptz '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
    ORDER BY updated_at, id
)
TO '/data/export/case_file_delta_2026_07.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true,
    ENCODING 'UTF8'
);
```

### B. Verify count from same query

```sql id="export-count"
SELECT count(*)
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE updated_at >= timestamptz '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
  AND updated_at <  timestamptz '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00';
```

### C. Store manifest

```sql id="export-manifest"
CREATE TABLE audit.data_export_manifest (
    id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    export_name text NOT NULL,
    file_path text NOT NULL,
    query_description text NOT NULL,
    row_count bigint NOT NULL,
    exported_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    exported_by text NOT NULL DEFAULT current_user
);
```

---

## 33. Common Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Memakai row-by-row `INSERT` untuk jutaan row | Lambat | Gunakan `COPY`. |
| Load langsung ke final table dari file eksternal tidak trusted | Constraint failure atau data kotor | Raw staging + validation. |
| Tidak pakai column list | Salah mapping saat schema berubah | Selalu tulis column list. |
| Tidak membedakan empty string vs NULL | Data semantics salah | Set `NULL` option eksplisit. |
| Path file dianggap path client padahal server | File not found / permission error | Gunakan `\copy` untuk client file. |
| `COPY PROGRAM` diberikan ke role luas | Server command execution risk | Admin-only. |
| Tidak `ANALYZE` setelah load besar | Planner salah estimasi | Jalankan `ANALYZE`. |
| Banyak index saat load besar ke table kosong | Load lambat | Load dulu, index belakangan. |
| Tidak simpan load metadata | Sulit audit/retry/debug | `load_run` table. |
| Tidak idempotent | Re-run membuat duplicate/corruption | Natural key + `ON CONFLICT`. |
| `ON_ERROR ignore` tanpa reject monitoring | Data hilang diam-diam | `REJECT_LIMIT`, progress/reject audit. |
| Export tanpa order/filter jelas | Hasil tidak reproducible | Query explicit + order stable. |
| Import date langsung tanpa staging | Format ambiguity | Load text lalu parse eksplisit. |

---

## 34. Decision Matrix

| Problem | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Import CSV kecil dari laptop | `\copy ... FROM local.csv CSV HEADER` |
| Import besar dari server directory | `COPY ... FROM '/server/path'` |
| Import compressed file server-side | `COPY FROM PROGRAM 'gzip -dc ...'` |
| Import untrusted partner file | Raw staging text + validation + transform |
| Need skip limited bad rows | `COPY ... ON_ERROR ignore REJECT_LIMIT n` |
| Need audit bad rows | Reject table pattern |
| Need idempotent import | Natural key + `ON CONFLICT` |
| Need fast load into empty table | `COPY`, create indexes after, `ANALYZE` |
| Need export for analyst | `\copy (SELECT ...) TO local.csv` |
| Need export audit file | `COPY (SELECT ...) TO server path CSV HEADER` |
| Need move full schema/db | `pg_dump`, not just `COPY` |
| Need migrate huge partition | Load staging partition, validate, attach |

---

## 35. Review Checklist

| Checklist | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Source trust | Apakah file trusted atau perlu staging text? |
| File location | File ada di server atau client? |
| Format | CSV/text/binary? delimiter/quote/null/header jelas? |
| Encoding | Apakah UTF-8 atau perlu konversi? |
| Column list | Apakah column mapping eksplisit? |
| Validation | Apa required fields, type checks, enum checks? |
| Reject handling | Di mana row buruk dicatat? |
| Idempotency | Apa natural key/import key? |
| Transaction | Apakah perlu all-or-nothing? |
| Constraints | Constraint mana ditegakkan di staging vs target? |
| Index | Apakah load lebih cepat jika index dibuat setelah load? |
| Analyze | Apakah statistics diperbarui setelah load? |
| WAL/disk | Apakah load besar aman untuk WAL/disk/replication? |
| Audit | Apakah load/export punya manifest dan row count? |
| Security | Siapa boleh `COPY`, file access, dan export data? |
| Monitoring | Apakah `pg_stat_progress_copy` dicek untuk load besar? |

---

## 36. Minimal Production Pattern

```sql id="minimal-load-run"
CREATE TABLE staging.load_run (
    load_id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    source_system text NOT NULL,
    source_file text NOT NULL,
    started_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    finished_at timestamptz,
    status text NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('STARTED', 'FAILED', 'SUCCEEDED')),
    raw_row_count bigint,
    rejected_row_count bigint
);

CREATE TABLE staging.raw_case_import (
    load_id uuid NOT NULL,
    line_number bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY,
    external_case_id text,
    case_number text,
    opened_at_text text,
    priority_text text,
    status_text text,
    raw_loaded_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE staging.case_import_reject (
    load_id uuid NOT NULL,
    line_number bigint NOT NULL,
    reason text NOT NULL,
    raw_row jsonb NOT NULL,
    rejected_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

```sql id="minimal-copy"
COPY staging.raw_case_import (
    load_id,
    external_case_id,
    case_number,
    opened_at_text,
    priority_text,
    status_text
)
FROM '/data/import/cases.csv'
WITH (
    FORMAT csv,
    HEADER true,
    NULL '',
    ENCODING 'UTF8'
);
```

```sql id="minimal-transform"
INSERT INTO case_mgmt.imported_case (
    source_system,
    external_case_id,
    case_number,
    opened_at,
    priority,
    status
)
SELECT
    'PARTNER_A',
    external_case_id,
    upper(trim(case_number)),
    opened_at_text::timestamptz,
    priority_text::integer,
    status_text
FROM staging.raw_case_import r
WHERE load_id = :load_id
  AND priority_text ~ '^[0-9]+$'
  AND status_text IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'CLOSED')
ON CONFLICT (source_system, external_case_id)
DO UPDATE SET
    case_number = EXCLUDED.case_number,
    opened_at = EXCLUDED.opened_at,
    priority = EXCLUDED.priority,
    status = EXCLUDED.status;

ANALYZE case_mgmt.imported_case;
```

---

## 37. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `COPY` vs `\copy` | Paham server-side vs client-side file access. |
| 2 | CSV options | Bisa handle delimiter, header, quote, null, encoding. |
| 3 | Import/export basics | Bisa load dan extract table/query dengan aman. |
| 4 | Staging table | Bisa menerima data eksternal tanpa merusak target. |
| 5 | Validation/reject table | Bisa isolasi row buruk dan audit penyebab. |
| 6 | Idempotent load | Bisa rerun import tanpa duplicate/corruption. |
| 7 | Bulk performance | Bisa memilih index/constraint/analyze strategy. |
| 8 | Error handling modern | Bisa memakai `ON_ERROR`, `REJECT_LIMIT`, dan monitoring. |
| 9 | Partition/batch loading | Bisa load data besar per periode/partition. |
| 10 | Operational runbook | Bisa menjalankan import/export production dengan manifest, counts, dan rollback plan. |

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjut paling tepat: **Cheatsheet PostgreSQL Schema Migration Strategy: Lock-Aware DDL, Expand-Contract, Backward-Compatible Migration, Large Table Migration, Constraint Validation, Rollback vs Forward-Fix, dan Deployment Safety**.

---

**Sources:**

- [PostgreSQL: Documentation: 18: COPY](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 1:37:20 AM

# Cheatsheet: PostgreSQL Schema Migration Strategy

## 1. Scope

Cheatsheet ini fokus **PostgreSQL-only** untuk strategi perubahan schema yang aman di production:

- lock-aware DDL
- expand-contract migration
- backward-compatible migration
- large table migration
- constraint validation
- index migration
- column rename/drop/type change
- partition-aware migration
- rollback vs forward-fix
- deployment safety
- migration review checklist

PostgreSQL schema migration bukan sekadar menjalankan `ALTER TABLE`. Di production, yang paling penting adalah memahami **lock**, **table rewrite**, **scan besar**, **index build**, **transaction duration**, dan **kompatibilitas antar versi aplikasi**.

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="schema-migration-map"
mindmap
  root((PostgreSQL Schema Migration))
    Risk Model
      Lock
      Table Rewrite
      Table Scan
      Index Build
      WAL Growth
      Disk Spike
      Replication Lag
    Migration Style
      Expand
      Backfill
      Validate
      Switch
      Contract
    DDL Safety
      Lock Timeout
      Statement Timeout
      Concurrent Index
      Not Valid Constraint
      Validate Constraint
    Large Table
      Batch Update
      Partial Backfill
      Progress Monitoring
      Analyze
    Compatibility
      Old App
      New App
      Dual Write
      Dual Read
      Feature Flag
    Recovery
      Rollback
      Forward Fix
      Idempotent Scripts
      Metadata Tracking
```

**Core idea:** migration production harus didesain agar **aplikasi versi lama dan versi baru bisa berjalan bersamaan sementara waktu**. Ini penting untuk rolling deploy, blue/green, canary, failover, dan rollback aplikasi.

---

## 3. Prinsip Utama

| Prinsip | Makna |
|---|---|
| Expand dulu, contract belakangan | Tambahkan struktur baru tanpa merusak struktur lama. |
| Backward compatible | Schema baru harus masih bisa dipakai aplikasi lama selama transisi. |
| Avoid long exclusive lock | Jangan biarkan DDL menunggu lock lama lalu memblokir traffic. |
| Avoid table rewrite besar | Rewrite table besar bisa butuh waktu, WAL, dan disk besar. |
| Validate async | Tambahkan constraint dengan `NOT VALID`, lalu validasi setelah data siap. |
| Build index concurrently | Untuk table production besar, index build biasa bisa memblokir write. |
| Backfill batch | Jangan update jutaan row dalam satu transaction tanpa alasan kuat. |
| Forward-fix first | Untuk schema production, memperbaiki ke depan sering lebih aman daripada rollback destructive. |
| Verify, then contract | Drop column/constraint/table hanya setelah yakin tidak dipakai lagi. |

---

## 4. Risk Model: Apa yang Bisa Salah?

| Risiko | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Lock blocking | `ALTER TABLE` menunggu transaction lama | API ikut blocked. |
| Table rewrite | Ubah tipe column besar | WAL/disk spike, replication lag. |
| Full table scan | `SET NOT NULL`, add constraint tanpa `NOT VALID` | Migration lama dan update bisa terkunci. |
| Index build blocking | `CREATE INDEX` biasa | Insert/update/delete blocked. |
| Invalid index | `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` gagal | Index tidak dipakai tetapi tetap bisa menambah overhead. |
| Schema incompatibility | Drop/rename column saat app lama masih jalan | Runtime error. |
| Backfill besar | `UPDATE` semua row sekaligus | Bloat, lock, WAL, replication lag. |
| Rollback salah | Drop column lalu perlu data kembali | Data loss. |

PostgreSQL otomatis mengambil lock untuk banyak command agar object tidak berubah secara incompatible saat command berjalan. Lock mode berbeda memiliki konflik berbeda, dan `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` adalah yang paling membatasi karena berkonflik dengan operasi lain termasuk read biasa. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html))

---

## 5. Guardrail Wajib untuk Migration

Gunakan timeout pendek agar migration gagal cepat jika tidak bisa mendapat lock, bukan menunggu lama lalu membuat antrean traffic production.

```sql id="migration-guardrail"
BEGIN;

SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '5min';
SET LOCAL idle_in_transaction_session_timeout = '1min';

-- DDL / metadata change here

COMMIT;
```

`lock_timeout` membatalkan statement jika menunggu lock lebih lama dari batas yang ditentukan; berbeda dari `statement_timeout`, timeout ini hanya terjadi saat menunggu lock. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL juga tidak merekomendasikan setting `lock_timeout` secara global karena akan memengaruhi semua session. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-client.html))

---

## 6. DDL Risk Matrix

| Operation | Risiko | Catatan Aman |
|---|---|---|
| `ADD COLUMN nullable` tanpa default | Rendah | Biasanya metadata-only. |
| `ADD COLUMN` dengan constant non-volatile default | Rendah-menengah | PostgreSQL current menyimpan default di metadata dan tidak rewrite semua row. |
| `ADD COLUMN` dengan volatile default | Tinggi | Bisa rewrite seluruh table. |
| `ADD stored generated column` | Tinggi | Bisa rewrite table. |
| `ALTER COLUMN TYPE` | Tinggi | Umumnya rewrite table dan rebuild index. |
| `SET NOT NULL` langsung | Menengah-tinggi | Perlu verifikasi seluruh table kecuali ada bukti constraint valid. |
| `ADD CHECK/FK NOT VALID` | Rendah-menengah | Skip scan awal, enforce row baru. |
| `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` | Menengah | Scan table, tapi lock lebih ringan. |
| `CREATE INDEX` biasa | Tinggi di production | Write blocked selama index build. |
| `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` | Lebih aman untuk write | Lebih lama, tidak boleh dalam transaction block. |
| `DROP INDEX` biasa | Menengah-tinggi | Normal drop mengambil `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` pada table. |
| `DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY` | Lebih aman | Ada caveat; tidak boleh transaction block. |
| `TRUNCATE` | Tinggi | Mengambil `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE`, bukan pengganti delete aman. |
| `DROP COLUMN` | Tinggi secara kompatibilitas | Data hilang secara logical dan app lama bisa rusak. |
| `RENAME COLUMN` | Tinggi secara kompatibilitas | App lama langsung error jika masih pakai nama lama. |

PostgreSQL current menyatakan `ADD COLUMN` dengan default non-volatile tidak perlu meng-update setiap row saat `ALTER TABLE` berjalan, sedangkan volatile default, stored generated column, identity column, atau domain dengan constraint dapat menyebabkan table dan index ditulis ulang. Mengubah tipe column juga biasanya menyebabkan rewrite table dan index rebuild, serta bisa membutuhkan disk sementara hingga sekitar dua kali ukuran table/index terkait. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-alter.html))

---

## 7. Expand-Contract Pattern

```text id="expand-contract-flow"
1. EXPAND
   Tambah column/table/index/constraint baru tanpa menghapus yang lama.

2. BACKFILL
   Isi data lama ke struktur baru secara batch.

3. DUAL READ / DUAL WRITE
   Aplikasi mendukung struktur lama dan baru sementara.

4. SWITCH
   Aplikasi pindah membaca/menulis struktur baru.

5. VALIDATE
   Pastikan data benar, constraint valid, index dipakai.

6. CONTRACT
   Hapus struktur lama setelah tidak ada consumer.
```

### Contoh: memindahkan `customer_name` ke table baru

**Step 1 - expand**

```sql id="expand-customer-ref"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_customer (
    case_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
    customer_name text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

**Step 2 - backfill**

```sql id="backfill-customer-ref"
INSERT INTO case_mgmt.case_customer (
    case_id,
    customer_name
)
SELECT
    id,
    customer_name
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE customer_name IS NOT NULL
ON CONFLICT (case_id) DO NOTHING;
```

**Step 3 - add FK later, not valid**

```sql id="fk-not-valid"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_customer
ADD CONSTRAINT fk_case_customer_case
FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
REFERENCES case_mgmt.case_file(id)
NOT VALID;
```

**Step 4 - validate**

```sql id="fk-validate"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_customer
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT fk_case_customer_case;
```

**Step 5 - contract later**

```sql id="contract-drop-old-column"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
DROP COLUMN customer_name;
```

PostgreSQL `ADD CONSTRAINT ... NOT VALID` melewati scan awal table tetapi tetap menegakkan constraint untuk row baru; constraint baru dianggap sepenuhnya valid setelah `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT`. Untuk FK, `ADD FOREIGN KEY` memakai lock yang lebih ringan daripada banyak bentuk `ADD CONSTRAINT`, dan `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` memakai `SHARE UPDATE EXCLUSIVE` lock. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 8. Pattern: Add Nullable Column Aman

```sql id="add-nullable-column"
BEGIN;

SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '1min';

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN external_ref text;

COMMIT;
```

Kemudian aplikasi mulai menulis column baru.

Jika perlu default untuk row baru:

```sql id="set-default-after-add"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ALTER COLUMN external_ref SET DEFAULT 'UNKNOWN';
```

Default baru hanya berlaku untuk `INSERT`/`UPDATE` berikutnya dan tidak mengubah row existing. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-alter.html))

---

## 9. Pattern: Add Column dengan Default

### Aman untuk constant default

```sql id="add-column-constant-default"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN source_system text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INTERNAL';
```

Pada PostgreSQL modern, constant default non-volatile tidak harus rewrite seluruh table saat `ALTER TABLE`.

### Hindari volatile default langsung

```sql id="bad-volatile-default"
-- Hindari pada table besar
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN migrated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp();
```

Lebih aman:

```sql id="safe-volatile-column"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN migrated_at timestamptz;

-- backfill batch
UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
SET migrated_at = now()
WHERE migrated_at IS NULL
  AND id BETWEEN :start_id AND :end_id;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ALTER COLUMN migrated_at SET DEFAULT now();
```

Volatile default seperti `clock_timestamp()` dapat memaksa update setiap row saat `ALTER TABLE`; dokumentasi menyarankan menambah column tanpa default lalu mengisi nilai dengan `UPDATE` jika ingin menghindari operasi panjang. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-alter.html))

---

## 10. Pattern: Backfill Batch

Jangan lakukan ini pada table besar:

```sql id="bad-one-shot-backfill"
UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
SET external_ref = 'UNKNOWN'
WHERE external_ref IS NULL;
```

Lebih aman batch by primary key:

```sql id="batch-backfill"
UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
SET external_ref = 'UNKNOWN'
WHERE external_ref IS NULL
  AND id >= :start_id
  AND id <  :end_id;
```

### Progress check

```sql id="backfill-progress"
SELECT
    count(*) FILTER (WHERE external_ref IS NULL) AS remaining,
    count(*) AS total
FROM case_mgmt.case_file;
```

### Batch planning

```sql id="batch-ranges"
SELECT
    min(id) AS min_id,
    max(id) AS max_id,
    count(*) AS row_count
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE external_ref IS NULL;
```

| Batch Strategy | Cocok Untuk |
|---|---|
| By `id` range | Table punya monotonic primary key. |
| By `created_at` range | Time-series / partitioned table. |
| By partition | Audit/history/event table. |
| By `LIMIT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` | Worker paralel controlled. |

---

## 11. Pattern: Backfill Worker dengan `SKIP LOCKED`

```sql id="backfill-skip-locked"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM case_mgmt.case_file
    WHERE external_ref IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    LIMIT 1000
)
UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file c
SET external_ref = 'UNKNOWN'
FROM batch
WHERE c.id = batch.id
RETURNING c.id;
```

**Rule:** gunakan batch kecil, commit sering, dan monitor WAL/replication lag. Backfill besar adalah write workload production.

---

## 12. Pattern: Add `NOT NULL` Aman

### PostgreSQL 18+ direct `NOT VALID` not-null constraint

```sql id="not-null-not-valid-pg18"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT nn_case_file_external_ref
NOT NULL external_ref
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT nn_case_file_external_ref;
```

PostgreSQL 18 mendukung not-null constraint sebagai table constraint dan `NOT VALID` untuk foreign-key, `CHECK`, dan not-null constraints; `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` kemudian memeriksa row existing. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

### Cross-version pattern: `CHECK` dulu, lalu `SET NOT NULL`

```sql id="not-null-check-pattern"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_external_ref_not_null
CHECK (external_ref IS NOT NULL)
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_external_ref_not_null;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ALTER COLUMN external_ref SET NOT NULL;
```

`SET NOT NULL` biasanya memerlukan scan table, tetapi PostgreSQL dapat melewati scan jika ada valid `CHECK` constraint yang membuktikan tidak ada `NULL`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 13. Pattern: Add Check Constraint Aman

Langsung scan table:

```sql id="bad-check-direct"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_status_valid
CHECK (status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'ESCALATED', 'CLOSED'));
```

Lebih aman untuk table besar:

```sql id="check-not-valid"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_status_valid
CHECK (status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'ESCALATED', 'CLOSED'))
NOT VALID;
```

Kemudian:

```sql id="check-validate"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT ck_case_status_valid;
```

Dengan `NOT VALID`, PostgreSQL tidak scan table saat menambah constraint, tetapi constraint tetap berlaku untuk row baru atau row yang di-update. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 14. Pattern: Add Foreign Key Aman

```sql id="fk-add-not-valid"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_task
ADD CONSTRAINT fk_case_task_case_file
FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
REFERENCES case_mgmt.case_file(id)
NOT VALID;
```

```sql id="fk-validate-later"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_task
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT fk_case_task_case_file;
```

Sebelum validasi:

```sql id="fk-orphan-check"
SELECT t.case_id, count(*)
FROM case_mgmt.case_task t
LEFT JOIN case_mgmt.case_file c
    ON c.id = t.case_id
WHERE c.id IS NULL
GROUP BY t.case_id
LIMIT 50;
```

**Rule:** cek orphan rows dulu. Jangan menunggu `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` gagal setelah scan besar.

---

## 15. Pattern: Add Unique Constraint Aman

Jangan langsung:

```sql id="bad-unique-direct"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT uq_case_number UNIQUE (case_number);
```

Lebih aman:

```sql id="unique-index-concurrently"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX CONCURRENTLY ux_case_file_case_number
ON case_mgmt.case_file (case_number);
```

Kemudian convert ke constraint:

```sql id="unique-using-index"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT uq_case_file_case_number
UNIQUE USING INDEX ux_case_file_case_number;
```

PostgreSQL docs menyebut pola `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` lalu `ALTER TABLE ... ADD CONSTRAINT ... USING INDEX` sebagai cara menambahkan constraint tanpa memblokir update table dalam waktu lama. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 16. `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`

```sql id="create-index-concurrently"
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_case_file_status_created
ON case_mgmt.case_file (status, created_at DESC);
```

**Caveat penting:**

| Caveat | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Tidak boleh dalam transaction block | Jalankan sebagai statement terpisah. |
| Lebih lama | Butuh dua scan table dan menunggu transaksi tertentu. |
| Hanya satu concurrent index build per table | Hindari paralel di table sama. |
| Bisa meninggalkan invalid index | Jika gagal, index invalid perlu drop/reindex. |
| Tetap memberi beban CPU/I/O | Tidak memblokir write, tapi bisa memperlambat workload. |

`CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` tidak mengunci table terhadap insert/update/delete, tetapi memerlukan dua scan dan lebih banyak kerja; jika gagal, index invalid bisa tertinggal dan tetap mengonsumsi overhead update. Command ini juga tidak boleh dijalankan dalam transaction block. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html))

### Cek invalid index

```sql id="invalid-index-check"
SELECT
    n.nspname AS schema_name,
    c.relname AS index_name,
    i.indisvalid,
    i.indisready,
    i.indislive
FROM pg_index i
JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = i.indexrelid
JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace
WHERE NOT i.indisvalid
ORDER BY n.nspname, c.relname;
```

### Recovery

```sql id="drop-invalid-index"
DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF EXISTS case_mgmt.idx_case_file_status_created;
```

---

## 17. Drop Index Aman

```sql id="drop-index-concurrently"
DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF EXISTS case_mgmt.idx_old_case_file_status;
```

Normal `DROP INDEX` mengambil `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` lock pada table dan memblokir access lain sampai selesai; `DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY` menghindari blocking concurrent select/insert/update/delete, tetapi hanya bisa satu index per command, tidak mendukung `CASCADE`, tidak bisa dijalankan dalam transaction block, dan tidak berlaku untuk index partitioned table. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-dropindex.html))

---

## 18. Pattern: Rename Column Tanpa Merusak App Lama

`ALTER TABLE ... RENAME COLUMN` terlihat sederhana, tapi tidak backward-compatible.

### Hindari langsung

```sql id="bad-rename-column"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
RENAME COLUMN case_number TO reference_number;
```

### Expand-contract rename

```sql id="rename-expand"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN reference_number text;
```

Backfill:

```sql id="rename-backfill"
UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
SET reference_number = case_number
WHERE reference_number IS NULL;
```

Tambahkan constraint/index baru:

```sql id="rename-new-index"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX CONCURRENTLY ux_case_file_reference_number
ON case_mgmt.case_file (reference_number);
```

Aplikasi baru dual-write `case_number` dan `reference_number`, lalu setelah semua consumer pindah:

```sql id="rename-contract"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
DROP COLUMN case_number;
```

**Rule:** rename column di production harus dianggap sebagai **drop old + add new**, bukan operasi kosmetik.

---

## 19. Pattern: Change Column Type Aman

Langsung:

```sql id="bad-type-change"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ALTER COLUMN priority TYPE bigint;
```

Untuk perubahan tipe yang berisiko rewrite besar, gunakan column baru:

```sql id="type-expand"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN priority_bigint bigint;
```

Backfill:

```sql id="type-backfill"
UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
SET priority_bigint = priority::bigint
WHERE priority_bigint IS NULL
  AND id >= :start_id
  AND id <  :end_id;
```

Dual-write aplikasi, lalu switch read ke column baru. Setelah aman:

```sql id="type-contract"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
DROP COLUMN priority;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
RENAME COLUMN priority_bigint TO priority;
```

Mengubah tipe column biasanya menyebabkan table rewrite, kecuali kasus tertentu seperti tipe lama binary-coercible ke tipe baru; index juga dapat dibangun ulang jika PostgreSQL tidak bisa membuktikan index lama tetap equivalent. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 20. Pattern: Split Table Besar

Contoh: memindahkan payload besar dari table utama ke detail table.

### Step 1 - create detail table

```sql id="split-table-create"
CREATE TABLE case_mgmt.case_payload (
    case_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### Step 2 - backfill batch

```sql id="split-table-backfill"
INSERT INTO case_mgmt.case_payload (case_id, payload)
SELECT id, payload
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
WHERE payload IS NOT NULL
  AND id >= :start_id
  AND id <  :end_id
ON CONFLICT (case_id) DO NOTHING;
```

### Step 3 - FK not valid

```sql id="split-table-fk"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_payload
ADD CONSTRAINT fk_case_payload_case
FOREIGN KEY (case_id)
REFERENCES case_mgmt.case_file(id)
NOT VALID;
```

### Step 4 - validate

```sql id="split-table-validate"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_payload
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT fk_case_payload_case;
```

### Step 5 - application switch, then contract

```sql id="split-table-contract"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
DROP COLUMN payload;
```

---

## 21. Pattern: Partition Migration

### Attach existing table sebagai partition

```sql id="attach-partition-precheck"
ALTER TABLE staging.case_event_2026_07
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_event_2026_07_bound
CHECK (
    created_at >= timestamptz '2026-07-01 00:00:00+00'
    AND created_at <  timestamptz '2026-08-01 00:00:00+00'
);
```

```sql id="attach-partition"
ALTER TABLE audit.case_event
ATTACH PARTITION staging.case_event_2026_07
FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-07-01 00:00:00+00')
TO ('2026-08-01 00:00:00+00');
```

Saat attach partition, PostgreSQL dapat melakukan full scan untuk memastikan row cocok dengan partition bound; scan ini bisa dihindari jika table yang akan di-attach sudah memiliki `CHECK` constraint valid yang membuktikan bound tersebut. `ATTACH PARTITION` mengambil `SHARE UPDATE EXCLUSIVE` lock pada parent dan `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` pada table yang di-attach. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

### Detach partition concurrently

```sql id="detach-partition-concurrently"
ALTER TABLE audit.case_event
DETACH PARTITION audit.case_event_2024_07 CONCURRENTLY;
```

`DETACH PARTITION CONCURRENTLY` memakai lock level yang lebih rendah untuk mengurangi blocking, tetapi tidak boleh dijalankan dalam transaction block dan tidak diperbolehkan jika partitioned table punya default partition. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 22. Pattern: Drop Column Aman

Drop column adalah migration paling sering diremehkan.

### Checklist sebelum drop

```sql id="find-column-dependencies"
SELECT
    dependent_ns.nspname AS dependent_schema,
    dependent_view.relname AS dependent_object
FROM pg_depend d
JOIN pg_rewrite r ON r.oid = d.objid
JOIN pg_class dependent_view ON dependent_view.oid = r.ev_class
JOIN pg_namespace dependent_ns ON dependent_ns.oid = dependent_view.relnamespace
JOIN pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid = d.refobjid AND a.attnum = d.refobjsubid
WHERE d.refobjid = 'case_mgmt.case_file'::regclass
  AND a.attname = 'old_column';
```

### Safer two-step

1. Stop writing old column.
2. Stop reading old column.
3. Monitor query/log usage.
4. Drop in later release.

```sql id="drop-column-safe"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
DROP COLUMN old_column;
```

**Rule:** jangan drop column pada release yang sama dengan release aplikasi pertama yang berhenti menggunakannya. Beri satu atau lebih release gap.

---

## 23. Pattern: Replace Enum / Status Model

Untuk status yang sering berubah, `text + CHECK` atau lookup table sering lebih fleksibel daripada enum. Jika sudah terlanjur enum dan perlu ubah model:

### Add new status column

```sql id="status-expand"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN status_v2 text;
```

### Backfill

```sql id="status-backfill"
UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
SET status_v2 = status::text
WHERE status_v2 IS NULL;
```

### Add check not valid

```sql id="status-check"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_status_v2
CHECK (status_v2 IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW', 'ESCALATED', 'CLOSED', 'CANCELLED'))
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_status_v2;
```

### Switch app, then contract

```sql id="status-contract"
ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
DROP COLUMN status;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
RENAME COLUMN status_v2 TO status;
```

---

## 24. Pattern: Materialized View Migration

### Create new version

```sql id="matview-v2"
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW reporting.case_summary_v2 AS
SELECT
    status,
    assigned_user_id,
    count(*) AS total
FROM case_mgmt.case_file
GROUP BY status, assigned_user_id
WITH NO DATA;
```

### Index for concurrent refresh

```sql id="matview-index"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX reporting_case_summary_v2_key
ON reporting.case_summary_v2 (status, assigned_user_id);
```

### Populate

```sql id="matview-refresh"
REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW reporting.case_summary_v2;
```

### Switch view name

Gunakan wrapper view untuk stable interface:

```sql id="view-wrapper"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW reporting.v_case_summary AS
SELECT *
FROM reporting.case_summary_v2;
```

**Rule:** jangan jadikan aplikasi bergantung langsung pada nama materialized view internal jika sering berubah. Pakai view wrapper.

---

## 25. Migration Metadata Table

Minimal tracking table:

```sql id="schema-migration-table"
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS migration;

CREATE TABLE migration.schema_migration (
    version text PRIMARY KEY,
    description text NOT NULL,
    applied_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    applied_by text NOT NULL DEFAULT current_user,
    checksum text,
    success boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);
```

Record migration:

```sql id="schema-migration-insert"
INSERT INTO migration.schema_migration (
    version,
    description,
    checksum
)
VALUES (
    '20260713_001',
    'add external_ref to case_file',
    'sha256:...'
);
```

**Rule:** semua schema change harus bisa diaudit: kapan, siapa, apa, checksum script, dan sukses/gagal.

---

## 26. Pre-Migration Inspection Queries

### Long transactions yang bisa menghalangi DDL

```sql id="precheck-long-xact"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    state,
    now() - xact_start AS xact_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE xact_start IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY xact_start ASC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Lock wait saat migration

```sql id="precheck-lock-wait"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    now() - query_start AS query_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE wait_event_type = 'Lock'
ORDER BY query_start ASC;
```

### Table size

```sql id="precheck-table-size"
SELECT
    'case_mgmt.case_file'::regclass AS relation,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_table_size('case_mgmt.case_file')) AS table_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_indexes_size('case_mgmt.case_file')) AS index_size,
    pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size('case_mgmt.case_file')) AS total_size;
```

### Estimated rows

```sql id="precheck-estimated-rows"
SELECT
    relname,
    reltuples::bigint AS estimated_rows
FROM pg_class
WHERE oid = 'case_mgmt.case_file'::regclass;
```

---

## 27. Migration Lock Debugging

### Blocked vs blocker

```sql id="migration-blocker-query"
SELECT
    blocked.pid AS blocked_pid,
    blocked.query AS blocked_query,
    blocker.pid AS blocker_pid,
    blocker.query AS blocker_query,
    now() - blocker.query_start AS blocker_age
FROM pg_stat_activity blocked
JOIN pg_locks blocked_locks
    ON blocked_locks.pid = blocked.pid
JOIN pg_locks blocker_locks
    ON blocker_locks.locktype = blocked_locks.locktype
   AND blocker_locks.database IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.database
   AND blocker_locks.relation IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.relation
   AND blocker_locks.page IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.page
   AND blocker_locks.tuple IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.tuple
   AND blocker_locks.virtualxid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.virtualxid
   AND blocker_locks.transactionid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.transactionid
   AND blocker_locks.classid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.classid
   AND blocker_locks.objid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.objid
   AND blocker_locks.objsubid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.objsubid
   AND blocker_locks.pid <> blocked_locks.pid
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocker
    ON blocker.pid = blocker_locks.pid
WHERE NOT blocked_locks.granted
  AND blocker_locks.granted;
```

### Cancel migration session

```sql id="cancel-migration"
SELECT pg_cancel_backend(:migration_pid);
```

### Terminate blocker hanya setelah validasi

```sql id="terminate-blocker"
SELECT pg_terminate_backend(:blocker_pid);
```

**Rule:** migration harus lebih mudah dibatalkan daripada membatalkan traffic aplikasi.

---

## 28. Transaction Boundary Strategy

| Migration Type | Transaction? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Add nullable column | Ya | Guardrail timeout. |
| Add check/fk `NOT VALID` | Ya | Cepat commit. |
| Validate constraint | Bisa ya | Bisa lama; monitor. |
| Create normal index | Ya | Tapi bisa block writes. |
| `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` | Tidak | PostgreSQL melarang di transaction block. |
| `DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY` | Tidak | PostgreSQL melarang di transaction block. |
| Backfill batch | Ya per batch | Commit kecil. |
| Large type rewrite | Hati-hati | Bisa sangat lama; prefer expand-contract. |

`CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` dan `DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY` tidak dapat dijalankan dalam transaction block. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html))

---

## 29. Rollback vs Forward-Fix

| Situation | Prefer |
|---|---|
| Add column gagal sebelum commit | Rollback transaction. |
| Add index concurrently gagal | Drop invalid index, retry. |
| Backfill salah sebagian | Forward-fix dengan corrective update jika data bisa dihitung ulang. |
| Drop column sudah commit | Restore dari backup/PITR atau forward-fix dari source lain; rollback script tidak cukup. |
| Rename column memecahkan app | Forward-fix tambah compatibility view/column jika mungkin. |
| Constraint baru terlalu ketat | Drop constraint atau add corrected constraint. |
| App rollback setelah schema expand | Seharusnya aman jika expand backward-compatible. |
| App rollback setelah contract | Berisiko; jangan contract sampai rollback window lewat. |

**Rule:** migration destructive harus ditunda sampai tidak ada kebutuhan rollback aplikasi versi lama.

---

## 30. Deployment Sequencing

### Safe sequence untuk breaking schema change

```text id="deployment-sequence"
Release A:
  - Expand schema
  - App masih pakai schema lama

Release B:
  - App dual-write / dual-read
  - Backfill data lama

Release C:
  - App read schema baru
  - Constraint/index baru divalidasi

Release D:
  - Stop writing old schema
  - Monitor

Release E:
  - Contract: drop old column/table/index
```

### Jangan lakukan ini

```text id="bad-deploy"
1. Rename/drop column
2. Deploy app baru
3. Berharap tidak ada app lama yang masih jalan
```

Rolling deploy membuat app lama dan baru bisa overlap. Schema harus tahan overlap itu.

---

## 31. View-Based Compatibility Pattern

Jika perlu mengganti table internal tetapi menjaga interface:

```sql id="compat-view"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_mgmt.v_case_file AS
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    reference_number,
    status,
    created_at,
    updated_at
FROM case_mgmt.case_file;
```

Aplikasi/reporting diarahkan ke view stable.

**Caution:** view membantu read compatibility, tetapi write compatibility melalui view butuh rule/trigger dan bisa menambah kompleksitas.

---

## 32. Trigger-Based Dual Write Pattern

Gunakan hanya jika benar-benar perlu menjaga dua struktur sinkron saat transisi.

```sql id="dual-write-trigger-function"
CREATE FUNCTION case_mgmt.sync_reference_number()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.reference_number IS NULL THEN
        NEW.reference_number := NEW.case_number;
    END IF;

    IF NEW.case_number IS NULL THEN
        NEW.case_number := NEW.reference_number;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
```

```sql id="dual-write-trigger"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_case_file_sync_reference_number
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON case_mgmt.case_file
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION case_mgmt.sync_reference_number();
```

**Rule:** trigger compatibility harus temporary dan punya tanggal/versi untuk dihapus. Jangan biarkan trigger migrasi menjadi business logic permanen tanpa review.

---

## 33. Large Table Migration Playbook

```text id="large-table-playbook"
1. Measure table size, index size, row count, write rate.
2. Identify operation: metadata-only, scan, rewrite, or index build.
3. Add guardrail: lock_timeout, statement_timeout.
4. Prefer expand-contract over rewrite.
5. Build indexes concurrently.
6. Backfill in batches.
7. Validate constraints after data is clean.
8. Analyze after large data changes.
9. Monitor locks, WAL, replication lag, bloat.
10. Contract only after rollback window.
```

PostgreSQL docs note that table/index rebuilds on large tables can take significant time and temporarily require as much as double disk space. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 34. Migration Review Checklist

| Area | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Compatibility | Apakah app lama dan baru bisa berjalan bersamaan? |
| Lock | Lock apa yang mungkin diambil? Berapa lama? |
| Timeout | Apakah ada `lock_timeout` dan `statement_timeout`? |
| Rewrite | Apakah operation rewrite table/index? |
| Scan | Apakah operation scan table besar? |
| Index | Apakah index dibuat `CONCURRENTLY` jika table live? |
| Constraint | Apakah bisa `NOT VALID` lalu validate? |
| Backfill | Apakah batch, idempotent, dan resumable? |
| Disk/WAL | Apakah cukup disk untuk rewrite/index/WAL? |
| Replication | Apakah migration bisa menyebabkan lag? |
| Rollback | Jika app rollback, schema masih kompatibel? |
| Forward-fix | Jika migration commit tapi salah, langkah perbaikannya apa? |
| Observability | Query lock/progress/row count tersedia? |
| Contract | Apakah drop/rename ditunda sampai aman? |

---

## 35. Common Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Rename/drop column di release yang sama dengan app change | App lama error | Expand-contract. |
| `ALTER TABLE ... SET NOT NULL` langsung pada table besar | Scan lama, update blocked | Backfill + `NOT VALID`/valid check pattern. |
| `CREATE INDEX` biasa di production hot table | Write blocked | `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`. |
| `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` di transaction block | Error | Jalankan terpisah. |
| Tidak cek invalid index | Update overhead diam-diam | Query `pg_index`, drop/retry. |
| Backfill jutaan row sekali jalan | WAL/bloat/lock besar | Batch + commit kecil. |
| Type change langsung pada table besar | Rewrite besar | Add new column + backfill + switch. |
| Drop old column terlalu cepat | Rollback app mustahil | Tunggu rollback window. |
| Constraint langsung validasi saat add | Lock/scan panjang | `NOT VALID`, validate kemudian. |
| Tidak set timeout | Migration menunggu lock dan membuat antrean | `lock_timeout`. |
| Tidak analyze setelah perubahan besar | Planner salah | `ANALYZE`. |
| Menganggap rollback script selalu aman | Data bisa hilang | Forward-fix dan backup strategy. |

---

## 36. Decision Matrix

| Kebutuhan | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Tambah optional field | `ADD COLUMN nullable` |
| Tambah required field | Add nullable → app write → backfill → validate → `NOT NULL` |
| Tambah FK ke table besar | `ADD FOREIGN KEY ... NOT VALID` → validate |
| Tambah unique constraint | `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` → `ADD CONSTRAINT USING INDEX` |
| Tambah check constraint | `ADD CHECK ... NOT VALID` → validate |
| Rename column | Add new column → dual-write → switch → drop old |
| Change type besar | Add new typed column → backfill → switch → drop old |
| Drop unused index | `DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY` |
| Hapus data lama time-series | Drop/detach partition, bukan delete massal |
| Move payload besar | New table → backfill → dual read/write → contract |
| Partition attach data besar | Pre-check constraint → attach |
| App rollback possible | Jangan contract dulu |
| Breaking migration | Split menjadi beberapa releases |

---

## 37. Minimal Production Migration Template

```sql id="migration-template"
-- 001_expand_case_file_external_ref.sql

BEGIN;

SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '1min';
SET LOCAL idle_in_transaction_session_timeout = '1min';

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS external_ref text;

COMMIT;
```

```sql id="migration-backfill-template"
-- 002_backfill_case_file_external_ref_batch.sql

UPDATE case_mgmt.case_file
SET external_ref = 'UNKNOWN'
WHERE external_ref IS NULL
  AND id >= :start_id
  AND id <  :end_id;
```

```sql id="migration-constraint-template"
-- 003_validate_external_ref.sql

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ADD CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_external_ref_not_null
CHECK (external_ref IS NOT NULL)
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT ck_case_file_external_ref_not_null;

ALTER TABLE case_mgmt.case_file
ALTER COLUMN external_ref SET NOT NULL;
```

```sql id="migration-index-template"
-- 004_index_external_ref.sql
-- Run outside transaction block.

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_case_file_external_ref
ON case_mgmt.case_file (external_ref);
```

---

## 38. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | DDL lock model | Bisa menilai risiko lock tiap migration. |
| 2 | Metadata-only vs scan vs rewrite | Bisa memprediksi dampak migration. |
| 3 | Expand-contract | Bisa membuat schema change backward-compatible. |
| 4 | Constraint `NOT VALID` | Bisa menambah constraint tanpa scan awal besar. |
| 5 | Concurrent index | Bisa membuat/drop index dengan downtime minimal. |
| 6 | Backfill batch | Bisa mengisi data lama tanpa mengganggu workload. |
| 7 | Large table migration | Bisa menghindari rewrite besar dengan column/table baru. |
| 8 | Deployment sequencing | Bisa mendukung rolling deploy dan rollback app. |
| 9 | Forward-fix strategy | Bisa memulihkan migration salah tanpa destructive rollback. |
| 10 | Review checklist | Bisa melakukan migration review seperti senior engineer/DBA. |
